# v2026.10.10

> **Draft for review**
>
> This is the proposed full release page, not a release announcement or a merge-ready page. Complete source and credit checks, release-wide accounting and independent final review remain pending. No final release totals are claimed here.
>
> The proposed code cutoff is [1cdcfc00d84a63bd93954592a7778b7f3ab5d47c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1cdcfc00d84a63bd93954592a7778b7f3ab5d47c), a release-branch snapshot rather than a claimed final tag.

OpenClaw v2026.10.10 brings Chat Details beside the conversation, an experimental Advisor that can suggest course corrections during a task, and split speech mode for browser Talk.

## Chat Details Panel

The main chat view now gathers session information and task progress in [Details](/web/control-ui/chat#session-details), moving the old progress and pull-request bars away from the space above the message box. On wide chat panes, Details opens beside the conversation without covering it; when it has opened automatically, it hides as the pane narrows. It can also be manually toggled with the Details button.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and details</summary>

- Details brings session information and progress together and frees space above the message box. [#161674](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161674), closing [#161650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161650). Contributions from [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski). See [chat Details](/web/control-ui/chat) and [progress cards](/tools/progress-card).
- Details groups the available session information and actions into sections you can open as needed. [#172436](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172436) addresses [#172429](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172429). Requested and design-reviewed by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). See [Control UI](/web/control-ui).
- Details opens automatically beside a wide conversation; an automatically opened panel hides when the chat pane narrows, but you can open it manually over a narrow conversation. [#170707](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170707), co-authored by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A session's sidebar hovercard now lets you inspect its attached automations and open a selected job without changing it. [#172240](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172240), closing [#172236](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172236). Contribution from [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The Subagents panel now shows running and finished swarm workers, including older children, and lets you read their transcripts without editing them. [#168162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168162). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The Layout menu now collects pane controls, and the wait indicator opens the active subagents. [PR #168845](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168845). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Open Session Details from the chat header without losing access to message actions. [PR #168494](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168494). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Expanded activity shows nested tool calls again, and image or video previews continue working after their pane is parked and reopened. [#169468](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169468), thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Switching agents resumes that agent's newest loaded personal conversation when one is available, and its activity indicator stays accurate ([#170134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170134)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The composer no longer repeats the working indicator; progress remains in the transcript and Stop remains available ([#170211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170211)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the co-authorship.
- Subagent activity shows total request time when its start is known, without an extra wait line above the message box ([#170325](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170325)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- After a parent update or reload, the wait timer reflects the latest completed parent turn ([#170397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170397)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- If a run ends after using tools but without a final reply, its saved progress remains visible with an Interrupted label. [#170705](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170705) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Related changes cover [saved streamed answers](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170697) and [recovery markers](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170699).
- See retry and fallback progress while a reply is running, then a failure notice with recovery actions if it ends unsuccessfully ([#170820](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170820), [reported issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170798)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A failure notice and its recovery actions return after a reload or reconnect during the run ([#170971](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170971)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Subagents stays closed until you open it, while child activity remains visible in the conversation ([#171167](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171167)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Details opens sections with pull requests or automations, omits sections confirmed empty and remembers sections you collapsed. [#171533](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171533) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Activity summaries keep the same description when expanded, with source links available even while collapsed. [#172573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172573). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Saved agent progress remains visible while tools run and after reloading an active conversation. [#172585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172585). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Chat status and the completed-turn recap now put token counts before elapsed time. [#172549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172549). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Sessions awaiting approval fit on one sidebar line without hiding the approval indicator. [#172618](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172618). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Details shows the supplied workspace label or, without one, its directory name; Copy path still gives you the full path. [#172657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172657). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Details leaves transcript actions and the question footer reachable, with less empty space beneath the conversation ([commit 320b088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/320b0884c23e40c8765e5417c42b9636d7133a4b), [commit dc0ef48](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/dc0ef48154f58877a50b29d2f9bc575420fccbb7), [commit 7e256f7](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7e256f7302d401bcbfa41347bf3fe2b88a729090)).
- Opt-in failure narration keeps the task's title for OpenClaw MCP commands, [#168132](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168132). Thanks to [ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559).
- Control UI chat no longer shows raw command-completion notices, while the agent still receives them, [#168236](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168236). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Claude CLI no longer reports a tool as started when interrupted input becomes plain text, [#168578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168578). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- With model-call diagnostics enabled, request counts and timing now include background titles, activity recaps and plugin model calls, [#168411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168411). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Completed global-scope commands remain visible to their owner in Processes in [#169302](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169302). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Subagent wait indicators and controls stay accurate while the Control UI loads overlapping child lists. [#167672](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167672). Thanks to [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Claude CLI can show how many edits are being prepared while tool input streams, before execution begins. [#167789](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167789), [#167742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167742). Thanks to [ZengWen-DT](https://github.com/ZengWen-DT), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [umeranjum17](https://github.com/umeranjum17).
- Running subagent rows now show current tool activity and an available count of calls. [PR #169719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169719). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A built-in session fork shows unfinished tool calls as pending and retains finished results in later forks. [#171580](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171580), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Long streamed file writes and edits through OpenAI-compatible models show live progress again. [#168614](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168614). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

## Advisor

Experimental [Advisor](/web/control-ui/feature-reference) can check an agent's ongoing work and suggest a correction if it finds a clear wrong turn, such as repeating an unsuccessful approach or drifting from your request. It is off by default, uses extra model calls when it reviews a task, and can miss a problem or suggest an unnecessary change.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and details</summary>

Enable Advisor in Settings → Agents & Tools → Labs. You can adjust its review intervals in Configure. Advice does not run tools, approve actions or guarantee that the agent's work is correct.

- When enabled, experimental Advisor checks ongoing work and may offer a brief correction when a review finds a clear wrong turn. [#169564](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169564) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

</details>

## Talk

Browser Talk adds [split speech mode](/nodes/talk/realtime-sessions#split-speech-in-the-browser), so spoken requests can go through the agent and conversation you are already using. It uses your configured speech and transcription services, including local transcription, while keeping that agent's normal tool permissions and approvals.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and details</summary>

Configure speech output and transcription before using split Talk, and allow microphone access in a secure browser context. Split mode has no realtime voice switching or camera input; leaving it unset keeps existing realtime Talk.

- Read aloud and dictation now use configured speech services, while split browser Talk sends spoken requests to the current chat agent. [#170343](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170343), [#45508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/45508), [#113049](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/113049). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@mcfex](https://github.com/mcfex) for the self-hosted speech report and [@mike-nott](https://github.com/mike-nott) for the browser Talk report.
- If Talk loses its voice connection after an agent request was accepted, the answer can still finish and appear in the conversation; pressing Stop still cancels it. [#171377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171377), [#171310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171310). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the fix and [@jonathanlindsay](https://github.com/jonathanlindsay) for the report.
- Authenticated Talk follows ordinary tool permissions and approvals instead of adding a second spoken confirmation for actions already permitted. [#159697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159697). Thanks [@brokemac79](https://github.com/brokemac79).
- Spoken Talk requests no longer stop immediately just because the voice connection finished setting up. [#170083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170083). Thanks [@brokemac79](https://github.com/brokemac79).
- A second Talk question after tool use no longer hits the identified message-ordering error on affected Anthropic routes, while updated instructions remain available. [#171267](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171267), [#171227](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171227). Thanks [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@marleyman5000-boop](https://github.com/marleyman5000-boop).
- Browser Talk now keeps new spoken questions from Gemini 3.8 Live and Extended Thinking alongside their answers after Stop and reload; previously lost speech cannot be recovered by this fix. [#171506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171506), [#171505](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171505). Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Silence that produces an empty transcript no longer appears as a transcription-service error, and configured fallbacks can continue. [#171514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171514), [#171512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171512). Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

## Folder linking

Start a conversation in an [existing folder](/nodes/session-hosting#work-in-an-existing-device-folder) on a compatible paired computer, even if the folder is not a Git repository. The agent works with the files in place and does not remove the folder when the conversation ends. These conversations stay on that computer, without cloud dispatch, Gateway Files or attachments.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and details</summary>

- The paired computer's `nodeHost.workerRuns.folders` setting controls access. Leave it unset for existing directories, specify permitted roots to limit selection, or use an empty list to disable device-folder sessions. [#169722](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169722) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

</details>

## ChatGPT and Claude connections

The optional [MCP Relay](/plugins/mcp-relay) lets you connect ChatGPT or Claude to your OpenClaw conversations without opening an internet-facing Gateway port or handing the app your Gateway password. Choose each app's access in Connected apps and revoke it there when needed. The relay can read content passing through it, so grant access only to apps and a relay you trust.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and details</summary>

The plugin is off by default. Pair through Gateway sign-in or a single-use code. ChatGPT can also open the full OpenClaw interface when the Gateway and relay support it. Full role access can include administration and approvals. See [MCP Relay setup](/plugins/mcp-relay).

- The relay adds opt-in pairing and a Connected apps grant, while trusted plugins provide remote Control UI access under that person's permissions without sharing Gateway credentials. [#168233](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168233), [#170540](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170540). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

## Event-triggered automations

An automation can now start when a connected MCP server reports a [supported event](/automation/mcp-events#create-an-event-automation), rather than waiting for a timer to check for changes. Give the event instructions for your agent and the connected app need not stay open. This opt-in integration supports an experimental MCP Events profile and needs a public HTTPS callback; expose only its callback route, not the rest of OpenClaw.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and details</summary>

[MCP Events setup](/automation/mcp-events) covers supported servers and callback routing. Only the OpenAI webhook profile is supported, not every draft Events feature. A received event is not a completed agent task, and an event lost upstream may not be recoverable. Before downgrading, remove event jobs and check database compatibility; you may need a compatible pre-upgrade backup.

- Supported MCP events can start an automation with its existing instructions. Forward only `/plugins/mcp-events/callback/` to the Gateway. [#165399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165399), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

## Conversation permissions

Conversation owners can now set [separate send and receive choices](/concepts/session-tool#communication-preferences) for messages between conversations. Choose Always, Ask to approve each new message, or Never to block new messages in either direction. Both default to Always, and older OpenClaw versions will not enforce these choices if you downgrade.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and details</summary>

These choices govern new messages between conversations, not messages from people or external channels. Already accepted messages, requested replies and delegated work still complete under their existing obligations, and other access restrictions still apply. See [conversation communication preferences](/concepts/session-tool#communication-preferences).

- Session settings now let you allow, individually approve or block new messages sent to and received from other conversations. Human input and external-channel messages are unchanged. [#162316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162316). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

## Multiple Microsoft Teams bots

Different OpenClaw agents can now use [separate Microsoft Teams bot identities](/channels/msteams/configuration#multiple-bot-accounts) from one Gateway, so each can appear under its own bot name instead of sharing one identity.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and details</summary>

Set up an Azure Bot registration, credentials and callback endpoint for each bot; an existing single-bot setup keeps its route. See [multiple Teams bot accounts](/channels/msteams/configuration#multiple-bot-accounts).

- Multiple Teams bots can be bound to different agents, with a separate callback route for each named account and the original endpoint retained for the default bot. [#112811](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/112811), [request #71058](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/71058). Thanks to [@jimmypuckett](https://github.com/jimmypuckett), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@ashukla147](https://github.com/ashukla147).

</details>

## Before updating

- Regular Windows Tauri companion installers and automatic updates are paused for this release. Opt-in desktop-test builds and the separate Windows Hub release are unaffected. [Publication change](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173656).
- [Existing public conversation links](/web/urls#public-session-transcripts) now show additional progress, tool summaries and images. Review or disable shares you do not want to expose that content; image contents are not redacted.
- If you manage Node yourself, use Node 24.22.1 or newer within Node 24, or Node 26.12.1 or newer, for the certificate-security updates. See [Node requirements](/install/node-compatibility).
- Keep a verified backup before database upgrades if you may need to roll back. Restore the matching backup and older OpenClaw build together; rolling back the program alone does not roll back your data. See [backup and recovery](/cli/backup).
- Review conversations and [raw local logs](/gateway/security/secrets-and-storage#logs-and-transcripts) before sharing them because they can contain credentials. [Incognito](/concepts/session#incognito-sessions) keeps OpenClaw conversation content in memory, but Codex-runtime submissions may still be saved locally; use the OpenClaw runtime if that logging is unacceptable. Providers, tools and plugins may retain their own data.
- Some older Mac pnpm installations need a [one-time manual upgrade](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171797). Restore any truncated multiline environment values before reinstalling the service.
- Matrix shared-account installations should stop older writers and upgrade together. The [unsafe-save recovery procedure](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160707) can lose keys received since the last successful snapshot.

  </details>

- Choose where a personal background appears and adjust how strongly it shows through. Hiding your image keeps it for later; Remove deletes it.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Appearance offers placement, presentation and visibility controls for personal backgrounds. Hiding an image keeps it; Remove deletes it. Contrast accessibility settings suppress decorative artwork. [#168301](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168301), closing [#153797](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153797). Contributions from [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). See [Appearance settings](/web/control-ui/settings).

  </details>

- Choose Swedish for the Control UI, with English shown wherever a translation is not yet available.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Select Swedish in the Control UI or use a Swedish browser locale. Untranslated interface text falls back to English, and the docs translation pipeline is prepared for Swedish. [#62063](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/62063), [docs #270](https://github.com/openclaw/docs/pull/270). Thanks [@yeager](https://github.com/yeager) and [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- Enable experimental speech bubbles in Settings → Labs, then use View → Speech bubbles to choose the layout for each conversation. The setting is off by default, and your per-conversation choice is remembered in that browser.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Enable speech bubbles in Settings → Labs, then choose the view separately for each conversation. Commentary and tool activity have distinct presentation in bubble view. [#169347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169347). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).

  </details>

- [Publicly shared conversation pages](/web/urls#public-session-transcripts) now look more like a chat, with clearer previews when you share an eligible link.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Refresh the existing public conversation page with a chat-style layout, light and dark themes, and copy controls. [#168156](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168156). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Eligible public links show a session preview or recorded work receipt, with a generic fallback when the publication is unavailable. [#169527](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169527), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Find document downloads in both chat and Files. File details you expanded stay open when you switch tabs.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Visible document downloads in the chat transcript and Files panel. [PR #171171](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171171), [issue #171098](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171098). Thanks [LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [arquiteto-raiz](https://github.com/arquiteto-raiz).
  - Preserve reader file disclosure state across tab changes ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0ed829e7fe4e178ea9e4693427c70e73139290d8)).

  </details>

### Performance and diagnostics

- Checking on helpers or recovering their runs no longer has to scan every unrelated saved run.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Checking or waiting for a helper no longer scans unrelated saved runs. [#170998](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170998). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restart recovery selects the relevant subagent children without repeatedly scanning the full registry. [#171067](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171067), with related startup work in [#171046](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171046). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- A helper can tell you it finished without waiting for its token count; the completion report marks missing usage as unknown.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - A helper can report completion even if its token usage has not arrived yet. If a helper started during a parent reset cannot access memory, start it again from the current parent session. [#170400](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170400), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- When enabled for a model, your active conversation and manual compaction get priority over background work.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Interactive turns and manual compaction receive higher priority than background work when the per-model option is enabled. [PR #129434](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/129434). Thanks to [m888m](https://github.com/m888m) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- [Troubleshooting tools](/gateway/diagnostics) now show where slow requests spend time and give clearer clues when something fails. If you enabled content tracing, review that setting before upgrading because traces can include additional redacted conversation data.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Add preparation-stage timings to slow chat-send diagnostics and skip redundant skill inventories for recently attested workspaces. [#169307](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169307) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Analyze saved CPU profiles from a source checkout, including the JavaScript callers of native work, in [#170904](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170904). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - CLI backend plugin registration warns when `modelProvider` is missing and explains how to bind a provider. Standalone backends remain supported. See the [plugin guide](/plugins/cli-backend-plugins). [PR #173000](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173000), [issue #172969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172969). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [xtimor](https://github.com/xtimor).
  - Shows labeled provider request time per credential in model connection checks and avoids creating an agent session for the check. [#169554](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169554), [issue #169066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169066). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@dabase](https://github.com/dabase).
  - Adds paired request and response metadata to prompt-cache drop diagnostics without including prompt content or raw keys. Available fields depend on the transport. [#169630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169630) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Give response callbacks the HTTP status and headers of rejected OpenAI and Azure SDK requests. [PR #169688](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169688). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Processing and estimated wait are available in model-call diagnostics and Gateway stability output when the provider supplies valid final statistics. [#170369](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170369), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Uraroga](https://github.com/Uraroga) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137557).
  - OpenAI tier downgrade logs include response and account-route details that can be matched with cache-drop warnings. [#170949](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170949). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Distinguish steering and queued follow-up waits from startup in slow `chat.send` logs and Prometheus metrics. [#169006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169006), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Worktree preparation reports why template reuse falls back. The first template still needs to be built. [#169239](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169239). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include a bounded, redacted error tail when spawn-broker shutdown fails ([#170829](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170829)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Slow pre-acknowledgement chat requests log admission and persistence subphase timings to help operators locate waits. [#171250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171250), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Opted-in tracing can include the user's message even if no model call follows, and the model's final answer when one is available. Gemini CLI does not provide its composed prompt on run-level traces. [#156603](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156603), [related request #144294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/144294). Thanks to [ryan-dyer-sp](https://github.com/ryan-dyer-sp).
  - Extend OpenTelemetry message-duration histogram buckets through one hour. [#128785](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128785). Thanks to [patrick-erichsen](https://github.com/patrick-erichsen).
  - Identify a confirmed foreign process holding the Gateway port in status and channel diagnostics, with guidance for resolving the conflict. [#161106](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161106), [reported in #161024](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161024). Thanks to [yashas-13](https://github.com/yashas-13), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - Routine successful Gateway activity moves to debug logs, and Prometheus gains egress and tool-catalog counters. [#170509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170509). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid another Gateway-blocking heap scan when starting an operator CPU capture on Node 26 with the stall flight recorder already active. Other captures can still scan, and Bun uses the inspector backend. [#170932](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170932) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Add finer Prometheus histogram buckets for Gateway RPC latency estimates. The additional series increase scrape and render cost. [#171062](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171062) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Expose SQLite writer request phases in Prometheus, including time spent transferring framed requests and results. [#171082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171082) by [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Attribute more Git launches and count streaming workspace inventory launches in the child-process metric. [#171465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171465) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Identify more child-process launch owners, including agent execution and helpers. [#171582](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171582) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include collected objects in a Gateway heap profile with `includeCollected`. [#171686](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171686) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Garbage collection metrics include collection kind and main-thread or instrumented worker labels. Workers without the telemetry connection and other unregistered isolates are outside this coverage. [#171923](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171923) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include underlying causes and storage codes in post-ready Gateway maintenance warnings. [#172138](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172138). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include queue wait and available holder information in slow agent database reservation warnings. [#172459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172459). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway and node service stop and uninstall errors include status and recovery hints in text and JSON output. [#176335](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176335). Thanks [@555aaditya](https://github.com/555aaditya) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Stale Gateway cleanup warnings include the reason cleanup failed. [#174590](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174590). Thanks [@shoaibulhaque](https://github.com/shoaibulhaque) and [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).

  - Provide a source-checkout report for slow session updates. [#171148](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171148) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show phase timings for slow cloud worker dispatch when diagnostics are enabled. [#171749](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171749) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Expose worker message counts and known payload bytes by direction and kind in Prometheus. [#171940](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171940) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Usage reports and conversation lists can return available results without waiting for unrelated histories or background work.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Cold Usage partial reports leave uncached sessions pending without waiting for transcript scans ([#170093](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170093)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use less temporary memory for fresh ordered session pages. [#171968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171968), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Kept an empty usage refresh from creating an unused agent database and disturbing a session event handoff. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/927c8b25d016f4fb54ff55c9d6fa3fc004c23716) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Usage history rebuilds use compact monthly projections and yield to foreground writes. [#169816](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169816), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Usage reports can catch up after startup database preparation without another transcript update. [#169846](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169846), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Background usage totals refresh after a quiet period, while explicit usage and cost reads flush pending data. [#171378](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171378) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Requested session totals refresh ahead of subsequent unrelated bulk work. [#171480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171480) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Concurrent usage refreshes can commit while a report is being prepared. [#171433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171433) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce cold usage-refresh overhead for large session inventories and add more useful database worker diagnostics. [#171868](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171868), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Refresh background usage and estimated-cost totals according to elapsed time after a clock correction ([#184212](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184212)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Batch large session usage reads to reduce failures from worker input pressure during concurrent reports ([#183407](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183407)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse transcript preview facts for session lists and remember the next cron-session expiry. [#171449](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171449) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse unaffected session rows after unrelated plugin reloads while refreshing agents whose relevant settings changed. [#171519](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171519) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse session and catalog display data during repeated requests. [#171724](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171724) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid loading chat presentation code at session maintenance worker startup until it is needed. [#171697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171697) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Encode transcript messages once for broadcasts to multiple subscribers. [#171701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171701) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Improves health and status responsiveness while large fleets prepare session information. [#168649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168649) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT).
  - Moves agent-session preparation reads off the Gateway's main thread during concurrent requests. [#168755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168755) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduces database writer contention during transcript maintenance and names known long-running tasks in stall warnings. [#168801](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168801) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Release superseded session-list presentation data sooner to reduce retained Gateway memory during session changes. [#169056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169056), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Resolve session targets without waiting for unrelated display rows to rebuild or succeed. [#169082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169082), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Resolve full session IDs and labels from matching metadata without waiting for unrelated sessions. [#169148](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169148), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Catalog updates refresh affected agents' session information without rematerializing unrelated agents' sessions. [#169210](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169210). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session-list polling and creation reuse roster and model information where appropriate. [#169263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169263). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Committed session-list notifications can finish after their producing task ends, including queued Swarm launches. [PR #170231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170231). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Connected clients can receive committed session rows after the request that changed them finishes or is cancelled, instead of falling back to a list refresh. [PR #170281](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170281). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Project, sharing-member, and GitHub option pickers avoid redundant reads and overlap independent work. [PR #170324](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170324). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session lists and activity recaps retain less data for large session inventories. [#170517](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170517). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Return retained observed-project snapshots while discovery refreshes in the background. On a cold listing, session-only repositories appear after discovery ([#170887](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170887)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Previously discovered session catalogs return available pages while providers refresh. Cold discovery retains its wait budget. [#171263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171263), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session ID lookup loads only the selected saved entry and checks that it has not been replaced. [#171326](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171326), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session listing avoids redundant validation work on prepared rows. [#171731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171731) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Sidebar session-list packing uses fewer temporary allocations, following earlier list work in [#171724](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171724) and [#171731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171731). [#171860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171860) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse agent roster and row-target work during sidebar session-list selection. [#172474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172474). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce repeated allocations in session-list readiness checks. [#172485](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172485). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Saved-session `chat.metadata` requests with `includeModels=false` avoid waiting for unused worker placement information. [#175606](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175606). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Handle unreadable rows in session listings and release queued collector holds after their parent ends ([#187589](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187589)). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).

  </details>

- Typing the first message in New Session stays responsive even with a large model catalog.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Reduce repeated work in the New Session cloud environment picker. [#170651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170651) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - New Session typing avoids repeated work while editing a draft. [#171511](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171511) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- Side chat and project choices can open while unrelated conversations are still being prepared.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Restore Side chat without waiting for unrelated session writes ([#170968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170968)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let project listings proceed during unrelated state writes ([#171069](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171069)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let project and session-catalog pickers respond without waiting for unrelated session row preparation after a restart. [#171714](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171714), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway client documentation explains how to reconcile an optional in-flight run snapshot after replaying cursor catch-up messages. [#171183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171183), thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [NianJiuZst](https://github.com/NianJiuZst).
  - Git repository identity workers avoid loading unrelated preparation code, reducing retained memory in the measured workload. [#171926](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171926) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
