Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jonathanlindsay](https://github.com/jonathanlindsay), [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225) and [@Card4Philippe](https://github.com/Card4Philippe).

  </details>

- [Memory indexing](/concepts/memory-builtin#how-indexing-works) now batches conversation updates after a quiet period instead of repeatedly working during active turns. Recent conversation text may therefore take longer to appear in memory search.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Growing session transcripts retain unchanged indexed chunks and only embed and write changed content. [#103201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/103201), with thanks to [wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [wangbaobisheng](https://github.com/wangbaobisheng) and [Hawkeye2611](https://github.com/Hawkeye2611). See [#40919](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/40919).
  - Managed local non-batch indexing limits concurrent file jobs to one. [#137484](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137484), with thanks to [shojikumaru](https://github.com/shojikumaru), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [wtom](https://github.com/wtom). See [#136175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/136175).
  - Targeted session updates avoid loading unrelated forgotten-session records. [#168098](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168098), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). See the separate related report [#167838](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167838) from [nkarkare](https://github.com/nkarkare).
  - Index setup and rebuilds move more database work away from the Gateway thread. [#168860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168860), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Completed agent database opening requests no longer remain unnecessarily in memory. [#168349](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168349), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session transcripts are indexed after a quiet period, with pending work deferred while turns are running. Searches use the published index and do not force pending transcripts to be indexed. [#170404](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170404), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Handled session indexing failures are retried during idle periods, with older pending changes retained. [#170719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170719), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A failed rebuild gets its final automatic retry after the rebuild cooldown, while memory maintenance avoids redundant cache and index reads. [#170792](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170792), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh agents can initialize indexing so later session turns become searchable. [#170220](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170220), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recurring memory sync reuses committed index information to reduce database work on the Gateway thread. [#169058](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169058), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Memory searches reuse read-only connections to reduce setup work. [#170183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170183), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session indexing batches changes and preserves unrelated session caches. [#170302](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170302), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Published memory-index reads reduce contention with concurrent writes. [#170407](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170407), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Memory startup and built-in status do less database work on the main thread. [#171322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171322), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Large, mostly unchanged memory sources reuse unchanged vectors, avoiding work that can delay conversation writes. [#171402](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171402), thanks to [salad-maker](https://github.com/salad-maker).
  - Memory retrieval workers retain less memory while preserving search and Forget behavior. [#172392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172392), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Bounded temporary embedding-cache growth during full rebuilds while preserving cache reuse ([#183089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183089), [issue #182886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182886)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
  - Background memory indexing waits until its agent database is ready and checks access before proceeding ([commit 51d97ef](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/51d97efee0de2bdf39d76278a24d30c7d343317c)).
  - Kept pending indexing work after an expected background rebuild interruption without consuming an error retry ([commit 71692b0](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/71692b03a277790e5781705dcdfeb6f4ba0c810f)).
  - Reduced unnecessary memory retention after native agent database opening ([commit 06e6524](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/06e6524335e7eed5f44dbd3521c0bb5b60a8322d)).

  </details>

- Project lists and workspace preparation avoid waits on unrelated conversations creating or archiving worktrees.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Return a conflict instead of overwriting a session created by a competing request. [#169625](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169625) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let ordinary session and transcript reads proceed while an unrelated write is busy. [#169838](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169838) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Project listings and the project picker no longer fail just because an unrelated session changes during discovery. [#170824](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170824) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Managed-worktree branch suggestions can remain cached for up to 30 seconds. The chosen ref is validated when a worktree is created. [#170626](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170626) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). The related [#138555](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138555), reported by [@aleps001](https://github.com/aleps001), concerned a child-answer problem fixed earlier.
  - Concurrent sessions can read an actively retained warm worktree template without waiting for a mutation lock. [#170903](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170903) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Managed repository maintenance can remove redundant Git packs, including duplicates previously skipped by consolidation limits. [#170935](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170935) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Worktree creation errors include redacted timing details when preparation stalls. [#170835](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170835) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Slow session update logs distinguish archive and other request phases. [#170961](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170961) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid redundant history downloads when preparing pinned commits in existing partial clones. [#170970](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170970) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Related pack consolidation work in [#170935](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170935).
  - Reduce capacity admission waits for concurrent repository-backed worktree creation. [#170983](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170983) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Share pending template preparation waits among concurrent creates in the same process. [#171075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171075) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse unchanged branch inventories in repositories with large pack directories. [#171604](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171604) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Limit repeated Git housekeeping during frequent worktree archives while cleanup continues. [#171680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171680) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep Gateway Git inspection local in partial clones. Optional facts may be unavailable when their objects are missing, while explicit transfers remain available. [#171799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171799) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Allow eligible partial-clone pack consolidation after a deferred snapshot without fetching promised objects. [#171841](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171841) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Detect the Git object format directly during source-only worktree preparation, including repositories without a HEAD commit. [#171856](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171856) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - Let unrelated sessions write between commands in an append batch. [#171225](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171225) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Agent preparation and long conversations use less memory and repeat less work, especially when many agents or models are configured.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Move bundled inference and Models logout authentication storage work off the Gateway thread. If the same profile reconnects while logout cleanup is still running, you may need to select that account again. [#171510](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171510), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid copying stored session metadata that is excluded from model context when preparing follow-up turns. [#171574](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171574), thanks to [joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
  - Reduce memory used to prepare optional session forks for long conversations. If fork capture fails, the parent request can continue. [#171683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171683), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep lean local-model Tool Search directories compact while leaving full descriptions and schemas searchable. [#171588](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171588), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [chitowntony](https://github.com/chitowntony).
  - Avoid loading unrelated memory and context plugins for simple completions, image descriptions and one-shot decisions. [#171822](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171822), thanks to [htuyer121](https://github.com/htuyer121) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [gorkem2020](https://github.com/gorkem2020) for tracing the affected paths, and [Cyb3rb1ade](https://github.com/Cyb3rb1ade) for the [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/163029).
  - Move full Linux Codex cleanup process scans off the Gateway thread. [#171865](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171865), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce agent roster and related chat-read work for large model and agent configurations ([#169123](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169123)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Moves GitHub discovery into the background so repository-backed agent turns can reach the provider sooner. Tool visibility may briefly reflect the previous account during a refresh. [#169488](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169488) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Warm turns reuse a session settings snapshot and local computer discovery facts. [#170185](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170185), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Large agent fleets avoid repeated session-path and configuration work after Gateway readiness. [#170612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170612), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT).

  - Reuse validation results for unchanged plugin settings across configuration revisions. [#171592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171592) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - [Code Mode](/tools/code-mode/quickstart) uses less processing time and temporary memory to prepare its tools across repeated turns. [#187161](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187161). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Cached conversation lists no longer keep obsolete lists in memory after conversation details change. [#187111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187111). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Model catalogs use less memory when many workspaces and provider models are configured. [#187179](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187179). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- Publish PR now points to the credential, verification or workspace problem that is stopping your pull request.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Distinguish credentials, verification and workspace problems in Publish PR, [#168223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168223). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

### Cloud workspaces

- Cloud projects can use supported setup commands from a [Dev Container file committed with the project](/gateway/cloud-workers/environment-file). The worker also recognizes common JavaScript lockfiles when installing dependencies.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  Only selected Dev Container setup commands are supported; values in the recipe do not become environment variables for your conversation.

  - Cloud recipes support selected Dev Container setup and lifecycle commands, plus pnpm, Yarn, npm and available Bun lockfile detection. Recipe environment values do not propagate to session processes. [#170862](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170862) · [Environment file guide](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/7e15622f37bd0ced194a3b0fccacb4ad6bc78d85/docs/gateway/cloud-workers/environment-file.md) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- A later empty-workspace cloud session can start on a [prepared worker](/gateway/cloud-workers/warm-images#ready-workers) when a compatible one is ready. The first still needs a new machine, and keeping ready workers running incurs provider charges.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  A prepared worker must already be available within your configured pool limits.

  - Ready-worker reuse for empty workspaces follows the configured pool limits and requires a compatible reserve to be ready. [#170929](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170929) · Related work [#168371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168371) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Linux cloud workers can install the [Node version a project needs](/gateway/cloud-workers/setup-and-bundle-installation#automatic-linux-node-toolchains) during setup. Package-manager preparation also handles more pinned projects.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Linux workers can install a checksum-verified Node runtime and select a repository-declared version. Linux images need compatible system tools and HTTPS access to nodejs.org, while macOS and Windows still need Node supplied by the image or setup. [#170796](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170796) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use global Crabbox settings for cloud worker discovery and suppress Corepack download prompts in detected pnpm and Yarn preparation. Project-scoped commands retain their workspace. [#171706](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171706) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Run fallback Corepack and its package manager on the worker Node for affected pinned repositories while keeping the requested Node for repository scripts. Package manager engine checks see the worker Node. [#171147](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171147) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Clarify cloud setup, repository preparation, snapshots and reuse in the documentation. [#171767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171767) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Correct the worker placement reference to explain that a concurrent placement change can reject a turn without an automatic retry. [#171205](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171205) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Cloud setup can use [operator-approved secrets](/gateway/cloud-workers/environment-file#setup-secrets) to reach trusted services without putting credentials in the repository or its saved worker image. Bind only trusted destinations because a service can return a credential in its response. Existing `setupEnv` profiles need `openclaw doctor --fix` and destination-bound stored secrets before new provisioning; this requires supported Linux workers and clients.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Approved cloud setup integrations can send credentials only to their bound destinations, including services that use HTTP Basic authentication. [#170810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170810) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Repositories can request an approved secret name, but cannot choose its value or destination. Missing bindings fail rather than exposing plaintext. The feature needs a supported Linux coordinator and compatible Crabbox helper and proxy-aware clients; bind only trusted services because a response can return credentials. [#171073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171073) · Related work [#170810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170810) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

### Setup, updates and documentation

- On a fresh supported desktop install, [setup](/start/onboarding-overview) now guides you through running OpenClaw locally or connecting to a remote Gateway.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Fresh Linux and Windows desktop setup prepares a local Gateway and opens onboarding, with manual recovery choices when needed. [#169512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169512) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Windows x64 desktop builds support local CLI and Gateway setup, a regular installer and the Windows stable update channel. Remote connections remain available if the signed runtime is missing. [#169522](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169522) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh Mac setup shows progress and offers guided dashboard onboarding when the Gateway supports automatic setup, with a manual fallback. [#169568](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169568) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac setup follows the dashboard through window changes and keeps the native setup page visible when an installation step is removed. [#170613](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170613) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac setup and status messages gain translations across 21 locales. [#170221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170221).
  - Local app-hosted setup saves a usable Gateway token and reduces runtime preparation and connection delays. If a required token cannot be saved, setup stops with a retryable error. [#169403](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169403). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh Macs can show AI setup choices sooner. [#169472](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169472). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Standalone macOS Bun reports missing or incompatible SQLite libraries with setup guidance. [#168564](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168564). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh macOS profiles can start the Gateway and continue automatic setup through supervised retries. [#173246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173246) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh Windows setup no longer mistakes its own state initialization for another process performing offline maintenance. [#176174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176174), [issue #176163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176163) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@tjarrau-dev](https://github.com/tjarrau-dev).
  - Fresh local setup in non-release Linux and Windows desktop builds uses matching staged core and Codex packages without npm on the user's machine. The first verified copy can take several minutes. [#171117](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171117) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Linux and Windows desktop apps reclaim unreferenced runtime files after a healthy activation while retaining current and previous selections and active references. [#171489](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171489) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Bundled desktop runtimes omit unused development files. The measured signed Apple Silicon Mac bundle was 62.5 MB smaller; the same staging change applies to bundled Linux and Windows desktop packages. [#172054](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172054) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Windows desktop setup guides recommend Windows Hub and document Tauri as an alternative. [#171114](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171114) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The Mac installation guide includes architecture-specific installer links pinned to version 2026.10.3 and the releases index for newer builds. [#171560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171560) Thanks to [@brianmcguire](https://github.com/brianmcguire) and [@lkanowski](https://github.com/lkanowski).
  - Packaged non-release Mac builds use same-commit official plugins for setup, avoiding Codex plugin and core version mismatches. Large plugin bundles can fall back to staging Codex alone, and explicit source choices remain respected. [#170776](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170776) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- You can make supported settings and [provider changes](/concepts/model-providers/control-ui-and-keys#configure-providers-in-the-control-ui) without stopping the local Gateway. Some first-time setup and CLI credential commands still require it to be stopped, and saved changes may need a reload or restart before they take effect.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Protects CLI model-auth and MCP configuration changes from conflicting writes while the Gateway is running. Commands without a supported live path require stopping it first. [#168135](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168135). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Route basic agent creation through the running Gateway and protect direct state writes from conflicting owners. Later provider, configuration and baseline-setup changes add their supported live routes. [#168417](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168417). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Debug proxy captures follow the active Gateway or exclusive offline owner, [#168459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168459). Update an older Gateway or stop it before offline capture. Run the Gateway when proxy child commands change other OpenClaw state. Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Supported CLI settings changes can be saved while the Gateway runs. An updated CLI talking to an older v2026.10.7 Gateway may report failure even after saving a reload-off change, so check the saved setting before retrying. [#182951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182951). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Allow affected `session.store` configuration writes ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/943da4fc9366e2acc1710542897b4e229064c6c1)).
  - Apply agent creation and updates while another agent is being deleted, without requiring another save or restart. [#171459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171459), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - CLI secret changes use the running local Gateway, or work offline when it is stopped. If that Gateway cannot be reached or is too old, the write is refused. Stop it before a manual model catalog refresh. [#168108](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168108). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject malformed UTF-8 piped into model token and API-key commands before it can overwrite saved credentials. [#167251](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167251). Thanks to [Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Disable external HTTP hooks at startup when their secret cannot be resolved, while letting the Gateway start. A rejected reload keeps the previous valid hook settings. Supply the secret to the Gateway service and reload or restart, or disable the hooks. [Hook configuration](/gateway/config-hooks), [#165019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165019). Thanks to [eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - Show or hide a secret without submitting the prompt, while keeping the typed value. [#169925](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169925). Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
  - Config and root-level private writes preserve existing shared state-root permissions while keeping sensitive files and private child directories private. [#171869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171869), following the report in [#169011](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169011). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Config saves involving secret references can acknowledge a change already applied by the watcher instead of waiting for a timeout. [#171922](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171922) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), following work in [#109792](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/109792) and [#169249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169249).
  - Preserve provider setup results and activation guidance when temporary credential cleanup fails. [#172379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172379), reported in [#172368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172368). Thanks to [@skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@giannis88](https://github.com/giannis88).
  - CLI configuration writes with reload off save without directly restarting an updated Gateway. Older Gateways may still restart. [#184392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184392) Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).
  - CLI settings writes accepted for restart now report success and a restart message; the new setting is active only after that restart. [#184532](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184532) Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).

  </details>

- An update can bring the Gateway back up before optional Doctor checks finish; repairs needed for startup still happen first.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Adopted update candidates defer eligible optional Doctor checks until after readiness. Required repairs remain before startup, and unusable selectors fall back to inline lint. [#171374](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171374), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update reports retain optional Doctor information and warnings, and deferred-check advice includes `openclaw doctor --lint --all --severity-min info`. Readiness errors remain errors. [#171420](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171420), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Online schema checks avoid a full staging copy for eligible databases. Recovery and incomplete WAL cases can still need a snapshot. [#171984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171984) by [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- Getting-started, installation, backup and troubleshooting guides now explain their steps and recovery choices more clearly, while keeping existing links working.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Clarify database reference guidance for search freshness, reconnect ownership and schema 22 rollback. [#171181](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171181) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Operator guides clarify worker cache behavior and when batched usage totals refresh. [#171393](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171393), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Document the existing Poolside provider and separate ACP setup. [#132896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/132896), [related report #121635](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/121635). Thanks to [vmbbz](https://github.com/vmbbz), [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), and [amyduquette](https://github.com/amyduquette).
  - Reorganize the earlier 2026.10.1 release page, Markdown mirror, archive links, and contribution record for easier browsing. [#167818](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167818). Thanks to [hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  These wording changes are by [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  - Clarifies getting started, installation, provider, update, and security guides while preserving existing links. [#168693](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168693)
  - Makes CLI, recovery, backup, and troubleshooting guides easier to follow. [#168694](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168694)
  - Explains concepts and automation behavior more directly. [#168695](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168695)
  - Clarifies Gateway setup, operation, and recovery guides. [#168696](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168696)
  - Makes security and channel setup instructions easier to understand. [#168697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168697)
  - Clarifies tool, node, and plugin setup and usage guides. [#168699](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168699)
  - Simplifies reference and CI guidance while keeping links to renamed headings. [#168702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168702)
  - Clarifies macOS Gateway and managed Node messages and platform guides. [#168704](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168704)
  - Makes iOS guides and release diagnostics more direct. [#168705](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168705)
  - Clarifies Android and Wear guides and the screenshot lane description. [#168706](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168706)
  - Improves Linux setup guidance and the unrecognized managed-installation message. [#168707](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168707)
  - Makes agent and command execution messages clearer about safe retries and checking state. [#168709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168709)
  - Clarifies Gateway setup and security diagnostics. [#168710](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168710)
  - Makes CLI, Doctor, and service help and repair messages easier to follow. [#168711](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168711)
  - Explains Codex and provider setup requirements and failures more directly. [#168713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168713)
  - Clarifies messaging and calling help, warnings, and approval receipts. [#168714](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168714)
  - Makes workspace and memory plugin messages clearer about paths, paused recall, and safe retries. [#168715](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168715)
  - Clarifies browser, tool, plugin, and approval help and diagnostics. [#168716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168716)
  - Clarify product direction for optional hosting, local and self-hosted use, and separate Gateways with host or container isolation for mutually untrusted users ([#170923](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170923), [#170921](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170921)). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  - The environment reference documents the existing ClawHub install telemetry opt-out, `CLAWHUB_DISABLE_TELEMETRY`. [#114376](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/114376). Thanks [@kobihikri](https://github.com/kobihikri) and [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  - The Neon guide covers existing custom-provider setup for its Chat Completions, Responses, and Anthropic Messages endpoints. [PR #113734](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/113734). Thanks to [mezotv](https://github.com/mezotv), [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), and [clarkbw](https://github.com/clarkbw) for review advice.

  - Doctor's guide clarifies that standalone checks can run after an update before starting the Gateway. [#170550](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170550). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - Add a native Ollama configuration example for llmman that retains tool use with an explicitly configured model. [#171444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171444), thanks to [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin).

  </details>

### Models, voice and media

- On Linux x64 CPU systems, [guided setup](/plugins/llama-cpp#managed-local-ocr-and-vision) helps you download and test local models for reading text in images and describing pictures. A separately configured cloud chat model may still receive the original image.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Guided local OCR and vision setup with verified model downloads and separate checks for both tasks. [#148193](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148193), [#148056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/148056). Thanks [@duyufansh2008](https://github.com/duyufansh2008) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Choose Fish Audio’s [Drama 3 preview](/providers/fish-audio) for speech and Talk if you want to try it; the existing voice default does not change.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Fish Audio supports the opt-in Drama 3 preview for speech and Talk, with one voice per request. [#170473](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170473). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Choose [Gemini 4 Argon](/providers/google#gemini-4-argon) on supported Google and Vertex routes, including its available thinking levels. Google API-key setup now selects it as your primary model, so check that choice if you want to keep another default.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Update the recommended-model list to include Gemini 4 Argon and revise the leading order. [#171429](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171429), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for the approved curation.
  - Support Gemini 4 Argon selection, thinking levels and tool-call replay through Google and Vertex. Thinking cannot be fully disabled, and explicit caches and the Live API are unsupported for this model. [#171852](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171852), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Shared models can use the [Agents API](/plugins/agentsapi#shared-model-tuning); its hosted service handles conversation compaction itself.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Allow Agents API to be selected for shared models with OpenClaw compaction settings. The hosted runtime manages its own compaction. [#171813](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171813), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Select Muse Wonder 1.0 Standard or choose maximum thinking for Wonder and Spark 1.3 Standard.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Muse Wonder 1.0 Standard selection and max thinking for Wonder and Spark 1.3 Standard ([#170232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170232)). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

### Skills, plugins and automations

- Open an older run directly from automation history, or search delivery error text to find a failed run.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Open older linked runs directly and return to the normal history with Show all runs. [#169023](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169023), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Run-history search includes delivery error text ([#170958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170958)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Pause scheduled Team Reports without losing existing reports or the option to run one manually.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Team Reports can pause automatic scheduling without disabling manual reports or access to stored reports ([#171840](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171840)). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- Undo a [Skill Workshop](/tools/skill-workshop#undo) review to restore the skills it edited and archive any new skills it created.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Review-wide undo restores edited skills and archives skills created by the review. Button-capable channels can offer Undo, and plain-text channels can use `/learn undo <id>`. [#167996](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167996). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Background reviews wait for ten model iterations, so feedback can take longer to appear. A foreground skill change resets the count. [#168679](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168679). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Change the default destination for background skill notices, with optional channel delivery and Undo. [#170025](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170025) by [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Guide background reviews toward focused, reusable procedures. [#170277](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170277) by [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Plugin authors can move affected storage, session and other SDK calls to [asynchronous replacements](/plugins/sdk-migration/sqlite-async). Migrate deprecated synchronous calls by December 31, 2026; some unsupported calls already reject synchronous use.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Async conversation route-owner preparation replaces deprecated synchronous hooks for plugin authors. [#169593](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169593), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Legacy synchronous Mention Inbox recording queues work after commit, with `recordCommittedInputAsync` available when completion is needed. [#169594](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169594), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Async alternatives cover session, approval routing, sandbox and harness reads while synchronous adapters remain deprecated. [#169622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169622), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Remove five never-stable synchronous SDK names and retain published compatibility APIs. The affected names are `withOpenClawAgentDatabaseRuntime`, `loadMemorySessionMetadataBatch`, `createAccountScopedBindingAdapter`, `prepareConversationRouteOwners`, and `captureSessionEntryCurrentCheck`. [#170034](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170034) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update Plugin SDK and database worker migration guidance for awaited APIs. [#169879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169879) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore the deprecated session header link helper for existing browser plugins. Current headers do not provide a link for it, so migrate to a custom accessory before its planned removal after January 12, 2027. [#169746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169746) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Restore 29 private SDK exports used by existing community plugins while their authors migrate. [#173777](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173777). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and [@FtlC-ian](https://github.com/FtlC-ian) for the report.
  - The keyed-state V2 store supports awaited operations and conditional atomic updates. Existing stored data needs no migration. [#168478](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168478). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The SQLite V2 store offers authority-bound commands, explicit preparation and operations that preserve absent storage. Memory Core adopts it for selected writes. [#168857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168857). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prepared session patches and authority-aware route updates give plugin authors guarded session mutations. Legacy paths remain supported until the next SDK major. [#168453](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168453). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Native harness integrations gain V2 binding lifecycle and asynchronous ownership hooks, with synchronous compatibility retained. [#168479](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168479). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Approval publication can be awaited while the synchronous publisher remains available for migration. [#168482](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168482). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - V2 conversation-binding services and adapters provide awaited binding operations. Bundled Discord, Feishu, iMessage, Slack and Telegram paths adopt them. [#168483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168483). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Plugins can prepare message-action descriptions asynchronously, including discovery for Matrix accounts with saved credentials. [#168484](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168484). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Approval and placement operations have migration paths for legacy synchronous placement methods and opaque approval callbacks. [#168855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168855). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) for the reused updater compatibility work.
  - Solid 2 views require the compiler and runtime and an explicit JSX import-source pragma. The X replies view uses the same plugin view contract. [#168408](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168408). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Plugin authoring, manifest and SDK migration guides use clearer language for existing behavior. [#168701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168701), [issue #168322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168322). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Set `fallbacksOverride` in `before_model_resolve` to replace a run's fallback chain. An empty list stops the run if its selected model fails, while omitting the override keeps configured fallbacks. [#168893](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168893), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@dwty11](https://github.com/dwty11) for the [report and clarification](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/118946).
  - Malformed fallback overrides are treated as an empty list, preventing a malformed value from causing a crash or unintended fallback. [#169702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169702), thanks [@Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx) and [@dwty11](https://github.com/dwty11) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/118946).
  - Use V2 GitHub publication request and personal-action capabilities and async lifecycle methods. Existing writer signatures and completion timing remain supported until the next SDK major. [#168954](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168954), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Replace retired synchronous GitHub publication coordinator calls with async or V2 methods. Personal OAuth compatibility methods have a separate contract. [#169581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169581), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Move synchronous placement readers to their async equivalents. These readers were deprecated, and the placement changes below describe their later compatibility limits. [#169086](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169086), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Await skill-command discovery for channel menus. The synchronous SDK list methods remain available during their deprecation period. [#169089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169089), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use async credential preparation and provider-selection APIs. Released synchronous methods retain their behavior through the next SDK major. [#169111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169111), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Await session metadata, conversation, model-menu and speech-preference preparation. Synchronous SDK calls remain available with warnings. [#169122](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169122), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use `loadCombinedSessionStoreForGatewayAsync` for cross-session visibility. The synchronous loader remains available through the next SDK major. [#169523](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169523), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Replace deprecated unbound native-session deletion callbacks with the documented factories. Session context consumers can finish with the snapshot they captured, even if the transcript changes afterward. [#169553](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169553), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Await transcript reads, statistics, catalog titles and input completion. Legacy deletion callbacks now run after the session commit, and failed companion cleanup may need a safe retry. [#169566](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169566), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session input completion avoids a false failure after an unrelated write. Plugin authors can migrate recorder callbacks to `completeProcessingAsync`. [#169573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169573), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Move Memory session metadata reads to `loadMemorySessionMetadataAsync` or `loadMemorySessionMetadataBatchAsync`. The synchronous forms are deprecated until the next SDK major. [#169579](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169579), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use async state APIs for plugin hosts, including snapshot publication, cron path resolution, transcript search views and web-search provider lists. Before async preparation, the synchronous TTS path resolver uses explicit config or environment settings, or the default path. [#169580](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169580), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Replace the synchronous session catalog title helper with `readSessionTranscriptCatalogTitleAsync`. [#169620](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169620), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Await replacement placement and Mention Inbox methods. Synchronous placement `get` and `getMany` work only for prepared facts, and some other older methods throw or return unavailable. Legacy Mention Inbox recording is queued, so use `recordCommittedInputAsync` when the caller must wait for completion. [#169626](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169626), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retained synchronous storage adapters can now emit migration warnings while Board and plugin metadata reads use async APIs. [#169631](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169631), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Await single or batched transcript statistics and use `onPreparedAgentRunStart` for prepared run-start callbacks. Applicable synchronous APIs remain during the current major. [#169637](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169637), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Move deprecated synchronous config, auth, model registry, ACP, session-link and Reef hooks to their async replacements. Legacy calls remain supported during the deprecation period and may warn. [#169714](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169714), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Add `forkSession` for questions against prepared session context, subject to session access, supported providers, and retained context. [#170146](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170146) by [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Allow authorized plugins to steer supported active runs with hidden, attributed guidance and an explicit next-turn fallback. [#170152](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170152) by [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Deprecation notices name the replacement for a plugin API you use. [#171752](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171752), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Adds awaited helpers for historical transcript-key lookup and TTS prompt preparation, with prepared preferences available for supported synchronous TTS calls. [#171443](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171443), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Lets admitted agent hooks and Gateway services share plugin-owned data for the same run. [#171307](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171307), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Lets plugin authors filter agent-event subscriptions by phase before unwanted events are copied. [#171534](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171534), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Adds an opt-in metadata-only event payload for short top-level scalar values. Full payloads remain the default. [#171610](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171610), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Aligns bundled plugins with current host APIs. Installing newer bundled plugins separately on older hosts is unsupported, and FaceTime declares a minimum host version of 2026.10.3. [#171633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171633), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Registered plugin callbacks again receive `resolvePath` and `inlineView`, including when verifying ONNX models in an explicit directory. [#184110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184110), thanks to [Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668).
  - Independently validate affected plugin settings when schema validation runs without acceleration. [#182514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182514), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject oversized bounded arrays and sets before element traversal in the patched Zod 3 consumer, including A2UI catalog schemas. Invalid-input diagnostics and refinements can differ. [#187304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187304), thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Reduce allocation and CPU work for adjacent transforms on plugin-backed model streams. [#185083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185083), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Document five async plugin SDK replacements and their narrow import paths while retaining existing synchronous contracts. [Commit 8fc31c9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8fc31c91811524b3bae3091a3861b5febd160151).
  - [A direct commit improves conversation lookup, Gateway shutdown, plugin run context and skill discovery, alongside reusable custom snooze times](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/993c748b53ca3cd781167886061aa879d576a87b).
  - Retired synchronous GitHub publication methods now name their awaited or V2 replacement before rejecting calls in [#169750](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169750). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - Plugin authors have until December 31, 2026, to migrate deprecated synchronous SDK calls; this change does not remove those calls. [#171775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171775), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Asynchronous execution-approval readers and synchronous API migration guidance for plugin developers in [#169584](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169584). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- See available ClawRouter pool capacity and which models you can use in supported Control UI deployments, without sending the API key to your browser.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - View pool headroom and model eligibility in the Control UI. The configured API key stays on the Gateway. [#169514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169514), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use the model-request credential for the ClawRouter pool dashboard. [#170041](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170041) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Companion clients can find and preview official ClawHub packages, with verification of the exact package they receive.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Supplies official ClawHub catalog discovery, package previews and exact-artifact verification for companion Gateway clients. [#171622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171622), thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).

  </details>

- Claw authors can add lowercase categories to a manifest without losing them when it is saved.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Accepts and preserves optional lowercase categories in Claw manifests. [#171532](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171532), thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).

  </details>

## Bug fixes

### Conversations and interface

- Switching agents or filtering conversations now shows matches for the selected agent and owner, not results left over from the previous view.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep the selected owner's known name visible when a session filter has no results. [#169166](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169166) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Filtered sidebar sessions refresh when you switch agents without showing results from the previous agent. [#169774](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169774), thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Show your personal conversations across configured agents ([#170072](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170072)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Correct Involving me and named-owner filters for catalog sessions ([#170865](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170865)). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) for the report and contribution.
  - Keep participation and owner information available in large conversation lists ([#171138](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171138)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show matching sessions in saved-filter sidebars while custom group details load. Group names and order update afterward. [#171671](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171671), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Find conversations involving you under the corresponding filter ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4a2f4fd98edd156713f24b07683b24662cdf0698)), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Reconnecting or refreshing the conversation sidebar no longer repeatedly drops the selected chat or briefly replaces its list with empty rows. Archived conversation dashboards also stay out of Pages.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Restore saved Sessions and Pages views, pinned destinations and identity while reconnecting, including for large session lists. [#169326](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169326) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The selected conversation loads before background refreshes of off-page pinned sessions. [#170047](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170047), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep group names and default selections current after overlapping refreshes ([#170074](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170074)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Load the sidebar conversation list with smaller responses ([#170082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170082)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the newly chosen conversation selected after a delayed action from the previous one ([#170260](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170260)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Cached sidebar rosters appear with the dashboard shell, widget shimmer is removed, and filter controls retain chosen focus ([#170358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170358)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Refresh conversation lists without repeatedly fetching unchanged information ([#170987](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170987), related [#165150](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165150), [#166591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166591), and [#170082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170082)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Skip unused swarm detail work in sidebar and dashboard session lists ([#171104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171104)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse unchanged sidebar rows while refreshing counts and filters ([#171220](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171220)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep session lists available during native ownership changes or pending row updates. A persistently changing row can temporarily show configured model information until a later refresh ([#171300](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171300)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session lists reuse selections during unrelated activity while keeping visible row information current. [#171418](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171418) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Routine session updates need fewer repeated full sidebar list reads. [#171595](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171595) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep sidebar running indicators moving during session updates and catalog header spinners stable during unread updates. [#171689](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171689), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete); [#172303](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172303), thanks to [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Resolve titles for accessible session links outside the sidebar and show hover cards when card data is available. [#172558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172558). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
  - Exclude archived session dashboards from Pages, including restored sidebar snapshots and additional pages of results. [#172460](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172460). Thanks to [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev) and [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
  - Stop repeated sidebar refreshes on personal Gateways with legacy participants and keep those conversations visible. [#172575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172575). Thanks to [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Avoid repeated category registration work when creating or assigning a session to an existing category. [#172523](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172523). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid traversing the agent roster again during session catalog delivery. [#172612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172612). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the sidebar's conversation state aligned with Gateway lifecycle changes. [Merge](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b132a0a133bd9328f7bbcde6b66df6fcf30b4422)

  </details>

- [Conversation search](/concepts/session-search#control-ui-search) now finds names written in equivalent Unicode forms and phrases separated by punctuation. If searching transcripts takes too long, it suggests narrowing the search.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Match equivalent Unicode forms in the session catalog ([#184847](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184847)). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668).
  - Clear the Sessions search with its button or Escape without resetting other filters. [#146954](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146954). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - Find matching conversations when search words are separated by standalone punctuation or non-indexed symbols. [#168117](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168117). Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Give transcript searches enough time to use their worker budget and suggest narrower searches after a timeout ([#171190](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171190)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Match equivalent Unicode forms in session title search and command-palette ranking ([#184374](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184374)). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- File searches tell you when nothing matches instead of leaving a blank panel.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Show an empty-search message in filtered Files views instead of a blank panel. [#169114](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169114) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- [Unsent drafts](/web/control-ui/offline-and-reconnect#warm-reload), attachments and follow-up messages stay with the chat you wrote them in when you navigate away or reconnect. If a send is still unconfirmed, check the chat history before sending it again.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Change saved model and reasoning preferences during a reply for unstarted queued work. The picker shows the saved preference, and explicit per-message reasoning choices take precedence. [#160440](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160440), [#160428](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160428). Thanks [@hxy91819](https://github.com/hxy91819) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Type, attach files, and queue follow-ups while a new chat starts. They follow the initial prompt in order, and failed creation keeps the input for retry. [#165091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165091). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - In a connected Control UI chat, typing `/stop` cancels work and clears queued follow-ups in the selected non-global session. The Stop button and offline typed stops remain specific to the active run. [#167860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167860), [#167859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167859). Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep files attached to `/steer` and `/redirect` messages, including for retry after a rejected submission. [#165105](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165105), [#165104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/165104). Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Confirmed queued messages clear a false delivery warning while uncertain messages continue to warn. [PR #168284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168284). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Large WebChat images are staged so workspace file tools can open them, including in sandboxes. [PR #168537](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168537), [issue #103198](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/103198). Thanks to [@synthalorian](https://github.com/synthalorian), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [@woohahahaaa](https://github.com/woohahahaaa).
  - Messages with surrounding whitespace use the same normalized text on first inference and later replay. [PR #168725](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168725). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Chat preparation reuses fresh session facts to reduce repeated database and worker reads. [PR #168316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168316). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid false media errors and duplicate file cards when a WebChat attachment was already committed. Previously cached duplicates are not rewritten. [#169093](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169093) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prevent duplicate history entries for prompts with inline directives. Existing duplicate transcripts are not rewritten. The related [issue #168843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168843) covers a separate problem. [#169434](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169434) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Retain prepared model catalogs and pending Settings edits during preference changes. RPC clients without editable drafts can request a compact configuration patch acknowledgement. [#169253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169253) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show an error after repeated preference-save conflicts while retaining the edit for a later retry or reconnect. [#169367](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169367) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep dictated text and a chat being created with the draft you actually started, even while the browser connection opens. [#169692](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169692), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An accepted message without a confirmation ID reports unknown on the first response and replay without sending again. [#169650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169650), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Offline queued messages retain individual reconnect badges without a duplicate count band ([#170352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170352)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Accepted chat input and its interaction time are saved together before acknowledgment ([#170250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170250)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid making a ready conversation wait behind unrelated saved-chat work before accepting a message ([#170342](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170342)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Confirm eligible messages sent to idle chats once the input is saved ([#170376](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170376)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep an original request above a later steering message while a new conversation is being set up. [#170723](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170723) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Acknowledge chat sends sooner when unrelated database writes are in progress ([#170816](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170816)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject edited retries using the same request key for newly fingerprinted messages, including after in-memory state is lost ([#171174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171174), [reported issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171173)). Older records and records created without an available device identity retain the earlier comparison. If the identity changes and a retry conflicts, check the conversation history before sending with a new key. Thanks [@SeanLiu326](https://github.com/SeanLiu326) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Warm conversations reuse session information to reduce database work. [#171337](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171337) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show current queue labels after reconnecting while an earlier unconfirmed send remains paused for review. [#171866](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171866), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Remove an empty second composer after an interrupted session start while keeping any actual follow-up draft. [#172318](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172318), thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Refresh applicable filtered sidebars promptly after session creation and clear accepted prompts and attachments from the draft. Failed creation and newer unsent drafts remain available. [#171768](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171768), thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) for reporting and directing the fix.
  - Keep a prompt sent immediately after stopping a preparing turn in the queue so it can run after the stopped turn settles. Recovering a queued reply also keeps its chat identity. [#172685](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172685), fixing [#172658](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172658). Thanks to [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) for the report and fix.
  - Avoid false storage warnings, unwanted retries and restored drafts after a queued message has already been retired ([#186171](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186171)). Thanks [@syxscott](https://github.com/syxscott) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Close stale command and skill menus after a submitted draft clears, while keeping menus for retained or newer drafts ([#184869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184869)). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
  - Restored pasted-text chips, attachment previews and capability-menu interactions in the composer. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0c8464cf802c2f7ca62f2bc0def9303f98d0dde1)
  - Settled accepted terminal-state writes before releasing canceled turns. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/04cb1cbfab59e5dda6db7ee56ab0ab1be4500f75)
  - Loaded persisted transcript state when an append needs a retry and the session has no in-memory snapshot. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e65bd3be4134f1453bb4ab26e68704c678086b58)
  - Report slow chat sends while avoiding unnecessary work when no standing intent matches. [Merge](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/210340a16a91aa77b4b1cb61a4d8a18a80ae6ba9)
  - Restore queued follow-ups after an eligible interruption ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/333b2e303bee777b8882f9139e32629953ee79d1)), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Fixes affected chats showing the same reply twice, losing the end of a streamed answer or displaying messages in the wrong order after recovery.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Deliver eligible background completions to their original WebChat conversation without an external messaging target. [#158067](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158067), [#147387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/147387). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and reporter [@oywino](https://github.com/oywino).
  - Avoid a false WebChat error when a Claude CLI turn has already delivered its final answer through the message tool. [#167736](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167736), [#167709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167709); related separate report [#169614](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169614). Thanks [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Hide recognized serialized tool-call markup from streamed and final replies while keeping surrounding text and literal code examples. [#138204](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138204), [#138198](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138198), [#153594](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153594). Thanks [@ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), [@ruiling-smartbear](https://github.com/ruiling-smartbear), and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep an authorized channel message in history when required preflight compaction fails, so a later turn can see it. The compaction error still appears. [#160462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160462), [#86592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/86592). Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and reporter [@aacodex401](https://github.com/aacodex401).
  - Retain the original message or tool result when a plugin returns a non-message replacement and normal plugin error handling applies. Configured fail-closed handling still applies. [#161098](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161098), [#160641](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160641). Thanks [@xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and reporter [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80).
  - Let implicit group thread follow-ups remain silent when group silence is allowed, without a misleading missing-response retry. Explicit mentions and commands still require replies. [#164877](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164877), [#141556](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141556); related separate report [#160750](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160750). Thanks [@shannon0430](https://github.com/shannon0430), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [@zabealbe](https://github.com/zabealbe).
  - Live replies and saved history stay in chronological order around worker updates. [PR #168250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168250). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A remaining chat observer continues receiving updates and narration after another observer unsubscribes. [#169693](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169693), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Newly committed message previews appear promptly in resident session lists ([#170105](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170105)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep conversation branches available when saved history is reconciled ([#170079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170079)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore eligible chats after restart even if another conversation had steered them ([#170252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170252)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the request and co-authorship.
  - Preserve a main-session recovery claim when a follow-up arrives after interrupted work, so the earlier request can resume first. [#170647](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170647) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Load earlier messages when the recent history page contains only hidden activity. [#170731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170731) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for reporting the issue.
  - Focus the folder path and keep the save action reachable on short screens in group defaults. [#170566](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170566) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep new and previously saved failure notices out of later model requests ([#171327](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171327)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Observe late worker errors after cancellation and limit the raw size of default history pages. Oversized pages ask for a smaller limit, while an oversized message returns an error ([#170898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170898)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show a saved completed reply when its message event was missed. [#171766](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171766), thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) for reporting and co-authoring the fix.
  - Keep images with their headings in live web chat and reloaded history. [#172438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172438). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for raising the problem.
  - Hide stray reply actions on pre-tool progress messages while retaining actions for eligible answers. [#172620](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172620). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
  - Include confirmed delivered final replies when session history rebuilds a conversation, addressing one cause of repeated Telegram responses. [#179399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179399), related to [#166482](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166482). Thanks to [@sobolevbel](https://github.com/sobolevbel), [@gkoch02](https://github.com/gkoch02), and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - Keep CLI-backed group conversation context across room events and mentions when automatic replies are enabled. Visible room-event output still requires an explicit message-tool send ([#182273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182273), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182177)).
