  - Use OpenClaw-owned tools for Codex one-shot `agent exec` runs and retain completed answers and payloads if later cleanup fails. Cleanup failure still returns a failure exit code. [#173516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173516) Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@Compassboot](https://github.com/Compassboot) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161576).
  - Keep long Codex node exec-server exit diagnostics readable when truncation falls inside a UTF-8 character. [#183932](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183932) Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Confirmed final messages and explicitly final reactions to the current conversation can complete the turn. [#170653](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170653), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng).
  - A final reply already delivered to the current conversation remains complete through provider retries. [#170675](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170675), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for the report and [@Kimiyu-186](https://github.com/Kimiyu-186) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A saved final CLI reply stays successful when subsequent settlement fails. [#170757](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170757), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@elikampf](https://github.com/elikampf) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169804).
  - Codex receives the current group-chat speaker's stable identity beside their request. [#170709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170709), thanks to [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@oEdyb](https://github.com/oEdyb) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170159).
  - Stop can cancel the intended run while participant display information updates. [#170652](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170652), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Follow-up messages avoid being steered into a closed reply, and memory maintenance uses the active run's context budget. [#170635](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170635), thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Reopening a chat after a disconnect or reload now brings back its available messages and reading position instead of showing an empty chat or another conversation. Save unsent work and reload tabs left open from the older interface to receive the update-recovery fix.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Reload an open tab onto the current build when an old asset is missing after an update. Doctor also removes the obsolete retained asset cache. [#168105](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168105), [#128055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128055). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).
  - Load native plugin assets together on authenticated reload and keep the app document available if the plugin catalog fails. [#168059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168059). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show cached conversations at the latest messages with saved timing and task progress while reconnecting. [#168154](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168154). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Previously visited chats restore saved sidebar rows and navigation while reconnecting. [PR #168206](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168206). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Authorized conversation-link reloads can show the configured assistant identity before the connection completes. [PR #168265](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168265). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A uniquely matched cached dashboard name link can show its saved layout and side transcript while connecting. Board content waits for authorization. [PR #168663](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168663). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Conversation drafts survive route changes and reconnects, and Back history is corrected after default-agent initialization. [PR #168612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168612). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Cached conversations no longer briefly show the empty-chat welcome during the first connection. [PR #168185](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168185). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Refresh after a server update returns to the same chat and preserves its link. [PR #168941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168941). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Standalone question pages offer Retry after a temporary refresh failure and restore the answer held on that page. Drafts are not persisted by this change. [PR #168984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168984). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Control UI startup defers optional automation and System busyness code until it is needed. [PR #168506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168506). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reserve the measured height of previously viewed Canvas widgets and MCP Apps during cached chat reloads. Content and window changes can still resize them. [#169304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169304) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reveal an unvisited chat after its initial transcript position settles. [#169334](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169334) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reopen the existing chat after automatic recovery reloads while preserving its draft and dashboard parameters. [#169194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169194) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Cached transcripts and saved reading positions stay steadier while fresh history loads. [#170052](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170052), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Load chat without waiting for unrelated workspace identity and Board code ([#170345](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170345)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid an unnecessary refresh prompt after reconnecting to a Gateway on another origin ([#170090](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170090)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show settled chat history sooner on reload while preserving the reading position. [#170662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170662) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep session rows visible as the sidebar switches from cached to live data. [#170708](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170708) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce repeated sidebar updates and load unopened menu items when first needed. [#170625](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170625) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Load closed Inbox and sidebar menu renderers on first use, reducing initial JavaScript downloads. First use can require a network connection. [#170648](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170648) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Apply saved appearance and sidebar preferences earlier on authenticated same-origin reloads. [#170649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170649) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Load older chat history only when requested after a reload ([#171009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171009), related [#170662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170662), [#170731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170731), and [#170789](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170789)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fix repeated reloads for authenticated users opening shared chat links after a deployment. If access changes during the handoff, the page asks the reader to reload or log in ([#171244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171244)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep open Control UI tabs connected when a busy Gateway delays application heartbeats. If application messages stall while the transport remains healthy, recovery waits for a transport close or tab activation ([#171253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171253)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recover startup and view downloads after a temporary network failure, subject to reload safeguards. [#171825](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171825), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve an opening connection when a Control UI tab wakes and resume Logs from its previous position after reconnecting to the same Gateway. [#172864](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172864), related to [#172507](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172507). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@najef1979-code](https://github.com/najef1979-code).
  - Preserve the saved sidebar when another tab has not yet resolved its profile, including the follow-up that keeps the existing cache scope ([#185762](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185762), [#187594](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187594)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).
  - Show a loading state for an uncached, disconnected chat deep link until its history or session identity is known ([commit 2bf00b8](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2bf00b83d30972963435ecef4e7baa30338b77d0)).
  - Load the saved sidebar without making the initial page download as large. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4f4741bfe2b6b49e75af89107be87224b5fcfc9e)
  - Reduced asset requests for chat and new-session startup. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/926602532b623c60e281e35759dc8af33e77cbd7)
  - Refreshed profile appearance preferences after reconnecting with the same client. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1f961c5f7fa988cd65c9f69e1c435c774cdcfbd6)
  - Reloaded logs from the start after a Gateway restart, retaining previous rows until the new read succeeds. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0cc89c8e3a0f92e3851e7647f062f7f28bff9978)
  - Restored data connections needed by the board, dashboard and debug views. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/da8802bc54ef00ae19ac854cec98a507879c684c)

  </details>

- Stop now more reliably targets the turn you chose, without cancelling separate follow-up messages unless you use a control that explicitly clears them. If a stop is still settling, check its outcome before assuming the work ended.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep the conversation view following the right turn while Stop moves between active runs ([#170349](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170349)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid a misleading Stop error when the requested turn finishes while cancellation is being prepared. [#170542](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170542) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep a slow sessions-tool Stop active and return `settling` while cancellation continues. The response does not mean cancellation has completed. [#176603](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176603), addressing the report in [#169077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169077). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@hannnnn-l](https://github.com/hannnnn-l).
  - Continue a stopped conversation after terminal recovery records are cleared, keeping its history intact ([#181866](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181866)). Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).
  - Continue a killed conversation in the same session when an abandoned recovery record blocked new messages ([#181992](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181992), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181952)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@najef1979-code](https://github.com/najef1979-code).
  - Stop selected native Codex descendants with their parent and preserve children that complete during Stop. [PR #169700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169700). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Release a Codex parent chat after Stop when its selected child was already interrupted. [#171541](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171541), thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Keep a stopped Codex conversation ready for another message after reloading the web UI. [#171656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171656), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve the replacement process when Stop interrupts the wait for a previous Codex process before the new thread is written. The next turn still waits for the previous process to exit. [#173580](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173580) Thanks to [@LLagoon3](https://github.com/LLagoon3), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) for validation, and [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167280).
  - Repeated cancellation callbacks preserve a confirmed subagent cancellation and its pending parent notice. [#176206](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176206) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stop can interrupt the current run's owned Codex descendants after a parent thread is reused, without cancelling older or unrelated work. [#181475](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181475) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Keep `/stop` bound to the work selected before authorization in [f28e5ba](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f28e5ba25b1ffc6e2980cbc65b5f714f4533718a).
  - Let Codex Stop settle during native client shutdown while replacement writers still wait for the old process to exit in [67a3798](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/67a3798b5bf6579475075bcfccc4bf349bafa09b) and [1e1d5f1](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1e1d5f11672a4b634ace27096971d6f3a417fd4c).
  - Fix Stop during queued turns, keep configured reasoning choices available and continue following the chat after a scroll that moved nowhere in [fcb89a3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/fcb89a331a1f59eedde59c4b3a338febdb199cb7).
  - Identify AI service connection failures and remind users to check completed work before retrying ([#168247](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168247)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Apply the first-event timeout to model requests that never return response headers. Cold loading before headers consumes that budget; adjust the provider timeout if needed using the [custom provider guide](/concepts/model-providers/custom-providers) ([#168282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168282)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx) for the investigation.
  - Cancel active OpenAI-compatible HTTP runs through session-wide Stop and return the known run ID ([#168080](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168080)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve partial Gemini responses at generation limits without executing incomplete function calls. The updated computer-control driver also includes upstream fixes for macOS application enumeration and cursor behavior and Windows installation ([#168103](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168103)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep unfinished reasoning out of Chat Completions replies when a tool call is followed by a token limit or content filter ([#168294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168294)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Refresh agent names and avatars in open Control UI views after a Gateway identity-file save without losing the chat or draft. Direct filesystem edits still need a reload or reconnect ([#168170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168170)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Remove the duplicate waiting line when individual subagent activity is already visible in Control UI ([#168173](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168173)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prevent CLI migration provider discovery, preview and import from changing state while the running Gateway owns it. Gateway-owned memory imports continue to work. [#168419](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168419). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep Codex plugin discovery during onboarding within its intended timeout when the system clock changes. [#168735](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168735). Thanks to [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Session Stop covers authorized OpenAI-compatible HTTP runs admitted or queued before the runner starts. [Early HTTP run cancellation](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168359). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Cancelling a queued CLI-backed turn takes effect without waiting for the active turn to finish, and the cancelled turn does not run. [#169303](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169303), [prior investigation](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160804). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@changeroa](https://github.com/changeroa).
  - Keep shared Control UI state in sync with the Gateway. If a subscription acknowledgement is lost, refresh or reconnect to update a stale view; a list changing while it loads can still skip or repeat entries. [#169375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169375). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Report finalized abandoned session deliveries with their queue IDs and count them as failures in batch recovery. Check the conversation before retrying manually because the earlier attempt may already have had effects. [#169932](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169932), [issue #169807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169807). Thanks [@huyengiang101086-spec](https://github.com/huyengiang101086-spec), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot).
  - Give controllers a shorter polling hint after a managed deployment begins draining, so they can notice completed work sooner. [#169585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169585). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A late deletion confirmation no longer closes or switches away from a selected replacement session in [#170056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170056). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Offline chats show guidance when a cloud session's initial message remains unresolved, while follow-up messages can still queue. [#170515](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170515). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Clarify that follow-ups can be queued while the first message is unresolved, but wait for it to be resolved before delivery ([#170802](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170802)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Valid request failures leave a healthy Gateway connection open. A successful hello is still needed for normal initialization. [#171303](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171303), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Deliver the first `sessions_send` message to a newly created main session under the Always defaults ([7b125134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7b125134f3860743c55696b3f273ed82abe535dc)).
  - Accept valid follow-up input when the preceding terminal write finishes before writer admission ([f8ea55fc](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f8ea55fcf7a8d35212c77e4b7116281b15253165)).
  - Preserve aborted results and stop reasons during session deletion and Stop, and wait for the canceled owner to settle ([6bb8525d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6bb8525d548881f86d550f5476a4f804b77daed6)).

  </details>

- Fixes long chats freezing when you jump to a message, images failing to enlarge and split diffs misaligning related lines. Narrow screens also make more room for chat controls and titles.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Follow the writer’s caret and whitespace in a shared draft preview. [#168158](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168158). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep edited annotation comments open when the transcript scrolls, including after a resize. [#153379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153379), [#153103](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153103). Thanks [@xydt-juyaohui](https://github.com/xydt-juyaohui), [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf), and [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
  - Allow Canvas widget videos to enter fullscreen and prevent clipped captions in chat and pinned dashboards, including saved widgets. [#168143](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168143). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Narrow subagent transcripts use space previously reserved for hidden avatars. [PR #168221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168221). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Canvas and inline app widget actions remain clickable beside Details in narrow chat panes. [PR #168328](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168328). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Inline widgets retain playback and local state across same-account reconnect recovery and, in browsers supporting atomic moves, when the final reply appears. Safari still reloads the widget on final-reply transfer. [PR #168181](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168181). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid loading plugin views for ordinary tool output and retain selected plugin-view drafts across transcript hide and restore. [#169346](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169346) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Dismiss link previews when keyboard focus leaves, including after a preview mounts late. [#169073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169073) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Load less overlay code initially, keep keyboard menu selections in view and preserve transcript following through programmatic scroll corrections. [#169128](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169128) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Bring back the Terminal side panel and make the Browser view respond to its activation control. [#169182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169182) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - YouTube playback continues when an unchanged chat transcript refreshes. [#169541](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169541), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show chosen colors on recent, pinned and imported conversation rows ([#170382](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170382), [request](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170371)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the co-authorship.
  - Load larger older-history pages when requested without making the initial chat load as large ([#170481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170481)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the co-authorship.
  - Compact Summary and Transcript tabs in the Meetings reader, with keyboard navigation retained ([#170314](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170314)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prevent the Control UI from freezing when jumping to an offscreen chat message. [#170789](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170789) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Align the sidebar avatar and search with the navigation controls. [#170688](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170688) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Restore spacing between colored sidebar sessions. [#170706](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170706) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reserve stable image preview space for new uploads and older images, which still open at full size ([#170819](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170819)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session titles and overflow controls fit on phone-sized rows, and the Layout menu stays within the screen and scrolls to its actions. [#171413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171413) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mobile headers make room for session titles, while Panels opens Details and available tools. Widening the window restores the saved desktop layout. [#171430](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171430) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Swipes over inline visualizations continue scrolling the conversation with momentum. [#171468](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171468) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The mobile sidebar backdrop is adjusted to avoid a delayed browser-bar tint when the drawer closes in Safari. [#171617](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171617) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Chat tool Diff and Raw tabs use a tighter layout and retain the chosen view. Failed and skipped results open in Raw. [#171552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171552) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fold functions, classes and other supported code regions, with hidden change counts in the placeholder. [#172350](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172350), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Align related lines in split diffs and highlight changed text in session diffs and tool edit previews. [#171935](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171935), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep large session diffs responsive while keeping changed lines visible. [#172257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172257), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let session readers preview, download and play attachments belonging to messages they can currently read. Fixes [#172130](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172130) in [#172202](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172202), thanks to [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) for the report and fix.
  - Give existing conversations more room above the idle composer, which still expands for multiline drafts. [#171637](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171637), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the design request.
  - Remove the empty session sidebar footer when there is no development-branch badge. [#171644](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171644), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep repository labels readable in narrow session Details panels while allowing PR titles to wrap. [#171995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171995), thanks to [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
  - Search the reaction picker by emoji name or shortcode while keeping quick reactions and emoji paste. [#172577](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172577). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
  - Stop Canvas document replacement and report the error if cleanup of the old document fails. A partial deletion is not rolled back. [#175343](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175343). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve browser zoom and reset shortcuts in loaded image previews while unmodified keys continue to control the image ([#180872](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180872)). Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
  - Fix lightbox zoom changing the percentage without enlarging images with known dimensions ([#181374](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181374), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180943)). Thanks [@AmelioMansour](https://github.com/AmelioMansour) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Bring back the Files action in the hosted chat header. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3a697ca4265b8302dca2b0191748a231c7c16432)
  - Made the Latest button hide immediately at the newest message. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/af93d66c072d4e3ef2568c17a05943e972e027cc)
  - Preserved table controls across pane moves and retired pending image previews on disconnect. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/521393ecd7d11374b80c16068226b83bd75a3979)
  - The earlier table-retention change was [reverted](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9605dd68cb6d07fe26475c9e54731d2c68aba669) before the follow-up addressed both tables and image previews. [Original change](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/042db1e7064930a08c534c4487864d7bfb8c5287)
  - Use native tooltip lifecycle events, with one opening or closing event per transition ([#170841](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170841)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Fixes keyboard focus disappearing when menus, dialogs or chat controls refresh, and restores visible focus indicators and labels for assistive technology.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">

  - Return keyboard focus to a surviving heading after plugin removal from Settings or the catalog-backed Plugins hub. [#161111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161111), [#127327](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127327). Thanks [@MoerAI](https://github.com/MoerAI) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - Restore Security pairing eligibility after reconnect, Escape handling in Chat Details and recovery after a successful manual refresh. [#169046](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169046) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Open composer controls and draft recovery errors follow language changes without losing the draft or focus. [#169574](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169574), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Focused links keep people hovercards open, and terminal header actions work when hosted outside the panel. [#169725](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169725), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep keyboard focus and notices usable in dialogs and long menus ([#170387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170387)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Close Sessions filters with Escape and return focus while keeping the search text. [#170565](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170565) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Improve keyboard navigation, focus handling and selection counts in Usage filters. [#170567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170567) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the dismissal menu visible near screen edges and return keyboard focus to your draft after dismissal or Escape. [#170568](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170568) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Close plugin setting action menus before leaving settings and prevent empty menu clicks from toggling values. [#170569](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170569) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let Activity filters wrap into view when space is tight or zoom is enlarged. [#170570](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170570) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep rename-button focus and participant avatars during header updates. [#170631](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170631) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep progress, reaction, and image menus usable after document changes and pointer selection ([#170814](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170814)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The New Session environment device list scrolls on narrow screens, and dismissing its picker returns focus. [#171441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171441) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Sidebar menus improve focus handling and reveal the selected agent on short screens. [#171463](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171463) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The session group environment menu focuses the selected choice when reopened by keyboard and returns focus after Escape. [#171343](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171343) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Refresh connection and automation labels in open person activity cards when the language changes, while keeping focus. [#172140](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172140), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Refresh visible and accessible progress-card labels when the language changes. [#172184](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172184), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Remove old recovery links when a rich toast is replaced and preserve focus through unrelated refreshes. [#171817](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171817), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show a keyboard focus outline in the Chat and New Session editors in forced-colors mode. [#180135](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180135), fixing [#180134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180134). Thanks to [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Name the focusable chat transcript for assistive technology ([#180857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180857), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180814)). Thanks [@bajiaoshan](https://github.com/bajiaoshan), [@carlopignatari-boop](https://github.com/carlopignatari-boop) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  - Restore the keyboard focus indicator for Settings search in forced-colors mode ([#181225](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181225)). Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
  - Let Shift+Tab leave composer suggestions without selecting one or changing the draft ([#181145](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181145)). Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
  - Keep Shift+Enter available for a newline while suggestions are open ([#181999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181999)). Thanks [@AmelioMansour](https://github.com/AmelioMansour) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Ignore repeated key events when a command-palette shortcut is held ([#183067](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183067)). Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
  - Return keyboard focus to the previous control after a modal closes, while respecting focus deliberately moved elsewhere ([commit 4a7d536](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4a7d53616a5dbee3b0a8e2efc1b5ed7185325b13)).
  - Kept the Unarchive action attached and focused when its banner refreshes. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a640fff46b13a827c7f63f2e4fdd5c8342115971)
  - Restored Escape dismissal for hover-only chat position-rail previews. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b73d6bf0a600f1c0ca115889e2075544a1ee30ef)
  - Restored modal focus immediately when the target is ready, or after an inert background becomes usable. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/eec5e318ecaa95def03d5c27254d12db149856fa)
  - Preserved focus in an open mobile drawer menu when swipe support loads. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a8a349b15b20240d387770378072c2ca51889596)
  - Returned focus to a temporarily disabled login control once it becomes available and kept cached provider information visible during initial synchronization. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2cf64f1bc4360f3caed91ce2303464fde703cd03)
  - Waited for tooltips to render and restored focus to the active chat sidebar tab. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/91a860e080610d5f1d7ac833b698effdf9e0ef4b)
  - Prevented a redundant tooltip during menu fade-in from consuming the first Escape. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2f5bdf2f870849a70961dcf6239fe51779e526ef)
  - [Modal and drawer link menu positioning](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/131ad6e8f6e077598664e954ab60c40dfcddbb87), thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Device menu focus stays on the selected action when approval actions appear and returns to the trigger after selection. [#170784](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170784) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Fixes chosen language and appearance settings failing to update open views, along with missing agent avatars and labels that did not match their controls.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Team agent headers no longer show an extra creator avatar beside the agent identity. [PR #168575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168575). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Repeated requests for missing avatars are reduced. Some supported fallback profile photos may not appear. [PR #168640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168640). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Settings, configuration help and guides use clearer wording. [PR #168703](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168703), [plain-language issue #168322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168322). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - The agent emoji picker handles disconnection and restores its trigger on reconnect. [PR #168759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168759), [UI migration issue #168114](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168114). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Worktrees keeps repository drafts during asynchronous refreshes and updates its header when the locale changes. Visible log times follow the current timezone when rows update. [PR #168767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168767). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update open board labels and controls when the Control UI language changes. [#169415](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169415) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Translate the helper panel's approximate call count into 20 languages. [#169781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169781).
  - Reconnecting refreshes profile appearance while preserving pending local choices, and leaving New Session stops its detached placeholder animation. [#170007](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170007), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Participant contribution counts and timestamps refresh after count changes. [#169858](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169858), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Group existing settings for messages between conversations more clearly ([#170390](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170390)). Thanks to [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
  - Choose System or a supported language from the normal Appearance settings picker ([#170237](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170237)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Embedded widgets share theme reads and continue receiving live palette updates ([#170240](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170240)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Appearance text drafts survive unrelated settings refreshes ([#170319](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170319)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The sidebar community link uses the correct Follow OpenClaw on X label for its tooltip and accessible name. [#171462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171462) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Read-aloud controls and interrupted voice-reply guidance gain translated records. [#171333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171333)
  - Session snooze date and time labels gain translated records. [#171381](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171381)
  - Match the reaction control's appearance to the other message actions. [#172517](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172517). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
  - Apply locale-aware capitalization to recent chat footer timestamps. [#172550](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172550). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
  - Restore data-URL agent avatars on session surfaces that request an unversioned avatar URL ([#182730](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182730), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182688)). Thanks [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@migelizaga-rgb](https://github.com/migelizaga-rgb).

  </details>

- Fixes settings edits being lost during background refreshes and form controls showing an old choice after the saved setting changes.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep Advanced settings and the current reading position visible during reconnect. Editing pauses while the schema refreshes. [#159875](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159875), [#152874](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/152874); related separate report [#153143](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153143). Thanks [@MoerAI](https://github.com/MoerAI), [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski), and [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - Settings shows validation reasons beside invalid scalar values and clears them after correction. [PR #168329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168329), [issue #127329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127329). Thanks to [@MoerAI](https://github.com/MoerAI), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Clarify the Versions menu with plain-text previews, a current marker and optional help. [#169206](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169206) Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
  - Reveal the relevant advanced Memory group when opening a Settings search result. [#169022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169022) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the Saved status timer running through unrelated refreshes so Apply changes can reappear. [#169246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169246), part of the broader [Control UI migration](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168114). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Versions help opens for the current chat, and its menu closes when navigating away or using browser Back. [#169794](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169794), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Group rename and New session defaults dialogs close on reconnect or connection switch. Reopen the group menu to make changes on the intended connection. [#170067](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170067), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve focused drafts through loader updates on migrated Control UI pages. [#170571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170571) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep Reset visible in the narrow-screen Automations Jobs filter. [#170580](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170580), [migration tracking #168114](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168114). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Save the suggested profile on selection, with the usual provider checks when a worker is allocated ([#170875](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170875)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Explain missing prompt-reaction permission and how to address an external reaction explicitly ([#171264](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171264)). Thanks [@Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - New Session menus close when changing agents or opening the new session, and Escape closes a nested agent menu first. Failed parent-session reads retry after a session-list refresh. [#171561](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171561) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Publication account menus close with Details, and pull request check disclosures retain their open state across navigation. [#171349](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171349) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve MCP catalog focus and open pairing request details during refreshes while actions use current data. [#171894](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171894), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show a configuration schema error when reconnecting to a Gateway that lacks the required schema method, while retaining the previous schema. [#176039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176039). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Step the current numeric Settings draft instead of the inherited default, while retaining default stepping for blank fields ([#182076](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182076)). Thanks [@AmelioMansour](https://github.com/AmelioMansour) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Kept the web-search checkbox unchecked when global policy disables search, including with a stale override. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f74d65712bb5a7386ebbd746bae79d5618f05066) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restored missing web-search guidance for dashboard requests when search is unconfigured. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3b01cee14cfeb14080d5927340355206e5990936)
  - Allow revision-checked `config.patch` calls to remove explicitly named agents while preserving omitted siblings. Array replacements still require exact acknowledgments. [#152052](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152052), [reported in #152047](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/152047). Thanks to [maximilien](https://github.com/maximilien) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Restart without making an unnecessary copy of the shared database just to identify the previous owner. [#169097](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169097), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Workspace skill watches exclude unrelated sibling state while retaining live skill refresh in [#170229](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170229). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Common core configuration operations avoid unrelated plugin secret-target discovery while retaining plugin and channel credential handling. [PR #170270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170270). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid unnecessary plugin discovery for unrelated configuration edits while retaining relevant model catalog normalization ([#170861](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170861)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Agent creation and settings saves avoid redundant skill discovery and unrelated reader revival during reload. [#171309](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171309), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid a false Gateway restart warning when credential removal supersedes a model update ([9c253952](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9c253952601063a8eccc1f733ac55e95cc9961cc)).
  - Complete affected configuration changes during reload without accidentally re-enabling suppressed channels ([6cbfacd8](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6cbfacd8d801318e39574fbc8ff45ed6c0e5f75e)).
  - Avoid a misleading refresh prompt on the first Gateway connection. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d0298744306f8bc30468cf9d2726544c6a2517c0)
  - Keep configuration form fields current as their underlying values change. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/84ce5954d111273c1e7a1e32f86451ea5768bde6)

  </details>

- [Incognito conversations](/concepts/session#incognito-sessions) now keep OpenClaw conversation contents in memory instead of an Incognito database, and usage refresh uses less memory. That history expires within 24 hours or when OpenClaw restarts, whichever comes first; providers, tools, plugins and Codex diagnostics may still retain their own data.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Identify Incognito chats more clearly. The notice explains that session history expires after 24 hours or a restart, whichever comes first, while providers process messages and tools, plugins, or uploads may retain data. [#154651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154651). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Incognito guidance documents the Codex diagnostic-log limitation and the OpenClaw-runtime alternative. [PR #168966](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168966). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Allow an administrator to reload an existing Incognito conversation during its Gateway session. [#169025](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169025) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Refresh the Public badge in an open chat when another window changes sharing. [#169078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169078) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Disable Delete for adopted Claude and Codex sessions whose native transcripts cannot be removed through that action ([#171031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171031), [reported issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169185), [related Codex issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169184)). Thanks [@chelsealong](https://github.com/chelsealong), [@bricelb](https://github.com/bricelb) for the report, and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for verification.
  - Stop session readers from repeatedly polling operator-only features and show clearer access guidance ([#171223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171223)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Kept a retained incognito session findable by ID after the state directory changes while its in-memory owner remains active. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6cdd20ae2d0a4a3a8aef500031e55f4ee8d53095)
  - Keep Incognito conversation content in process memory rather than an Incognito database. Plugin authors accessing Incognito sessions must await the updated SDK calls. [#170712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170712) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - [Memory use during Incognito usage refresh](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1cbc801f5ae2d6f7372d508a1099d40de135cfe7), thanks to [@HerbertHuerto](https://github.com/HerbertHuerto) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Fixes unrelated device activity interrupting a Control UI reply and replaces misleading connection or session errors with the relevant reason and next step in affected cases.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep the destination readable in browser tab titles during a disconnect. Connection status remains available in the app and favicon. [#167388](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167388). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
  - Show the active follow-up's own duration instead of the time taken by an earlier completed spawn on the Sessions page. [PR #168666](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168666), [issue #168664](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168664). Thanks to [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Show the normal New Session place choices while loading instead of a misleading worker warning for local sessions. Required-worker placement still gates sending. [#169402](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169402) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reduce dashboard gallery refresh payloads by omitting session fields the cards do not use. [#169062](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169062) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show Gateway CPU use separately from the whole computer's utilization, in comparable core units. The process reading excludes subprocesses. [#169791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169791), thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Control UI turns continue through unrelated paired-device activity while actual revocation still stops affected work. [#169457](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169457), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for the [report and reproduction](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169134).
  - Explain why a computer is unavailable and show it only once in the command menu ([#170456](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170456)). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Clear old Inbox sign-in warnings when switching agents ([#170149](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170149)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore Mentions after the profile starts, with spaced retries if it is temporarily unavailable ([#170507](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170507)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid a duplicate agent roster request on dashboard reload. [#170637](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170637) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show a connection-opening timeout explanation before the Control UI retries. [#172626](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172626), related to [#172507](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172507). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), reporter [@najef1979-code](https://github.com/najef1979-code), and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for related investigation.
  - Warn in Gateway logs when a Control UI pull request subscription fails to prepare and suggest retrying it. [#177038](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177038). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Release cached Control UI assets when the UI is disabled and start a fresh cache when it is re-enabled. [#177042](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177042). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Let the development UI reconnect through its configured proxy after a Gateway disconnect without restarting Vite. [#177066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177066). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Accept equivalent case variations of configured agent IDs in authorized HTTP and WebSocket tool calls. [#168127](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168127). Thanks to [yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Refused agent turns distinguish a missing session-store schema from a read failure without exposing raw paths or database messages in [#169965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169965). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Chat Completions, Responses and embeddings request bodies have a 60-second upload deadline. See [#170094](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170094) and [#162422](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162422). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco).
  - OpenAI-compatible chat completions and responses return an actionable HTTP 409 for archived sessions, including streaming requests. Restore the session in the Control UI before retrying. [PR #170299](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170299), reported in [#169679](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169679). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@lqsyyy](https://github.com/lqsyyy) for the report.
  - One-shot `openclaw agent --local` commands release cached provider connections after a reply so the process can exit. [PR #170391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170391). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Approved device connections skip an unnecessary pairing recovery lookup. [#170594](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170594). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let unrelated session writes proceed during credential reads and provider observations for authentication health ([#170807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170807)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse a named Gateway owner's profile facts before the first connection snapshot ([#170832](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170832)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid repeated authentication-file checks during dashboard startup and model authorization while refreshing changed credentials ([#170842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170842)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Routine auth and catalog work avoids misleading deprecation warnings, while version guidance is logged as information. Explicit deprecated API calls still warn. [#171229](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171229), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep authenticated local Gateway access when Tailscale Serve is disabled by policy. Restore the Serve policy and restart the Gateway to recover remote access. [#171509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171509) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Show the restart instruction once when a Gateway read detects lost state ownership. [#171568](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171568) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Foreground Gateway startup failures report a redacted reason without debug mode. [#172107](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172107), reported in [#172053](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172053). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), reporter [waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault), and co-author [skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223).
  - Unchanged provider credential refreshes no longer cause valid HTTP hook wakes to receive a 503. [#172619](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172619), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - `/session unbind` warns when no binding was removed instead of reporting success. [#184857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184857) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway monitoring warns when it cannot open an adopted Claude or Pi transcript because of an access error, while continuing to retry. [#182118](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182118) Thanks [@xiaopan-xydt](https://github.com/xiaopan-xydt) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Fixes update status showing an older failure after a successful update or hiding a warning that still needs attention, so you can see the result and next step.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Report the real update outcome after a chat-requested update instead of a false Doctor cleanup permission error. [#170070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170070), thanks [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the investigation, and [@ezimerman](https://github.com/ezimerman), [@yoyo837](https://github.com/yoyo837), [@aia007731-lab](https://github.com/aia007731-lab) and [@justinforsound](https://github.com/justinforsound) for [reports](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168750).
  - Add an Updates action and stable or beta availability badge to the account menu. Dev users can still update manually without a new-update notice. [#172544](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172544). Thanks to [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Prevent an older background check from overwriting the first manual update status publication. [#169913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169913). Related tracing reports [#112278](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/112278) and [#112486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/112486) are not resolved by this change. Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Status treats a qualifying targetless failed update as history after a healthy Gateway changes its recorded identity. Records without a saved running identity still warn. See [#170063](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170063) and [#169950](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169950). Thanks [@amittell](https://github.com/amittell), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web).
  - Update confirmations and delayed native fallback stay with their original Gateway and profile in [#170086](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170086). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update verification avoids a false failure when recorded migration has advanced the database schema, and improves saved status guidance. See [#170130](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170130) and [#169422](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169422). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@angeliti999](https://github.com/angeliti999).
  - Failed-update reports retain Doctor recovery instructions until process settlement is safely verified in [#170207](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170207). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Slow state snapshots no longer consume the next command's inspection window when updating from a 2026.10.1 or newer updater. An installed updater containing this change also flags deferred optional checks and suggests `openclaw doctor --fix` after the update. [PR #170374](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170374), fixing [#170333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170333). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for the report, and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for policy advice.
  - Warnings explain when optional original-state capture failed but the update can continue, and distinguish the later database recovery snapshots. See [recovery limits](/cli/update/how-updates-run#recovery-limits). [PR #170380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170380), reported in [#170332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170332). Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the policy explanation.
  - Package update journal mismatch messages identify changed filesystem bindings and show the profile-aware `openclaw update repair` command. [PR #170385](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170385), with reports and discussion in [#170031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170031) and [#169352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169352). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@council10-kera](https://github.com/council10-kera), [@dave160926-ops](https://github.com/dave160926-ops), and [@SergeiAug](https://github.com/SergeiAug).
  - Failed-update diagnostics retain the Doctor inventory link when it fits. For an exceptionally long path, they say the link is unavailable and keep the inventory on disk. [#170638](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170638). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The compact Inbox update action stays disabled and shows that it is checking until its target freshness check finishes. [#170491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170491). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Successful update headlines include the number of warnings that need attention, while keeping the successful outcome. [#170490](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170490). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Fixes eligible conversation forks failing and exports with spaces in their filenames breaking, while archive actions better handle interrupted cleanup. Archiving a conversation tree can still partly succeed, and Undo only restores changes it can still reach.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Confirm before bulk archiving a selection with active sessions. Cancelling leaves the selection untouched. [#169040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169040) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Begin archive preparation without waiting for unrelated session writes; the final archive still waits for its write and rechecks authority ([#171198](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171198)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Allow archiving after successful cleanup of orphaned worker provisioning. Failed cleanup still prevents the archive ([#171233](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171233)). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) for the report and repair direction.
  - Export conversations and trajectories to filenames containing spaces without breaking the path. [#171616](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171616) Thanks to [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Avoid opening recognized live SQLite files and hardlinks during session checkout diff capture. [#174017](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174017). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reconcile archive retention beyond the first page so ordinary cleanup does not leave stale missing-copy warnings ([#181746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181746), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181722)). Thanks [@xiaopan-xydt](https://github.com/xiaopan-xydt), [@tre1d](https://github.com/tre1d) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore message forking for eligible session-writing guests, preserving preceding context and putting the selected message in the new draft ([#185486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185486), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/185414)). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).

  </details>

- Fixes Mentions and Activity losing available items after a refresh or agent change. A paired device already displaying a completed conversation can also skip the redundant completion notification.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep failed dashboard widget removal errors visible through an unchanged refresh. [#169003](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169003) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use singular English wording for one session or person in Activity, including accessible descriptions. [#169231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169231) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Inbox automation alerts stay available when switching agents within the same scope, while agent-specific authentication refreshes. [#169677](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169677), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Dismissing an absent or already dismissed Inbox mention returns the current authorized Inbox instead of failing during unrelated session activity. [#169836](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169836), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Hide suggested tasks during launch and restore failed or unconfirmed tasks for retry. [#170752](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170752) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recover the mention Inbox after a session replacement, with bounded background retries ([#170896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170896)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Clear a suggested task's old error after a successful retry without clearing newer or unrelated errors. [#171640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171640), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid running an Activity recap again when an earlier request already completed it. [#172334](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172334), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the fix and [Mike-MWT](https://github.com/Mike-MWT) for reporting [#172186](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172186).
  - Avoid unnecessary roster planning when delivering opt-in Control UI activity recaps ([#182182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182182)). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Agent finished Web Push skips the paired device actively viewing the finished conversation and resumes normal delivery when it is hidden, switched away or disconnected. [#183535](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183535) Thanks [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks [@pmochine](https://github.com/pmochine) for the report.

  </details>

- Plugin download counts now use the correct million or billion label.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Display plugin download counts with the correct compact unit at million and billion boundaries. [#149103](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149103). Thanks [@Piyushrathoree](https://github.com/Piyushrathoree) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- GitHub Actions widgets can show a marked older result while a previously successful view refreshes. Unchanged HTML widgets also keep their sandbox address across board refreshes.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Previously successful GitHub Actions widget reads can return a cached result marked `stale: true` while refreshing. Cold or changed-scope reads still wait, and a failed refresh causes the next read to retry. [PR #168774](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168774). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse sandbox URLs for unchanged HTML widget policies on repeated board reads ([#171332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171332)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- The community banner no longer shows Pinterest’s Save overlay when the integration respects its opt-out.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Prevent Pinterest integrations that honor image opt-outs from placing Save controls over the community banner. [#171903](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171903), thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- Fixes commands reporting the wrong failure, including missing local Gateway access mistaken for a token problem. JSON command failures now give scripts an error type and message where supported.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - `openclaw dashboard --json` failures include standard error type and message fields alongside the existing reason. [PR #168812](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168812). Thanks to [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve whole Unicode characters when widget syntax-error messages are clipped. [#176207](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176207). Thanks to [@hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Include the shared error type and message in onboarding and setup JSON failures, [#168488](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168488). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Correct `openclaw path` line numbers in JSONC files with a byte-order mark, [#168582](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168582). Thanks to [LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Explain when the TUI cannot find a local Gateway instead of reporting a misleading missing-token error in [#169413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169413). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Tool errors now distinguish malformed inputs from permission denials. [PR #176104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176104), [issue #176066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176066). Thanks [yinx65588-boop](https://github.com/yinx65588-boop) and reporter [BGreezy](https://github.com/BGreezy).
  - CLI resource cleanup errors appear on stderr even when they arrive after the reporting deadline. [PR #173566](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173566), contextual [issue #139485](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139485). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph) for the related report and investigation.
  - `openclaw logs` rejects a combination of `--local-time` and `--utc` before fetching logs. [#176262](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176262), [issue #176261](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176261). Thanks [@YangManBOBO](https://github.com/YangManBOBO) for the fix and report, and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - `openclaw path set` rejects blank numeric replacements instead of silently writing zero. [#178692](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178692). Thanks [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stop malformed tool-call argument lengths from being mistaken for authentication errors and producing misleading sign-in advice. [#168500](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168500), [issue #168450](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168450). Thanks to [@TYY-2005](https://github.com/TYY-2005) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [@r3n3x](https://github.com/r3n3x) for the report.
  - Busy `openclaw gateway suspend --json` returns one parseable JSON result and a nonzero exit status. [#176795](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176795). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Return structured command errors for Policy check, compare and watch in JSON or piped output. Watch appends a single-line error to its existing stream ([#181758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181758)). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve the original guarded response if cleanup fails and report the cleanup problem ([#181655](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181655)). Thanks [@tianhaotian](https://github.com/tianhaotian).
  - Database command operational failures return an error object with a type and message. [#183393](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183393) Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Caught update cleanup exceptions use the same error shape and retain available recovery details. [#184166](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184166) Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668).

  </details>

- [Public conversation links](/web/urls#public-session-transcripts) now show progress messages, short grouped tool summaries and images that earlier public pages left out. Review existing shares because these additions also appear on links you already published.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  Tool-result text and reasoning stay hidden, while other supported attachments appear as filenames. Review existing public links as described in Before updating; image contents are not redacted.

  - Show progress, short grouped tool summaries, images and attachment names on public conversation pages, with pages filled by visible items. [#169644](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169644), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Long HTML conversation exports display their transcript and navigation again. Regenerate older exports to get the fix.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Restore the transcript and navigation in long HTML session exports. Previously generated exports need to be regenerated. [#151029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151029). Thanks [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).

  </details>

- Tool results and conversation text now keep their original wording in the agent's context and chat history, instead of automatically changing code or file contents. Review chats before sharing them because file reads may include credentials.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep file reads, code and other conversation text unchanged in agent context, saved chats and public shares. An interrupted private completion from an older release may need its outcome checked and a fresh request started if an exact-input retry is rejected. [#169063](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169063) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Usage reports now include matching conversations beyond the first 1,000 and label missing measurements as unavailable rather than zero. You can export complete selections while other chats refresh, but incomplete selected data still blocks export, and missing provider measurements cannot be recovered.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Mark interrupted or unmeasured responses as unavailable across status, chat, session metadata, and usage summaries. Missing provider measurements cannot be reconstructed. [#120008](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/120008), [#119244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/119244); related open report [#151347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/151347). Thanks [@OWWZO](https://github.com/OWWZO), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [@EvolveAegis](https://github.com/EvolveAegis).
  - Reject malformed `usage.cost` agent selectors instead of silently reporting costs for the default agent. [#158696](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158696). Thanks [@alix-007](https://github.com/alix-007) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
