  - Show million-token values in `m` units in `/usage full` and custom usage templates. [#164684](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164684). Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Usage overview filtering, totals and paging cover matching sessions beyond the first 1,000. Detailed reports and exports remain available. [#169660](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169660), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Grynn](https://github.com/Grynn) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127245).
  - Show available usage results while slow rows still calculate, labeling those rows as computing. Reports have a 15-second deadline and a 20,000-instance limit after creator filtering. [#171336](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171336) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Usage backfill handles changed ownership of custom or shared stores and resumes slow batches. Reports can show partial totals while backfill progresses. [#171632](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171632) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Usage exports refuse incomplete data and offer guidance to refresh or narrow the range. [#171613](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171613) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The Usage export menu improves keyboard focus and dismissal. [#171347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171347) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Usage session details stay current as the view changes, and unavailable values update when the language changes. [#171630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171630) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Return marked partial totals and narrowing guidance when a usage read exceeds its time budget. [#171904](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171904), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Export a complete selection as JSON or CSV while unrelated sessions refresh. Incomplete selected sources still block export. [#171679](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171679), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show provider-reported totals for selected timeline intervals even when the By Type breakdown is incomplete. [#171872](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171872), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep retained transcripts with legacy colon-containing session IDs in usage reports without interrupting background refresh. [#172316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172316), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Fixes saved chats losing attachment references or supported model choices during recovery and maintenance. Attachment identity missing from older records and choices already deleted cannot be reconstructed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Use less memory while saving large chat histories and report their size when a save is slow. [#171540](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171540) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep new internal runtime continuations out of visible user history while retaining them for model replay. Existing stored markers are not rewritten. [#137273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137273), [related report #124244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/124244). Thanks to [LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [jpazvd](https://github.com/jpazvd).
  - Sessions using a symlinked state folder remain usable after a Gateway restart without changing stored paths. [Symlinked session state](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168361). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Activity refreshes blank previews after archived conversation history is restored. [Restored conversation previews](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168397). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Completed transcript searches no longer show an indexing warning just because an unchanged database writer initialized. [Transcript search readiness](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168550). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Accept valid worker transcript bytes from another JavaScript realm so affected saved-session history can load. [#168915](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168915), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce repeated session database reads during turns and retain managed-image attachments if an exact session read fails. [#169053](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169053), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Limit database and foreground transcript worker pools to reduce memory pressure on high-core hosts, and expose worker counts by script. [#169110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169110), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore cached-row invalidation after a failed database writer discovery followed by a successful write. [#169113](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169113), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recover session reads overtaken by committed writes so a completed agent reply can return normally. [#169174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169174), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A session read retries once after a transient invalidation, allowing the turn or final reply to continue. A second invalidation still returns the error. [#169199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169199), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169181). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@beru-ant-king](https://github.com/beru-ant-king).
  - Transcript rebuilding can reach affected sessions after compaction when a database revision is unknown. [#169467](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169467). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reduce acknowledgement work for messages sent to ready existing sessions, while accepting input durably before acknowledging it. [#169596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169596). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve attachment identity in transcript mirrors and accept completed-turn receipts across equivalent database paths. Older filename-only history cannot recover missing attachment identity. [#169710](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169710). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session creation avoids waiting on some unrelated database writes in [#170092](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170092). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Native transcript reads restore history when a read overlaps an archive in [#170096](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170096). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session repair and deletion skip archive work for empty transcripts while protecting later additions in [#170122](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170122). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Eligible active-session writes reuse resident transcript context to avoid redundant reads in [#170199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170199). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Eligible Gateway connections reuse resident profile facts and avoid an extra database worker read in [#170228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170228). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session observation saves digests and activity recaps with fewer writer requests. [PR #170271](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170271). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep fewer duplicate copies of a chat's saved history in memory. [PR #170304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170304). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Activity summaries can reuse a complete resident transcript across ordinary metadata entries, reducing worker reads in eligible warm sessions. [PR #170340](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170340). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Operators can enable SQLite worker timing at startup with `OPENCLAW_DIAGNOSTICS=sqlite.worker-timing`. Empty admission updates also avoid unnecessary bookkeeping. [PR #170235](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170235). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Connected `openclaw sessions cleanup --all-agents` waits for the Gateway result, and routine cleanup avoids redundant work. [#170618](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170618). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the fix and [personaltrainerkoichan](https://github.com/personaltrainerkoichan) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170474).
  - Idle maintenance uses fewer wakeups and database operations. While the healthy-idle delay histogram is paused, idle snapshots do not include a delay percentile, but callback stalls are still tracked. [#170531](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170531). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve already streamed text when the transcript writer is available, without saving unfinished tool calls ([#170697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170697)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retry configured session history cleanup after a blocked checkpoint recovers, while keeping history until a checkpoint completes ([#170913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170913)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce database reads during chat preparation ([#170745](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170745)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve an existing Gateway startup tracing setting across service reinstall or repair ([#170725](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170725), [#169808](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169808)). Thanks [@wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@hbgangcn1](https://github.com/hbgangcn1).
  - Park idle maintenance until database writes, authentication failures, or pairing activity require it ([#170755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170755)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid an unnecessary database statistics refresh after large history-only cleanups, reducing interruptions to session writes. [#170977](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170977), related [#170913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170913) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Rewrite a loaded transcript without copying its text again on the Gateway's main thread. [#171019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171019) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Speed up pending approval replay when many unrelated approvals are waiting. [#171047](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171047) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Release stale SQLite receipt and admission metadata when sessions are deleted or workers retire. [#171088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171088), related [#170855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170855) and [#171012](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171012) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let automatic session cleanup proceed during unrelated activity while rechecking the sessions selected for cleanup and their protections. [#171154](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171154) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retire idle transcript workers after five minutes to release memory after bursts of activity. A later read can incur a cold start. [#171155](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171155) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session cleanup makes fewer unproductive passes over protected entries and resumes when relevant protection ends. [#171211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171211), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Surplus shared-state readers retire after 30 idle seconds while one stays warm. [#171231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171231), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Compatible scoped SQLite reads reuse a bounded worker pool, reducing process churn for repeated reads. [#171299](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171299), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Idle maintenance uses fewer wakeups. Healthy non-authority directory watches recover missed events on a five-minute interval, while authority-sensitive and unhealthy watches retain their 30-second recovery interval. [#171318](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171318), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Empty maintenance inventories wait for relevant changes or deadlines instead of repeatedly querying the database. [#171320](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171320), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Larger installations avoid repeated full placement scans and share plugin inventory reads during skill discovery. [#171323](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171323), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Surplus transcript history and search workers retire after 30 idle seconds and restart when needed. [#171354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171354), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Repeated inference can reuse published authentication rows, avoiding redundant credential worker reads after session writes. [#171726](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171726) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway SQLite exchanges avoid repeatedly publishing unchanged admission facts. [#171788](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171788) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session settlement avoids repeatedly inspecting long conversation data. [#171850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171850) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Loading bounded transcript context avoids copying message data twice. [#171862](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171862) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Agent database workers no longer run large automatic checkpoints during transcript commits. Passive maintenance handles checkpointing. [#172009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172009) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Warm transcript writes avoid a transaction-held metadata wait when another session registers concurrently. [#172059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172059) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Full transcript reads decode once per event, reducing temporary JavaScript work on large histories. [#172112](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172112) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce the data serialized and transferred when appending to long durable sessions. [#172462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172462). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse a broker for isolated Gateway agent file reads while retaining their separate-process boundary. [#172476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172476). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Following logs avoids repeated scans of old log content on subsequent polls while retaining intended secret masking. [#172885](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172885), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@najef1979-code](https://github.com/najef1979-code) for the [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172507).
  - Closing or replacing a transcript database releases its cached history pages. [#172521](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172521), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Closing a history reader also clears cached reset-window references to its database. [#172541](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172541), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor preserves supported explicit session runtime choices, including in archived sessions. Choices already deleted cannot be reconstructed, and unavailable or invalid choices can still be cleared. [#173498](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173498), thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@s-salamatov](https://github.com/s-salamatov) for the [earlier report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/83098).
  - Session snapshot construction reuses validation of unchanged frozen session facts. [#172533](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172533), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Allow session resets to pass completed, inactive subagent history while still cleaning up unfinished work ([#179731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179731)). Thanks [@hidaba](https://github.com/hidaba) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce unnecessary provider dependencies retained by transcript history workers ([#181936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181936)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session-fork snapshots account for retained data more completely. Snapshots near the existing budget may be evicted sooner. [#182544](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182544) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Defer the remaining expired subagent cleanup backlog during shutdown and resume it after restart ([#185859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185859)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Fixes recovery instructions disappearing while a chat is loading and makes the failed-reply Retry and Copy error controls easier to find.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep keyboard focus on the copy button when draft recovery shows its copied confirmation. [#171591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171591) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show login recovery and its copy control while the chat route is still loading ([06afa0d9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/06afa0d926363318e1aa252ac9c019e75989aac2)).
  - Simplify the failed-reply card and make retry and error copying easier to find. [#172349](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172349). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Fixes missing Turkish navigation labels and refreshes translations in Workboard and Labs.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Refresh Workboard and Labs translation catalogs. [#172216](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172216).
  - Translate Turkish navigation tabs and nested section labels. [#128836](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128836), [reported in #128834](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128834). Thanks to [husodrn46](https://github.com/husodrn46) and [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- Fixes reasoning previews appearing twice when a turn finishes, while the chosen reasoning and model controls remain available through supported catalog refreshes.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Show reasoning as it arrives when you have enabled that view, then replace its preview with saved history without repeating it. [#117721](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/117721), [#88079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/88079), [#163567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/163567). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - With View → Reasoning enabled, each tool step can retain its preceding reasoning. Saved reasoning replaces previews in `on` mode, while `stream` previews disappear when the run ends. [PR #168898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168898). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@leapdragon](https://github.com/leapdragon).
  - Hide an unsupported context percentage when the selected model's capacity is unknown, while retaining available provider plan usage. [#169341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169341) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Chat shows fallback and return dividers and hides recovered same-run diagnostic errors. A named fallback candidate may have been skipped. [#169773](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169773), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
  - Compact context ring beside the model picker, with its usage details retained ([#170370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170370)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Respect the configured reasoning policy when showing session thinking options for runtime-routed models. [#170589](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170589), [issue #170528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170528). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for the fix and [@EmeraldPi](https://github.com/EmeraldPi) for the report.
  - Retain session thinking labels and options when a later catalog row omits the model. [#170659](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170659) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the effort slider available during a model catalog retry when the retained selected model is enabled ([commit 66c4b4a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/66c4b4a524dfcf571f1c529919aa84d668636266)).
  - Show the applicable agent verbosity in Control UI and local TUI history, with session overrides taking precedence ([#182059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182059)). Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

### Agents, Goals and automations

- Scheduled jobs keep a saved future retry when OpenClaw restarts instead of running it early.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Startup recovery preserves the scheduled retry time. [#171775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171775).

  </details>

- You can now guide the same running conversation from the Control UI or an administrator-linked messaging account. The task keeps its original permissions, which may be broader than those of the incoming account.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  [#188029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188029). Thanks to [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant). See [channel identity links](/concepts/user-model#channel-identity-links).

  An administrator must approve the account link. When collaborative steering is off, shared-token or password access can continue work only when the Control UI side has administrator authority and the channel side has current authorized-owner authority. New tasks use the submitting person's current permissions, and a shared credential does not establish who used it. Known bot messages do not use this exception.

  </details>

- Automation history now returns distinct run IDs for scheduled and on-exit runs whose IDs were missing from history responses.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  [#188848](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188848), included through [#188878](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188878). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Explicit manual request IDs remain unchanged, and imported older history can use its stored IDs.

  </details>

- Fixes a parent conversation being unable to answer while its [helper](/tools/subagents/operations) is still working, recovering from an interruption or waiting to deliver a result.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Let an eligible parent chat continue after repeated restarts instead of waiting behind an interrupted helper result. [#184100](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184100). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Continue protected conversations and private completions while child work is active, with current tool permissions preserved. [#174900](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174900) Thanks to [@enki](https://github.com/enki), [@Ricardodpalmeida](https://github.com/Ricardodpalmeida), [@Stronggenetics](https://github.com/Stronggenetics), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A parent can resume after all native children finish while their results await delivery. [#175246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175246) Thanks [@quqi1599](https://github.com/quqi1599), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Stronggenetics](https://github.com/Stronggenetics).
  - After a restart interrupts a quiet subagent, affected parents can receive a turn to reconcile unfinished work. [#175278](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175278) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Codex sessions can reply after recovering completed native children without conflicting with their own pending recovery writes. [#177564](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177564) Thanks [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen).
  - Parents can resume after a native child's follow-up finishes while its result awaits acknowledgment. [#177577](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177577), [#172837](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172837) Thanks [@linancn](https://github.com/linancn) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An interrupted native child can remain resumable without blocking an idle parent from replying. [#179099](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179099) Thanks [@wtfichigo](https://github.com/wtfichigo) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Let Claude CLI parent replies finish when nested work belongs to a subagent, while the parent's own background work still holds the reply open. [#182267](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182267) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@goslingmanagment](https://github.com/goslingmanagment).
  - Retain recovery notifications while a main session is awaiting preparation ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2251880c0f1df0cc22c56d73c7b6f8a57046ec59)).

  </details>

- Helper status now distinguishes work still in progress from a result that failed to reach its parent, and fixes old completed results reappearing after a restart in affected cases.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Show a failed helper delivery as failed, not as work still waiting, while keeping genuinely pending results visible. [PR #155019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155019), [issue #154834](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/154834). Thanks to [linhongyu510](https://github.com/linhongyu510) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), with the report from [jerryliang122](https://github.com/jerryliang122), investigation by [ifyouhavethisemail-afk](https://github.com/ifyouhavethisemail-afk), [assimetria-ai](https://github.com/assimetria-ai), and [waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault), and related reports from [itanyplus](https://github.com/itanyplus) and [rozenpl](https://github.com/rozenpl).
  - Codex sessions recognize still-pending child agents started on earlier turns. [#167368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167368), [#167354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167354), [#160737](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160737), [#167277](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167277). Thanks to [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
  - Let Codex plugins track outer tool calls until their recorded outputs arrive, helping them avoid treating an active run as idle. [#171661](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171661), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Stop permanently undeliverable Codex child completions from returning after registration or restart. `openclaw doctor --fix` can repair proven duplicate or obsolete assignments. [#173018](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173018) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jekmanis](https://github.com/jekmanis), and [@gisk0](https://github.com/gisk0).
  - Completed nested Codex work no longer leaves a stale assignment that repeats a warning after restart. [#179050](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179050), [#178395](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/178395) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@mmartoccia](https://github.com/mmartoccia).
  - After a restart, a replaced child no longer leaves its old task unresolved. The original parent receives a private notice, and parent reset leaves the replacement child alone. [#170513](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170513), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Completed helper [results and follow-ups](/tools/subagents/announce#private-parent-completion) now reach their original conversation after more supported restarts without repeating a result already delivered. Cancelled or replaced conversations do not automatically receive the old work.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep recorded helper results available when an authorized Claude CLI chat resumes. Account boundaries that cannot authorize replay receive metadata only. [#164175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164175), [#171195](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171195), [#159512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159512), [#154716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/154716). Thanks to [etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [potterdigital](https://github.com/potterdigital), [synapse-homeroot](https://github.com/synapse-homeroot), [cassthebandit](https://github.com/cassthebandit), [holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen), [oldrich333](https://github.com/oldrich333) and [xdom-jr](https://github.com/xdom-jr).
  - Prevent successful session completions from being rejected after their own writes and keep queued replies eligible for retry. [PR #169646](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169646). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A recovered subagent continuation can finish silently when its results have already been delivered, avoiding a duplicate final answer in that restart scenario. [#181260](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181260) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep a completed child result for an authorized in-place Resume after restart. Replacing the parent session does not deliver the old result to the new conversation. [#182587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182587) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Route nested Claude CLI background-agent results according to whether the launcher consumed or forwarded them, preserving the final answer in both cases. [#182516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182516) Thanks [@goslingmanagment](https://github.com/goslingmanagment), [@dmsrg399](https://github.com/dmsrg399) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve ACP input and completion notifications during session and dispatch races in [af03aa0](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/af03aa072fc53ae697dcc2baba7772c6e6fe4eba). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve eligible nested Claude CLI background task answers across snapshot orderings ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8bf0e03aa413f53694def4fad3b2d4e79e2c726d)). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Restart recovery leaves active subagent result delivery and cleanup to finish. [#170486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170486), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Completed child-result batches do not rerun the requester, and interrupted work resumes only when its original authority and child batch are available. [#171158](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171158). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recover eligible accepted agent-to-agent updates after a restart, in order, without rerunning the sender. Cancelled updates and updates from reset, archived, replaced or access-revoked sessions are not automatically delivered. [#171717](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171717), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Return visible child completions and failures to a yielded guest session. [#171796](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171796), thanks to [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) for the fix and [skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223) for investigating the [reported handoff problem](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171647).
  - Avoid retrying a subagent result that was already delivered when receipt recording fails, and retain useful errors for failed handoffs. [#171657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171657), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gave human browser-chat follow-ups in retained child sessions their own recovery record. [#172017](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172017). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Kept stopped requester turns cancelled when a subagent-result settlement is retried. [#172286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172286). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).

  </details>

- When a plugin tool opts in to sending the final reply, that reply now waits for the other tools in the same group to finish, so their errors or unfinished work are not hidden.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Wait for all tools in a group before an opted-in plugin reports a final answer, including failures. [#165291](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165291), [#164558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164558). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Fixes new Codex helpers starting successfully but failing when they try to ask their configured model for an answer.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Let visible subagents started during a parent turn reach their configured model and fallback sequence. [PR #169984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169984). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Codex native children can make model requests after startup without inheriting an expired startup context. [#176311](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176311), [#162119](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162119), [#165739](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/165739) Thanks [@enki](https://github.com/enki).

  </details>

- New conversations and side questions now wait when all model-request slots are occupied instead of failing immediately. Waiting adds time, and a parent whose helpers also need occupied slots can still stall.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Queue new turns and side questions at the existing 64-root inference limit. Waiting adds latency, and parents holding all roots while waiting for children can still stall until a root is freed. [#181895](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181895) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Changing the policy of a busy Codex conversation now leaves its current model and settings intact if the change cannot be applied, instead of switching models unexpectedly.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - When a configuration or policy change encounters a busy Codex thread, model fallback stops and the existing binding and configuration remain available. [#181204](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181204), [#181046](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181046) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@stronggenetics](https://github.com/stronggenetics) and [@jason-allen-oneal](https://github.com/jason-allen-oneal).

  </details>

- Fixes a failed quota check preventing later work from continuing in the affected recovery path.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Contain quota suspension lookup and marker-write failures so later work can proceed ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2fdae7f27e20089ed7f3ef62a800be934f965a28)).

  </details>

- Fixes unfinished cleanup after a failed Docker-backed command start, cleanup failures caused by Node startup instrumentation, and false Linux cleanup failures after the owned processes have exited. If cleanup is unconfirmed, inspect its status before assuming the command stopped.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  [#183247](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183247), backported in [#188851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188851), retains cleanup after a failed start. [#184044](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184044), adapted in [#188746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188746), keeps inherited Node startup hooks out of OpenClaw's process supervisors. The service-child retirement fix from [#188188](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188188), adapted in [#188962](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188962), corrects the Linux cleanup report. Commands keep their original environment. Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Stopping a [native Codex helper](/plugins/codex-harness) now waits for that helper's commands to finish stopping before confirming cancellation, without stopping unrelated work. If cleanup fails, a later Stop can retry; an interrupted reply alone does not mean its commands stopped.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  [#187646](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187646), adapted for the release in [#188974](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188974). Thanks to [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). This also covers a helper already interrupted before Stop or still working after its parent yielded. A helper that finishes successfully during Stop keeps its result after cleanup.

  </details>

- Fixes finished helpers leaving resources behind after a temporary cleanup failure, while avoiding a second cleanup for work already settled before restart.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Stop or recover only the helper belonging to the selected conversation, using its recorded identity. Ambiguous watches and incomplete visible spawns report an error. [#167966](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167966). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retry cleanup of completed Codex subagent subscriptions after a temporary failure while protecting active follow-ups. [#173547](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173547) Thanks to [@Kimiyu-186](https://github.com/Kimiyu-186).
  - Finish cleanup when a native child ends while its parent is waiting for an inline reply, allowing descendant settlement to complete. [#181933](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181933) Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
  - Reduce unnecessary idle maintenance work when completed subagent runs are retained. [#181560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181560) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Completed subagent launches release retained authorization resources and parent context. [#170855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170855), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Completed interrupted subagent cleanup is not repeated on restart. [#171091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171091). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Helper failures now keep their actual error details, and a progress update no longer appears to be a finished answer. Startup warnings about old helper records are summarized.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Report when two helper continuations conflict and surface cleanup failures in [#185386](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185386). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Startup combines legacy subagent authority warnings into a single summary with a count and sample IDs. [#170506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170506), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Subagent failure logs retain useful errors, and progress-only output is no longer counted as a visible final reply. [#170959](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170959). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- When many helper results arrive together, shortened findings say that text was omitted, and discarded records no longer use the parent's available reply space.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep discarded Claude helper records from consuming the parent's available reply space in [#184352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184352). Retained output and the existing line and frame limits still count. Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [jaxonparrott](https://github.com/jaxonparrott).
  - Bounded large subagent completion batches and marked truncated findings. [#172068](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172068). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171864).

  </details>

- Fixes some replies and helper follow-ups failing to start when another agent launches them in a separate conversation.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Prevent a conversation-writer mismatch from stopping independently launched `sessions_send` and helper turns. [PR #170077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170077), [issue #166734](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166734). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [mariorivas1](https://github.com/mariorivas1) and [ghbalf](https://github.com/ghbalf).

  </details>

- Fixes an agent waiting for your answer to a question you never saw. Blocking questions now reach the conversation that started the request.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Send a blocking [agent question](/tools/ask-user) to the conversation that started the request, so the person can see and answer it. Credential questions retain their existing handling. [PR #170039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170039). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- After OpenClaw restarts, eligible unfinished chats can continue with saved progress and partial answers intact. Cancelled work and tool actions whose effects are uncertain are not automatically repeated.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Continue eligible interrupted chats once their session or workspace is ready. [#168650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168650) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recovers eligible interrupted main chats when a shutdown marker was missed and reports archived recovery entries more clearly. [#168651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168651) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Return interrupted delegated work to its original parent, including quiet children and nested parents, while preserving pending final replies and respecting cancellation and reset. [#169144](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169144), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve the sender label on replayed inter-session messages, including forwarded messages. [#169130](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169130), thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Restart recovery retains eligible queued or preparing turns. Delegated work still needs reconstructable sender authority. If recovery stops, WebChat offers **Resume in new session** and other channels can use `/new` or `/reset`. [PR #170384](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170384). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retain restart recovery for a canceled main-session run whose cleanup finishes before its recovery record is saved ([#170699](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170699)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Continue eligible main dashboard turns interrupted by a backend restart drain ([#170858](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170858)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep unread watcher context for an ordinary turn after restart without creating replay turns or outbound replies ([#170781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170781)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Wait for pending worker placement before admitting a chat, retain interrupted-run recovery information, and allow selected trace diagnostics after a native reply. Session and worktree handling also avoid repeated work ([#170860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170860), [#170845](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170845)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recovered turns use a background lane so they do not fill the interactive lane. [#171242](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171242), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve partial output and continue eligible replies interrupted by a Gateway restart. [#172234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172234). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Record accepted unfinished replies for recovery before restart shutdown cancels them. [#172412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172412). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recover eligible authenticated CLI and API tasks after steering and repeated restarts while rechecking current authority. [#172356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172356). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the reason for permanent restart-event admission failures and stop retrying those failures indefinitely. [#172322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172322). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Return the established authority error when a recovery continuation is rejected after its authority closes. [#172195](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172195), related to [#172183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172183). Thanks to [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
  - Reuse plugin-managed grant authority during recovered operator runs while continuing policy and revocation checks. [#172482](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172482). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Delegated work remains eligible to resume when its completion check meets a Gateway shutdown window. [#172852](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172852), thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - After an unresumable delegated turn is retired during restart recovery, a conversation can accept a fresh authenticated message. The interrupted work is not replayed, and outstanding delivery or execution work can still block recovery. [#176682](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176682). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@enki](https://github.com/enki), and [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333). [@SamuelThornburg](https://github.com/SamuelThornburg) supplied an additional lab reproduction.
  - Recover eligible delegated child work and inline follow-ups after restart without duplicate completion notices. Missing execution records or grants cannot be recreated ([#181373](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181373)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restored child and descendant work waits until it is ready to continue instead of blocking later user messages. [#184022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184022) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@borclaw](https://github.com/borclaw) for the report.
  - Let later messages proceed after restart while a restored child remains paused ([#185999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185999)). Thanks to [@vacinc](https://github.com/vacinc).
  - Preserve the restart cancellation reason when a reply finishes during a session restart ([45e2bced](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/45e2bcedec8f0561a409faeeff4034327f4dba71)).

  </details>

- New configurations for supported Codex models now use corrected capacity defaults; existing configurations are not changed by this fix.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Use corrected capacity defaults for new configurations of supported GPT-5, GPT-5.1, GPT-5.2 and GPT-5.3 Codex models. [#114891](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/114891). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@snotty](https://github.com/snotty) for related evidence.

  </details>

- Fixes an unfinished [Goal](/tools/goal#automatic-continuation) stopping after an ordinary reply when the assistant still has work to do. In supported chats, it can take another turn toward the same objective without a new prompt.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  Automatic continuation covers built-in chat and supported local and cloud-worker Codex sessions, not other external runtimes, heartbeat turns or independent scheduled work. It stops when the Goal completes, pauses, becomes blocked or reaches a limit. Saved Goal state alone does not start work at boot. See [Goals](/tools/goal#automatic-continuation).

  - Keep working on an unfinished Goal in supported chats after a normal reply, until it finishes, pauses, stops, becomes blocked or reaches a limit. Saved Goals do not start work at boot. [#167897](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167897), thanks to [jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Fixes the Resume action being unavailable for an achieved Goal. You can continue that Goal in the same conversation, and the control shows when a resume attempt still needs confirmation or a retry.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Show that Goal Resume is still in progress on desktop and mobile, with a Retry resume control if confirmation does not arrive. [#169789](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169789), thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Achieved goals can be explicitly resumed without recreating the goal or conversation ([#171999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171999)). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) for reporting the issue.

  </details>

- Fixes an authorized scheduled follow-up losing the access it needs to finish work in the same conversation. If a one-time check is still waiting, it can schedule its next check on the same job.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Let newly scheduled follow-ups in the same authenticated conversation retain permitted access and schedule another check on the existing one-time job ([#176422](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176422)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- [Scheduled jobs](/automation/cron-jobs) now keep their selected account and allowed tools in affected handoffs, and supported deliveries appear in the conversation or topic that received them. Older jobs without a saved account selection can keep using environment or provider-config keys, but must explicitly select a stored account with `model@profile` before using it; model fallback will not bypass that choice.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep the selected model account on a new isolated job and report when its access is no longer valid ([#172590](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172590)). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Scheduled CLI jobs can use their native login without silently adopting a stored OpenClaw profile ([#172717](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172717)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Scheduled Discord message reads and edits work with Claude CLI authentication without a conflicting host account binding ([#172794](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172794)). Thanks [@patrick-erichsen](https://github.com/patrick-erichsen).
  - Fix false authorization failures when concurrent scheduled runs use the same unchanged saved API-key or OAuth account. [#179333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179333), [issue #179331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/179331). Thanks to [brandon-julio-t](https://github.com/brandon-julio-t) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Automation owners replacing allowed tools can capture fresh native app access, with guidance when capture is unavailable ([#170498](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170498), [reported in #170298](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170298)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@fbuechler](https://github.com/fbuechler) for the report.
  - Scheduled agents without an interactive sender can inspect or remove their own job within existing permissions ([#170864](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170864)). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Keep permitted tools available to an accepted manual run after its initiating request ends. Revoking the initiating device after activation no longer stops that run; disabling or removing the job still cancels it. [#168037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168037), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Gateway-pinned commands reject conflicting host or node targets before execution ([#176997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176997), [#169256](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169256)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm) for the report.
  - Keep delivered agent, command, and script results in the receiving chat or topic. Cross-agent destinations and native-only content without a text representation can produce a warning instead of a conversation entry. [#168996](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168996), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [Kimiyu-186](https://github.com/Kimiyu-186).
  - Keep a repeated automation result in later model context. [#169369](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169369), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Allow confirmed automation announcements to be committed during an active conversation turn, also helping restart handoff proceed. [#181506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181506). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Announce cron wakes retain the context of an existing CLI-backed chat across the next reply ([#176218](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176218), [#176208](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176208)). Thanks [@eliavb43](https://github.com/eliavb43) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Targeted system events from the selected conversation reach the isolated heartbeat prompt, including when the regular heartbeat schedule is disabled ([#175642](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175642), [#175599](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/175599)). Thanks [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@seriouslysean](https://github.com/seriouslysean) for the report.
  - Repair eligible older dashboard chat roots with Doctor so linked automations regain the appropriate memory access. [#182345](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182345). Thanks to [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
  - Keep configured-key jobs usable alongside the account-selection rules. [#188980](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188980). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Fixes eligible scheduled runs missed after a restart or clock change not catching up. Already completed slots are not run again; cancelled jobs stay cancelled, and work that may already have had an effect is not automatically repeated.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Make timed, manual and event runs share scheduling capacity, with supported waiting jobs eligible to resume after an interruption. [#169364](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169364), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168072).
  - Restore recorded cron results once and mark uncertain active runs interrupted instead of replaying commands, tool effects, or deliveries. Queued timed requests can resume, while manual or event requests whose transient payload is lost are skipped. [#169946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169946), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168072).
  - Keep eligible dashboard automation recovery available when restart draining interrupts a turn. [#170162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170162), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An interrupted early manual run preserves a separate scheduled one-shot occurrence. The interrupted attempt remains failed with unknown delivery and is not replayed ([#171727](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171727)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recurring jobs advance past a cancelled stop-time slot without replaying it ([#171988](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171988), [#171896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171896)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for the report.
  - Restart catch-up avoids repeating slots covered by a successful or skipped long run while keeping genuinely missed and forced occurrences ([#172497](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172497)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recover eligible missed recurring slots on normal ticks. Jobs configured to skip missed runs continue to do so, and an idle scheduler does not wake before its stored deadline. [#179928](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179928), [issue #179927](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/179927). Thanks to [andrewbowlds](https://github.com/andrewbowlds) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore admission of eligible catch-up runs after a restart or forward clock jump. [#187558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187558). Thanks to [romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda), [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd), and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Blocker explanations no longer turn completed runs into execution failures ([#170267](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170267), related [#161698](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161698) and [#165811](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/165811)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Delegated scheduled work waits for a child's result after a heartbeat-only handoff and honors an intentionally silent child ([#172407](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172407)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Cancelled runs retain unfinished cleanup and delivery work while returning cancellation promptly ([#170264](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170264)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - On-exit watches wait for command process cleanup before proceeding ([#171832](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171832)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Request cancellation when an already paused job is explicitly disabled during a forced run. Cron tool guidance also now describes workspace and sandbox inheritance for current conversations correctly. [#182350](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182350). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep a stopped task cancelled after restart even if one of its helpers finished while the task was stopping, while retaining the helper's result. [#169453](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169453), thanks to [freeqaz-openai](https://github.com/freeqaz-openai).
  - Optional late subagent and `sessions_send` results can settle silently while replies to actual requests remain required. [#172509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172509), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@tre1d](https://github.com/tre1d) for the [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/158332).

  </details>

- Automation history and alerts now distinguish a job that ran from a result that could not be delivered. If a one-time job fails and will not run again, the notice tells you to use Run Now or reschedule it.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Ask the original conversation to handle an eligible one-time job failure, or show the owner an alert if that repair cannot start. [#169102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169102), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [erlightbourn-source](https://github.com/erlightbourn-source) and [lonexreb](https://github.com/lonexreb) for identifying the failure-alert defect.
  - Keep cancelled or restart-retired one-shots quiet and record terminal failures for Doctor and eligible notifications or repair. [#169314](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169314), with related work in [#131590](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131590) and [#167908](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167908), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [lonexreb](https://github.com/lonexreb), and [erlightbourn-source](https://github.com/erlightbourn-source).
  - Avoid a premature still-failing alert when a recurring run began before repair finished. Jobs continue running during repair. [#170073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170073), [issue #170014](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170014), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Automatic repair waits keep their timeout across system clock changes ([#171097](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171097), [#170014](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170014), related [#170073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170073) and [#171094](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171094)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - A failure alert refused before sending can trigger the existing in-app fallback for the job owner ([#173347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173347), [#173077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/173077)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Winoren](https://github.com/Winoren) and to [@Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot) for the report.
  - Failed automation notices show a concise recovery summary while leaving the agent's response visible ([#170799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170799)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Tell you to use Run Now or reschedule when a local-provider failure leaves no future automatic run. [#169329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169329), with related local-provider alert work in [#169278](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169278), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep an unresolved announcement destination from turning a successful cron run into an execution error or triggering execution backoff. The report and delivery diagnostic remain in history. [#168380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168380), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Stop labeling unattributed channel failures as model timeouts in cron alerts and history. [#168091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168091), thanks to [KhanCold](https://github.com/KhanCold), [geekforlife](https://github.com/geekforlife) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167865), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for confirming the landing.
  - Preserve the scheduled turn's identity when a primary model times out and a configured fallback continues it. [#168509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168509), thanks to [synthalorian](https://github.com/synthalorian), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [wongwilliam425-ui](https://github.com/wongwilliam425-ui) for [the related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168456).
  - Let a configured fallback receive the original saved prompt after a provider timeout without duplicating it. [#168605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168605), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [wongwilliam425-ui](https://github.com/wongwilliam425-ui) for [the related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168456).
  - Avoid false authorization errors in authenticated local model server logs during cron connectivity checks. [#169795](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169795), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [franssauermann](https://github.com/franssauermann) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157140).
  - Avoid an extra unhandled-rejection warning when a recovered scheduled job fails. [#169525](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169525), thanks to [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).

  </details>

- Fixes automation editing losing the selected job after reconnecting or changing views, and keeps a submitted Create & run now action tied to the job being created.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Reconnect to the same Gateway with the selected automation and its run still open. [#169032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169032), [issue #152963](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/152963), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
  - Reload the latest settings after a stale pause or resume is rejected, so you can review them and retry deliberately. [#169290](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169290), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve the selected editor and a submitted Create & run now action when navigating during creation. [#169698](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169698), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A just-completed job can be edited or disabled without a false state-change error ([#172528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172528)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Delivery account suggestions use configured IDs instead of display names ([#171057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171057), [#171056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171056), related [#134368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134368)). Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii).
  - Job action and run-history menus use shared focus and selection behavior ([#170917](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170917)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The Automations model catalog can load after a fresh agent database is initialized ([#171321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171321)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Give clearer local errors and schedule examples for malformed `cron add` and `cron edit` input. Natural-language schedules remain unsupported. [#168163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168163), thanks to [xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Command automations preserve an explicitly configured `__proto__` environment override ([#171770](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171770)). Thanks [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).
  - Isolated cron runs avoid duplicating prior session lineage, and cleanup processes older oversized entries in pages ([#172072](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172072), [#171913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171913)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for the report.
  - Cron history cleanup avoids loading unrelated large payloads, reducing the risk of shared state-worker exhaustion ([#174530](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174530), [#174078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174078)). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and to [@asikorskiy](https://github.com/asikorskiy) for the report.
  - Scheduled run activation and completion avoid repeated database work ([#170874](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170874)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Release a delayed cron write and retry so other state writes have a chance to proceed. [#168291](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168291), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168072).
  - Keep busy cron operations from holding the shared database writer while waiting for host callbacks. [#168619](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168619), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168072).
  - Apply cron job edits to current data so concurrent runtime updates are preserved. [#169766](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169766), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168072).
  - Keep published session metadata consistent with fresh reads to avoid false ownership conflicts in scheduled work. [#169899](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169899), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep conversations counted as running in Online while delegated descendants are active, without counting the same conversation twice. [#168186](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168186), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Combine eligible session-creation notices into a bounded Home summary, leaving room for reminders and other notifications. Older entries can be omitted and the pending summary is lost on restart. [#168239](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168239), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stop repeated Dreaming malformed-key log errors for heartbeats using a session named `heartbeat`. [#168502](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168502), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [michaelrobertexp-source](https://github.com/michaelrobertexp-source) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168021).
  - Standing-intent expiry and cooldown maintenance runs at known deadlines and catches up after restart ([#171234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171234)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Standing-intent fire limits and cooldowns accept valid numeric strings ([#172329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172329), [#172297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172297)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@daddyclint82](https://github.com/daddyclint82) for the report.
  - Reject a FIFO roster promptly while continuing to support regular JSON files and symlinks to them. [#183614](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183614). Thanks to [Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep hot reload running while cron monitor reconciliation has a scheduled retry. [#170951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170951), [report #170644](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170644), earlier [report #135433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/135433) and [#148592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148592). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Navras98](https://github.com/Navras98), and [@davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini).

  </details>

- Fixes agents continuing from an older request after an interruption or missing newly discovered tools in affected CLI chats. The current request and permitted tool choices remain available.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep the original request available when a built-in or authorized CLI turn resumes after interruption, [#168279](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168279). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the agent focused on your current request unless you ask it to return to earlier work, [#168906](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168906). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [alter](https://github.com/alter) for related investigation.
  - `/btw`, `/side`, and `/loop` preserve multiline questions and recurring prompts, including indentation. [#176293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176293), [issue #176250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176250). Thanks [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for reporting the issue.

[#176577](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176577), [issue #175670](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/175670). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@jackylecalvez2](https://github.com/jackylecalvez2) for reporting the issue.

Thanks [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@Card4Philippe](https://github.com/Card4Philippe).
  - Agent guidance no longer recommends disabled chat update and restart commands. [#182001](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182001), related [issue #181956](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181956). Thanks [@MonkeyLeeT](https://github.com/MonkeyLeeT) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@Card4Philippe](https://github.com/Card4Philippe) for reporting the issue.

  - Preserve the order of captions and images in OpenAI Responses tool results in [#169119](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169119). Thanks [@Shenrui-Ma](https://github.com/Shenrui-Ma) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

Thanks [@TUARAN](https://github.com/TUARAN), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [@kseczhouyb](https://github.com/kseczhouyb).

Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@lator57-gif](https://github.com/lator57-gif).

[#168726](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168726). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Supply earlier-message metadata and policy-permitted history lookup after an account switch, without automatically replaying the transcript or durable context ([#168781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168781)). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Fixes new agent setup being blocked by stale session or model information, and shows supported workspace, model and access choices for the agent you are creating.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Let explicitly authorized local coding helpers use the tools granted to them ([#168085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168085)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - Recognize the current agent's model aliases as slash shortcuts ([#168287](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168287), [#168122](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168122)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and co-author and reporter [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Local-provider preflight skips count toward the configured failure-alert threshold. Jobs stay scheduled, and full failure-alert opt-outs still apply. [#169278](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169278), [issue #168026](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168026). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  - Keep an open agent database connection associated with its original database when a symlink is retargeted. [PR #169701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169701). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Skip stale multiple-store sentinel registrations during configured-agent startup and add standalone Doctor cleanup for invalid registrations. Cleanup is deferred during updates. [PR #170043](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170043), [issue #169764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169764). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [ourcoms2018](https://github.com/ourcoms2018).
  - Keep the revoked status and remove the Revoke button after a successful revocation. [PR #169695](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169695). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- If a tool times out, OpenClaw now keeps completed output and tells you when its effect is unknown, rather than assuming it is safe to run again. Automatic retry is limited to work known not to have started or safe to repeat.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Finish optional Ultrafast model discovery within its time limit even if the computer clock changes ([#168144](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168144)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
