  - Keep the session fork metadata timeout stable across clock changes ([#168298](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168298)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Release a canceled startup's queue position so a later attempt can proceed to its own handoff wait. Replacement writes still wait for the old process to exit ([#168347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168347)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Keep the native Codex inference connection timeout steady when the system clock changes after admission. [#168731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168731). Thanks to [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep Codex app-server process inspection within its intended time budget when the host clock changes. [#168736](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168736). Thanks to [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - Resume queued chats after a plugin reload when the exact old thread is unloaded, and preserve startup failures and cancellations as their intended outcomes. [#182104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182104) Thanks [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - R2 downloads resume after transient interruptions during verification or restore. Starting a new command begins a new download. [#171292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171292), reported in [#171165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171165). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini).
  - Added a safe retry or explicit failure for detected text-only tool calls. [#171898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171898), with related reports [#138245](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138245) and [#168744](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168744). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@5jrfrbd6z9-spec](https://github.com/5jrfrbd6z9-spec) and [@chitowntony](https://github.com/chitowntony).
  - Allowed consented Muse chats with conversation tool restrictions while retaining the native permission boundary. [#172361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172361). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserved recovered final replies and required complete, identified delivery before recording success. [#171929](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171929), related to [#155119](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155119). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - When a tool times out before it finishes, report the interruption and keep completed output without automatically rerunning the turn. [#172983](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172983) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@budzation](https://github.com/budzation) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172899).
  - Preserve blank lines when agents read files across pages, including reads that start on a blank line. [#173835](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173835) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep recognized final exit, signal, timeout and launch-failure messages when long command output is shortened. [#174030](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174030) Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Honor explicitly preserved login environment credentials in Claude CLI readiness checks so affected child-agent requests can proceed. [#175114](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175114) Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Interrupted tool recovery requires safe tools and refuses CLI runtimes that cannot enforce them. The same backport improves Codex handoffs and command results, preserves the active request during compaction, supports OpenClaw goal continuation in supported local and cloud-worker Codex sessions, and provides a masked GitHub reconnect flow in capable Control UI sessions. Native Codex automatic goal continuation remains disabled. [#180270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180270) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Native session read failures show their actual error, allowing eligible timeouts before execution to use the existing scheduled-job retry policy. [#175654](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175654) Thanks [@enki](https://github.com/enki).
  - Keep internal Gateway and plugin response timeouts on their elapsed-time budget when the computer clock changes. [#171094](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171094), related [#108423](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/108423) and [#154321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154321). Thanks to [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Fixes some shutdowns waiting on processes that already exited and leaves more worker and reader resources cleanly closed. If cleanup cannot be confirmed, inspect its status rather than assuming the process stopped.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Finish shutting down retained worker connections even after their worker list has cleared. [#168862](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168862) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [@Captain69G](https://github.com/Captain69G).
  - Idle Gateways stop periodic plugin-state expiry sweeps. Expired values remain unreadable, while inactive namespaces can retain expired rows on disk until a later write. [#169524](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169524). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stall diagnostics add callback labels and a bounded trailing CPU sample on supported Node 26 processes. The sample describes recent activity rather than identifying one callback as the cause. [#169535](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169535). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid retaining empty request activity records and length-specific cumulative tool metric series. Exact parameter lengths remain on tool spans. [#169786](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169786). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let an admitted wake callback finish under its captured handler, and start listeners added during an event with the next event. [#169648](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169648). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway shutdown and restart finish their lifecycle records before shared database workers close in [#169983](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169983). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Idle Gateways defer unobserved health, GitHub discovery, and upstream checks until needed. [PR #170389](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170389). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway shutdown waits for accepted session updates to finish preparation before closing connected clients. Existing client delivery checks still apply. [#170555](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170555). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Completed and failed operations release owned resources more consistently, including the OpenRouter sign-in listener and Tlon HTTP resources. [#170439](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170439). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor and startup inspections release temporary plugin captures, and rejected file-lock handoffs release the acquired lock. [#170530](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170530). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stalled transcript indexing receives a five-second grace during Gateway shutdown before cancellation. Saved transcripts and required cleanup remain protected, and unfinished indexing can be rediscovered after restart. The grace is not a total shutdown deadline. See the [restart recovery guide](/gateway/restart-recovery). [#170493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170493). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169777), and [O96a](https://github.com/O96a) for shutdown advice.
  - Ordinary shutdown gives execution cleanup time to release worktrees, and a warning-only Codex profile check no longer holds up startup. [#171308](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171308), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - After a validated external drain, supervised restarts skip repeated waits and settle queued work while preserving restart recovery and final transcript writes. [#171399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171399), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Drain status reports blockers promptly, and the deployment controller can retry transient transport failures within its original deadline. The changed controller executable and library need to be adopted together, and a failed poll does not authorize interruption. Gateway hello also no longer waits for watched-session membership preparation. [#171442](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171442), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Finish shutdown sooner when attached command trees have exited. The plugin SDK process runtime also exposes a latched `exited()` check on the process-tree handle. [#171725](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171725) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Clean up streaming observation when a client disconnects while authorization is pending. [#171713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171713) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prevent an early Gateway exit when an explicit Node heap budget is combined with `--exit-on-contradictory-flags`. Workers inherit the configured ceiling in this diagnostic mode. [#171490](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171490) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Busy shutdown cancels in-flight worker preparation while preserving recovery state. [#171843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171843) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway stall monitoring removes process-wide asynchronous hook overhead. Task labels now cover synchronous scopes, and some stalls during healthy idle periods may go unreported. [#172531](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172531), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Foreground transcript readers release database resources during cleanup, addressing a Windows cleanup hang. [#172506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172506), thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
  - Inspect macOS processes natively for supervisor cleanup and Codex containment, including a bounded recheck when an exiting process is briefly unavailable. Migrate the process-runtime SDK from `DarwinProcess.uid` to `owner`. [#182215](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182215), combining [#174147](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174147), [#174284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174284), [#175368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175368), [#176576](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176576) and [#176612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176612). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve file-backed standard input when a service-child supervision anchor retires. [#187804](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187804). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Keep orphaned-process cleanup on elapsed time so clock changes do not shorten or extend its shutdown grace period ([#181335](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181335)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Keep spawn-broker child cleanup and confirmation deadlines stable across system-clock changes ([#181360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181360)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Honor a stop signal during restart drain after the existing cleanup completes ([#186055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186055)). Thanks to [@freeqaz](https://github.com/freeqaz).
  - Deliver confirmed service-child cancellation exits to existing and late exit listeners ([7fdc3dcd](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7fdc3dcd45be661e4e032281af7371a5dd415cbb)).
  - Avoid duplicate recurring maintenance runs under clock drift, including the exact half-period boundary. [Scheduler fix](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/344b71032447521feaff383f619667801dff6d83) and [boundary follow-up](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/741a6d2cf6e791c26b194d500fdf4708ec0abce4), thanks to [Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
  - Avoid transient Skills worker filesystem watches that could delay shutdown, observed on macOS. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a6491a1b37e138700d9db52e8ee9bafcebe9466a)
  - Close retained agent directory watchers during shared-state shutdown. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7e4c0f7378ea285e405bdb9cfca3207d2529d30f)
  - Close idle SQLite readers before broker shutdown to avoid an extra wait during Linux Gateway shutdown and restart. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/075dc2f6777726c52c808228f8dd43ee746d81e4)
  - Reduce potential memory retention when a scheduler scope closes after its parent has stopped. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d8af435b5ba1495f35f5df6a48b740dd2029d77a)
  - End pending device scope-upgrade waits as interruptions when the Gateway scheduler shuts down. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0dc75157e1b1f9abfafd661b1e16276990c0f649)
  - Preserve empty arguments and the positions of later arguments in generated systemd service commands. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/82600d8d4828223dcad244612312d30d69d9763a), thanks to [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) and [gideonpraise](https://github.com/gideonpraise).
  - Restore structured cleanup duration, escalation timing and signal failure fields for tools inspecting service-child cleanup errors. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/edd07a352505e6443c853f835b78a2ab6a57e9c9), thanks to [romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).

  </details>

- Fixes valid nested helper results being rejected, and tells you when the helper has used up its result-submission retries instead of implying later submissions were checked.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Accept valid nested JSON objects and arrays when a helper returns them as a JSON string. [#150209](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150209), [reported in #150167](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/150167). Thanks to [chelsealong](https://github.com/chelsealong), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [residentb2b](https://github.com/residentb2b).
  - Report when a collector has exhausted its structured-output retries instead of presenting the previous schema error as a new validation result. Later calls are not evaluated after the budget is spent ([#181515](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181515), [#181442](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181442)). Thanks [@sukhdeepjohar](https://github.com/sukhdeepjohar), [@oldrich333](https://github.com/oldrich333) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Fixes authorized CLI-backed tool calls being refused while another command runs, or failing before the tool actually starts because of a local connection race.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Allow authorized host commands to run together without a false denial caused by another command's usage record. [PR #171143](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171143). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - CLI-backed agents avoid a local connection reuse race that could fail a tool call before it started. Long-running responses remain supported. [PR #172980](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172980), [issue #172871](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172871). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
  - Gateway-owned session ID lookup and creation for agent requests. [PR #172110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172110). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Fixes agents getting stuck after you answer a question, steer an active turn or continue a long chat after compaction. Pending helper results remain available for supported recovery after interruptions.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep existing agents available while you add or remove a different agent ([#169249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169249)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Wait for the requested agent to finish starting without holding up ready agents ([#168826](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168826)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks [@xiaozishan](https://github.com/xiaozishan) for the report.
  - Prevent a settled database write from leaving an agent stuck rejecting messages when a separate read remains open ([#169064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169064)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Skip completed child-session reads during subagent restart recovery so an unreadable completed session does not block later active runs ([#169034](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169034)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep unconfirmed subagent completion announcements eligible for bounded recovery after transcript contention ([#169015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169015)). Thanks [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep accepted steering messages in order and their consumption status aligned with the saved conversation ([#169061](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169061)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Resume the waiting agent after you answer its question. [#169306](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169306) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - On Linux, steering an active native Codex conversation no longer hangs and blocks later messages. [#169343](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169343) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Restores compatible Codex follow-ups after conversation preparation fails, including while a background command continues. Incompatible policy changes remain refused while that work is active. [#169431](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169431) Thanks [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Preserves separate watched-task results across a Gateway restart when tasks share a child session. Replacing the same task still invalidates its older result. [#169489](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169489) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fixes a next message failing while saved conversation history is rebuilt after native compaction. [#169583](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169583) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keeps recent context consistent in long conversations when messages arrive during a read. [#169619](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169619), [related issue #127629](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127629). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Workspace prompt prefixes remain steadier as capabilities change, and live and replayed failed-turn histories agree. [#170516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170516), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An agent can continue once after malformed tool arguments follow consumed failures that were all proven not to have started. [#170615](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170615), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@r3n3x](https://github.com/r3n3x) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170522), [@fransqaas](https://github.com/fransqaas) for related work and [@akushonkamen](https://github.com/akushonkamen) for the investigation.
  - Responses tool calls using a supported alias no longer fail with a false tool-name change, including when spawning a child. [#170751](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170751), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Deferred agents become available before background transcript repair completes. [#170849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170849), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Delayed worker results remain valid when unchanged agent database schema facts are republished. [#170194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170194), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

### Messaging, voice and media

- Fixes Telegram repeating a spoken answer when its final caption uses different paragraph breaks from the streamed text.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Captioned final speech in Telegram no longer repeats the same answer when its paragraph breaks differ from the streamed text. [#181161](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181161), fixing [#181070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181070). Thanks [@mushuiyu886](https://github.com/mushuiyu886), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@julianloperar](https://github.com/julianloperar).

  </details>

- Fixes speech-only heartbeat replies not being delivered and keeps the spoken answer available to later OpenAI Completions turns.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Speech-only heartbeat replies can be spoken and delivered under the existing voice settings, and later OpenAI Completions turns can retain the spoken answer in context. [#170357](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170357), [#170027](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170027), [#170062](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170062). Thanks [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Twilight-Networks](https://github.com/Twilight-Networks) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for the replay probe.

  </details>

- [Talk voice and transcription choices](/nodes/talk/realtime-sessions) now keep the settings you selected when providers refresh. Language hints reach automatically found Python Whisper, and supported GPT-Live subscription voice accepts a valid pasted ChatGPT access token with an account ID.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Talk choices remain available during provider refresh. [#172556](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172556). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Talk keeps a configured custom Azure Speech endpoint when only the voice is overridden. [#151253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151253). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Automatically discovered Python Whisper receives a configured or requested language hint. [#164841](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164841), [#164840](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164840). Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - GPT-Live subscription voice can use a valid pasted ChatGPT access token carrying an account ID for Voice Call, Discord and Talk. [#166211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166211). Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Talk voice reads and selections clean up authorization resources after success or error. [#170386](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170386). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Talk provider choices retain the selected speech provider's configuration during readiness checks. [#170412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170412). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Talk checks speech, transcription and realtime providers together when building the provider list. [#170806](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170806). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Talk provider choices no longer repeat the same realtime setup while loading. [#171099](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171099). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Talk shows the applied settings rather than an unapplied edit; pending or rejected changes can appear once a reload succeeds. [#171607](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171607). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Talk provider choices can load without checking the same transcription and audio models repeatedly. [#172538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172538). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - ElevenLabs batch transcription receives configured options, including explicit false values. [#177975](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177975), fixing [#168580](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168580). Thanks [@HeZ2z](https://github.com/HeZ2z), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [@exGeni](https://github.com/exGeni).
  - Talk speech and realtime provider keys accept different case or surrounding whitespace. Select a provider explicitly when more than one is configured. [Commit 4e1f951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4e1f9518dfaab09f128762ed689389cf9d8a0df9). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) and [@gideonpraise](https://github.com/gideonpraise).
  - DeepInfra and OpenRouter speech requests use a two-minute default timeout. Configured and per-call timeouts take precedence. [#175614](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175614). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Fixes spoken replies going missing when speech arrives after the text, and fixes the first-reply crash in iPhone relay Talk. Telegram voice captions and macOS Talk replies also keep their accompanying text in the affected cases.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Delayed automatic speech joins the answer it belongs to, even after a reload ([#171090](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171090), related [#170937](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170937)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Free-text TTS tags keep the spoken reply and Telegram voice caption visible. [#137329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137329), [#137281](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137281). Thanks [@wangyan2026](https://github.com/wangyan2026), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@eiriktorheim](https://github.com/eiriktorheim) and [@vobornik](https://github.com/vobornik).
  - Talk closes an owned session when its valid ID has surrounding whitespace. [#167066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167066). Thanks [@hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Voice Wake matches case-sensitive Signal group IDs when selecting a session route. [#170823](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170823). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Local speech generation no longer includes spoken-message previews in debug logs. [#170999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170999). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - iPhone Gateway-relay Talk fixes a first-reply crash and improves built-in speaker playback and interruption. [#168416](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168416), report [#168415](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168415) and related [#167810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167810). Thanks [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and related reporter [nathan-85](https://github.com/nathan-85).
  - macOS Talk waits for the accepted run to finish and speaks its matching complete reply, including slow responses. [#164485](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164485), reports [#153145](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153145) and [#127558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127558). Thanks [PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [rsaeks](https://github.com/rsaeks).
  - iOS Talk microphone recovery notices and host-entry guidance are translated in 21 non-English locales. [#181265](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181265).

  </details>

- Fixes a disconnected FaceTime call blocking the next authorized call until OpenClaw restarts.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - A disconnected FaceTime call releases the bridge without requiring a Gateway restart before the next authorized call. [#154230](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154230), [#154228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/154228). Thanks [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin), [@omarshahine](https://github.com/omarshahine) and [@beeven](https://github.com/beeven).

  </details>

- Voice Call commands now keep their JSON results readable by other tools and reject unsupported call-brief files instead of waiting indefinitely.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Voice Call steer returns clean JSON without startup text mixed in. [#169430](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169430). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Voice Call commands reject special files supplied as call briefs. [#183417](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183417). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Voice Call diagnostics reject nonregular custom logs without blocking on POSIX. [#172743](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172743). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- [Image generation and editing](/tools/image-generation) now reject invalid size and shape options before contacting a provider, while supported URL-backed images can be returned as results. Video results show which options a provider ignored. If you pinned a retired Together Wan 2.2 or Kling video model, choose a supported model before generating again.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - ComfyUI image, video and music workflow polling keeps the configured timeout across system clock changes. [#162779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162779). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Image generation and editing tell you when a resolution is unsupported before sending a request. Supported values are 1K, 2K and 4K. [#170154](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170154). Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - OpenAI-compatible image generation and editing accept URL-backed results under the existing network and image-size policies. [#170278](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170278), [#162717](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162717). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), reporter [@lucthaobybgkw3232-eng](https://github.com/lucthaobybgkw3232-eng) and [@acedatacloud-dev](https://github.com/acedatacloud-dev) for the compatibility report.
  - Video generation reports ignored provider options in text and JSON results. [#171172](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171172). Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559).
  - Image generation and editing reject malformed shape settings before sending a request or creating a file. Positive numeric ratios remain supported. [#172082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172082). Thanks [@KrasimirKralev](https://github.com/KrasimirKralev) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Together video defaults to Wan 2.7 and uses a longer polling window. Explicit retired Wan 2.2 and Kling IDs need replacement. [#172364](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172364). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Generated images, music and video can wait behind a busy chat without reporting failure too early. Text and image attachments keep their expected characters and file formats in the affected cases; a permanent delivery failure still reports the problem.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Windows-1252 text and CSV attachments retain accents and symbols when their MIME type has no charset. [#165771](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165771). Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Uploaded attachments remain available if the request cannot confirm it was accepted. You still see the error, and no agent run starts. [#167369](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167369). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Generated image, music and video results queued behind a busy chat avoid a premature failure warning. A permanent delivery failure still reports the problem and retains the media reference. [#172637](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172637), with the earlier proposal in [#154064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154064). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen) and [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668).
  - Public image attachments use extensions matching their resolved formats, including queued deliveries. [#182494](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182494). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Background media handoff deadlines use elapsed time. [#185704](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185704). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Images staged for sandboxed WebChat messages now load for the agent; the same merge also fixes Linux service detection, model listing and plugin inspection. [Commit e26afcf](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e26afcff4beb0cfb90a1ca6e9cbde7d713472895).
  - When a generated-media batch fails, cleanup starts in request order and preserves the original save error. [Commit a316adf](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a316adfcce2e03619282801914a3daf7383cdf1c).

  </details>

- A playback conversion can still return its completed result if closing the output file reports a problem, and cache cleanup now reports its own failures.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Playback conversion reports output-file close failures while preserving the conversion result. [#174668](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174668). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Playback cache cleanup reports errors and avoids an extra eviction when an entry has already disappeared. [#175634](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175634). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Replies in long channel threads can include the latest 50 messages when the thread window is available. Reactions, shared contacts, quoted messages and native channel menus also stay attached to their intended messages in the affected channels.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Long channel threads provide the newest 50 replies as context when that window can be selected reliably. [#120824](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/120824), [#98870](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/98870). Thanks to [@Linux2010](https://github.com/Linux2010), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@qingminglong](https://github.com/qingminglong).
  - Reaction lists show available names and person IDs. [#151128](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151128). Thanks to [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Teams direct messages show useful progress steps on separate readable lines. [#157704](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157704), [#157702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157702). Thanks to [@rdbartram](https://github.com/rdbartram) and [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - Reactions and reaction lists target the current channel-thread reply. [#166582](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166582), [#166178](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166178). Thanks to [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Incoming dice rolls include the emoji and value in agent and reply context. Group mention requirements continue to apply. [#137112](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137112), [related outbound request #137110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137110). Thanks to [@adeepn](https://github.com/adeepn) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Voice notes and audio uploads can show their duration when `ffprobe` is available. Uploads still proceed if probing fails. [#142552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142552), [#142548](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/142548). Thanks to [@szafranski](https://github.com/szafranski) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Shared contact cards and replies to them retain the contact name and phone details without treating those details as a bot mention. [#144602](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144602). Thanks to [@JamesY023](https://github.com/JamesY023) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Quoted reply text stays attached to its message when automation context is present in a CLI-backed agent prompt. [#166869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166869). Thanks to [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Authorized Telegram private-topic reactions reach their originating session when cached topic context is available. On a cache miss they fall back to the base DM, or are dropped when `requireTopic` is enabled. [#175946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175946). Thanks to [xiaopan-xydt](https://github.com/xiaopan-xydt) and [RaphazZze](https://github.com/RaphazZze) for the report.
  - Messaging actions can target thread and topic sessions when you opt in, with the usual permissions still applying. [#168846](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168846). Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Discord keeps delivered status, menu, model-picker and plugin replies with their commands in conversation history. [#169200](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169200). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Slack keeps delivered command menus and their commands in conversation history. [#169236](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169236). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep delivered Mattermost model and provider pickers and selection confirmations in history ([#169255](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169255)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - A new request no longer gets confused with earlier messages in its thread ([#169817](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169817)). Thanks to [yousan](https://github.com/yousan) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve multiline group slash-command arguments such as `/btw` questions and `/loop` prompts ([#183606](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183606)). Thanks to [L4XB](https://github.com/L4XB) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Feishu merged forwards include available sender and timestamp information, with sender-name lookup bounded to the first 50 rendered messages. [#150214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150214), [#150165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/150165). Thanks to [@chelsealong](https://github.com/chelsealong), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@peach980](https://github.com/peach980).

  </details>

- Fixes Microsoft Teams messages stalling on malformed replies or slow pasted images, and makes message reads and lists easier to read. A Teams allowlist whose names cannot be resolved still blocks access.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Give slow pasted images in Microsoft Teams a longer download budget and preserve a separate budget for optional message context ([#170437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170437)). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [EbulenConsult](https://github.com/EbulenConsult) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169917).
  - Teams message reads and searches show message content and metadata in human-readable CLI output. [#171153](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171153). Thanks to [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Microsoft Teams list items following fenced code blocks render separately instead of being absorbed into the code block. [#182959](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182959). Thanks to [L4XB](https://github.com/L4XB) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Prevent malformed Teams replies and attachment HTML from stalling message handling ([#183438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183438)). Thanks to [LeizhenZhang](https://github.com/LeizhenZhang) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep fully unresolved Teams team, channel, and group sender allowlists restrictive at startup ([#184989](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184989)). Thanks to [eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - Update the affected Teams authentication dependencies, including for managed npm plugin installations ([#187293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187293)). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and upstream contributor [Robbie-Microsoft](https://github.com/Robbie-Microsoft).

  </details>

- [Matrix encryption state](/channels/matrix/encryption) now refuses competing writers and invalid saved snapshots before they can replace retained room keys. If separate installations share an account, stop older writers and upgrade them together. An older version may already have lost keys it never saved; explicit snapshot recovery can lose keys received since the last good save. Room policies also stay with the intended room when its display name changes.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Matrix refuses overlapping encrypted-state writers and invalid saved snapshots before restoring them. Doctor can inspect that state and guide an explicit recovery. [#160707](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160707). Thanks to [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit) and [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
  - Allow channel plugins to use optional asynchronous account hooks and allow Matrix persisted-state reads without blocking the Gateway thread. Bundled Matrix requires a matching V2 host. [#168481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168481). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Pace Matrix live-preview updates after slow sends and edits ([#169975](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169975)). Thanks to [xili-ytl](https://github.com/xili-ytl), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80) for the [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/106644).
  - Matrix tool and mention policies use room identity rather than an editable display name, while configured aliases remain supported. [#171645](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171645). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168134).
  - Matrix replies to ordinary room messages now follow your silence and visibility settings; commands still receive their expected response. [#179506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179506). Thanks to [xiaopan-xydt](https://github.com/xiaopan-xydt), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [doomy](https://github.com/doomy) for the report.
  - Matrix streaming previews no longer repeatedly highlight incidental mentions; the final message still includes its mentions ([#183372](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183372)). Thanks to [L4XB](https://github.com/L4XB) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore Matrix execution approval requests to the originating room and approver DM when the native handler is async-only ([commit 9356d64](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9356d64ceac6cc6187997677467584e10a3a9236)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- [Telegram](/channels/telegram/messaging) now keeps slow voice notes and messages waiting behind a busy reply eligible for retry instead of silently losing them. Saved ordinary final text can recover after eligible delivery failures, but if Telegram accepted the first send and its acknowledgment was lost, the reply may appear twice. On the first legacy-queue import, messages older than 24 hours are marked failed and will not run, including ones that were never processed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Telegram polling starts in source-checkout and captured-plugin layouts, including packaged builds. [#154286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154286), [#154180](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/154180). Thanks to [@xydt-juyaohui](https://github.com/xydt-juyaohui), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@markfietje](https://github.com/markfietje).
  - Replaying the same audio message suppresses duplicate transcript echoes while its receipt is retained, up to 24 hours and subject to the receipt cap. [#162565](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162565), [related cross-account report #146311](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/146311). Thanks to [@dankarization](https://github.com/dankarization) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Group voice mentions are recognized when a group-scoped rule allows audio transcription. Direct-only rules continue to exclude group audio. [#163746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163746). Thanks to [@jewseppi](https://github.com/jewseppi) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep Telegram's temporary progress preamble visible during verbose tool runs while sending diagnostics separately. [#168225](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168225). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Show authored preamble text without the internal heading in Telegram previews. [#168281](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168281). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve tables and their placement when optional Telegram rich replies include neighboring quotes or lists. [#168383](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168383), [issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168366), [rich-message guide](/channels/telegram/rich-messages). Thanks [@dhruv1220](https://github.com/dhruv1220), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@barry-ssb](https://github.com/barry-ssb).
  - Count the full Telegram command progress row against the configured line limit. [#168464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168464), [issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130144). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [@ml12580](https://github.com/ml12580).
  - Render Telegram progress notes as prose instead of monospace code. [#168723](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168723). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - An eligible saved Telegram text answer gets one recovery send after an ambiguous network failure; the first send may already have reached the chat. [#168738](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168738). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Avoid duplicate Telegram answers in chat history when another message queues during streaming. [#169019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169019). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - A Telegram voice note or message waiting behind a busy reply can still be processed after a timeout while its original attempt is pending. Existing retry limits apply. [#169202](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169202), [related issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127229). Thanks [@diregoblin](https://github.com/diregoblin), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@Grynn](https://github.com/Grynn), [@travellingsoldier85](https://github.com/travellingsoldier85), [@PaGrom](https://github.com/PaGrom) and [@cablackmon](https://github.com/cablackmon) for the reports and investigation.
  - Avoid a session-wide scan when mirroring Telegram deliveries in long histories ([#169663](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169663)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - On the first import of older Telegram messages, mark those over 24 hours old failed instead of running an old instruction. This also loses genuinely unprocessed old messages; messages imported before this fix are unchanged ([#170068](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170068), [replay limits](/channels/telegram/transports)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [angeliti999](https://github.com/angeliti999) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169784).
  - Report fractional Telegram attachment limits accurately in warnings and model context ([#170677](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170677)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Wait for pending Telegram photo albums before ordinary follow-up text ([#170690](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170690)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Long streaming replies avoid repeated visibility-check work after a preview is visible. [#171417](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171417), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171416). Thanks to [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Telegram respects its IPv4-first preference on affected dual-stack networks and can retry timeouts proven to occur before a send. See [Telegram troubleshooting](/channels/telegram/troubleshooting). [#171564](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171564). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Dat1GUI](https://github.com/Dat1GUI) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171438).
  - A command sent to an archived Telegram direct-chat session now fails rather than retrying forever. Restore that session before starting new work. [#171810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171810). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@dankarization](https://github.com/dankarization) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171754).
  - Edited media captions render rich content and plain fallbacks correctly, while spoken-reply code keeps its spacing and checklist markers handle mixed-case input. [#171877](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171877). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Telegram polling on Bun can save its checkpoint and resume from it after a clean restart. [#178909](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178909). Thanks to [brianmcguire](https://github.com/brianmcguire), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [qwexs](https://github.com/qwexs) for the report.
  - Telegram accounts with mixed-case keys use their own group and topic mention settings and account-specific mention patterns. Status audits use the intended account groups too. [#180654](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180654). Thanks to [sc-10749](https://github.com/sc-10749) and [yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for the report.
  - Telegram sticker descriptions respect `tools.media.image.enabled` when image understanding is explicitly disabled, avoiding an extra image-model request. [#182006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182006). Thanks to [wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web), [Card4Philippe](https://github.com/Card4Philippe), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve Telegram questions and buttons during concurrent progress-draft cleanup ([#183028](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183028)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [sercada](https://github.com/sercada).
  - Resolve the implicit default Telegram bot's root secrets alongside named accounts ([#183618](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183618)). Thanks to [555aaditya](https://github.com/555aaditya), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [rodricksz4h5](https://github.com/rodricksz4h5).
  - Keep Telegram group membership audit deadlines stable across system-clock changes ([#185290](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185290)). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Eligible saved Telegram text answers can use the existing recovery path after a local send timeout; a lost acknowledgment can still result in a duplicate ([#185465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185465)). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Avoid a false failed status for delivered Telegram replies in ordinary group reply chains ([#185899](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185899)). Thanks to [moonbow0407](https://github.com/moonbow0407), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and reporter [alexph-dev](https://github.com/alexph-dev).
  - Keep a blank line between a Telegram bullet list and a following parenthesized numbered section ([commit bf381ef](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/bf381ef5f0c484d4a8603ff099142abc49723293)). Thanks to [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) and [gideonpraise](https://github.com/gideonpraise).

  </details>

- Telegram final answers now keep their link to the original question after progress updates, while queued button clicks retain the time they were received.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Use the receipt time for Telegram button interactions, including queued replays ([#170210](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170210)). Thanks to [maliv2](https://github.com/maliv2), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [zori7](https://github.com/zori7) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169881), and [Irish-Joseph](https://github.com/Irish-Joseph) for [related work](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170957).
  - In `first` or `batched` reply mode, only the first eligible message-tool send in a turn uses the implicit reply target. [#171123](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171123). Thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@SanderHelgesen](https://github.com/SanderHelgesen) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171102).
  - Final answers after progress cards retain their intended reply link in `first` and `batched` modes. [#171127](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171127). Thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@SanderHelgesen](https://github.com/SanderHelgesen) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171093).

  </details>

- Links, cards, lists and long code replies now display correctly in the affected channels. Channel text shown in terminal reports can no longer act as terminal control characters; JSON and stored messages keep their original content.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Eligible human DM senders can show their Telegram portraits in Home and chat after a background lookup. The first uncached message uses initials. [#166248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166248). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Voice captions preserve literal TTS directives inside code examples. [#171178](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171178). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Lists, checklists and quotes render readably in media captions while code examples stay literal. [#171180](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171180). Thanks to [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@SanderHelgesen](https://github.com/SanderHelgesen) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171106).
  - Bare links in rich replies remain tappable when link previews are disabled. URLs containing `&` still need a labeled Markdown link. [#171216](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171216). Thanks to [@Irish-Joseph](https://github.com/Irish-Joseph), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@SanderHelgesen](https://github.com/SanderHelgesen) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171111).
  - Show readable post cards using available anonymous preview metadata. [#168213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168213), [issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168212). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Streaming and final replies can remove repeated CRLF-separated paragraphs while preserving the separators and fenced code. [#183023](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183023). Thanks to [gdpu11](https://github.com/gdpu11) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Google Chat conversational replies render supported formatting, including during typing updates. [#141752](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141752), [#141625](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141625). Thanks to [@ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), coauthor and reporter [@pbreault](https://github.com/pbreault), and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - WhatsApp Web and Desktop stickers can reach the agent as image attachments again. [#164210](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164210), [#163559](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/163559). Thanks to [@andrewwbuilds](https://github.com/andrewwbuilds), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [@abacha](https://github.com/abacha).
  - Use Mattermost's supported button format in agent guidance. [#168368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168368), [issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168081). Thanks [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [@hannnnn-l](https://github.com/hannnnn-l).
  - Display Slack Markdown links beginning with `@`, `#` or `!` as labels instead of generating mention or channel markup. [#168438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168438), [issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168252). Thanks [@KhanCold](https://github.com/KhanCold), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Message command reports show channel-supplied text safely in a terminal, including send results, errors and reactions. JSON keeps the original data. [#176266](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176266). Thanks to [wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Human-readable channel capability probes and diagnostics neutralize terminal controls while JSON retains the original values. [#178234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178234). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Catalog-only channel labels in `channels list --all` neutralize terminal controls in human-readable output while JSON retains the original labels. [#178238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178238). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reply chunking no longer mistakes inline backticks for an unclosed code fence and formats the following prose normally. [#180588](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180588). Thanks to [AmelioMansour](https://github.com/AmelioMansour) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - Slack pin tables show the message text, author and timestamp, and message tables render remote values safely for terminals. [#171042](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171042), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171041). Thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Incoming Feishu rich posts preserve nested backticks within one code block. [#171222](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171222). Thanks to [@rudycelekli](https://github.com/rudycelekli) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Incoming Tlon messages preserve nested code fences for the assistant. [#171584](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171584). Thanks to [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559).
  - Slack messages and rich-text tables retain dates supplied without fallback text, using UTC for the timestamp. [#172182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172182). Thanks to [@fhgffy](https://github.com/fhgffy) for the fix and [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172180), [@skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Slack and Discord channel rules now stay with the channel they were set for, rather than applying to another channel or thread with the same name. Supported credentials and owner approvals also follow the intended account, while changed permissions apply to messages still waiting to start.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - An unverified group-DM sender cannot use privileged actions or replay approvals; ordinary allowed messages can still receive replies. [#165801](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165801). Thanks to [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - Keep channel-specific setup options available after empty or flag-like shared option values. [#167939](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167939). Thanks [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi).
  - Changes to send and receive permissions now apply to messages still waiting to start. [#168548](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168548). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show agents message actions relevant to their current channel and bindings, while keeping configured-channel discovery for trusted scheduled runs. [#168875](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168875), [related issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/14785). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@abacha](https://github.com/abacha) for the schema measurements and action mapping.
  - Preserve historical webhook callback settings when a trusted official plugin update is deferred, avoiding a migration blocker seen with Microsoft Teams. [#169035](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169035), [issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168872), [migration guide](/gateway/doctor/config-migrations#channel-webhook-listeners). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@sirius1024](https://github.com/sirius1024).
  - Protected storage for supported channel credentials, and removal of the unnecessary key from fresh managed llama.cpp setup ([#169732](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169732)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [dh-js](https://github.com/dh-js) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141812).
  - Preserve the originating X post and restrict work-session reply targets ([#169747](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169747)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep channel capability checks within their per-account timeout when the system clock moves backward ([#170500](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170500)). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Remove affected inbound message text from Tlon, LINE, Twitch, iMessage, IRC, WhatsApp, and Mattermost diagnostics, and give managed Signal accounts distinct Doctor-assigned ports ([#170815](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170815)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Still-allowed X mentions can proceed after an unrelated authorization change instead of triggering model fallback or a generic error reply. [#171384](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171384). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - ClickClack bound-message routing reuses published binding information while continuing to deny revoked or uncertain access. [#171458](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171458). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Diagnostics for the affected channels no longer include the identified private message text or pairing codes. [#171606](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171606). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Channel reloads skip unavailable credentials for disabled idle accounts. [#171734](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171734). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171367).
  - Slack tool and mention rules stay with the same channel after it is renamed. Direct name matching remains available through the explicit `dangerouslyAllowNameMatching` setting. [#171828](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171828). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Discord channel rules no longer apply to an unrelated channel or thread just because it has the same name. [#171829](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171829). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for the related Matrix report.
  - Discord owners configured as `discord:user:<id>` regain voice command access and eligibility for owner-derived execution approvals. Account-specific approvers take precedence. [#172385](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172385). Thanks to [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Slack owners configured as `slack:user:<id>` can be selected for automatic execution approvals when no explicit account approvers replace them. Workspace-qualified owner entries remain excluded from this approval path. [#172424](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172424). Thanks to [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Channel status and Doctor clear an obsolete restart-loop warning after recovery, even when an account remains stopped and needs attention. [#176182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176182). Thanks to [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin) and [iaford](https://github.com/iaford) for the report.
  - Channel discovery warns when it cannot read plugin installation records and suggests `openclaw doctor` to inspect plugin state. Installed channels may be missing from the resulting list. [#177252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177252). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - After a manual channel-account stop times out, an explicit start can recover the account once cleanup succeeds. Automatic restart remains suppressed until that start. [#177444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177444). Thanks to [YangManBOBO](https://github.com/YangManBOBO) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Named Google Chat and X accounts no longer deactivate the implicit default account's SecretRef credentials. [#180450](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180450). Thanks to [555aaditya](https://github.com/555aaditya).
  - Reject oversized bounded arrays and sets early in the embedded Discord Activities SDK parser ([#187297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187297)). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Correct IMAP sender parsing for nested-comment spoofing in sender-bound token admission ([#187301](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187301)). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Keep prepared session-to-session sends working across metadata-only updates while respecting changed communication permissions ([commit 4efde33](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4efde337b43de030fba6676fa3806db77e61349a)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Replies that finish while a channel is busy, reloading or recovering have more chances to reach the right conversation, and a delivery warning no longer replaces an answer already delivered or held for recovery. A send whose outcome is unknown is not blindly repeated.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Silent maintenance turns suppress attachments and other rich content as well as text, while preserving errors, actual voice messages and notices explicitly marked for delivery. [#129244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/129244). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Final answers retain text after internal tool blocks containing quoted closing markers. [#149775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149775). Thanks to [@bodapatisaikrishna](https://github.com/bodapatisaikrishna) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Replies that add a correction or other new text after a message-tool send are delivered, including their repeated opening. [#151921](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151921). Thanks to [@amittell](https://github.com/amittell) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Channel tools remain available to later messages after the agent or operator task that started the account ends. [#159396](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159396), [related #152965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/152965), [#158070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/158070) and [#161151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161151). Thanks to [@ceckert](https://github.com/ceckert), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@slinkoff](https://github.com/slinkoff) and [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Queued follow-ups and collected inputs use the current provider after a plugin reload, allowing the affected replies to resume. [#159715](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159715), [#158375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/158375). Thanks to [@mbelinky](https://github.com/mbelinky), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and reporters [@chenkai8654](https://github.com/chenkai8654), [@N4DH](https://github.com/N4DH) and [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
  - Eligible long replies using reply instructions avoid repeated parsing and can arrive faster. [#166782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166782), [#166781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166781). Thanks to [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve interrupted completion replies for recovery before later messages. [#167814](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167814), [issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167766), [restart recovery guide](/gateway/restart-recovery). Thanks [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@marknilsn](https://github.com/marknilsn).
  - Keep non-steering messages queued behind subagent or command announcements until cleanup finishes. [#168077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168077), [queue steering guide](/concepts/queue-steering). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep background task replies in their originating WebChat and preserve reply destinations when mirroring transcripts. Reef also stops treating a 40-character revision hash in a GitHub HTTPS commit, blob or tree link as a secret solely because of its entropy, while other checks still apply. [#168248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168248), [Reef guide](/channels/reef). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep recognized silent reasoning placeholders silent when their final token ends in punctuation. [#168571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168571), [related reports](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/165025) and [#138662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138662). Thanks [@liwenjie200543](https://github.com/liwenjie200543), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), reporters [@eonicman](https://github.com/eonicman) and [@cipp-ashe](https://github.com/cipp-ashe), and [@he-yufeng](https://github.com/he-yufeng) for the investigation.
  - Keep queue recovery from interrupting a message whose original sender is still active. [#169010](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169010). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Let ordinary non-steering queued messages without a source identity proceed when unnecessary recovery information cannot be read. [#169138](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169138). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Send an eligible replacement for an immediately failed reply before later replies in the same turn, including text replacing a failed voice note. Buffered delivery can place the replacement later ([#169305](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169305), [voice output guidance](/tools/tts/output)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include a redacted reply error in diagnostic events when diagnostics and debug or trace file logging are enabled ([#169503](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169503)). Debug logs can still contain private content. Thanks to [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc) and [keshavbotagent](https://github.com/keshavbotagent).
  - Record intentionally filtered ClickClack scaffold-only messages as suppressed instead of uncertain delivery ([#169509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169509)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve a completed answer after one invalidated session read ([#169987](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169987)). Thanks to [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Suppress token-only uppercase fragments of the silent reply marker while preserving attachments and configured heartbeat acknowledgements ([#170190](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170190)). Thanks to [maliv2](https://github.com/maliv2), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [rboy1](https://github.com/rboy1) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170158).
  - Keep a successful channel send marked delivered if a later delivery observer fails ([#170482](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170482)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep routine channel bookkeeping from making old conversations appear newly active ([#170743](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170743)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Wait for a pending current reply before sending replacement answers, media, or failure messages ([#170937](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170937)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Deliver a failure notice for eligible requester continuations refused at initial plugin-runtime admission during reload ([#171021](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171021)). Thanks to [johan-eilertsen](https://github.com/johan-eilertsen) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reply preparation no longer waits for unrelated session retention cleanup. [#171136](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171136). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Eligible Web Push notifications can send after pending session updates settle, and delivery failures have more useful logs. Failed sends are not automatically retried. [#171145](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171145). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Canceling an input during preparation preserves later queued work. [#171718](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171718). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Queued inbound messages can retry when preparation fails before durable acceptance, with later preparation failures handled without replay. [#172080](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172080), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171837). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@bitfroest](https://github.com/bitfroest) and [@nissl24](https://github.com/nissl24).
  - Stalled channel identity writes time out and retry safely instead of waiting indefinitely. If retries are exhausted, the error points to `openclaw gateway status --deep`. [#172244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172244). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@desksk](https://github.com/desksk) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172091).
  - Streaming ordinary technical prose that mentions tools or functions avoids repeated full-reply trace-filter scans. [#172631](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172631). Thanks to [JasonBuildAI](https://github.com/JasonBuildAI) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An answer already delivered or awaiting delivery recovery no longer triggers a false missing-answer notice. [#173798](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173798). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Agent webhooks waiting for execution capacity can return an overload response instead of premature success. Session conflicts can also return their proper response. [#175111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175111). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - A rejected background typing callback is reported without stopping later keepalive ticks or causing an unhandled rejection. [#175381](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175381). Thanks to [musatoktas](https://github.com/musatoktas), [Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot) for the report, and [cxbAsDev](https://github.com/cxbAsDev) for the earlier proposal.
  - When recovery checks an interrupted send with an unknown outcome, matching permanent errors settle the delivery as failed while transient errors remain retryable. The uncertain message is not blindly resent. [#180767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180767). Thanks to [wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web) and [Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot) for the report.
