  - The optional extendable reply wait keeps its intended time budget when the system clock changes. [#182057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182057). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let an admitted reply settle before a queued command takes over, preserving reply accounting and command order ([commit 3678292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3678292a09f253000fd9b475b262106fbe5fde1c)).
  - Keep streamed completion separate from pending source delivery so final replies and attachments are handled correctly, including during timeout recovery ([commit b52afef](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b52afef09152302461eea247e47a385cfe419366)).
  - WhatsApp retries reuse the same message ID for an uncertain send, reducing the chance of duplicate replies. [#142434](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142434), [#142398](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/142398), [related acknowledgement issue #122758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/122758). Thanks to [@ashawwal](https://github.com/ashawwal), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@he-yufeng](https://github.com/he-yufeng).
  - Synology Chat paces each webhook independently while preserving order and spacing for messages to the same webhook. [#144991](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144991), [#127491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127491). Thanks to [@Abhishek-B-R](https://github.com/Abhishek-B-R) and reporter [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Feishu sends a complete static reply when a streaming preview was accepted but the final update was rejected. The earlier preview remains visible. [#152086](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152086), [#139443](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139443). Thanks to [@kqtxmaster-boop](https://github.com/kqtxmaster-boop), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@ArthurNie](https://github.com/ArthurNie), [@Simon2256928](https://github.com/Simon2256928) and [@ruirui666888](https://github.com/ruirui666888).
  - A WhatsApp message that decrypts after an empty placeholder can still reach the agent, while duplicates stay suppressed. This cannot recover a message already marked complete. [#162078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162078), [#161829](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161829). Thanks to [@stanleys12](https://github.com/stanleys12), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), reporter [@hundredhandsio](https://github.com/hundredhandsio), [@jmejia-appropia](https://github.com/jmejia-appropia) and [@gabrielrojasc](https://github.com/gabrielrojasc).
  - Keep Google Chat's missing-typing-message fallback working when a 404 response's error body cannot be read. [#168107](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168107). Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and reporter [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Ignore IRC CTCP queries before pairing or agent dispatch, while allowing normal messages and actions through. [#168113](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168113). Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for landing the fix.
  - Reef state operations run off the Gateway thread with less repeated database work. [#168340](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168340). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Accepted channel-bound ACP turns can deliver replies after five minutes without replaying their input. [#170880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170880), reported in [#170827](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170827). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@fanmeng-felix](https://github.com/fanmeng-felix).
  - Long channel `/compact` commands can finish within their configured timeout without being replayed by the ingress watchdog. [#171011](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171011), reported in [#170982](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170982), with a related proposal in [#171027](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171027). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@bluet](https://github.com/bluet), and [@PratikRai0101](https://github.com/PratikRai0101).
  - Stopping an ACP turn no longer suppresses a separate queued follow-up. Cancelling the follow-up itself still stops its output. [#170883](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170883), related to [#170833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170833). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@fanmeng-felix](https://github.com/fanmeng-felix).
  - A steered follow-up keeps its own failure reply and attachments after an earlier message-tool reply. [#170916](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170916). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Tool progress now finishes its update before the answer starts streaming, keeping long step titles readable within the configured display limit.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Fit long tool titles within the configured progress line limit while retaining useful path endings and status. [#168312](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168312). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Send verbose progress once and settle it before streaming answer previews. [#168345](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168345). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- A2A replies and retries now stay with the task that started them; unsupported task references fail clearly instead of starting a different task. Discord and WhatsApp command actions also reject unsupported input before doing work.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Discord normalizes plugin command names and skips names over 32 characters with guidance for a shorter alias. Slack spelling is preserved. [#126643](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/126643), [#126633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/126633). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - WhatsApp login validates its `start` and `wait` actions before invoking either operation. [#158719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158719). Thanks to [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [@mcaxtr](https://github.com/mcaxtr).
  - A2A push configuration requests return the protocol's push-not-supported error. [#167594](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167594), [#167591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167591). Thanks to [@clawSean](https://github.com/clawSean) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reject A2A task references explicitly instead of starting unrelated work. To start a new task in the same conversation, omit `taskId` and reuse `contextId`. [#167596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167596), [issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167592). Thanks [@clawSean](https://github.com/clawSean) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep mention-like text literal in Slack compact progress posts and edits ([#169760](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169760)). Thanks to [KhanCold](https://github.com/KhanCold).
  - Retry Discord message edits, deletes, pins, and unpins after transient failures, including reconciliation of an ambiguous delete ([#169806](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169806)). Thanks to [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), and to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for confirming the merge.
  - Deliver Tlon events using CRLF or CR line endings or multiple data lines ([#170125](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170125)). Thanks to [ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559).
  - Keep A2A retries with a retained message ID on their original task and match replies to the tasks that started them ([#170469](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170469)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [rh7](https://github.com/rh7) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169916).
  - Keep the Gateway running when a Buzz relay disconnects during authentication ([#170591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170591)). Thanks to [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [jarvis-modern-mail](https://github.com/jarvis-modern-mail) for the [report and investigation](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/147269).

  - A2A filters host notices from peer replies and reports failed runs on their originating tasks. [#172064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172064). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171985).

  </details>

- A Feishu settings reload no longer lets an old connection shut down the replacement, while stopping Tlon or Zalo prevents pending connection work from reopening it.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Retire unused Telegram token throttlers after token rotation or account removal ([#169536](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169536)). Thanks to [xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee).
  - Keep Feishu replies working when a settings reload overlaps an older monitor's cleanup. [#169226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169226), [issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169190), [independent investigation](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170054). Thanks [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), reporter [@junniar](https://github.com/junniar) and [@PratikRai0101](https://github.com/PratikRai0101) for the independent investigation.
  - Keep a stopped Tlon account disconnected when in-flight reconnect authentication finishes ([#169267](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169267)). Thanks to [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Cancel an outstanding Zalo poll when its account stops or restarts ([#169270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169270)). Thanks to [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).

  </details>

- IRC detects more silent connection failures, reconnects, and shows the current error during recovery. Messages missed while disconnected are not replayed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - IRC status shows disconnection and reconnect errors during recovery, then clears the error after reconnection. [#171053](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171053), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/101083). Thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii) and [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino).
  - IRC detects a silent connection failure and starts the existing reconnect and channel rejoin flow after roughly four minutes. Messages missed while disconnected are not replayed. [#172961](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172961). Thanks to [rodricksz4h5](https://github.com/rodricksz4h5) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- [Discord voice consultations](/channels/discord/voice-channels) continue after a voice change during a call. Update and restart OpenClaw to apply that fix. If a call ends while an answer is still being prepared, the answer can continue in the same channel's text chat unless you cancelled it.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep Discord agent consultations working after an in-call voice change. Update and restart the Gateway to apply the fix ([#171029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171029), [voice selection guidance](/nodes/talk/realtime-sessions#choose-a-talk-voice-from-chat)). Thanks to [ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [brainmonger](https://github.com/brainmonger) for the [report and investigation](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171013).
  - If a Discord voice call ends before its answer is ready, that answer can continue in the same channel’s text chat. Explicitly canceled replies send nothing. [#172380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172380). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@brainmonger](https://github.com/brainmonger) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172233).

  </details>

- An unreadable email no longer blocks later valid IMAP automation messages, and stalled sign-in or send attempts in the affected channels now report or recover from their specific failure. The skipped email remains in the mailbox and is not processed automatically.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - IMAP skips an unreadable email so later valid messages can reach automation. The original remains in the mailbox, and the skipped message is not processed or resubmitted through channel dead letters. [#173231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173231). Thanks to [pgondhi987](https://github.com/pgondhi987).
  - Slack relay recovery waits for received-event writes to finish after a socket error. [#173407](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173407). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mattermost slash commands and model-picker interactions explain when a full pairing queue prevented a request from being saved. The bot owner can approve or dismiss pending requests in Settings → Channels → DM access requests before the user retries. [#178581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178581). Thanks to [HeZ2z](https://github.com/HeZ2z), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for the report.
  - iMessage bridge recovery recognizes structured send deadlines so later replies can recover. Failed sends are not automatically replayed, and recovery can also be triggered while a send's delivery is unsettled. [#180900](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180900). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Slack Socket Mode retries an isolated `invalid_auth` and can reconnect without a Gateway restart. Three such failures without a successful connection still block the channel, and definitive credential failures block immediately. [#181056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181056). Thanks to [mushuiyu886](https://github.com/mushuiyu886) and [marns](https://github.com/marns) for the report.
  - A2A outbound sends with a missing peer URL or unresolved token are treated as permanent pre-dispatch failures, avoiding futile queue retries. Correct the configuration and reload when credentials cannot be resolved. [#181326](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181326). Thanks to [potoior](https://github.com/potoior) and [exGeni](https://github.com/exGeni).
  - SMS rejects unsupported prototype-named MMS content types before sending them to Twilio. Already-staged media with those types returns HTTP 415. [#181773](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181773). Thanks to [Jimmy-xuzimo](https://github.com/Jimmy-xuzimo), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) for the report.
  - Feishu and Lark QR registration keeps its intended polling window when the system clock changes during setup. [#182180](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182180). Thanks to [mikasa0818](https://github.com/mikasa0818) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Overlapping WhatsApp plugin instances keep separate quoted-message caches, so a replacement cannot read or clear the retiring instance's quotes. [#182550](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182550). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- Fixes long Discord replies breaking at line endings, Mattermost final answers being hidden behind stale previews, and LINE loading animations ignoring reply settings. Signal disconnects now show a useful reason for recovery.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Signal reports an unexpected event-stream closure as the reason for recovery, helping operators check the daemon or proxy. [#182746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182746). Thanks to [wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [888achtachtacht-commits](https://github.com/888achtachtacht-commits) for the report.
  - Recognize an iMessage self-chat reply as an echo when it returns under the chat ID. A same-text message can still be suppressed while a pending echo marker remains ([#183383](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183383)). Thanks to [sukhdeepjohar](https://github.com/sukhdeepjohar), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [kunalchaudhary82](https://github.com/kunalchaudhary82).
  - Keep WhatsApp QR login waits stable across system-clock changes ([#183394](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183394)). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Attempt to close the Discord connection when startup fails after an overlapping restart ([#184033](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184033)). Thanks to [MarMarLabs](https://github.com/MarMarLabs) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fix CRLF boundaries in long Discord replies while preserving line endings when inline code crosses a boundary ([#184435](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184435)). Thanks to [xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Follow reply policy and typing settings for LINE direct-chat loading animations ([#185306](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185306)). Thanks to [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Send a fresh Mattermost final post when newer relevant activity has overtaken its preview ([#185422](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185422)). Thanks to [hannnnn-l](https://github.com/hannnnn-l) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Avoid bursts of Mattermost preview edits after a slow send ([#185483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185483)). Thanks to [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Settle cancellation cleanly when an X account stops or reloads during subscription setup ([#186226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186226)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The merge includes Discord message-action retries and model status identifying the credential source selected for the current route ([commit 8548651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8548651ff64ef4086abc492ea357b53b0b9bb913)).
  - The merge includes the fix that stops pending Tlon reconnect work from reopening streams after shutdown ([commit 9a41e1b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9a41e1b76ec6f5903101d2570b38a8f456ece605)).

  </details>

- Retrying a meeting transcript from Summary or Transcript now restores all available pages. Capture settings return you to the selected meeting and filters, and periodic captions can continue after the joining command disconnects.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">

  - Failed automatic captures clean up unchanged empty candidates. The same change retains pending provider model choices and corrects authentication selection for explicit Gateway connections. [#137092](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137092). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Teams and Zoom meeting commands validate explicitly empty `--mode` and `--transport` values before connecting. [#167871](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167871). Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Restore every transcript page after an authorized retry from Summary or Transcript. [#169000](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169000), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Return to the selected meeting and library filters after opening capture settings. [#169279](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169279), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

Thanks [@sukhdeepjohar](https://github.com/sukhdeepjohar) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@sercada](https://github.com/sercada) for the related report.

### Memory and search

- Starting OpenClaw no longer opens every agent's memory index at once. Each index opens when needed, while plugin reloads resume indexing that was already active.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Restores on-demand memory-index startup. [#174718](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174718).

  </details>

- [Memory search](/concepts/memory-search#how-search-works) can find notes that a natural-language question previously missed, keep relevant older dated notes eligible and rank weak keyword matches below closer matches in meaning. These are improvements to existing search, not a guarantee that every exact match comes first. If you set a minimum search score yourself, lower it if useful results disappear after the ranking change.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Natural-language questions can recover notes through a fallback when strict body and filename searches find nothing, and hybrid search can score keyword matches outside the initial vector window. [#161165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161165), with thanks to [wangjx-xydt](https://github.com/wangjx-xydt), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini) and [AXEG0](https://github.com/AXEG0). See [#160839](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160839) and [#162764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162764).
  - Relevant older dated notes can appear in search again, though newer notes may still rank ahead. [#168692](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168692), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [DrDanielSingleton](https://github.com/DrDanielSingleton), [S1gv4rd](https://github.com/S1gv4rd), [davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini), [rkcollins](https://github.com/rkcollins) and [h6y3](https://github.com/h6y3). See [#157392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157392).
  - Keyword relevance remains distinct when dated notes decay, with changed hybrid scores for tuned thresholds. [#167004](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167004), with thanks to [MonkeyLeeT](https://github.com/MonkeyLeeT), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [AXEG0](https://github.com/AXEG0) and [n0mad-ai](https://github.com/n0mad-ai). See [#162764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162764).
  - Eligible standalone headings stay with following content in built-in memory search. [#168867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168867), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [TomSchuman](https://github.com/TomSchuman) and [maximilliangrand](https://github.com/maximilliangrand). See [#149758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/149758).
  - Agents can answer from sufficient supplied context and retrieve memory when facts are missing. [#121115](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/121115), with thanks to [arhxam](https://github.com/arhxam), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [shannon0430](https://github.com/shannon0430) and [petergaultney](https://github.com/petergaultney). See [#120968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/120968).
  - In eligible private chats, French questions about past discussions can trigger Active Memory escalation when the initial lookup is weak. [#171370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171370), thanks to [RaphazZze](https://github.com/RaphazZze). See the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171369).
  - Hybrid memory search gives weak keyword overlap less influence than a closer match in meaning. Tuned minimum scores may hide lower-scoring results, and rare identifiers or filename matches may move in the results ([#177450](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177450)). The related [exact-recall report #162764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162764) remains open. Thanks [@serg0x](https://github.com/serg0x), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [@axeg0](https://github.com/axeg0).

  </details>

- Memory search now explains more embedding-provider failures and keeps a [previously usable index](/concepts/memory-builtin#safe-index-recovery) available when rebuilding fails. After a database worker stops, a later request can reopen the existing index, although searches during recovery can still fail.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Software vector scoring can use less CPU and temporary memory on large indexes when sqlite-vec acceleration is unavailable. [#164773](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164773), with thanks to [etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). See [#114913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/114913) and [#128140](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128140).
  - Memory status and Doctor explain when a requested sessions source is excluded because transcript indexing is not enabled. [#160320](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160320), with thanks to [Olli0103](https://github.com/Olli0103), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [benjaml4](https://github.com/benjaml4). See [#159872](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159872).
  - When an index records vector dimensions, mismatched query embeddings produce an error with an agent-specific force-rebuild command. [#168465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168465), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Enabled loop detection recognizes repeated memory searches with unchanged ordered hits. [#169088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169088), thanks to [ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [PatrickTrent](https://github.com/PatrickTrent).
  - Reduced unnecessary session metadata reads during memory-search visibility checks ([#181247](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181247), [issue #181092](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181092)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini).
  - Kept Active Memory timeout streaks through cooldown expiry so another timeout renews the pause ([#181297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181297), related [issue #180978](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180978)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@nikolay-bratanov](https://github.com/nikolay-bratanov).
  - Applied the existing LanceDB recall deadline across cold provider initialization, embedding, and search for provider adapters ([#182340](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182340)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Kept LanceDB recall deadlines stable when the system clock changes ([#184711](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184711)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Applied the same elapsed-time protection to cold-start embedding deadlines ([#186037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186037)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Added a Gateway warning when an all-zero query embedding skips vector recall, with guidance to check the embedding service and retry ([#183475](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183475), related [issue #183425](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/183425)). Thanks [@lonexreb](https://github.com/lonexreb), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [@feiniaoivp](https://github.com/feiniaoivp).
  - Embedding deadlines use elapsed time, preventing system clock changes from causing unnecessary retries or accepting late results. [#169360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169360), thanks to [Bartok9](https://github.com/Bartok9) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - OpenAI OAuth embeddings pick up renewed credentials for direct and batch requests. An eligible same-origin expired-token response gets one retry. [#172237](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172237), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [digitalfrost84](https://github.com/digitalfrost84).
  - Failed full reindexes retry with bounded backoff while a valid published index remains available for searches. [#138786](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138786), with thanks to [grynn](https://github.com/grynn) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). See [#138775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138775).
  - Embedding requests split at declared provider input limits before sending. Custom compatible providers must declare their limit to use this path. [#141686](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141686), with thanks to [vildevev](https://github.com/vildevev) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). See [#139040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139040).
  - After a recognized limit rejection, embedding retries use a valid reported item limit to avoid unnecessary rejected batches. [#140508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/140508), with thanks to [saladinyao-cyber](https://github.com/saladinyao-cyber) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Managed llama.cpp memory indexing can use its local service when a retained API-key reference is unavailable. Other local providers still require their configured credentials. [#158108](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158108), with thanks to [ysumatta](https://github.com/ysumatta) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Invalid or empty embedding results now show an error rather than silently dropping search matches. A failed rebuild keeps the previous usable index. [#168263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168263), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - After a memory database worker stops, a later request can reopen the existing index. Searches during recovery may fail, and uncertain writes are not repeated automatically. [#171494](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171494), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Memory status and Doctor resolve a selected third-party plugin's saved credentials and report the provider unavailable if they cannot be resolved. [#172135](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172135), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [PaulClawitzki](https://github.com/PaulClawitzki).
  - Doctor explains that indexing may stop when neither primary nor fallback embeddings work, and gives agent-specific credential or keyword-only recovery guidance. [#172223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172223), thanks to [serg0x](https://github.com/serg0x) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Memory reads through the CLI and supported plugins now reach the intended agent’s permitted notes more consistently, while private or untrusted content remains subject to its existing access checks.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Trusted workspace profiles and memory reach the model when a third-party memory plugin lacks a provenance classifier. Untrusted or invalidly identified content remains excluded. [#171241](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171241), thanks to [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Sessions opened at an explicit memory path can append and reopen messages with an ordinary canonical session key. [#171284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171284), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restored memory authority for eligible owner sessions and cross-agent subagents, and enforced role and admin checks on private memory reads ([#181359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181359)). Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
  - LanceDB resolves its default store from the active state directory, and Doctor checks that store for its legacy-memory migration. [#168944](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168944), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Memory search and standard session-backfill preview, apply and rollback route through the running local Gateway, as do Matrix verification status, bootstrap and device commands. Other operations require exclusive offline ownership. [#168471](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168471), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Memory Core writes from other processes require the Gateway or exclusive offline access. Workboard reads overlapping a change can briefly show an older snapshot until the next request. [#168841](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168841), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Refused a deleted or replaced source database before disclosing or writing backfilled memories ([commit 3d9595f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3d9595fe12769ce63cb54992911728b95442e243)).

  </details>

- During memory setup, you can optionally test an [embedding provider and model](/concepts/memory-search#supported-providers) before saving them. Skipping or failing the check leaves existing settings intact, and interrupted import setup can return to its result and Continue action.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Guided and classic local onboarding and memory configuration offer the optional provider and model check. Skipping, a failed check or declining to save leaves existing settings in place. [#163693](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163693), with thanks to [PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [actwebmaster](https://github.com/actwebmaster). See [#16670](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/16670).
  - A completed memory import can show its result and Continue action after the onboarding view is reactivated. [#168791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168791), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Selected Memory Core refreshes its cached managers after provider settings change, so corrected embedding settings can take effect without restarting the Gateway. [#151263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151263), with thanks to [ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [Mirksen](https://github.com/Mirksen). See [#151055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/151055).
  - Non-interactive embedding-only onboarding accepts `openclaw onboard --non-interactive --accept-risk --auth-choice llama-cpp --llama-cpp-embedding-only`. Enabled local-memory agents must agree on the embedding model. [#170153](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170153), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [dh-js](https://github.com/dh-js) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141793).
  - Provider-reported prompt and total token counts reach `/v1/embeddings` responses. Unavailable, invalid or incomplete usage returns zero rather than an estimate. [#154721](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154721), with thanks to [xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Configured model IDs and authorized overrides can contain slashes when the prefix is not a registered provider. [#161170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161170), with thanks to [xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Memory embedding requests now respect the chosen provider’s private-network rules, including when a service redirects to a private address. If your embedding service depends on that route, you may need to allow it explicitly; invalid Google or LM Studio headers identify the affected setting.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Provider private-network denial now applies to memory embeddings, including remote overrides, and redirects to another private origin require permission. [#169847](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169847), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [dmorn](https://github.com/dmorn) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/94166), and [mushuiyu886](https://github.com/mushuiyu886) for [earlier policy work](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/94344).
  - Gemini embedding and batch requests and LM Studio discovery, preparation and embedding requests honor explicit private-network denial. Private DNS opt-in belongs on the embedding provider's ID. [#170173](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170173), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx).
  - Invalid or unresolved Google and LM Studio embedding headers fail with the affected setting identified. [#169361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169361), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128536).

  </details>

- [Memory Wiki](/plugins/memory-wiki#search-and-retrieval) now finds matching filenames without scanning every page in those searches, matches equivalent Unicode spellings and recognizes more local Markdown links in lint and backlinks.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Compile and lint avoid a second vault compile after changes, and compile returns the actual updated-index count. [#169028](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169028), thanks to [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin) and [etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
  - Large vault status counts avoid the reported timeout. [#170553](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170553), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [migelizaga-rgb](https://github.com/migelizaga-rgb) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170466).
  - Identity validation uses less temporary memory for vaults with long compile histories. [#171845](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171845), thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Filename and extensionless-path lookups read matching candidates instead of every page. Page ID lookups and misses still scan the vault. [#171961](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171961), thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
  - Fixed wiki lint and backlinks for inline Markdown links with titles, parentheses, or angle-bracket destinations ([#181380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181380), [issue #181379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181379)). Thanks [@loading2332](https://github.com/loading2332).
  - Resolved percent-encoded local Markdown paths for lint and backlinks ([#181794](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181794)). Thanks [@L4XB](https://github.com/L4XB) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Matched equivalent composed and decomposed Unicode text in local Wiki search ([#184353](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184353)). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Memory Wiki keeps Notes inside its marked human section when regenerating concept pages, and memory maintenance leaves unreadable source files and [Dream Diary](/concepts/dreaming#dream-diary) entries untouched. If a file is damaged, preserve the original and repair a copy before retrying. Handwritten text outside the marked Notes section on older generated pages is not protected.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Handwritten Notes inside the marked section now survive regeneration of Memory Wiki concept pages ([#175707](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175707), [issue #175700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/175700)). Thanks [@YangManBOBO](https://github.com/YangManBOBO), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Kept stale OKF concept pages containing handwritten Notes when a concept is removed from the bundle ([#179657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179657)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retained malformed source pages and their synchronization state during bridge and unsafe-local pruning ([#174573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174573)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserved existing OKF concepts with unreadable source text and stopped imports with an unreadable root index before concept writes ([#176009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176009)). Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007).
  - Stopped memory maintenance from rewriting files with malformed UTF-8 ([#179016](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179016)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor now stops before merging malformed root memory files and explains how to preserve the originals and retry with a converted copy. If the legacy file was already archived, the warning identifies its location. [#169029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169029), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and co-author [wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), who reported [#168567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168567).
  - Doctor preserves local or custom Ollama embedding routes when migrating hosted Ollama chat settings. [#169661](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169661), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- ChatGPT imports now keep readable prose and pasted text from supported transcripts; Hermes memory imports reject malformed text instead of changing the source or destination. Replace unexpected special files with regular readable files, and convert a copy of malformed text before retrying.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Rejected special files in ChatGPT wiki import and preview. Replace the affected path with a regular file and retry. Symlinks to regular files remain supported ([#181755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181755)). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserved prose, pasted JSON, and embedded quotes in newly imported active-branch ChatGPT transcripts. Explicitly typed image parts remain omitted ([#182049](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182049)). Thanks [@L4XB](https://github.com/L4XB) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Hermes append-style `MEMORY.md` and `USER.md` imports reject malformed UTF-8 without changing the failed item's source or destination. Re-encode the source before retrying. [#172742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172742), thanks to [Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reported directory inspection and skill listing errors during preview. Resolve the access or storage problem and rerun preview before applying ([#175452](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175452)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoided blocking on pipes and other special files during migration. Replace an unexpected special file with an ordinary readable UTF-8 file and retry ([#180908](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180908)). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Memory can save a checkpoint before conversation compaction even when a provider omits token-use data. File-based checkpoints also reject exact copies of already saved blocks and can retry with new text.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - A transcript estimate can trigger a pre-compaction memory checkpoint when provider usage is missing or zero, without presenting the estimate as measured session usage. [#83178](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/83178), with thanks to [njuboy11](https://github.com/njuboy11) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). See [#83177](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/83177).
  - File-based pre-compaction flushes reject copied saved blocks and can retry with new text. Existing duplicates remain, and paraphrased repetition is not detected. [#168123](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168123), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [bit-777](https://github.com/bit-777) and [touraround](https://github.com/touraround). See [#164650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164650).

  </details>

- Paused or failure-disabled [Dreaming jobs](/concepts/dreaming#scheduling) now stay stopped during routine recovery, and an empty diary sweep writes no entry. To resume a stopped job, run `openclaw cron enable <job-id>`.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - A useful Dream Diary sweep writes at most one combined reflection per workspace, and an empty sweep writes none. [#167887](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167887), with thanks to [diregoblin](https://github.com/diregoblin) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A fresh Gateway no longer reports a false dreaming cleanup failure when an agent database is first created. [#168175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168175), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Memory sweeps no longer cause unnecessary recovery checks on Gateway restart. [#168631](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168631), with thanks to [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman). See [#168620](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168620).
  - Disabling or replacing Memory Core no longer waits for a timeout solely because a startup scan is running. [#168229](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168229), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Paused or failure-disabled Dreaming jobs keep their stopped state and failure history. Resume one with `openclaw cron enable <job-id>` ([#180694](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180694), [issue #180655](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180655)). Thanks [@Yun-0000](https://github.com/Yun-0000), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [@lisandromachado](https://github.com/lisandromachado).
  - Recorded Gateway-routed CLI memory search recalls in the requested agent's Dreaming workspace ([commit 8fc0987](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8fc0987fb086d68df8c345b50501c6b08e4f6e8d)).
  - Preserved malformed dream diaries during updates and omitted them from optional history ([#179022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179022)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Documented recovery for malformed dream diaries, including preserving the original and converting a copy before retrying ([commit 43c5a84](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/43c5a84ecaf9ce16a191f5498ed2550aca40da83)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Memory plugin reload can finish while slow cleanup of old interrupted dreaming sessions continues in the background. [#169945](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169945), thanks to [Stronggenetics](https://github.com/Stronggenetics) and [hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph) for the [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/152275).

  </details>

### Workspaces and paired computers

- Fixes managed-worktree cleanup stopping at read-only dependency folders or abandoning other worktrees when one cannot be removed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Repair eligible read-only directories during orphan cleanup and continue cleaning other worktrees when one fails. After fixing access, retry deferred cleanup with `openclaw worktrees gc --retry-deferred`. [#171305](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171305) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- A conversation on a [paired computer](/nodes/pairing-and-status) can continue once after a brief disconnect if its saved conversation and workspace recover. If a command might have run before the connection dropped, check what it did before trying it again. OpenClaw will not automatically repeat it.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Continue an eligible chat once after the paired computer reconnects and its saved conversation and workspace recover. Direct agent RPC turns do not receive this continuation. [#171867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171867) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Explain a paired-computer disconnect and remind you to check whether an interrupted command had an effect before retrying. [#171695](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171695) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep attempts to reconnect a paired workspace from waiting longer after the system clock changes ([#171977](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171977)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep remote workspace operations from timing out too early or waiting too long when the system clock changes ([#172256](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172256)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Keep remote attachment transfers from waiting longer after the system clock moves backward ([#173142](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173142)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep remote command progress updates on schedule if the computer's clock changes ([#182481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182481)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Keep a worker waiting for a free slot from waiting beyond its normal limit after clock changes ([#184042](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184042)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Wait until a native worker is attached before starting the first conversation turn ([#177184](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177184)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Run remote commands under the right service identity instead of a misleading inherited one ([#173304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173304), [#171542](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171542)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini).
  - Show the cause of a paired-computer worker failure in local warnings without exposing secrets ([#174750](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174750), [#173351](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/173351)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@unknown7751](https://github.com/unknown7751) and [@Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot).

  </details>

- Commands and events for one paired computer keep working while another device's pairing changes. If the computer itself loses its connection or permission, OpenClaw reports the actual problem instead of suggesting that every host is full.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Report a computer's current pairing or connection failure when a session cannot start there, instead of a misleading capacity error ([#168531](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168531)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Free a paired computer's worker slot after a forced stop by clearing processes that belong to the stopped worker ([#168746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168746), [report #160643](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160643)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), reporter [aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80), and [linhongyu510](https://github.com/linhongyu510) for the cleanup investigation.
  - Let paired-computer workspace maintenance finish even when its preparation outlasts the scheduler's initial wait ([#168780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168780)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep an already approved remote command attached to the right approval, with device policy checks intact ([#148612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148612)). Thanks to [beenhero](https://github.com/beenhero) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Warn when a remote node background or yield request runs synchronously ([#156553](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156553), [report #156524](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156524)). Thanks to [NianJiuZst](https://github.com/NianJiuZst), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and reporter [service-cyber](https://github.com/service-cyber).
  - Run a remote command after its valid approval instead of rejecting it because an internal plan reference was lost ([#161980](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161980), [report #151622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/151622), [related issue #142492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/142492)). Thanks to [Program-123](https://github.com/Program-123), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), reporters [gaojian125](https://github.com/gaojian125) and [enderwillsaveyou](https://github.com/enderwillsaveyou), and [rozenpl](https://github.com/rozenpl) and [daddydubzzz](https://github.com/daddydubzzz) for reproductions.
  - Avoid announcing a remote command as finished twice after its result has already appeared ([#164511](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164511), [related issue #154474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/154474)). Thanks to [holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and reporter [xiazixin](https://github.com/xiazixin).
  - Run authorized POSIX paired-node commands that end in whitespace ([#168120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168120)). Thanks to [yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Keep commands and events working for a paired computer when another device's pairing information changes. [#169486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169486) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Related to [#166652](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166652).
  - Keep a paired computer's commands and events flowing during another device's pairing update. A change to that computer's own permission still restricts its access. [#169575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169575) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [@gisk0](https://github.com/gisk0) for the diagnosis and alternative proposal.
  - Deliver delegated `sessions_send` replies and workspace conflict reports in the intended worker conversation instead of returning a false ownership error. [#168946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168946), addressing [#159801](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159801), by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Tell agents that paired-node directory browsing lists folders only, and suggest a file listing through node-host execution when permitted. [#169224](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169224) by [@PratikRai0101](https://github.com/PratikRai0101), with thanks to [@keiseisales](https://github.com/keiseisales) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169192).
  - Let a cancelled remote command settle even when its progress acknowledgment is late, so the computer can update once idle. [#169273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169273) by [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).
  - Restart or stop the Gateway despite a disconnected cloud worker, with a warning that its remote machine still needs cleanup later. [#168907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168907) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Refresh the paired-computer list with less work when many devices are connected. [#171876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171876) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Apply approved pairing-permission changes to the connected computer they belong to. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/94f180d9ab19d969d59298312cff8ed019e18b58)

  </details>

- You can [move an eligible conversation](/gateway/cloud-workers/session-lifecycle#move-a-session) from its session menu to a local or remote computer, or retry a failed move to the same destination. Moving an active remote conversation interrupts its current turn without replaying it. Leaving an offline device requires confirmation.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Move an eligible conversation from its session menu between local and remote computers, or retry a move that failed during shutdown without selecting a different destination. The existing placement chip remains available. [#171690](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171690) · [Session lifecycle](/gateway/cloud-workers/session-lifecycle) · Contribution from [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Missing required worker profiles and recoverable drafts ([#158903](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158903)). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [sallyom](https://github.com/sallyom), and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep chosen cloud profiles selected and use smaller, comparable defaults when no machine size is configured ([#156786](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156786)). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Keep the current turn running while another worker starts independently ([#155464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155464), [report #155333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155333)). Thanks to [bulingbuling688](https://github.com/bulingbuling688), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [rvelasquez](https://github.com/rvelasquez).
  - Clarify worker idle retention, optional worker-local inference, placement, and recovery guidance ([#168260](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168260), [issue #168255](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168255)). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Find repository environment settings and actions together, with shared controls under Advanced. [#170892](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170892) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - See whether a cloud computer is loading an image or preparing its environment, and why it needed a cold start. [#170895](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170895) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Cloud conversations can reuse [compatible prepared environments](/gateway/cloud-workers/warm-images#later-sessions-and-refresh) in more cases, including some starts after runtime updates. If a setup secret changes but its name and store reference stay the same, refresh the prepared environment before relying on results from that setup.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Crabbox 0.73.0 minimum and managed acquisition ([#168188](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168188)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse prepared images for eligible empty cloud workspaces, and wait until the next refresh before retrying a capture the provider cannot support ([#168371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168371)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Share overlapping cloud machine and operating-system metadata requests while allowing later refreshes ([#156605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156605)). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Optionally save a reusable image while reclaiming a cloud session ([#168454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168454)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep Crabbox command retry and cleanup time budgets consistent when the system clock changes ([#165889](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165889)). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - A compatible prepared image skips repeated profile setup ([#168892](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168892)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Start from a prepared image without reinstalling the runtime already stored in it. [#168974](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168974) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prefetch runtime files at boot for eligible Linux warm images with cron. When system Node resolves under `/usr`, the prefetch may also read unrelated files under that directory. [#169886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169886) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep warm-image cleanup within its elapsed-time budget across system clock changes. Timed-out deletions remain tracked for retry. [#169908](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169908) by [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Reuse a compatible Crabbox project image across runtime updates, installing a newer runtime after startup if needed. Changed setup or machine inputs still require a compatible image. [#170780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170780) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Refresh Cloud inventory after plugin activation and use canonical repository labels for worktree snapshots. Existing labels update on their next metadata write. [#171176](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171176) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An eligible first AWS cloud session can start while its snapshot uploads with Crabbox 0.78.0 or newer, once capture is confirmed. Another session may start cold until that image finishes saving. [#171729](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171729) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show one useful warning rather than repeated capture failures when a Crabbox image save is already paused. [#171059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171059) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - Honored Crabbox worker lifetimes during preparation and capture and waited for confirmed remote gate cleanup. Crabbox 0.77.0 or newer is required ([#172442](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172442)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Selecting a saved cloud profile that is not active yet now tells you to restart the Gateway and try again, rather than reporting success. Cloud workers also wait for a provider's quota reset before retrying.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Tell you when a saved cloud profile is not active in the running Gateway yet, with guidance to restart and try again. [#171025](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171025) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Wait for a provider's quota to reset before retrying a cloud worker that received a rate-limit response. [#171823](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171823), related to [#171760](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171760), by [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), with [@Kimiyu-186](https://github.com/Kimiyu-186) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Conversations on paired and cloud computers give a clearer reason when a model or credential is missing, a worker stops mid-reply, or a turn produces no answer. If a worker disappears while the Gateway stays up, text it already received can remain in conversation history, but interrupted tools are not resumed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Deliver the worker's finished reply before file synchronization completes; follow-up work still waits for synchronization, and a later conflict appears separately ([#169484](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169484)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Start replying sooner in measured warm paired-computer turns by avoiding repeated destination checks. [#168799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168799) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Finish a remote turn with less file-sync work when neither workspace changed. [#168933](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168933) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse approved model and credential preparation across provider requests within a Gateway-proxied node turn. [#169294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169294) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce repeated bundle inspection during node retention by reusing installed status for the current connection. A bundle altered during that connection may not be detected until launch, installation or explicit inspection. [#169325](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169325) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid full retention cleanup on capacity-only node updates. Inventory-only cleanup waits for a successful periodic reconciliation. [#169371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169371) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse successful disk samples for unchanged idle worker placements for up to 15 minutes. Activity or runner changes prompt a refresh on the next maintenance pass. Disk-pressure warnings remain advisory. [#169712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169712) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retry starting a session once if another worker's change interrupted its start. [#170085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170085) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use an explicitly selected OpenClaw runtime and model on a required worker even when the Gateway has no provider credentials. [#170132](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170132) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Check interrupted worker work after a Gateway restart before starting the next turn. [#170161](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170161) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - If a native worker disappears during a reply while the Gateway stays running, try to keep the text already received in conversation history and explain the failure. Interrupted tools are not resumed. [#170223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170223) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Tell you when a paired computer needs model or credential setup instead of pointing to an unrelated update or sign-in problem. [#170257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170257) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Ask a worker for an answer after its tools finish, or show a failure if it still returns nothing. A background command that is still running is reported as in progress. [#170426](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170426) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Cloud worker turns accept longer provider response IDs when both the Gateway and worker use the updated validator. [#170461](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170461) by [@sallyom](https://github.com/sallyom).
  - The worker inference guide clarifies that optional workers handle bound sessions while sessionless helpers run on the Gateway. A required profile blocks those helpers rather than moving them to the worker. [#170012](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170012) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Supported worker slots can be used again after a confirmed computer restart, without waiting for the old worker to reappear.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Free supported native worker slots after a confirmed computer restart. If the computer's boot identity is unavailable, OpenClaw keeps the safer existing recovery behavior. [#168863](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168863) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Worker setup refuses project-cache folders that are known to lead through a symlink.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Rejected known symlinked project-cache parents before worker toolchain setup ([#172079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172079)). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- Some new project conversations can begin replying while their checkout finishes, with file-dependent tools waiting until the [project folder](/concepts/managed-worktrees) is ready. Resetting an existing conversation can keep its project folder and working directory; choosing New Chat still leaves the previous worktree.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Include more default guidance for agents in new workspaces with small setup budgets. Existing workspace files are left alone ([#89894](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/89894), [report #75187](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/75187)). Thanks to [dwc1997](https://github.com/dwc1997), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [camerono](https://github.com/camerono).
  - Let the opt-in continuation-skip mode stop repeating workspace instructions after successful delivery in an already onboarded workspace ([#154923](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154923), [report #149867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/149867), [workspace configuration](/gateway/config-agents/workspace-and-bootstrap)). Thanks to [sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [playhard94](https://github.com/playhard94).
  - Preserve native managed worktrees, projects, and working directories through conversation resets. Control UI New Chat still leaves the previous worktree ([#160549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160549), [earlier rollover work #159506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159506) by [chelsealong](https://github.com/chelsealong)). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Let a project checkout proceed after a brief delay instead of reporting a false allocation failure ([#168214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168214)). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Preserve partial-clone filters and reuse clean worktree snapshot trees, leaving the checkout in place if required objects are missing ([#168682](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168682)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore accepted local file edits to their committed values after a worker reply is lost ([#168094](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168094)). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show files, not folders, in session activity meant to list files ([#167732](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167732), [related issue #96660](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/96660)). Thanks to [goldarowana](https://github.com/goldarowana), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and reporter [mmhzlrj](https://github.com/mmhzlrj).
  - Refuse `openclaw path set` edits on invalid UTF-8 in the opt-in oc-path plugin, preserving the original bytes. Repair or convert a backed-up copy before retrying ([#168491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168491), [related report #114895](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/114895)). Thanks to [wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and reporter [yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Begin replying in eligible new project conversations before checkout finishes, while file-dependent tools wait for the folder. Projects with setup hooks or other incompatible preparation still finish setup first. [#169136](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169136) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prepare a project's default branch once for concurrent conversations without disturbing the checkout already in use. [#169070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169070) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Open an already prepared project folder without waiting for other conversations' folders to finish allocating. [#169178](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169178) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse a successful default-base preparation for up to 30 seconds and allow an explicit ref to select an update immediately. [#169590](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169590) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore filesystem template use for full worktrees when a sibling uses sparse checkout. Targets with sparse settings, filters or external attributes continue through Git. [#169359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169359) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuse validated templates during concurrent Btrfs checkouts and choose Git for later creations when a warm Btrfs checkout measures more than 250 ms slower. [#169440](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169440) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduce resource use during bursts of worktree-backed session creation. [#168807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168807) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Free checkout names sooner during ordinary forced cleanup and delete retired files in the background. A crash or failed deletion can leave an inspectable `.openclaw-removing-*` directory. [#169690](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169690) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recover available missing partial-clone objects during an explicit deferred cleanup retry. Recovery is bounded and can continue on another retry. [#168864](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168864) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the first message and start its turn even when several project conversations are created at once. [#169107](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169107) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Guests can choose available project checkouts again without seeing host paths, and their New Session choices stay in their own profile. File and HTML previews also work in affected project conversations.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">

  - Keep a guest's New Session choices in that guest's profile ([#172114](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172114), [#172083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172083)). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).

Thanks [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@resinoftree97](https://github.com/resinoftree97) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - Corrected the guest scope example and clarified visitor, sandbox and publication guidance ([commit 952d3ac](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/952d3ac4dcb4100ea986a0c40d945b2b7f72d25e)).
  - Continued verified pack cleanup despite missing snapshot objects and reported maintenance progress ([#171978](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171978)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stopped managed cloning on destination inspection errors ([#175952](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175952)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduced redundant Git processes during session refresh and worktree path discovery ([#172472](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172472)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore guest worktree selection for registered and cloned projects without exposing host paths. [#171811](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171811), related to [#171792](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171792), by [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
  - Allow accepted guest child sessions to start after the parent yields, while retaining guest permissions. [#171668](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171668) by [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).

  </details>

- New files you stage with `git add` on the main computer can reach a cloud worker after its next completed workspace sync; unstaged new files do not. Accepted file changes and deletions also make it back without replacing the worker's local Git history.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Make a new file staged on the main computer available to a cloud worker after a completed workspace sync. Unstaged files stay outside that sync. [#170163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170163) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recovery retains the accepted workspace baseline so later worker deletions of synchronized files apply correctly. [#170213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170213) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Transferred folder repositories retain a credential-free HTTP, HTTPS or Git origin without a query or fragment when the node checkout has none. [#170224](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170224) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Bring accepted file updates back without replacing the remote computer's Git history, branches or remote settings. Node commits remain local and do not advance Gateway HEAD. [#170338](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170338) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Workspace reconciliation can continue after the reply writer closes. [#170556](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170556) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session views can leave the reconciling state when workspace settlement finishes. [#170575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170575) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Workspace tools remain usable after a session resumes from a child result or another internal continuation. [#170791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170791) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Worker recovery can find a placement added during an overlapping read on its next sweep. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3d99ff35fe59104aa3a66b5328e1d2c9ce30f044)
