  - Initial worker session creation keeps its placement stable while the first turn is claimed. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1ac1a34c11e43006ea9d6b822593495782976505)
  - Required-worker chats stay on the worker admission path across policy reloads. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f586b491f164fb6cf6d793999d2b4bca294d7031)
  - Valid required-worker turns can complete and publish workspace results. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a1ac97bcbe7b4e4b2985f4deccbb4856b2131581)
  - Node worker downloads reuse validated content for repeated files within one transfer, while keeping the resulting files independent. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5268c0790b7fbfcc395cc1e42a9c5384bc10c335)
  - Portable worker archives support database admission after relocation. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9e52f76fa4861178687812a00416531c892ed0df)
  - New Gateway worktree files staged with `git add` can join the accepted workspace at the next completed cloud worker reconciliation. Unstaged new files remain local. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/85bebbe71f9bb7cb308d223e1bc7afaf0dbb6f7a)
  - Eligible agent sessions with matching agents, directories and equivalent runtime configurations can reuse the prepared workspace while keeping their requested execution directory. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/59b6c109167434cac64ea41fecda871501374750). Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman) and [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).

  </details>

- [Sandboxed agents](/gateway/sandboxing/workspace-access) can read affected reply attachments, saved web-fetch results and cloud-worker skills again, without gaining access to the host command line. Images, video and music generated locally can also be copied into the requesting sandbox for further edits. Remote-only URLs are not copied, and an unavailable or read-only sandbox shows a warning instead.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Restore SSH sandbox reply attachments, including the WebChat failure reported in [#171043](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171043). [#171079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171079) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), thanks to [@valkyriweb](https://github.com/valkyriweb) for the report. Related attachment work in [#131616](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131616) and [#152633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152633).
  - Reject oversized local workspace document saves before replacing the previous file. The separate identity and avatar issue remains open in [#127507](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127507). [#171938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171938) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let eligible sandboxed conversations create draft pull requests again under their existing publishing policy. [#171803](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171803) by [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
  - Blocked host CLI execution for sandboxed spawned agents ([#178742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178742)). Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - Made saved web fetch output readable in local sandboxes, with an inline fallback when staging is unavailable ([#172499](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172499), [#172430](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172430)). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
  - Kept generated sandbox skills out of untracked Git changes ([#173560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173560), [#173546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/173546)). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
  - Restored Code Mode skill search and reads on cloud workers ([#182503](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182503)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Copy generated local files into the requesting sandbox for reuse, subject to the configured media cap and a 50 MiB limit per file. Remote-only URLs are not copied, and an unavailable or read-only sandbox produces a warning. [#171058](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171058) · [Sandbox workspace access](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/7e15622f37bd0ced194a3b0fccacb4ad6bc78d85/docs/gateway/sandboxing/workspace-access.md) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@adembektas](https://github.com/adembektas) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169922) and detached-completion investigation. The issue also references [#75683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/75683).
  - Fixes the unavailable SDK import used by OpenShell workspace and filesystem checks. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2249d6be1c3029d325010bee564477e8f9a4ae6e)
  - Rejected non-decimal timeout input before execution ([#179000](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179000), [#178997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/178997)). Thanks [@tuaran](https://github.com/tuaran) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reported portal access problems to the requesting browser and preserved open app state ([#172366](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172366)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Corrected source lines for `openclaw path resolve` and `openclaw path find` after standalone carriage returns ([#184866](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184866)). Thanks [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reduced retained memory for prepared session forks with Unicode-heavy requests ([#172032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172032)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- [Paired-computer setup](/nodes/session-hosting) keeps your selected profile and approved device when you retry, and reconnecting preserves the device's credentials.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserve the selected profile or container in QR setup's device-list and approval commands. [#178878](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178878). Thanks to [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Match generated machine join commands to the Gateway release and offer an installed-command alternative, with matching-build guidance for development or unpublished builds ([#168680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168680)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve paired-device credentials on reconnect and support explicit environment authentication ([#168653](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168653)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Accept copied node pairing request IDs with surrounding whitespace for approval or rejection ([#141286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141286)). Thanks to [hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen).
  - Reuse an approved pairing during node-only and cloud-worker bootstrap retries. [#168795](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168795) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Align device setup controls with the administrator requirement and retain eligible pairing approvals. [#170988](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170988) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Paired-computer services installed with `npx` now use a lasting package location instead of npm's disposable cache. Reinstalling the same profile can repair or update the service, and service commands work with custom state folders.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep `npx`-installed node services outside npm's disposable cache and allow same-profile reinstall to repair or update them ([#168644](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168644)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep Linux node service PATH entries available after login-shell startup, ahead of profile entries ([#136677](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/136677), [report #134354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134354)). Thanks to [harjothkhara](https://github.com/harjothkhara), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [abacha](https://github.com/abacha).
  - Node service lifecycle commands work with custom state directories without a mistaken Gateway identity refusal. [#170403](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170403) by [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Show how the paired-computer service is set to run, and offer guarded install choices to restore a saved Node or Bun command. [#170441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170441) by [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- A valid Incognito computer selection no longer fails just because cleanup of an unrelated database lookup failed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserve a valid incognito placement result when database reader cleanup encounters an unreadable discovery candidate ([83579fdb](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/83579fdb473aafd4274b1677a10a77679de1b389)).

  </details>

- A command naming a paired computer now runs on that computer even without a separate host setting. If it names conflicting host and device targets, OpenClaw rejects it instead of guessing where to run.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Node-only exec routing and rejection of conflicting host and node arguments. [PR #172327](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172327), [issue #172260](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172260). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), reporter [yozakura-ava](https://github.com/yozakura-ava), and [jayswu](https://github.com/jayswu) for tracing the routing and advising on coverage.

  </details>

### Skills, plugins and GitHub

- Skills that download an archive from a generic link can now unpack it when the skill supplies a supported archive filename.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Skill dependencies that explicitly request extraction can use a supported archive filename supplied by a generic download endpoint. [#141312](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141312). Thanks [@hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Fixes outdated Skill Workshop change and Undo notices appearing after the conversation is reset, replaced or deleted.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Suppress outdated change and Undo notices when the reviewed session has been reset, replaced, or deleted. [#170035](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170035) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- [Installed skills](/tools/skills#search-installed-skills) pick up external edits on refresh, keep more icons and homepages, and can retry safe reads after a paired computer briefly disconnects. If a skills directory has an unreadable or broken ignore file, it is skipped with a warning until you repair that file.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Pick up edits made to skill files outside OpenClaw on the next scheduled refresh, or scan immediately when you request it. [#170341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170341) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Save settings or create an agent without waiting for an unrelated skill scan to finish. [#171157](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171157) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show the skill's location and computer-specific guidance when you read it by exact name. [#170965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170965) by [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Retry safe Skill discovery and reads after a temporary paired-node transition. [#171208](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171208) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Check installer readiness only for selected skill dependencies. [#170967](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170967) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep skill icons and homepage links visible even when a metadata line ends with a comment. [#171224](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171224) by [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).
  - Continue using already available workspace skills during unrelated background scans; first discovery and a requested refresh still wait for a completed scan. [#171857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171857), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuses shared skill-root reads within a multi-workspace scan. [#171871](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171871), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@SBC2016](https://github.com/SBC2016) for the [related Windows startup report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170928).
  - Reduces memory used for skill discovery in a measured workload. [#171285](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171285), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep background Skill Workshop reviews from triggering reply hooks meant only for user messages. [#171651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171651), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Rejects local skill installs whose required description contains only a YAML comment. [#171812](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171812), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Skip a skills directory and warn when its ignore file is broken or unreadable. Repair the file so the directory can be included in a later scan. [#176681](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176681). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Sanitize skills library metadata in human-readable terminal output. JSON and requested file contents remain verbatim and may contain terminal controls. [#178313](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178313). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show no bundled skills in status and model prompts when you explicitly allow none. [#182731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182731), thanks to [SC-10749](https://github.com/SC-10749) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [Card4Philippe](https://github.com/Card4Philippe) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182637).
  - Preserve the completed-turn anchors needed for Workshop learning when an identical prompt snapshot is recorded during a later turn. [Commit 5a1cd1a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5a1cd1abf119a7393a3004acaafd6b18299ecf81).
  - Keep a background Workshop self-learning review running through later conversation turns. A session reset or permission change still cancels it. [Commit 3dc2d7a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3dc2d7ab2dc22acd7cc96dd267dcb0967bd31f6b).

  </details>

- [MCP tools](/tools/mcp#troubleshooting) can reconnect for future calls after a legacy server restarts, and duplicate structured results appear only once. A call whose outcome is unknown during the disconnect is not automatically repeated; inspect its effect before trying again.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Reconnect legacy SSE MCP tools for later calls when a server restart changes its address. A call with an unknown outcome is not replayed. [#167423](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167423), [issue #167371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167371). Thanks [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@abacha](https://github.com/abacha), and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep explicit MCP settings during a reload and remove an unwanted separator from later streamed text. A request interrupted before it runs can report that it was superseded. [#168981](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168981), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show one MCP structured result when the same JSON appears twice with different formatting, while keeping distinct text and recovery guidance. [#169180](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169180), thanks [@xilopaint](https://github.com/xilopaint) for the fix and [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169121), and [@jipeng6036-del](https://github.com/jipeng6036-del) for the implementation.

  </details>

- A connected [MCP App](/tools/mcp#interactive-apps-and-plugin-extensions) in the Dashboard keeps unsaved input and scroll position when you minimize and reopen its panel. Affected MCP sign-in, conversation recovery and Safari startup paths also work again.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep your unsaved input and scroll position in a loaded Dashboard MCP App when you minimize and reopen its panel while it is still connected. [#169441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169441), thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Complete MCP sign-in when its registered local callback address omits a port and otherwise matches. [#170876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170876) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [9fw2pq8sgb-art](https://github.com/9fw2pq8sgb-art) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170848). See the [MCP transport guidance](/cli/mcp/transports).
  - Keep the elapsed-time budget for pending MCP tool cleanup during clock changes. [#170495](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170495) by [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), with test contributions from [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Let affected POSIX conversations recover after an unavailable local MCP server is removed. [#171397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171397), thanks to [@joelagnel](https://github.com/joelagnel). The [related Windows issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw-windows-node/issues/1717) has other unresolved symptoms.
  - Preserves intentional leading and trailing whitespace in `mcp add` executable and working-directory paths. [#171620](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171620), thanks to [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Warn about literal secrets in MCP settings and included files without treating a populated environment reference as a literal secret. [#171737](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171737), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini). See [the reported warning](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171469).
  - Expands MCP Relay guidance for Codex, multiple Gateways and Team access, and corrects the embedded Control UI credential guidance. [#171575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171575), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- When MCP sign-in fails, the message now names the server and points you toward its credentials. Quiet tool calls can use their configured timeout, while accepted long-running relay jobs can finish beyond the submission deadline. A call with an uncertain outcome still needs checking before you retry it.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Accept mixed-case JSON content types from authenticated local MCP clients. [#172290](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172290). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reject external-link requests from retired or unfocused embedded MCP frames. [#172312](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172312). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Name the MCP server when its sign-in fails, with guidance to check credentials or sign in again. [#172331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172331). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@rclarke0](https://github.com/rclarke0) for the report.
  - Let an HTTP MCP call with no intermediate output use the full request timeout you configured. [#172510](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172510). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@xilopaint](https://github.com/xilopaint) for the report.
  - Release callbacks after MCP Events requests complete and retain a redacted cause when a transport fails. [#172613](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172613). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include the server name in accessible names for MCP sign-in and enable or disable actions. [#178162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178162). Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm) and [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
  - Attempt to remove temporary MCP configuration directories after failed Claude or Gemini CLI writes, including partial files that may contain credentials. Removal is best effort. [#179603](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179603). Thanks [@xiaopan-xydt](https://github.com/xiaopan-xydt) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@YangManBOBO](https://github.com/YangManBOBO) for the partial-write reproduction.
  - Mask recognized credentials in MCP CLI list and show output while preserving saved credentials. [#180321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180321). Thanks [@brandon-julio-t](https://github.com/brandon-julio-t), and [@wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web) for regression coverage.
  - Keep long MCP event descriptions from splitting an emoji or other supplementary character at the existing length limit. [#181741](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181741). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Let an already accepted MCP relay run continue past the deadline for submitting new requests. [#181863](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181863). Thanks [@crwohlfeil](https://github.com/crwohlfeil) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Restore MCP App initialization in affected Safari and macOS WebKit setups. [#182485](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182485), thanks to [anmol0b](https://github.com/anmol0b) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [technicalpickles](https://github.com/technicalpickles) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164447).
  - Keep the MCP App raw-details menu usable across transcript refreshes. [Commit fcfcc06](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/fcfcc060446494b1188b503b1c476c78a284a090).
  - Reopen an unexpectedly closed local MCP listener on the next CLI-backed run; a call that already failed is not retried automatically. [#183352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183352), thanks to [SC-10749](https://github.com/SC-10749) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [eli-pavlov](https://github.com/eli-pavlov) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139340).
  - Let the next turn proceed after a failed remote MCP session deletion if the local connection was cleaned up. The remote session may remain until it expires. [#182189](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182189), thanks to [aniketkrs](https://github.com/aniketkrs) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [goslingmanagment](https://github.com/goslingmanagment) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182174).
  - Keep MCP request deadlines and human-input allowances stable across system clock changes. [#184056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184056), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Preserve meaningful whitespace in MCP TLS client certificate and key paths. Previously saved paths that were already trimmed cannot be reconstructed automatically. [#181883](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181883), thanks to [wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Return the standard structured JSON failure for MCP relay commands. [#183361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183361), thanks to [Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve complete Unicode characters in the retained tail of a long MCP proxy error. [#183942](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183942), thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject malformed MCP schema identifiers while keeping valid relative references. A schema accepted before this change may now need fixing. [#187285](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187285), thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- [Plugin installation and inspection](/plugins/manage-plugins) show clearer failure reasons, and a broken plugin no longer prevents you from inspecting healthy ones. Configured tool guards also apply when an agent runs a standalone command.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Show a useful reason when a plugin command fails, while keeping JSON and debug output available. [#168850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168850). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - QA Lab lets accepted suites finish during shutdown, while interrupted plugin CLI changes stop retrying without replaying an uncertain accepted operation. [#158087](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158087). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - When another Gateway process owns plugin state, local plugin and hook changes now explain the conflict and how to retry. Existing Gateway plugin actions and offline marketplace reads remain available. [#168757](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168757). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Repeated plugin enable or disable requests leave the config file unchanged when the requested state is already applied. [#168633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168633). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Updates avoid repeatedly adding a redundant bundled plugin load path that Doctor removes. [#168226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168226). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Registry inspection clears a stale warning for a removed plugin load path. Use `openclaw plugins registry --refresh` to update the persisted ledger. [#168167](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168167), [issue #167847](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167847). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@giodl73-repo](https://github.com/giodl73-repo).
  - Standalone plugin setting maps show their Custom entries heading again, including in filtered results. [#157903](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157903). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen) and [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - External TypeScript plugins using NodeNext-style relative imports can start faster, with gains depending on the plugin graph. [#168391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168391), [issue #168130](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168130). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@jakeaadams](https://github.com/jakeaadams) for the report.
  - Apply configured [plugin tool guards](/plugins/hooks/tool-policy) to standalone `openclaw agent exec` commands before they run. [#159333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159333), [issue #158824](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/158824). Thanks [@joseairosa](https://github.com/joseairosa) for the report and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Summaries and new model completions from retained plugins can continue after an unrelated plugin reload. [#168916](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168916), thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman) for the fix and [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156026).
  - Inspect an incompatible plugin and its repair guidance without losing access to healthy plugin details. The incompatible plugin remains unable to run. [#168977](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168977), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Plugin changes now report pending migrations and cleanup failures more clearly, while completed migrations stop showing as pending. If an older plugin keeps using a file that has been retired and cleaned up, it may need a restart.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Clean up aged tokenless plugin files after the grace period, including files that are still open. Affected legacy producers may need a restart if their files are reclaimed. [#169198](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169198), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Dhotei](https://github.com/Dhotei) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/165035).
  - Show unexpected hook-pack inspection errors when a plugin installation is rejected. [#169370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169370), thanks [@wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web).
  - Deliver queued next-turn plugin context to Codex, ACPX, Agents API and Copilot, including on a retry within the same run. [#169558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169558), thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Report an error to extensions when cleanup of a canceled command cannot be confirmed, with the original command result available as the cause. [#169629](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169629), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Apply the subagent concurrency cap to plugin background completions. [#170273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170273) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [rogerallen1](https://github.com/rogerallen1) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/95746).
  - See which plugin migrations still need repair and which updates were skipped. [#170392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170392) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170334).
  - Remove confirmed completed migration warnings from update results and history while retaining warnings for pending work. [#170413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170413) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), with thanks to [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for the report and follow-up. See [update status and history](/cli/update/status-and-history).
  - Show state-maintenance and ownership recovery reasons in ordinary plugin command output. [#169907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169907) by [ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [jamiezigelbaum](https://github.com/jamiezigelbaum) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169862).
  - Keep plugin state reads current for Unicode key aliases after writes and deletions. [#170208](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170208) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Open Control UI panels again for affected trusted bundled and Nix-store plugins. [#170646](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170646) by [licheer-zte](https://github.com/licheer-zte), with thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [paulcam206](https://github.com/paulcam206). See the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170409).
  - Report failed LanceDB provider cleanup, which may require a Gateway restart. Stop the OpenTelemetry service before replacing its globals. [#170800](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170800) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Building bundled plugin browser views no longer rewrites tracked source manifests.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keeps bundled plugin browser builds from rewriting tracked source manifests while using freshly built browser metadata. [#172037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172037), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- In supported Gateway-hosted conversations, an agent can end its current turn to reload a plugin, then continue in the same conversation with the result. Existing permissions still apply. If the Gateway exits before the result arrives, inspect the plugin's state before trying again.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Let an agent use `reloadAndResume` in a Gateway-hosted conversation with turn-yield support, so its current turn ends before the plugin reloads and it can continue with the result. If the Gateway exits before the result arrives, inspect plugin state before retrying. [#171257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171257), thanks to [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson) as co-author and for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170881), and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for integration work.
  - Registers hooks from explicitly enabled standalone plugin files, including tool-call policies. Listing a file in load paths alone does not enable it. See the [tool-policy guidance](/plugins/hooks/tool-policy). [#171330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171330), thanks to [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for integration and verification.
  - Retry plugin install, enable, disable and reload actions after brief contention only when the Gateway confirms the action did not begin. An uncertain action is not repeated. [#171390](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171390), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Removes managed plugins' diagnostic subscriptions when their instances retire, including on reload or unload. Other resources such as timers and sockets still need their own cleanup. [#172052](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172052), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keeps plugin installation progress usable across hover and pinning, and returns focus to the install button when Escape closes it. [#171346](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171346), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reuses preparation for plugin inventories and stops reporting missing dependencies for providers disabled by discovery policy. [#171356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171356), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Defers llama.cpp execution modules until first use and reuses checked plugin paths during POSIX validation. [#171319](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171319), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the Gateway responsive while it prepares a large external plugin package. Starting that plugin or loading it through a synchronous CLI command may still take time. [#172750](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172750). Thanks [@ExoHumann](https://github.com/ExoHumann), and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for the related report.
  - Keep plugin settings and enabled or disabled choices when reinstalling or updating a plugin whose ID uses mixed capitalization. Existing ambiguous disabled aliases may need manual recovery with `openclaw plugins enable <plugin-id>`. [#180789](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180789). Thanks [@anmol0b](https://github.com/anmol0b), and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for the report and earlier fix.
  - Keep invalid numeric plugin settings on the same setup question so they can be corrected. [#176787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176787). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Clean up eligible retired plugin files periodically while the Gateway is running. Protected and mapped files remain in place. [#176851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176851). Thanks [@harjothkhara](https://github.com/harjothkhara) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@PaulD-LeWagon](https://github.com/PaulD-LeWagon) and [@viernesmybot](https://github.com/viernesmybot) for their reports.
  - Avoid false hook isolation failures when shared-memory properties are omitted from the cloned input, and reduce snapshot work. Shared memory delivered to a hook remains blocked. [#172301](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172301). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve system events that plugins select for deferred delivery. [#181138](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181138). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Installed plugins can appear in the list while remote catalog details are still loading; refresh later to see those details. Plugin cleanup now reports more failures without hiding healthy plugins.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep plugin service startup, reload and stop timeout budgets stable when the system clock changes. [#181663](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181663). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep plugin instance cleanup's timeout budget stable across system-clock changes. [#181675](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181675). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore catalog and chat activity artwork for MCP Relay and Muse Code. [#172299](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172299). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore catalog and chat activity artwork for Advisor and MCP Events. [#172716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172716). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Show normal-level warnings when plugin cleanup fails during session reset or deletion. Cleanup remains best effort. [#172513](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172513). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot) for the report.
  - Warn when a document extractor or bundled web provider fails to initialize while another remains available. [#174299](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174299). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include a bounded, redacted explanation in failed 1Password resolver responses. After restoring secret access, run `openclaw secrets reload` to retry affected owners. [#177060](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177060). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@gisk0](https://github.com/gisk0) for the related report.
  - Keep long plugin runtime errors from splitting an emoji or other supplementary character at the existing length limit. [#181742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181742). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Return installed plugin rows while remote catalog enrichment runs. A refresh shows completed details. [#184921](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184921), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve the exact stale install record during removal when its name collides with another plugin's alias. [#184628](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184628), thanks to [Links2008](https://github.com/Links2008) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and to [itanyplus](https://github.com/itanyplus) for [the related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182635).

  </details>

- Plugin settings and uninstall previews are less likely to lose your intended change when results arrive late, a secret is masked or a plugin ID uses mixed capitalization. Changing a masked secret's backend still requires its explicit ID.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Strip terminal controls and escape line breaks in plugin names shown in uninstall previews. [#183708](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183708), thanks to [wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep a late inspection result from showing a stale error or replacing pending uninstall controls. [Commit 1ac5ebc](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1ac5ebc04789ec97e017f185fbf85786cac5d0d4).
  - Show plugin action notices inside the overview detail panel. [Commit 35362bd](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/35362bd45e293a9144625853966be85374b31e2c).
  - Prevent a failed plugin's cleanup from interrupting healthy plugin and model preparation. [#183660](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183660), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let plugin setup retirement return its timeout result even when artifact removal is slow. Removal can continue afterward. [#187562](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187562), thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Reuse a live plugin inspection during model-catalog preparation without a duplicate registration error. [#187567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187567), thanks to [romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject malformed plugin SVG transforms without freezing the Control UI. [#182674](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182674), thanks to [SebTardif](https://github.com/SebTardif) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve Unicode characters at the length limits of ClawHub MCP scope and setup previews. [#182753](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182753), thanks to [wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666).
  - Complete authorized `reloadAndResume` requests after turn cleanup while retaining permission and revocation checks. [#182929](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182929), thanks to [PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [lisandromachado](https://github.com/lisandromachado) for [the related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180662).
  - Save other Control UI settings even when a plugin secret is fully masked. Changing that secret's backend still requires its explicit ID. [#183571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183571), thanks to [lonexreb](https://github.com/lonexreb) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [Valkster70](https://github.com/Valkster70) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/183562).
  - [Decision-provider configuration in plugin inspection](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a07acefb6abb8efe41aacd271aeca81cb71fa2bc).

  </details>

- Workboard cards now reflect the overall task result through model fallback rather than showing a false failure while work continues. Board tabs and menus are easier to reach on narrow screens, and after a storage problem is resolved, later Workboard, Logbook and Team Reports operations can recover without restarting the Gateway. An uncertain in-flight write is not replayed automatically.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Local Workboard commands make changes visible to the running Gateway. Offline dispatch does not start workers. [#168457](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168457). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Board widget HTML over the existing 10 MiB limit stops loading with a clear error. [#167260](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167260). Thanks [@hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - The Workboard guide explains the existing agent tool grants and the tools automatically granted to card workers. [#168222](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168222). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Workboard card status follows the overall task outcome through model fallback, avoiding false failures while work continues. Session-only links can settle on the next minute sweep. [#168215](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168215), [issue #167574](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167574). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223), [@asikorskiy](https://github.com/asikorskiy) for the report, and [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin) for the related proposal.
  - Leave unchanged widgets untouched during Board layout edits. [#168999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168999), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore later Workboard, Logbook and Team Reports operations after a storage failure once the underlying problem is resolved. An in-flight write may have an unknown outcome and is not replayed automatically. [#170138](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170138) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [Jeehut](https://github.com/Jeehut). See the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169981).
  - Load board summaries and session-linked cards in desktop chat instead of full card history. [#171135](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171135) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171078).
  - Prevent normal or forced promotion from reopening completed cards. [#171080](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171080) by [sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), with thanks to [SabaTech-dev](https://github.com/SabaTech-dev). See the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171048).
  - Reveal overflow-selected board tabs on narrow screens and support keyboard access to long widget menus. [#170767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170767) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Anchor Workboard details menus to their controls. [#170511](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170511) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid unnecessary database initialization for initial Board snapshot and widget reads. [#171245](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171245) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Completed Workboard cards stay completed if a later step fails, and manually moving a card to done or blocked releases its claim. That move does not stop a worker already running.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Restores review handoffs after dependencies finish. [#171414](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171414), thanks to [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@asikorskiy](https://github.com/asikorskiy) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171380), and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for verification.
  - Prevents promotion of cards outside `todo`, including when `force` is used. [#171743](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171743), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@SabaTech-dev](https://github.com/SabaTech-dev). See also [the promotion report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171048).
  - Preserves full run IDs and completion notifications, with a 512-character limit for new IDs. [#172235](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172235), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@gregbond](https://github.com/gregbond) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172203).
  - Load matching packaged Workboard assets from the Control UI origin when the Gateway is separate. Custom or mismatched plugins continue to use the Gateway asset route. [#172352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172352). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Show agents why a card claim was refused, including when a blocked card needs to be unblocked. [#172504](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172504). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reconcile cards linked to archived sessions, including multiple cards sharing a session or run. [#172505](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172505). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve completed cards and links after a later step in a multi-card operation fails. [#172615](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172615). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Release claims and settle active records on manual done or blocked moves. [#184241](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184241), thanks to [jondihaynes](https://github.com/jondihaynes) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep Workboard automation nudges on their intended cooldown through system clock changes. [#183356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183356), thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Include session changes from nested operations in Boards transaction receipts. [Commit bfea15e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/bfea15edc0d66d880c5f58744817fd6297844768).

  </details>

- [Workboard](/plugins/workboard) now records a finished task's result when its conversation is briefly still marked as running, and no longer mistakes newly starting work for a launch abandoned before restart.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  [Workboard lifecycle fixes](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/aad30d238ed5b7e347fe9adc66cd7b4ddf5caa9e). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Existing Workboard data can move between SQLite and experimental PostgreSQL storage with an offline, recoverable [database port](/reference/database-schemas/storage-changes). Stop the Gateway and embedded agents first, and back up both sides. Shared state and agent databases remain on SQLite.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Refuse selecting experimental PostgreSQL for an existing populated SQLite Workboard without the supported port. [#170266](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170266) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Port existing Workboard stores, including disabled ones, with a dry run and resume or rollback after an interrupted operation. [#171368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171368), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Offer experimental PostgreSQL-backed Workboard storage as an opt-in choice, without replacing shared state or agent databases. [#169214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169214), [storage design](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169254). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Report experimental database engine selection in Doctor. Workboard is the only supported PostgreSQL store, and a selected PostgreSQL store does not silently fall back to SQLite. [#169933](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169933), [portability design #169254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169254). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Experimental PostgreSQL Workboard writes avoid a duplicate admission request, and PostgreSQL connection secrets must use an environment, file or exec reference in [#170037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170037). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - PostgreSQL backup, restore, and update protection, including a refusal from `backup sqlite` that directs PostgreSQL users to `backup create` ([#170828](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170828)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- GitHub hover previews, repository searches and publication account options can show a previous result while refreshing, clearly marked as stale. Publication still checks current access before it proceeds.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Cached previews, repository search and account options return stale results while refreshing in [#169143](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169143). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Unchanged issue and pull-request hover previews reuse their response when refreshed in [#170508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170508). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Actions widgets and project searches share compatible GitHub reads in [#170825](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170825). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Cached shared-account publication options remain available and marked stale during native authentication refresh in [#170963](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170963). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Cached hover previews avoid a redundant credential read while retaining the check before delivery. [PR #171603](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171603). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Concurrent GitHub publication options requests share discovery for a worktree. [PR #171325](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171325). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- GitHub publication works again for eligible managed child sessions even when their parent is outside a publishable workspace. The child's own workspace and inherited permissions still govern what it can publish; this does not grant new publishing access or change where a PR goes.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Own-session PR publishing after a delayed verified profile attachment in [#169400](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169400), addressing [#169377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169377). Thanks [@Gigaxel](https://github.com/Gigaxel) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - New managed children retain permitted GitHub publication for eligible worktrees, subject to inherited restrictions and Gateway checks. [#171842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171842), addressing [#171782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171782). Thanks to [shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
  - Avoid unnecessary publication refusals during overlapping requests. [#172351](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172351). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Watched session PR refreshes and GitHub publication avoid redundant Git calls. [#172498](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172498), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - ClickClack discussion lookup and shared GitHub publication receipt discovery avoid duplicate local reads. [#172537](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172537), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- An accepted PR publication request can remain pending through a temporary session-access interruption. Check the original request's result rather than sending a duplicate.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep accepted publication requests pending during temporary session authority outages ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7f98077b2e30dd5b0c2aa271b9934f6414a106dc)). Thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).

  </details>

### Models and coding agents

- A bound Codex conversation can resume its original thread after the Codex service restarts. An input is retried only when the service confirms it was rejected before acceptance.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserves bound-thread history after a restart. [#172705](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172705), included in [#178169](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178169). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the combined backport.

  </details>

- Managed local Codex conversations can recover from a provider error or refresh their settings without replacing their history or interrupting another conversation's work.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  [#172287](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172287) and [#174540](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174540). Thanks to [@zhuyankarl](https://github.com/zhuyankarl), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  This applies to the local Codex service managed by OpenClaw, not an external service or a shared user-owned Codex home. If the old thread cannot finish closing, OpenClaw keeps its history and reports the problem instead of replacing it.

  </details>

- [Binding a conversation to Codex](/plugins/codex-harness/commands#commands-and-diagnostics) no longer fails just because its next reply is waiting to start. If the source conversation is already working, let it finish or stop it before binding.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  [Commit aad30d2](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/aad30d238ed5b7e347fe9adc66cd7b4ddf5caa9e), carrying the binding repair from [#189077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189077). Thanks to [@libaojiang](https://github.com/libaojiang) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  The transfer copies a limited amount of visible user and assistant history into a separate Codex thread, not the previous agent's tools or instructions.

  </details>

- Model pickers refresh when the catalog changes during an unfinished read, rather than keeping the older model list.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Starts a fresh catalog read after a change. [#174812](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174812).

  </details>

- If Anthropic model discovery fails, direct Anthropic sessions keep the supported context window you chose. Sonnet 5.5 usage estimates also use the corrected cached-input price.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Direct Anthropic sessions retain their selected supported context window when live model discovery fails. [#177702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177702), [issue #177701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/177701) Thanks [@nxmxbbd](https://github.com/nxmxbbd) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - New Sonnet 5.5 usage estimates use the corrected default cached-input rate. [#171723](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171723) Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- Fixes text corrections waiting until a Claude CLI task finishes instead of reaching its active turn. A running tool is not interrupted.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  Compatible text can reach the current Claude CLI turn at its next scheduling point; it does not interrupt a running tool. Images and text the current turn cannot accept still take the existing follow-up path.

  - Send compatible corrections into an active Claude CLI turn and keep a record of accepted text in the conversation; other input follows the existing follow-up path. [PR #159816](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159816), [issue #124731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/124731), [related issue #156848](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156848). Thanks to [kiranvk-2011](https://github.com/kiranvk-2011), [Shagrat2](https://github.com/Shagrat2), and [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).

  </details>

- Claude CLI now follows the MCP server choices you saved for this session on its next turn, even when the Claude process is already running.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Apply saved per-session MCP server selections on subsequent Claude CLI turns, including warm processes. [#168393](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168393), [issue #168207](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168207), [MCP guide](/tools/mcp). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@dprev](https://github.com/dprev) for the report.

  </details>

- Claude CLI can still use its permitted tools after a background command finishes, so it can continue the task instead of losing access to files or other allowed tools. Changes to session permissions still apply.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep permitted Claude and OpenClaw tools available when a background command finishes, without lifting current session restrictions. [#168670](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168670), [issue #168342](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168342), [CLI backends guide](/gateway/cli-backends). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for the report.

  </details>

- Switching away from Claude CLI and back no longer repeats the same question.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Avoid a duplicate question when switching away from Claude CLI and back ([#168965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168965)). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Plugin turns and side questions now start with the on-request approval settings you selected instead of losing them.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserve managed on-request approval settings for plugin turns and side questions so those requests can start under the configured policy. [#172639](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172639) Thanks to [@stevenlee-oai](https://github.com/stevenlee-oai).

  </details>

- A compatible Codex context refresh can continue the conversation without stopping its running preview or background command.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - A compatible Codex context refresh can continue a conversation without stopping a running preview or background command. [#177716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177716) Thanks [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen).

  </details>

- Fixes Codex refusing a correction while it saves conversation history.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Accept Codex corrections while the conversation history is being saved in [ca3798a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ca3798a38171b430162daa793425c7dd37111568).

  </details>

- Fixes one-shot Codex commands failing cleanup because another run shares their Codex process, and gives the process more time to save state before forcing it to stop. An unresponsive process can take longer to stop; check a cleanup failure rather than assuming it ended.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  [Isolated one-shot cleanup](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/142ad535c0807238acb4c03f4fe81b9d7a02519c), [clean-shutdown grace](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2eaf9aaad083a79ccac6a539307effe7ed9f1c8e) and [longer final grace](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/46db9eb756e9c8fd43cedad8e520c89b3fd9d6b1). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Codex no longer exposes its native shell when native code mode is disabled. Commands permitted through OpenClaw's own tools remain separate.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  [Native shell setting fix](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1aac7841296895394ead26b17c8e73bfe110c3ae). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Fixes a multiline correction to an ACP coding agent losing its line breaks.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserve multiline ACP steering in [#185456](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185456), fixing [#185455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/185455). Thanks to [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- [ACP replies in WebChat](/tools/acp-agents/delivery#delivery-model) now appear while the agent is working when live delivery is enabled, instead of waiting until the turn ends.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  [#182717](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182717), included through [#188730](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188730). Thanks to [@rjdotdev](https://github.com/rjdotdev), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and to [@Honghe](https://github.com/Honghe) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162072).

  Live and final text keep matching paragraph breaks. Automatic replies that are not meant for the user stay out of the live stream.

  </details>

- Fixes specific coding-agent conversations losing or duplicating history after a restart or compaction, and helps follow-ups stay with the right conversation. Recovery still depends on the agent and available saved history. If Codex still reports active work after waiting for cleanup, stop that work and retry.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep a subagent launch attached to its original request when a plugin rewrites tool arguments, reducing the risk of a duplicate launch after restart ([#185170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185170), closing [#185127](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/185127)). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - After you manually compact Claude CLI history, the next turn continues with that shortened history in a fresh process. [PR #157766](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157766), [issue #157483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157483). Thanks to [deepujain](https://github.com/deepujain) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), with the report from [von8794](https://github.com/von8794) and related evidence from [goslingmanagment](https://github.com/goslingmanagment).
  - Fixes duplicate messages in Claude CLI history when a forwarded message matches the owner's message, without losing who sent it. [#163521](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163521). Thanks to [rogeriochaves](https://github.com/rogeriochaves), [llmsommelier](https://github.com/llmsommelier) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Keeps generated setup prompts out of visible Claude CLI history and shows oversized tool output as a tool result, not something you said. [#166080](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166080), [#165041](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/165041). Thanks to [Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [IndySweeney](https://github.com/IndySweeney), [agierror](https://github.com/agierror) and [Heighte](https://github.com/Heighte).
  - After a Gateway restart, the first prompt can resume an initialized one-shot ACP conversation when the agent supports loading or resuming sessions. [#166368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166368), [#124852](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/124852). Thanks to [alkor2000](https://github.com/alkor2000), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [jhgaylor](https://github.com/jhgaylor).
  - A fork from the last completed turn no longer brings back conversation context you cleared with a newer reset. [#166660](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166660). Thanks to [Shenrui-Ma](https://github.com/Shenrui-Ma) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Fixes a long Codex conversation requesting a second compaction when Codex already shortened its own history and OpenClaw's history did not change. [#168051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168051), [#168015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168015). Thanks to [ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [robertoronderosjr](https://github.com/robertoronderosjr).
  - Hide recognized old requester instructions and duplicate imported turns from displayed chat history ([#168943](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168943)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Clarify display-only history cleanup and preserved transcripts in the [Claude CLI guide](/gateway/cli-backends) ([#168851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168851)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Keep local Claude CLI conversations' session-specific facts available without rewriting the shared instructions on every turn ([#168928](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168928)). Thanks [@alkor2000](https://github.com/alkor2000), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [@Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx). Thanks [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) for the report.
  - Wait for an earlier background task to finish cleaning up before resuming a cold conversation, without taking over its still-active thread. [#188170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188170), adapting [#187776](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187776). Thanks [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - If you know its exact ID, you can find and continue a Claude session beyond the normal browsing limit; browsing itself remains limited. [#176742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176742), [#176659](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176659) Thanks [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@ceo633](https://github.com/ceo633).
  - Claude CLI forks whose saved checkpoint was removed by compaction can use existing fresh-session recovery when authorized history and retry budget are available. [#181308](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181308), [#181254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181254) Thanks [@mushuiyu886](https://github.com/mushuiyu886) and [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored).
  - Keep earlier conversation history available for recall and memory indexing after an automatic idle or daily reset. [#184157](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184157) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@namest504](https://github.com/namest504).
  - Preserve the request prefix across thinking-level changes in supported native Codex sessions. [#182397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182397) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Refuse to reuse saved context if the current message was removed or rewritten in [777e85c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/777e85c9eece4e6dddb33c9e7ed18389a8e52ef3).
  - Resume an interrupted turn from saved input after its worker is lost ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4a73e919157bd9420376f6e198945b6a00c60405)).
  - Avoid repeating the same continuation instruction when compaction is retried ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d49faed633709262226f571dd3d4d1b9c2484d7d)).
  - Keep protected Codex follow-ups tied to a live context relay ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/368809d328482716baf1da66f8e589e5626697ee)). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- [Codex](/plugins/codex-harness) and Claude CLI setup now show more of the models and tools your installation and account can actually use, with clearer guidance when access or a plugin is missing.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Show how to enable Codex when a paired device lacks it, or choose a model served through the OpenClaw harness ([#168641](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168641)). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Make a CLI-backed subagent's requested light-context start omit workspace bootstrap files in [#169848](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169848), addressing [#169704](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169704). Thanks [@xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
  - ACP setup and the bundled router expose the existing `grok-build` agent target for users with the Grok CLI installed and authenticated. [PR #99193](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/99193), [related issue #99191](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/99191). Thanks to [TheAngryPit](https://github.com/TheAngryPit).
  - Tell Claude CLI which OpenClaw tools are registered and how to find deferred tools, including a message-delivery tool only when one is available. [PR #153032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153032), [related issues #157321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157321), [#139599](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139599), and [#147006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/147006). Thanks to [kiranvk-2011](https://github.com/kiranvk-2011) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), with related evidence from [lakemike](https://github.com/lakemike) and reports from [philknows](https://github.com/philknows), [destinedenergy](https://github.com/destinedenergy), and [wbumgardner-max](https://github.com/wbumgardner-max).
  - Find a qualifying installed Codex when starting a managed turn or showing its models ([#168228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168228)). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Discover native models on demand and retire unowned clients after 30 idle seconds, while keeping clients with active or retained work. [#168387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168387). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retire unused Codex processes after model discovery times out while allowing active work to finish. Future requests get a replacement client. [#168365](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168365), [issue #168159](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168159), [recovery guide](/plugins/codex-harness-runtime/recovery). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT) for the report and verification.
  - Let affected isolated Codex tasks and short utility requests use an available ChatGPT account route without adding an OpenAI API key. Your explicit account or utility-model choice still controls the request. [#168552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168552). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Use the account's native Claude Code model menu and effort levels for CLI selections, while retaining existing configured choices. [#168728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168728). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Generate automatic titles, conversation labels and Activity recaps with Haiku on eligible Claude CLI sessions whose main model is Opus or Sonnet, unless you chose another eligible model. [#168603](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168603). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Check native session forks against their selected runtime's capacity, using 100,000 tokens when capacity is unknown. [PR #169792](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169792). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Preserve literal underscores in imported agent names and avatar paths. [#171581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171581), thanks to [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) for the fix and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for the follow-up.
  - Let an ACP-only agent become available after a slow but healthy startup instead of requiring a Gateway restart. [#171447](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171447), thanks to [ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [potterdigital](https://github.com/potterdigital). See the [reported issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171395).
  - Let signed-in Claude CLI agents start again when an inconclusive account check had been mistaken for a logout. [#172471](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172471). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Avoided a false cleanup failure after Claude CLI stdin has already closed. [#171969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171969). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Prevented late Codex desktop watcher notifications from scheduling a restart during shutdown. [#172049](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172049). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep native session access waits within their intended elapsed-time budget when the system clock changes. [#172780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172780) Thanks to [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Codex Computer Use marketplace discovery keeps its elapsed-time allowance when the system clock changes during installation. [#178986](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178986) Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - When you explicitly enable ACP, keep its bundled acpx backend available at startup under your plugin policy. [#176876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176876), [#102205](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/102205), [#102221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/102221) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@ZOOWH](https://github.com/ZOOWH).
  - ACP agents with their own workspaces no longer fail readiness because an unused default workspace is absent. [#176879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176879) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - If Claude CLI skills plugin preparation fails, cleanup attempts to remove temporary files while showing the original failure. [#179611](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179611), [#179557](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/179557) Thanks [@xiaopan-xydt](https://github.com/xiaopan-xydt) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
