  - Preserve the remaining attempt budget when a core upgrade retains an older Codex runtime plugin. [#182995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182995) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@kip-claw](https://github.com/kip-claw), [@cjn119-ui](https://github.com/cjn119-ui), [@fray-ai](https://github.com/fray-ai) and [@sukhdeepjohar](https://github.com/sukhdeepjohar).
  - Allow ChatGPT subscription relay preparation to use the managed gateway-only proxy's default loopback settings. [#183232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183232) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@indra622](https://github.com/indra622) and [@ermaxinc](https://github.com/ermaxinc).
  - Keep the same permitted Codex tool descriptions across the main turn and its background completion, avoiding one cause of session resets without relaxing execution checks. [#183286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183286) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- More long conversations can keep going when their model runs out of room, by shortening earlier history or using a supported recovery path. A summary may leave out details, and a single oversized message may still need shortening. Eligible direct OpenAI API-key conversations now use [provider compaction](/providers/openai/advanced) by default; that compaction call can cost more than a local summary. You can turn it off in the model configuration.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - If repeated summary checks fail, safeguard compaction can continue with a best-effort summary and a warning that details may be missing. [PR #130393](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130393), [issue #120162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/120162). Thanks to [amittell](https://github.com/amittell), [alexm](https://github.com/alexm), and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Compaction retries receive whole missing identifiers where they fit, along with a count of omitted values. [PR #138415](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138415). Thanks to [amittell](https://github.com/amittell) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Dense CJK-heavy summaries can trim optional prose while retaining required requests and identifiers when those facts fit. [PR #138416](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138416), [related issue #119272](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/119272). Thanks to [amittell](https://github.com/amittell) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Requests assembled by compaction-owning context engines are checked for overflow before provider submission and routed to recovery when needed. [PR #139999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139999), [issue #139961](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139961). Thanks to [Phillis](https://github.com/Phillis) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The immediate estimate after OpenAI server compaction includes the foreground system prompt and tool costs. [PR #141341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141341), [issue #141340](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141340). Thanks to [ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - A supported long Chat Completions turn can shorten its history and continue when its output room runs out, without sending an incomplete reply or rerunning completed tools. Its live preview may pause while the reply is buffered. [PR #143840](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143840). Thanks to [forrystudio](https://github.com/forrystudio) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

[PR #150920](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150920), [related issue #81925](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/81925). Thanks to [amittell](https://github.com/amittell) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Native Ollama subagent spawns can reuse more of a stable prompt prefix. Results depend on the model template, cache slots and unchanged instructions. [#164804](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164804), [#166271](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166271). Thanks to [aaa2015](https://github.com/aaa2015), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [xyrolle](https://github.com/xyrolle).

  - Claude conversation checkpoints preserve opportunities for reuse after large documents, images and tool exchanges. Bedrock checkpoint eligibility and lifetimes follow model and profile support. [#168042](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168042). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Eligible direct OpenAI Responses and Anthropic API-key conversations now use provider compaction by default, with existing opt-outs and local recovery. An OpenAI provider-compaction call can cost more than a local summary, even if later turns reuse more cached context ([#168164](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168164)). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

Some older context may leave the model's view sooner ([#168157](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168157), [#159799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159799)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@arthur-drozdov](https://github.com/arthur-drozdov) and [@rsdancey](https://github.com/rsdancey).

Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT) for the report and verification.

Thanks [@TourAround](https://github.com/TourAround) for the report.

Thanks to [TheAngryPit](https://github.com/TheAngryPit) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Shorten a ChatGPT-signed-in conversation earlier, leaving more room for the next large message or tool result. [PR #169755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169755). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Continue eligible direct OpenAI API-key sessions with the provider's retained messages after compaction and restart. Invalid or oversized windows still use the existing fallback. [PR #169771](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169771). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  - Avoid repeating runtime context after every tool result in an OpenAI Responses turn. Existing duplicates remain until compaction. [PR #169632](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169632). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - Keep WebChat tool definitions and retained Responses replay IDs stable across turns and history pruning to support prompt-cache reuse. [PR #170050](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170050). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Read-only forks retain completed parent answers and tool results. [#170973](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170973). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prepared fork expiry no longer retains completed agent turns in memory unnecessarily. [#171012](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171012), following separate retention work in [#170855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170855). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

[#171134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171134), related to [#171085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171085). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@dustytext-bot](https://github.com/dustytext-bot).

Incognito turns with incompatible creation settings are refused while their history is preserved. - Fixes replies on affected model streams that repeated the opening, lost answer text after reasoning, or showed tool-call markup instead of a readable answer.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Prevent duplicated opening text in affected OpenAI Responses and managed Completions streams. [#172653](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172653) Thanks to [@Richard355168](https://github.com/Richard355168) for the fix and [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/136262), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for investigation and verification, and [@Gavin-ifjc](https://github.com/Gavin-ifjc) and [@Lamer217](https://github.com/Lamer217) for related reports.

  - Preserve valid multiline Google Interactions stream events and report malformed event JSON instead of silently dropping content. [#173612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173612) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Enabled live previews receive answer text after reasoning from OpenAI-compatible Chat Completions streams. Thanks to [leapdragon](https://github.com/leapdragon) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Hide recognized DeepSeek-format tool markup from replies and recover valid tool calls in managed turns. Direct calls and tool-free finalization filter the markup without executing it. [PR #169728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169728), [issue #159665](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159665). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [TOMUIV](https://github.com/TOMUIV).

- If you have [fallback models configured](/concepts/model-failover#model-fallback), a reply can switch to another eligible model after a safe-to-retry timeout, exhausted context recovery or an explicit organization token-quota error. A longer fallback chain can take longer overall; billing and sign-in problems still need their own remedies.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - A safe-to-retry reply that times out on the first model can try your configured fallback, with a separate time allowance for each model. [PR #141869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141869), [issue #141838](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141838), [related issue #103697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/103697). Thanks to [von8794](https://github.com/von8794), [alkor2000](https://github.com/alkor2000), and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - OpenRouter, Kilo Gateway, and other enabled providers that manage their own transient availability can be retried after upstream recovery. Credential, billing, and subscription blocks remain enforced. [PR #141825](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141825). Thanks to [clement-paradex](https://github.com/clement-paradex) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Repeated unchanged usage counters no longer keep a stalled Chat Completions request alive, while advancing token counts still indicate progress. [PR #145850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145850), [related issue #145203](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/145203). Thanks to [RileyJJY](https://github.com/RileyJJY) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with investigation by [anyech](https://github.com/anyech) and the report from [elharonyclaw](https://github.com/elharonyclaw).
  - Give each eligible retry or fallback its own recovery allowance, so a queued request may wait longer for the current reply to finish. [#164808](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164808), [#166497](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166497), [#165556](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/165556), [#145203](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/145203), [#133330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133330). Thanks to [anyech](https://github.com/anyech), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [vinsew](https://github.com/vinsew), [belbix](https://github.com/belbix), [tre1d](https://github.com/tre1d) and [aleps001](https://github.com/aleps001).
  - An unfinished ChatGPT Responses turn can recover from a normal connection close using the existing bounded recovery policy. [#167512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167512), [#167345](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167345). Thanks to [ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), reporter [mightyx](https://github.com/mightyx) and [aleps001](https://github.com/aleps001).
  - Continue an eligible model retry when an optional retry-status callback fails ([#169141](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169141)). Thanks [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng).
  - After shortening history still cannot fit a reply on the current model, try an eligible configured fallback without bypassing model locks or replay restrictions. [PR #170141](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170141), [issue #9986](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/9986). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [amirarenco-create](https://github.com/amirarenco-create), [KarimVKarmali](https://github.com/KarimVKarmali), [engagepy](https://github.com/engagepy) and [zhupeng0o0](https://github.com/zhupeng0o0).
  - Configured fallback can handle provider-wrapped transient errors such as 503 or 429 in text-only responses. [#170527](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170527), thanks to [@TUARAN](https://github.com/TUARAN), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@diegoln](https://github.com/diegoln) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170388).
  - An unclassified empty normal completion no longer consumes the model fallback chain after same-model recovery. [#171199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171199). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reset the rate-limit retry budget after a completed model response, and explain when automatic retries are exhausted. [#171760](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171760), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recognized SiliconFlow prompt-limit errors enter the existing compaction and retry path. [#181519](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181519), [#181518](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181518) Thanks [@xpeng5278-web](https://github.com/xpeng5278-web) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recognized vLLM context-limit errors enter the existing compaction and retry path or provide overflow guidance when recovery cannot make the prompt fit. [#181521](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181521), [#181520](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181520) Thanks [@xpeng5278-web](https://github.com/xpeng5278-web).
  - Apply Ollama web search's configured result count and per-attempt timeout. An explicit search count takes precedence. [#182052](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182052) Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino).
  - Align tool-call and result names when replaying provider-prefixed transcript entries to native Ollama. [#181549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181549) Thanks [@xpeng5278-web](https://github.com/xpeng5278-web).
  - Apply provider-specific thinking controls to Kimi K3.1 Pro and Highspeed. The direct Moonshot route also handles native video and reasoning replay. Kimi Code cannot turn thinking off. [#181923](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181923) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Avoid a false model-refresh warning when the optional Claude CLI is absent, and give installation guidance when its authentication is explicitly selected. [#182729](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182729) Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Shorten oversized Claude CLI option descriptions instead of rejecting the entire question set. Oversized question text and labels still fail. [#182938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182938) Thanks [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A temporary agent run no longer removes the default local llama.cpp model needed by a later memory search. [#182781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182781) Thanks [@rjdotdev](https://github.com/rjdotdev), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@kir1ll](https://github.com/kir1ll).
  - Retry smaller embedding batches for the specific Mistral aggregate-token-limit response. A single input that still exceeds the limit remains an error. [#183567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183567) Thanks [@lonexreb](https://github.com/lonexreb) and [@drewjocham](https://github.com/drewjocham).
  - Leave fully redacted HTTP 400 and 422 provider errors unclassified instead of incorrectly labeling them as request-format errors. [#183006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183006) Thanks [@amittell](https://github.com/amittell), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prevent part of a MiniMax tool argument from appearing in the visible reply when it contains a closing tag. [#183413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183413) Thanks [@gdpu11](https://github.com/gdpu11) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid Gateway stalls when assistant text resembles an incomplete or malformed tool call. [#183252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183252) Thanks [@LeizhenZhang](https://github.com/LeizhenZhang) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - When a provider explicitly says the organization's token quota is exhausted, move a safe-to-retry turn to an available configured fallback instead of repeating the same model. Other rate limits keep their usual retries. [#171873](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171873), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Switching models now keeps your requested thinking level, using a supported level for the current model and restoring your preference when you switch back. If an earlier switch already overwrote it, set it again once.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Self-hosted Kimi K3 thinking selections reach the supported reasoning effort while retaining the existing Off default. [PR #139980](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139980), [unrelated issue reference #139040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139040). Thanks to [jsf9789](https://github.com/jsf9789) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Selected Qwen chat-template reasoning levels reach backends that declare supported efforts, while unselected turns keep the server default. [PR #148397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148397). Thanks to [areslp](https://github.com/areslp), [hippoley](https://github.com/hippoley), and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Configured `top_p` sampling values reach supported providers, including zero. When both spellings remain, `topP` takes precedence. [PR #148538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148538), [issue #127561](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127561). Thanks to [Raymondriter](https://github.com/Raymondriter) and reporter [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Copilot now uses the matching account's reported context capacity when available instead of a guessed number, while respecting configured caps. Refresh the model list to replace an older cached estimate. [PR #156636](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156636), [related issue #156635](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156635). Thanks to [saju01](https://github.com/saju01) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), with design advice from [snotty](https://github.com/snotty), related reproduction from [vinaycodeaxe](https://github.com/vinaycodeaxe), and prior groundwork by [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) and [Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx).
  - An eligible MiniMax account can choose the M3.1 Flash Preview model and its five supported reasoning levels; OpenClaw no longer sends that model an unsupported thinking-off choice. [PR #160089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160089), [issues #160037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160037) and [#160491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160491). Thanks to [NianJiuZst](https://github.com/NianJiuZst), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [Takhoffman](https://github.com/Takhoffman), and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), with provider evidence from [php737](https://github.com/php737) and reports and investigation from [ns-spec](https://github.com/ns-spec), [Hansen1018](https://github.com/Hansen1018), and [olveww-dot](https://github.com/olveww-dot).
  - Select `openai/gpt-6-luna` for typed evaluations. ChatGPT and Codex subscription sign-in do not authorize this Platform API route. [#166320](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166320). Thanks to [jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [Kimiyu-186](https://github.com/Kimiyu-186) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - llama.cpp discovery honors the existing private-network opt-in for link-local servers. Enable it only for trusted endpoints because it also permits metadata addresses. [#163634](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163634), [#160389](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/160389). Thanks to [harshitgavita-07](https://github.com/harshitgavita-07), [davidgrinc](https://github.com/davidgrinc) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Direct Z.AI GLM-5.3 and GLM-5.3-Flash calls apply the existing reasoning policy when thinking is omitted or set to off. [#164407](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164407), [#164328](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164328). Thanks to [Ayushdevo](https://github.com/Ayushdevo), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [nina-grech](https://github.com/nina-grech).
  - Map native Ollama thinking controls to each model's advertised values, including XHigh when supported ([#168166](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168166), [#157762](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157762)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Agent commands, replies and scheduled runs now offer the thinking levels supported by their selected provider while respecting your explicit choice. [#168566](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168566). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Pass configured context caps to Codex catalog-model threads on start and resume ([#168986](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168986)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the thinking level you requested across model switches and show when the current model uses a lower supported level. [#169435](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169435), [issue #87925](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/87925). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hoobnn](https://github.com/hoobnn) and [@amittell](https://github.com/amittell).
  - Keeps a selected Codex Ultrafast tier across resumed and follow-up ChatGPT-authenticated turns when the account's model catalog supports it. [#169265](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169265), [issue #169264](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169264). Thanks [@SanderHelgesen](https://github.com/SanderHelgesen).
  - Preserves prior thinking-effort changes when OpenAI Responses streaming requests overlap in a conversation. [#169322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169322) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retains native Ollama reasoning across tool continuations and reopened sessions. Retained reasoning uses context and remains subject to compaction. [#169312](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169312), [related issue #147171](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/147171). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@wpu911](https://github.com/wpu911).
  - If your custom provider maps a model alias to the same served model, you can mark that configured row as equivalent to avoid a misleading model-change notice. Real fallback changes still appear. [PR #170009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170009), [issue #170008](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170008). Thanks to [xyrolle](https://github.com/xyrolle) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Avoid false model-route change notices for equivalent or dated OpenAI response IDs. [PR #169977](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169977), [issue #169976](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169976). Thanks to [xyrolle](https://github.com/xyrolle), [Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

[PR #169686](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169686). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Restore `/think off` for eligible native Claude CLI models without an Anthropic API key. Models requiring adaptive thinking continue to omit Off. [PR #169657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169657), [issue #169641](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169641). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Show the explicit Claude Code model name in the picker when the default alias resolves to that model. [PR #169670](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169670). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Update MiMo and Mistral recommendation order and include Muse Wonder in recommendations when a provider serves it. [PR #170106](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170106). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - OpenClaw uses a 260,000-token context cap for Ultrafast client and server compaction paths, respecting smaller configured limits. [#170844](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170844), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Standard, Fast and Ultrafast choices override an inherited speed tier where the model, account and route support them. The current request keeps its original choice. Keyboard navigation also reaches nested hovercard content. [#171783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171783), thanks to [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Allowed automatic child-session model selections are kept when stale routing is repaired. [#183728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183728) Thanks [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks [@Iolaum](https://github.com/Iolaum) for the report.
  - Recognize self-hosted reasoning aliases and make `/think off` work with underscore-prefixed Nemotron aliases. An explicit reasoning opt-out still applies. [#122022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/122022), [reported in #121597](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/121597). Thanks to [krell11](https://github.com/krell11) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Typed level directives recognize existing digit and underscore aliases, including `/verbose 1` and `/think extra_high`. [Typed level aliases](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168470), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168254). Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi), [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Deprecated-model errors use configured fallback or guide you to choose another model instead of suggesting a retry. [Deprecated model guidance](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168187). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Fixes a personal-account reply stopping during quota or credential refresh because an optional saved field was represented differently. See [#170065](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170065) and [#169850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169850). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@modlethub](https://github.com/modlethub) and [@Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx).
  - Personal Codex requests avoid an extra blocking credential read but still check account access before sending; a reconnect or account-profile merge can invalidate a prepared request. [PR #170330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170330). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve the selected Codex account configuration for online `status --usage --json`, avoiding a misleading generic expiration result ([#170692](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170692)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Stop retrying a request that has been withdrawn or permanently rejected, while keeping recovery for temporary errors and recoverable context limits ([#170925](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170925)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

Thanks to [xpeng5278-web](https://github.com/xpeng5278-web). See [#171673](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171673).
  - Async tool calls preserve reasoning replay context for later OpenAI Responses requests. [#171763](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171763) follows [#171431](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171431). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - DashScope and Model Studio routes use registered Qwen quota hooks. A custom native URL alone does not guarantee Qwen activation from a cold start. [#171750](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171750) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). The separate [input-length report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171673) from [xpeng5278-web](https://github.com/xpeng5278-web) is addressed by [#171712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171712).
  - If OpenClaw cannot safely add a turn to the conversation history, it stops that turn instead of switching models as though the provider failed. [#177956](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177956). Thanks [@hootrock](https://github.com/hootrock), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@hussont](https://github.com/hussont).
  - Preserve person-linked model account selections across session resets and rollovers ([#179433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179433), [#179432](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/179432)). Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino).
  - An isolated task can still choose from the models discovered for its agent. [#182952](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182952) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Settle fallback notices against the model admitted for the session after a successful reply. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b68da67ec7594e50734cb3333b2f3cf9707b8659), thanks to [joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).

  </details>

- You can sign in to supported providers from the terminal while your local Gateway keeps running, and saved credentials are more reliably reflected in the available-model list. Older Gateways and unsupported sign-in options still need the documented offline or update path.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Startup cleanup logs and skips an unreadable managed GitHub profile directory while continuing to clean up other eligible retired profiles. [PR #171226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171226), [issue #171035](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171035). Thanks [fhgffy](https://github.com/fhgffy) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Resolve identity before the first self-profile read ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/466573573374954076729309c765ba457505ba57)).
  - MiniMax Portal OAuth renews access tokens through their issuing region. [PR #160459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160459), [issue #77467](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/77467). Thanks to [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), with reports from [rongtianhua](https://github.com/rongtianhua) and [mychataa](https://github.com/mychataa).
  - ChatGPT device-code sign-in keeps its polling timeout through system clock changes. [#162828](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162828). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - OAuth refresh waits retain their timeout budget when the system clock changes. [#167619](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167619). Thanks to [sunnyshu0925](https://github.com/sunnyshu0925) and [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Save a supported provider API key through your running local Gateway and see its models without restarting. An explicit profile ID and unsupported setup paths still require offline setup. [#168588](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168588). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Complete supported provider sign-in in the terminal without stopping your local Gateway. Unsupported options still need offline setup; an older Gateway needs updating and restarting first. [#168671](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168671). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Resolve saved API keys correctly during provider model discovery so model lists can populate after sign-in. [#168617](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168617). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Use the configured managed proxy for a standalone `openclaw models list --refresh`. [#168554](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168554). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Make models found by an offline refresh available to a separate local agent turn while their provider credentials remain valid. [#168618](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168618). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reject the local-only `ollama-local` placeholder during Ollama Cloud setup and point to the hosted key page. Local Ollama setup is unchanged. [#168652](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168652). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Allow shared OAuth credentials to refresh during model discovery when historical or empty agent databases exist ([#168957](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168957)). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Lets a newly saved API key be tried on the next request after the old key hit a rate limit. [#169528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169528), [issue #151354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/151354). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [@blance2599](https://github.com/blance2599).
  - Fixes local Codex model discovery with an environment OpenAI API key in an isolated agent home without a selected profile. [#169350](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169350) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An explicit model authentication refresh reflects removed saved credentials. [#170201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170201), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Claude CLI authentication checks allow up to ten seconds and distinguish a timeout from a confirmed logout. [#170604](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170604), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@veritasaiflows-cell](https://github.com/veritasaiflows-cell).
  - Eligible account-only Codex models retain their subscription-backed route and OAuth profile during native model selection. [#170639](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170639), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Model Setup guides device sign-in and configures the selected agent for Muse Code. [#171526](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171526), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Provider Access offers the same sign-in while leaving the active model in place, so you can choose a Muse model separately. [#171790](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171790), thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Keep recommendations for currently served models visible after upgrades, even when older catalog rows and prices cannot be used. [#172622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172622) Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - A model-list update for one agent no longer interrupts another agent's pending model picker; operators also get a counter for model-list requests. [#172514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172514) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - If Sign in with ChatGPT expires, renew the credential and try your selected model once more before switching to a fallback. [#172593](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172593) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@vinsew](https://github.com/vinsew) for the [expiry](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172562) and [recovery](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172563) reports.
  - Reuse provider ownership lookups within each model registry. [#172481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172481) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore automatic conversation titles for custom Zhipu and Z.AI GLM endpoints, with a clearer warning when a title cannot be generated. [#172580](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172580) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@TommyLei666](https://github.com/TommyLei666) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171068).
  - Preserve valid `__proto__` keys in cloned Mistral tool schemas. [#172587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172587) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fix OpenRouter setup incorrectly rejecting valid keys or saved models because of a model identity mismatch. [#173292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173292) Thanks to [@chandraveshchaudhari](https://github.com/chandraveshchaudhari) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Recover valid DeepSeek tool requests in managed sessions through OpenRouter and compatible routes without exposing their markup or duplicating normal tool calls. Direct mode filters the markup without executing recovered calls. [#175058](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175058) Thanks to [@Linux2010](https://github.com/Linux2010), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@ZhimingQ](https://github.com/ZhimingQ) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174909).
  - Affected OpenAI models through GMI can use tools without the incompatible reasoning request. For those tool requests, enabled reasoning is automatically disabled or omitted. [#176709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176709) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - If you pinned an agent to a usable account, the model list no longer reports that account as missing. [#178522](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178522), [#178502](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/178502), [#178549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/178549) Thanks [@ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Admitted embedded and CLI agent turns retain their prepared model alias guidance during a configuration refresh. [#179192](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179192) Thanks [@ensignwesley](https://github.com/ensignwesley) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - OpenAI-compatible model retrieval accepts existing agent aliases and returns the canonical model object. [#180057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180057) Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - A billing failure now points you to account credit and usage limits instead of telling you to wait out a rate limit. [#180867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180867), [#180856](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180856) Thanks [@eliavb43](https://github.com/eliavb43).
  - Known OpenAI models without an explicitly configured custom route can use their official routes when stale catalog metadata would otherwise block credentials. [#181356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181356), [#181353](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181353) Thanks [@sasan1200](https://github.com/sasan1200).

  - Keep provider sign-in status visible while settings change, then refresh it when new account information arrives. [#171579](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171579), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- [Model pickers](/concepts/models#choose-a-model-for-a-session) make supported choices easier to find and show more useful errors when a configured model is missing. A catalog change does not replace a model you pinned earlier.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - `openclaw models auth list` now shows why a subscription account is blocked, when it expires and which account source and scope apply, in both text and JSON. [PR #133087](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133087), [issue #133065](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133065). Thanks to [Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Expanding a model catalog to additional providers can reuse unchanged plugin registrations and avoid repeated preparation work. [PR #162226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162226), [issues #161379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161379) and [#162230](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162230). Thanks to [netcrazed](https://github.com/netcrazed) and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), with verification by [kiralee](https://github.com/kiralee).
  - Copilot setup checks and Ask OpenClaw now work when you explicitly select a Copilot model. After replacing the packaged runtime, restart and verify setup again. [#163578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163578), [#163406](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/163406). Thanks to [lifuyue](https://github.com/lifuyue), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and reporter [tjegbejimba](https://github.com/tjegbejimba).
  - Model alias and fallback additions reject known-invalid provider names before saving when the provider inventory is available. When it cannot be checked, the selection saves with a warning. [#165744](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165744). Thanks to [Sedrak-Hovhannisyan](https://github.com/Sedrak-Hovhannisyan) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preparing model lists for large configured provider catalogs avoids repeated reference parsing. [#166223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166223). Thanks to [fede-kamel](https://github.com/fede-kamel) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - See Ollama Cloud usage in chat `/status` and `openclaw status --usage` when your configured, signed-in server runs Ollama 0.40.1 or newer. [#167474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167474). Thanks to [usimic](https://github.com/usimic) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - `openclaw infer model run` reports failed local checks and failed, timed-out or cancelled Gateway checks as failures even when partial text arrived. [#167874](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167874). Thanks to [ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Default Codex chats can use a ChatGPT-signed-in model listed by your account even if it is not yet in OpenClaw's built-in model list. [#168033](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168033). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Refresh LM Studio models with `openclaw models list --provider lmstudio --refresh`. Merge mode preserves configured rows and metadata; explicit replace mode keeps only configured models. [#168039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168039), [#150262](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/150262). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [jb-lopez](https://github.com/jb-lopez).
  - LM Studio models get more time for a cold start, and a failed capacity upgrade now gives recovery guidance. You can adjust the timeout using the [LM Studio guide](/providers/lmstudio). [#168044](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168044), [#141357](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141357). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - New Ollama chat choices leave out models meant only for embeddings. If you already saved one or a memory model is missing, the error explains how to choose or download the right model. Re-onboarding replaces your provider list, so save custom entries first ([#168075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168075)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Honor selected llama.cpp model aliases and profile-qualified references, and report credential-save failures during existing-server setup ([#168232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168232)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Refresh loaded llama.cpp router models with verified context limits and tool support while preserving explicit pins ([#168309](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168309), [#139802](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139802)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@guarismo](https://github.com/guarismo).
  - Show a healthy managed llama.cpp embedding server as ready even when its optional metrics endpoint is unavailable ([#168204](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168204)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - When you remove a configured provider with its own URL, its matching models disappear from the live picker. Models left by earlier removals are not cleaned up automatically. [#168724](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168724). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reduce synchronous work in personal model lists after a model configuration reload. [#168733](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168733). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Show account-listed OpenCode Zen models even when public metadata is missing, and explain client-restricted free-tier refusals without placing the shared credential in cooldown. Listing a model does not guarantee access. [#168601](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168601). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Choose an advertised GitHub Copilot model API when endpoint paths have a version prefix or trailing slash. [#168672](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168672). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Scheduled and chat turns no longer fail just because their model list updated during startup. [#168410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168410). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Repair configurations using the `https://ollama.com` origin with `openclaw doctor --fix` or update repair, including saved session and scheduled-job routes ([#168782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168782)). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve fallback eligibility and later default changes when selecting the agent's effective default model ([#169030](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169030)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Identify enabled external official Codex plugins older than 2026.9.7 before registration and show repair guidance. Plugin update failures also show their reason ([#168930](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168930)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks [@ezimerman](https://github.com/ezimerman) and [@ADLBAS26](https://github.com/ADLBAS26) for the reports. For an official npm installation, run `openclaw update repair` or `openclaw plugins update codex` and restart the Gateway. For a linked-source installation, update the source or remove the load-path override.
  - Reduce model availability lookup work for large native catalogs and the model picker ([#169048](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169048)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh setup checks available inference options and activates a verified model, while keeping an existing configured model. Codex access can use a shared home or a separate login. [#169628](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169628) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - AI choices can appear sooner during macOS, Control UI and CLI setup. [#169562](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169562) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - If model discovery fails, the picker now shows the reported error beside Retry and keeps your saved choices available. [#169316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169316) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep model browsing available while a refreshed catalog replaces the old one, without a brief picker failure. [#169478](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169478) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Show more of your configured fallback models and tell you how many will be removed before clearing the chain. [#169511](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169511) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Fixes a model supplied by an installed plugin failing when you open its session outside that agent's workspace. [#169539](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169539), [issue #169205](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169205). Thanks [@ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@AVCarp](https://github.com/AVCarp).
  - Restores the requested configuration for installed native ESM provider plugins after a failed catalog refresh. [#169321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169321), [related #169272](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169272). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - If a model-list change refuses a request before the turn starts, tell the client that it is safe to retry. [#169623](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169623) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fixes switching to an OpenAI model without the Codex plugin when the built-in runtime can serve it. An explicit Codex runtime selection still requires the plugin. [#169412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169412) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - If a successfully queried Ollama, LM Studio or supported OpenAI-compatible server does not list a model you configured, model diagnostics now suggest checking its name or downloading it. An offline server cannot confirm this, and custom models remain selectable. [PR #169708](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169708), [issue #39811](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/39811). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [LindalyX-Lee](https://github.com/LindalyX-Lee), [dsantoreis](https://github.com/dsantoreis) and [exisz](https://github.com/exisz).
  - A healthy agent keeps its commands and model choices during a reload even if another agent's model provider is unavailable. [#170805](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170805), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Models with invalid pricing are skipped with a warning while valid siblings remain available. [#170882](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170882), related to [#170458](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170458). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Managed Codex version checks avoid a pipe race that could leave Doctor without JSON output. [#171248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171248), reported in [#171169](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171169). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@dtump](https://github.com/dtump), and [@joshpetras](https://github.com/joshpetras).
  - The NVIDIA guide shows how to pair a text-only Nemotron primary model with a separate Super VL image-understanding model. [#171072](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171072). Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson) for confirming the model reference.
  - Removed unavailable bundled models and updated Cerebras, Together, DeepInfra and OpenCode defaults. The retired Groq model's oversized-request retry was also removed. [#171879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171879), with related [hosted catalog work](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167855) and [Doctor model-choice discussion](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169473). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Updated Google setup, Gemini aliases and model selection, including core Gemini 4 shorthand normalization. [#172339](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172339), following [Gemini 4 Argon support](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171852). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Set the default endpoint for key-only Qwen Coding Plan requests. [#172447](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172447). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - NVIDIA setup and fallback choices now offer Nemotron 3.5 Super VL in place of Nemotron 3 Super, with its declared video input support. Nemotron 3 Ultra stays the default. [#172449](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172449). Thanks [@nv-kasikritc](https://github.com/nv-kasikritc) and [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
  - Moved Kimi K3 immediately after GPT-6 Luna in the recommended-model list. [#172461](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172461). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Reduced repeated readiness work when listing large native model catalogs. [#172311](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172311). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Model choices for other agents stay visible in the Control UI when one agent's catalog changes. [#172418](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172418). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Added explicit Imagine Image 2.0 selection and errors for unknown bare image-model overrides. [#172445](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172445). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@JFLeonM](https://github.com/JFLeonM) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141547).

  </details>

- Fixes supported provider routes rejecting otherwise valid tool history, settings or follow-up turns. The affected routes keep their own model and account limits.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - OpenAI and Azure Responses output requests respect declared model limits. [PR #128282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128282), [issue #127560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127560). Thanks to [parker-fawcett](https://github.com/parker-fawcett) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), with the report from [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A conversation that used tools can continue on a managed Chat Completions model without tool support; earlier tool activity is passed along as text, not executable calls. [PR #148106](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148106). Thanks to [jimmy00415](https://github.com/jimmy00415) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep provider-specific request settings when a plugin hook replaces the outgoing request, whether the hook finishes immediately or later. [PR #160567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160567). Thanks to [YangManBOBO](https://github.com/YangManBOBO) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Send runtime context to compatible Responses servers using a user-role message they can accept. [#168546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168546), [issue #164791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164791), [provider roles guide](/concepts/context#runtime-context-and-provider-roles). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@flanker-stack](https://github.com/flanker-stack) for the report and investigation.
  - Let a model request try IPv6 if IPv4 fails when Node's address-family selection is enabled ([#168973](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168973)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@an2in](https://github.com/an2in), [@georgenaz](https://github.com/georgenaz), [@motookoyama](https://github.com/motookoyama), and [@orthogonalistic](https://github.com/orthogonalistic).
  - If you enable cache-age-based history trimming, apply it to eligible OpenRouter Grok and Gemini 2.5/3 conversations too. It remains off by default for them. [#169548](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169548), [issue #169133](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169133). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@gregb100](https://github.com/gregb100).
  - Responses tool continuations and transcript reloads avoid spurious assistant-history mismatch warnings. [#170901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170901). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Corrected Groq Qwen thinking and reasoning output, and marked the Groq and Cohere models as text-only. [#171914](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171914). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fixes Ollama requests failing when OpenClaw runs as an in-process Gateway from source. [#172013](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172013). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reduced repeated Ultrafast rejections within a run while retaining the saved preference. [#172315](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172315), following [tier selection work](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165990). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fixed thinking-off requests for Baseten DeepSeek V4 Pro 0813. [#172330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172330). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserved OpenAI Responses reasoning prefixes across session reopen and completed async calls across interrupted streams. [#172455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172455). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fixes Moonshot and Kimi rejecting some MCP tool definitions that omit a supported type in a choice of input shapes. [#172478](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172478). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@rmoskal](https://github.com/rmoskal) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/113130).

  </details>

- [Claude ACP sessions](/tools/acp-agents) now use an updated Claude Code binary that adds a remediation for the brotli decoder vulnerability.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
