  - The Claude ACP adapter is updated to 0.95.0, with Claude Agent SDK 0.3.309 and the corrected decoder. [#182664](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182664). Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).

  </details>

- Adding a working [model provider](/concepts/model-providers/control-ui-and-keys#configure-providers-in-the-control-ui) no longer shows another provider's discovery failure as a problem with the key you just saved. Discovery warnings name the unavailable provider when it is known.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  [#188541](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188541). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Fixes API-key setup for a new agent failing when its database is created during setup.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  [#175656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175656), adapted for the release in [#188842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188842). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the original repair and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) for the adaptation.

  </details>

- Fixes local ONNX model checks hanging on a pipe used as a model file or manifest; ordinary files and links to them still work.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - ONNX verification rejects pipe-backed manifests and model artifacts without hanging. Regular files and links to them remain supported. [#183621](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183621) Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Fixes a pinned Codex conversation failing before its next turn starts.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Restore turn preparation for pinned native Codex sessions after their writer is claimed ([#169101](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169101)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Fixes reasoning-capable utility models returning no visible title, observer digest or recap when their output budget was too small.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Gives reasoning-capable utility models room to produce visible titles, observer digests and recaps within the configured and model limits. [#169331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169331), [issue #153391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153391). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@dwalthour](https://github.com/dwalthour), [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- A history-free run that reaches its output limit now stops rather than sending the same request again.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - History-free runs stop at an output limit without replaying their request, and model-call logs carry correlated timing and completion information. [#170981](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170981), following [#161082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161082). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Shortening a conversation now keeps each tool result paired with the call that produced it.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preparation diagnostics distinguish request encoding without exposing prompt content, and context-engine input and output keep tool calls paired within the history limit. [#170830](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170830), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

### Tools, browsing and permissions

- If someone's access to a shared session is revoked while a tool is waiting, that pending tool checks access again before it runs. A dashboard save admitted before revocation may still finish.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Check current membership before a pending tool executes. [#170539](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170539) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Save dashboards backed by another database without blocking the Gateway during the write, after checking access to the source session. [#170541](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170541) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- A plugin can no longer change the arguments of a trusted-approved inline tool call under the original approval. Its changed call needs separate approval, so plugins that rewrite those calls may need an update; other file and tool fixes here cover specific invalid inputs, not every permission path.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Require separate approval when a plugin changes the arguments of a trusted-approved inline tool call. Transforming plugins must request approval for their final arguments, or the call stops. This applies to the inline path, not every deferred approval route, [#142700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142700) and [reported issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/152465). Thanks to [giodl73-repo](https://github.com/giodl73-repo) and [giodl73](https://github.com/giodl73).
  - Bounded thinking allowance for exec approval reviewers, [#150200](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150200). Thanks to [leapdragon](https://github.com/leapdragon) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve valid union values during argument repair, [#158974](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158974). Thanks to [Shenrui-Ma](https://github.com/Shenrui-Ma) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep tools available when literal schema examples or defaults contain dynamic-reference keys, [#160542](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160542). Thanks to [Shenrui-Ma](https://github.com/Shenrui-Ma).
  - Keep deeply nested tool schemas callable with a 128-level validation boundary, [#164836](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164836), with reports [#141306](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141306), [#153700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153700), [#153701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153701), [#153713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153713) and [#153743](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/153743). Thanks to [RXQ6](https://github.com/RXQ6), [qxr666-sga](https://github.com/qxr666-sga) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject overlapping agent edit and Skill Workshop patch matches, [#168125](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168125). Thanks to [yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Generic agent node invocations cannot bypass a denied browser tool through browser proxy commands. [#179880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179880). Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - Unsafe `test` and `[` predicates no longer pass automatically as safe builtins in POSIX exec allowlist mode. [#180880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180880). Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - Reject malformed UTF-8 secrets plans before applying them, [#167379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167379). Thanks to [Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Prevent redundant preparation of existing stores from revoking an active tool caller's authority in [#170658](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170658). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - `agent --message-file` now reads the exact path you typed, including spaces at its ends, rather than a nearby file. Only an exact `-` selects stdin for `agent exec`. [PR #171585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171585). Thanks [Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Browser fill now rejects a pipe or other special file as input instead of hanging; regular files and links to them still work. [#183420](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183420). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Completion installation and profile inspection reject pipes and other special-file profiles instead of hanging. [#181763](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181763). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep the transcript-reading tool available on turns without a caller, while still checking who may use it ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d499b183f2938c5ad3b3f6f0c79402863ee695c5)).
  - A multi-file `apply_patch` now catches predictable patch mismatches before writing to any of its files. [PR #160409](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160409), [issues #117742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/117742) and [#127496](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127496). Thanks to [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), with reports from [yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Denied filesystem glob checks in the experimental opt-in Codex sandbox avoid stalling the Gateway on crafted patterns. [#167296](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167296), [#166684](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166684). Thanks to [xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee) and [Takhoffman](https://github.com/Takhoffman).
  - When workspace-only file access is disabled, accept active session workspace images in Codex steering and reject sibling or shared workspace images in the covered cases. [#181528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181528) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Recheck a plain host assertion before embedded prompt submission when the host may have become inactive in [1fe1e63](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1fe1e63fe00d3cdb8da09920146cf273b8b4e2f8).
  - The runtime runs on the Gateway host and refuses policies it cannot enforce. [#170487](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170487), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Set `plugins.entries.agentsapi.config.forwarding.gatewayTools` or `forwarding.mcpServers` to `false` to disable the corresponding forwarding path. [#170728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170728), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- [Code Mode](/tools/code-mode/quickstart) can use browser observations and gives more useful errors when a wait, command or worker fails. Check whether an action already happened before retrying a failed wait.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Wrapped Browser observations and snapshot references in Code Mode, [#164833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164833) and [#164641](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164641). Thanks to [alkor2000](https://github.com/alkor2000) and reporter [potterdigital](https://github.com/potterdigital).
  - Recovery guidance for invalid Code Mode waits, [#128886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128886), [#128859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128859) and [#167610](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167610). Thanks to [sashankh](https://github.com/sashankh), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc), [shad0wca7](https://github.com/shad0wca7), [potterdigital](https://github.com/potterdigital) and [benprotzekmuntoni](https://github.com/benprotzekmuntoni).
  - Enabled-tool guidance when Code Mode rejects module access, [#158716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158716) and [#158675](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/158675). Thanks to [sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313), [pennymolt](https://github.com/pennymolt) and [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Reuse prepared catalog data across repeated Node Code Mode calls in [#169045](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169045). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Release closed-database metadata that could exhaust Code Mode worker memory in [#169956](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169956). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Keep control tool declarations stable across catalog changes in [#170282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170282), addressing [#170233](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170233). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@imadal1n](https://github.com/imadal1n).
  - Give invalid shell commands and Node worker memory exhaustion more useful errors in [#170947](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170947). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - When Code Mode runs out of worker memory, it now reports a bounded amount of captured output and attempts recovery instead of hanging or losing the whole result. [PR #172134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172134). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retirement of workers making frequent major collections near their heap limit, including when individual collections are cheap. [PR #172552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172552). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Per-worker GC and heap metrics use up to 64 reusable worker slots, with script-level attribution for overflow. [PR #172486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172486). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Node Code Mode leaves collection timing to V8 instead of forcing idle inspector collections between or during cells. [PR #172284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172284). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Healthy Node workers can be reused after settled validation, guest, and bridge failures without retaining failed-cell globals. [PR #172305](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172305). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A pending Code Mode command or wait can still be found for recovery after a nested tool call finishes, subject to the usual deadlines. [PR #171570](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171570), [issue #171366](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171366). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini).
  - Code Mode now distinguishes an error that stopped the code from a tool result reporting failure, and reminds you to check effects before retrying. [PR #171815](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171815). Thanks [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Truncated syntax feedback preserves Unicode characters at the clipping boundary. [PR #176230](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176230). Thanks [hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Suspended Code Mode runs expire according to elapsed time even when the system clock changes. [#180599](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180599). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Concurrent large shell analyses release parser memory sooner. [#183312](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183312). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Missing-subcommand JSON errors ([#184128](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184128)). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668).
  - Preserve cleanup uncertainty in buffered command results ([#187271](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187271), backport of [#184270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184270), addressing [#184237](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/184237)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).

  </details>

- An MCP agent now releases computer control when its run ends, so the session no longer keeps that control open. Recording and file transfer remain restricted on this path.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - MCP computer control follows the agent run and is released at its end. Recording and file transfer remain restricted on this path, [#151127](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151127) and [#147420](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/147420). Thanks to [ylcn91](https://github.com/ylcn91), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [vovarbv](https://github.com/vovarbv), [ghbalf](https://github.com/ghbalf) and [aleps001](https://github.com/aleps001).

  </details>

- The [browser panel](/web/control-ui/panels#browser-panel) now shows when a browser is starting and offers Retry if startup fails. Scrolling and slow-page handling improve too; on supported headless Chromium, a new modal passkey request is dismissed so you can choose another sign-in method. Actual passkey sign-in still needs a headed browser, and a prompt already stuck before connection may require reloading the page.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Cancel stalled browser navigation checks so profile stop can finish, [#154246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154246) and related [#141853](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/141853). Thanks to [LLagoon3](https://github.com/LLagoon3), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [Ewo8om3](https://github.com/Ewo8om3).
  - Pen scrolling in the Browser panel, [#158124](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158124). Thanks to [Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333) and [altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - Browser Use remote CDP setup example in the [remote browser guide](/tools/browser/remote), [#124188](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/124188). Thanks to [MagMueller](https://github.com/MagMueller) and [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Private browser handoff for dashboard and onboarding pairing in [#170289](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170289). Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - Browser startup progress, errors and retry in the Control UI in [#169215](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169215). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Native computer-control session renewal after expiry in [#169385](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169385), addressing [#169353](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169353). Thanks [@stackingrockss](https://github.com/stackingrockss) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A browser tab opened through Playwright now respects your timeout up to 120 seconds; a tab safely opened before page loading timed out can remain available in [#170053](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170053), addressing [#169944](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169944). Thanks [@wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@aleps001](https://github.com/aleps001).
  - Cancelling an in-progress browser open closes its new tab while leaving existing tabs open in [#170931](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170931). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Modal passkey requests no longer block other sign-in methods in managed headless Chromium in [#170857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170857), related to [#170856](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170856). Thanks [@trukhinyuri](https://github.com/trukhinyuri) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Pointer-targeted wheel and touch scrolling in nested Browser panel panes, with scrolling passed to an eligible parent at the edge. [PR #171215](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171215), [issue #171108](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171108). Thanks [wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and reporter [DarkJuanFra](https://github.com/DarkJuanFra).
  - Wheel scrolling for reversed vertical and horizontal lists in left-to-right and right-to-left layouts. [PR #171702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171702). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Chrome relay keeps your cache, header and service-worker bypass settings as you navigate an allowed page, without falsely reporting revoked access. [PR #173486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173486), [issue #173453](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/173453). Thanks [LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and reporter [ValantisV](https://github.com/ValantisV).
  - Browser proxy deadlines during clock changes ([#185753](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185753)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Invalid browser action inputs return a JSON error and exit with status 1 when JSON output is requested. [#180797](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180797). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Invalid browser tab indexes also return a structured JSON error before the action is sent. [#183603](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183603). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- [Web fetch](/tools/web-fetch) now keeps more numbers, code examples, headings and list boundaries intact, so retrieved pages are less likely to arrive garbled. It also excludes a recognized form of visually hidden text.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Decode UTF-8 pages and feeds with false UTF-16 metadata when no byte-order mark or HTTP charset overrides it, [#168115](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168115). Thanks to [yetval](https://github.com/yetval) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve numeric values in fetched JSON in [#170123](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170123). Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Fetched Markdown now keeps code text intact even if the page ends before its code block closes. [PR #171221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171221). Thanks [ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559).
  - Fetched pages now separate headings, paragraphs and list items instead of running them together. [#178569](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178569), [issue #178499](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/178499). Thanks [@HeZ2z](https://github.com/HeZ2z) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for reporting the issue.
  - Basic HTML extraction separates definition-list terms and values in text and Markdown. [#180557](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180557). Thanks [@AmelioMansour](https://github.com/AmelioMansour) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fetched text keeps readable link labels without fragments of unusual link destinations. [#180605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180605). Thanks [@AmelioMansour](https://github.com/AmelioMansour) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - HTML extraction preserves boundaries around more blocks, including quotes, captions, and navigation. [#181740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181740), related [issue #176244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176244). Thanks [@l4xb](https://github.com/l4xb) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for the related report.
  - Web fetch excludes text hidden by recognized zero-X or zero-Y inline scale transforms. [#181785](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181785), [issue #105438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/105438). Thanks [@Jimmy-xuzimo](https://github.com/Jimmy-xuzimo) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80) for reporting the issue.

  </details>

- [Web search](/tools/web) now honors more of your chosen provider settings, result counts and timeouts, and gives clearer errors when a search fails.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserve managed search timeout diagnostics in [#170372](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170372), addressing [#169972](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169972). Thanks [@xiajiahang](https://github.com/xiajiahang), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [@letsc4](https://github.com/letsc4). See the [web tools guide](/tools/web).

Thanks [@wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [@duardor968](https://github.com/duardor968) for reporting the issue.

Thanks [@mushuiyu886](https://github.com/mushuiyu886) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for reporting the issue.

Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@lator57-gif](https://github.com/lator57-gif).

[#171835](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171835), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [Glukmann](https://github.com/Glukmann) for [reporting the issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169072).

- Terminal commands now preserve more exact path and argument values, and shell suggestions cover more options. If you use installed Bash or PowerShell completions, regenerate them to pick up the fixes.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Complete quoted PowerShell option choices, [#167421](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167421). Thanks to [LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi).
  - Reset the log cursor when the selected file path changes, [#150791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150791) and [#150843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/150843). Thanks to [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Preserve Unicode joiners in exec and background process output, [#160403](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160403). Thanks to [Shenrui-Ma](https://github.com/Shenrui-Ma).
  - Avoid cold compile-cache writes delaying the exit of one-shot CLI commands in [#170684](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170684). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh validated shell snapshots can be reused without another validation shell, and process diagnostics identify more helper launches. [PR #172310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172310). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Shell completion suggests accepted global flags after subcommands. Regenerate cached completion scripts with `openclaw completion --write-state` to pick up the suggestions. [PR #171571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171571). Thanks [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Trajectory exports accept safe directory names beginning with two dots, such as `..diagnostics`. [PR #171609](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171609). Thanks [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) and [altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - Gateway SSH probes honor an explicitly specified port 22 even when the SSH alias specifies another port. [PR #171092](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171092), related [issue #127456](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127456). Thanks [zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Bash completion keeps suggestions within the option value being entered. [#183778](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183778). Thanks [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi).
  - Preserve writer identity through CLI reply completion ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/97e9b91f1f9413e32d31a8170dbafc8c9cfd7f3e)).
  - Separately installed ACPX can find the host bridges for enabled plugin and OpenClaw tools. The bridges remain opt-in. [#164272](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164272), [#164211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164211). Thanks to [jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and reporter [ajmtrz](https://github.com/ajmtrz).

  </details>

- Human-readable terminal reports now escape control characters in untrusted names and paths, making those reports safer to inspect. JSON and explicitly requested raw output may still contain the original characters.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Managed-worktree reports safely display control-bearing paths and names. [#181582](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181582). Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Model auth profile listings sanitize terminal controls in human-readable output. [#181584](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181584). Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - DNS setup previews safely display control-bearing paths. [#181585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181585). Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - MCP registry reports and errors sanitize terminal controls in human-readable output. [#181587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181587). Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Safe terminal rendering for readable environment values ([#184141](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184141)). Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007).
  - Gateway discovery reports escape control characters and line breaks from discovered beacons. [#183355](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183355) Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Storage list and initialization messages sanitize displayed locations. [#183359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183359) Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Shell completion installation messages sanitize profile paths and suggest restarting the shell when a reload command would contain terminal controls. [#184120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184120) Thanks [@alix-007](https://github.com/alix-007).
  - Session-list headers sanitize displayed store paths. [#184139](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184139) Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007).
  - Backup scheduling and record messages sanitize displayed paths and labels. [#184153](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184153) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007).

  - Sanitized paired-node camera metadata in the human-readable camera list. JSON retains the original values ([#175624](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175624)). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- After a background command, you can more reliably collect its actual result, including when a wait or cancellation fails. A running command is not a finished command, and a cancellation may still be cleaning up.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Wait recovery identifies resumable run IDs and, when the process tool is available and allowed, explains how to poll the original background shell session ([commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4d2255b5f901d33ac5900555f949e44f0d614eb5)).
  - Keep Codex and Agents API execution budgets steady when the system clock changes, [#155801](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155801). Thanks to [sunnyshu0925](https://github.com/sunnyshu0925) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Cancel a waiting CLI-backed turn without interrupting the active turn in [#169502](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169502). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Retry eligible queued background completions after a configuration change with current policy applied in [#169529](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169529), addressing [#169054](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169054). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and reporter [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
  - If a background command cannot send a completion notification, tell the agent to poll its original session for the result in [#169645](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169645). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - After cancelling a command, you can still retrieve a cleanup error once the run has settled. [PR #171708](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171708). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Background process polling keeps its intended timeout when the system clock changes. [PR #173241](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173241). Thanks [wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Delivered-turn settlement and history-confirmed Escape recovery in the TUI. [PR #171014](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171014), [issue #170974](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170974). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [KenYounge](https://github.com/KenYounge).
  - Interrupted CLI commands remove their plugin capture directories when signal cleanup completes. [PR #171162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171162), [issue #170773](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170773). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [rozenpl](https://github.com/rozenpl).
  - Fixes an immediate follow-up message being mistaken for a retry after you press Stop. [#172421](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172421). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Allowed a local follow-up while a stopped turn finishes cleanup when the destination remains unchanged. [#172458](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172458). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).

  </details>

- Workspace-restricted Gateway tools now require approval for affected delegated changes and reject local media outside allowed roots. Matching Google OAuth secrets are masked in console and log-tail output, but [raw local log files](/gateway/security/secrets-and-storage#logs-and-transcripts) can still contain credentials and need careful access.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Protect the active environment variable used by a whole-reference hook token from Gateway-hosted exec children, including across hook reloads ([#179199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179199)). Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc). The broader credential-inheritance report came from [@nierob-cmd](https://github.com/nierob-cmd) ([#178368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/178368)).
  - Closing MCP Relay's Connected apps view releases its rendered grant rows and prevents detached controls or pending pairing replies from acting on the closed view. [#184213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184213) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - For workspace-restricted sessions, Gateway-built delegate tools now ask approval before persistent changes and reject media outside allowed roots even when a caller omitted the file policy. [#185094](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185094) Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - Security audit follows linked workspace skills roots when checking for nested symlink escapes. [#182384](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182384) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@dineshsuthar123](https://github.com/dineshsuthar123) for the report.
  - Mask matching Google OAuth client secrets in console and log-tail output. Raw local file logs may still contain the secret. [#183193](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183193) Thanks [@leizhenzhang](https://github.com/leizhenzhang) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks [@osher86](https://github.com/osher86) for the report.
  - Preserve workspace policy for Gateway-created delegate and media tools ([#187307](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187307)). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - Local file logs now store raw values and WebSocket summaries omit text previews; direct reads of raw logs can expose credentials. This change is described in [#170023](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170023). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Authorized operators can still view and revoke standing grants on the Approvals page when its approval history fails to load, as long as the grants service is available.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Clarify expired and cancelled approvals and unconfirmed application of an approved setup change. [#172270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172270). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The Approvals page loads standing grants independently of approval history, so authorized operators can still see and revoke them. [#183769](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183769) Thanks [@AmelioMansour](https://github.com/AmelioMansour) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Diagnostics exports now keep the destination you entered, including meaningful spaces, and patch views retain trailing whitespace and the final newline.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserve whitespace in nonblank, non-home destinations for Gateway diagnostics and stability exports ([#181775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181775)). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve trailing whitespace and the final newline in Diffs patch viewers ([#181674](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181674)). Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Fixes OC Path failing to update YAML entries keyed by numbers, booleans or null while leaving neighboring entries unchanged. Very large patch hunks and affected Windows or Android storage paths also avoid specific file-operation failures.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Bun workspace helpers can run inline code without a false path-length error, while other workspace paths remain checked. [#171970](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171970) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - OC Path can now edit YAML entries keyed by a number, boolean or null without changing adjacent entries. [#171957](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171957) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update filesystem handling for affected Windows 9p mounts and Android/Termux temporary workspaces. [#172306](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172306), related to [#145589](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/145589) and [#171406](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171406). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Hawk5150](https://github.com/Hawk5150), [@iiAnthony](https://github.com/iiAnthony), [@jaisummagithub](https://github.com/jaisummagithub) and [@kukirin-ui](https://github.com/kukirin-ui).
  - Agents can apply very large file patch hunks without hitting the JavaScript argument-limit crash. [#174477](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174477). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Scripts using experimental `claws remove --json` now receive a JSON error for two invalid cleanup-option combinations instead of only a plain-text error.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Returns a parseable JSON failure for two invalid cleanup option combinations in the experimental `claws remove --json` command. [#172281](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172281), thanks to [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668).

  </details>

- New network results reached through Tool Search or Code Mode now keep their untrusted-source label in later replies, so memory can treat them like directly retrieved network content. Existing history and stored memory are not relabeled.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserves network-source labels through Tool Search and Code Mode for new tool results and subsequent replies. Existing history and stored memory are not rewritten. [#169560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169560), [issue #169332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169332). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@farisoftfry](https://github.com/farisoftfry).

  </details>

### Diagnostics and service maintenance

- Fixes update and Doctor cleanup failing after another database opening was refused.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Finishes closing affected shared database workers without reporting the refused opening again as a second cleanup failure. [#173813](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173813).

  </details>

- A refused database opening no longer unnecessarily interrupts other readers, and affected operations can retry.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserves shared readers and settles refused openings in the release branch. [#175987](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175987), [#163258](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163258) and [#179320](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179320), included in the combined backup and platform backport.

  </details>

- Status shows the selected model's actual context limit, and diagnostics distinguish a timed-out local worker from a provider error. Startup messages also report the setting or channel that needs attention.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Status, context and session reports use the selected model and runtime capacity instead of a stale or guessed limit. [#165991](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165991). Thanks to [snotty](https://github.com/snotty) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Startup warnings point to the work actually taking time, and channel start messages say why an account is starting. [#169221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169221). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A local worker timeout is reported as a local timeout, not as a provider HTTP 408; configured fallback remains available. [#156650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156650), [reported in #155627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/155627). Thanks to [YangManBOBO](https://github.com/YangManBOBO), [EndeavorPioneer](https://github.com/EndeavorPioneer), and [altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
  - Logs now identify session-list requests from the sidebar correctly. [#170512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170512). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The startup banner shows the effective thinking setting when it is off, rather than implying another setting is active. [#170627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170627). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) for the fix and [chughtapan](https://github.com/chughtapan) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170180).
  - Logbook warns about a missing vision model only when image analysis is actually waiting. [#170608](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170608). Thanks to [duyufansh2008](https://github.com/duyufansh2008).

  </details>

- Diagnostics keep useful failure details when a service cannot shut down cleanly, while optional logging failures no longer stop an agent's work.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Long-lived chats no longer keep growing their send-timing diagnostics without bound ([#171146](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171146)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recorded Codex and plugin activity can finish saving when the service closes or restarts. [#171293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171293), reported in [#171197](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171197). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@arquiteto-raiz](https://github.com/arquiteto-raiz).
  - Stall reports still identify the responsible script when its name contains quotes or escapes, without exposing unredacted details. [#170505](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170505). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - If optional cache or Anthropic diagnostic files cannot be saved, OpenClaw warns once and keeps the agent running. [#174622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174622). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jamie8johnson](https://github.com/jamie8johnson) and [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80).
  - A shutdown timeout now records which child process remained and whether the stop signal failed. [#175625](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175625). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Linux service inspection reports when it cannot confirm that its systemd check has stopped. [#175689](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175689). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev) for the earlier inspection work.
  - Linux update and service-repair checks report a systemd cleanup failure instead of treating unfinished inspection as complete. [#175703](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175703). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Startup recovery reports cleanup or ownership errors directly instead of incorrectly saying the service is missing. [#175726](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175726). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - Channel logs identify the event and underlying error when refreshing a message claim encounters a storage failure. [#171365](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171365). Thanks to [@ynaamane](https://github.com/ynaamane), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@yolo521-bot](https://github.com/yolo521-bot) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171034).

  </details>

- Scheduled maintenance keeps its timing after a clock correction, and a failed worker start no longer leaves its database in use.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Maintenance schedules stay on track after clock corrections; uncertain worker results refresh their connection before another attempt, and failed memory-worker starts finish cleanup before releasing the database. [#171415](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171415), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Gateway [readiness checks](/gateway/health) now wait for the final startup work to finish instead of reporting ready while it is still running.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  [#188873](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188873), its release adaptation [#188898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188898), and the [readiness check follow-up](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/591153e2fecccb86b1b4a30b3bb0b5eae913f600). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

### Desktop and mobile apps

- Saved Gateway connections in the Linux app keep their pairing when you switch between them or restart the app. Removing and re-adding a Gateway starts a fresh pairing.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keeps private persistent storage for each saved Gateway. [#158908](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158908).

  </details>

- The Mac Connection screen reports the actual local Gateway version and offers update or repair when needed, without proposing a downgrade for a healthy newer installation.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Uses the installed or connected local Gateway status instead of assuming the bundled version is running. [#181389](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181389).

  </details>

- [Mac permission prompts](/platforms/mac/permissions) now show which computer needs access and where to grant it. Complete the permission step on that Mac, then confirm a retry in chat.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Permission cards connect missing native Mac access to the app's grant flow and a user-confirmed retry. Computer Control can show multiple missing grants together. [#169635](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169635) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Chat permission cards offer the next step for the current macOS and OpenClaw access state, including requests handled in stages. [#171115](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171115) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - After a Screen Recording request, the card offers System Settings if access was denied. [#171175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171175) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Location cards direct you to System Settings after access is denied or unavailable, before offering to enable Location mode. [#171859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171859) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac setup does not inspect protected Chrome data until you explicitly ask to use the browser. [#171177](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171177) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Quick Chat no longer crashes while setting up permissions. [Quick Chat permission setup crash fix](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/94940fb3e3017a6fdb3eccb81c809a929a74a056).
  - When Host Desktop is disabled, chat offers setup guidance and lets an authorized connection enable it when the desktop server is ready. [#177888](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177888), [issue #174368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174368) Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm) and [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).
  - If the owner has granted and retained the native setup permission, eligible turns can use the access described in that prompt without repeating setup. Revoking consent still stops it, and other users do not inherit that access. [#185920](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185920) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Mac app navigation and panel controls no longer crowd or cover the native window buttons.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Desktop panel headers and navigation controls share the titlebar while leaving room for native window buttons. [#171131](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171131) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The rail divider and collapsed sidebar background stay below macOS window controls. [#172451](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172451) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Downloading a conversation or dashboard file in the Mac app opens the system Save dialog instead of replacing the page with raw file data.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Dashboard and conversation downloads in the Mac app use the system Save dialog instead of replacing the page with archive data. [#173654](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173654) Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- iPhone, iPad and Mac chat show when a reply is preparing, waiting or retrying, and sending works again after a connection returns. Pairing and error messages are easier to follow in supported languages.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - iPad pairing and Gateway onboarding messages have translations in more languages. [#185480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185480)
  - Apple chat now says when a reply is preparing, waiting or retrying, including labels available to screen readers. [#166356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166356), related [#139926](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139926). Thanks [Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin) and [noelillinger](https://github.com/noelillinger).
  - Apple chat shows when a helper is still working and provides a way to open that helper's conversation. [#166322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166322), related [#52960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/52960). Thanks [Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored) and [Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin).
  - On iPhone or iPad, arrow-key history no longer disrupts selected text or text still being composed with an input method. [#143056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143056). Thanks [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - iOS buttons have clearer dark-mode contrast and room for larger text, and the Settings title keeps its spacing with the sidebar open. [#166379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166379). Thanks [Rocuts](https://github.com/Rocuts), [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Apple apps reconnect after you change access or computer-use options. Mac Claude session browsing works through the app's worker and is unavailable while that worker is down; unchanged iOS reads no longer produce spurious updates. [#169617](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169617) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - iPhone manual connection setup explains invalid Gateway addresses before you try to connect. Enter a hostname or IP address without a scheme or port. [#169875](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169875), reported in [#144678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/144678). Thanks to [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino) and [@bjesuiter](https://github.com/bjesuiter).
  - iOS chat follows its message connection for online status, and dismissed warnings stay hidden through identical repeated failures. [#169912](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169912), reported in [#169911](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169911), by [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin).
  - If the Mac Dashboard receives an unexpected Gateway error, you can reload it; an old session link also retries the normal access check. [#170361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170361) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac Dashboard reload and Gateway error messages gain translations in 21 non-English locales. [#170475](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170475).
  - The existing subagent wait label gains translations in the iOS and macOS catalogs. [#170172](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170172).
  - Native chat startup and retry messages gain translated catalog entries for supported locales. [#171084](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171084).
  - Apple chat can send again when its connection returns and resumes the same conversation. [#171528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171528) Thanks to [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen) and to [@azuretek](https://github.com/azuretek) for the report.
  - A failed Mac Dashboard can reload after its authenticated connection returns to the same Gateway, without discarding a healthy page or draft. [#172646](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172646) Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
  - Mac Companion setup can finish AI verification with a saved sign-in after a wizard progress reply. [#171667](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171667) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@enter501](https://github.com/enter501) for the report.
  - The Mac Codex catalog remains available when the valid `nativeHookRelay` plugin option is configured. [#172592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172592) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac CLI startup diagnostics print literally when the app cannot resolve the local CLI. [#171802](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171802) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - App lists and copied transcripts remain usable when data changes during a read, though a changing list may be incomplete. Android Talk settings changed mid-load may require a refresh or restart, and an iOS Talk session that fails to close may need stale-session cleanup. [#169336](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169336). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Android now shows Gateway names with non-English characters intact and renders inline code correctly even after an unmatched backtick.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Android chat displays inline code correctly even if an earlier backtick was left unmatched. [#170478](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170478), with a follow-up from [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Android shows Gateway names containing non-English characters correctly, and a fallback Google video result identifies the operation that returned it. [#171920](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171920) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- On Wear OS, Retry stays reachable on round screens and larger text remains scrollable; replies, speech and navigation also recover from more interrupted states.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Wear OS recovery keeps Retry visible on round displays, while shared lists and controls fit below the clock and remain scrollable with larger text. [#170321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170321) by [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
  - Returning from the Wear keyboard keeps the current reply state, and final replies without a named sender no longer appear twice in history. [#171476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171476) Thanks to [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
  - When Pulse is unavailable, a Wear OS watch returns to Chat, including after restoring a saved session. [#172136](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172136) Contribution from [@IWhatsskill](https://github.com/IWhatsskill).
  - Wear OS reply speech and Stop remain responsive if the speech engine stalls. [#172147](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172147) Contribution from [@IWhatsskill](https://github.com/IWhatsskill).

  </details>

- On Android, embedded browser, desktop and terminal views are unavailable when an active certificate pin cannot be enforced inside them. Native chat remains available on that connection.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Embedded pages are refused on actively pinned connections because the embedded browser cannot enforce the native pin throughout the connection. A saved fingerprint that is not active does not trigger the restriction. [#180843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180843) Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).

  </details>

- The Mac app is less likely to leave a healthy Gateway on a recovery screen after an update, and Retry can repair an incomplete installation. Direct connections with a learned certificate pin can reconnect after a trusted renewal for the same host; explicit pins remain fixed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Native updates reduce stopped database backup and optional Doctor work while keeping recovery backups and required repairs. [#170087](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170087) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac updates avoid waiting on their own lock when equivalent temporary-directory paths are used. [#169860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169860) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac update and recovery checks recognize valid app-managed Bun services, addressing a recovery screen that could remain despite a healthy Gateway. [#170006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170006), with the report and investigation in [#169662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169662). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jokerman1302-eng](https://github.com/jokerman1302-eng), [@davidmackrory-byte](https://github.com/davidmackrory-byte) and [@josephsilvagnoli](https://github.com/josephsilvagnoli).
  - Mac updates report why a LaunchAgent inspection failed and suggest a deep Gateway status check. [#170429](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170429), reported in [#169995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169995). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot).
  - Mac service repair handles overlapping service loading after confirming the service is loaded, without interrupting a running Gateway. [#170740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170740) by [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Mac app recovery preserves a newer managed Gateway and can restore a missing Node service using its selected CLI. The fix requires updated app recovery code. [#170766](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170766) by [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - A verified external Mac terminal can restart the Gateway when an ancestor process cannot be inspected. [#170354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170354) by [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - The Mac app includes updated Peekaboo and process-handling dependencies. [#170713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170713) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway startup in the Mac app can reach readiness sooner when using bundled Bun. [#169543](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169543) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac app Retry can repair plugin links and clear an incomplete-update warning after the Gateway passes its required checks. [#171508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171508) Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and to [@shanselman](https://github.com/shanselman) for the report and investigation.
  - Updates under the macOS Gateway service prepare the selected pnpm installation's environment. [#171936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171936) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@AntaresGG](https://github.com/AntaresGG) for the report, and [@MoerAI](https://github.com/MoerAI) for the reproduction.
  - A Mac update started from a login shell can proceed when OpenClaw verifies it is not running inside the Gateway service, even if it cannot inspect the whole process tree. An older updater may still refuse the first update. [#172640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172640) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@giulianoaccorsi](https://github.com/giulianoaccorsi) for the report and diagnosis.
  - macOS service inspection and restart preserve multiline environment values and their whitespace. [#171740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171740) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval) for the report.
  - Two Mac Gateway recovery messages are translated in 21 non-English locales. [#172422](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172422).
  - Before migrating an app-managed installation, the updater tries to repair supported bundled plugins and shows any repair warnings in the Mac app. [#174257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174257), [issue #174120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174120) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@kazuyuki-eguchi](https://github.com/kazuyuki-eguchi).
  - On Macs with many skill folders, Gateway shutdown avoids waiting on every watcher separately. This applies to later stops after the updated Gateway is running. [#168273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168273). Thanks [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - A macOS LaunchAgent restart reports an error when its restart helper cannot start, instead of claiming that the restart was scheduled. [#175229](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175229) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Updated Mac apps can use compatible saved device credentials for remote Gateway connections after unrelated local database upgrades. [#166993](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166993). Thanks [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Direct Mac Gateway connections using a learned certificate pin can reconnect after a trusted renewal for the same host. Explicitly configured pins remain fixed. [#168151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168151). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev) for the [earlier renewal work](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164435).
  - The Mac status menu explains when an incompatible bundled worker needs an app update and offers an update action when available. [#157948](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157948), related [#157444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157444). Thanks [PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13).

  </details>

- Mac commands can find preferred tools when the app starts from Finder, the Dock or a login session. Relaunch after changing tool folders; if an older app added a wrapper to your shared PATH, remove that old entry manually.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Opening a named profile or temporary Mac app no longer adds its wrapper to your shared shell PATH. Named-profile wrappers still work by full path; remove old PATH entries yourself. [#169460](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169460). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - New Peekaboo skill dependency installs use the maintained OpenClaw Homebrew tap. Existing installations are not migrated automatically. [#166245](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166245), related [#166244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166244). Thanks [holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen).
  - Mac node commands find preferred tool folders across Finder, Dock, and login launches. [#171460](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171460) Thanks to [@brianmcguire](https://github.com/brianmcguire), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [@jamiezigelbaum](https://github.com/jamiezigelbaum) for the report.
  - Longer Mac companion commands can return within the requested time, and an unavailable result is no longer confused with an unreachable app. [#172117](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172117) Thanks to [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen), [@ylcn91](https://github.com/ylcn91), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [@Colton-Harris](https://github.com/Colton-Harris) for the report and diagnosis.
  - Mac skills now recognize the macOS platform name when checking whether they can run, install dependencies or find a required tool. [#172214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172214) Thanks to [@fhgffy](https://github.com/fhgffy), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [@jessecq1995](https://github.com/jessecq1995) for the related report.
  - Apple app builds find a supported Node executable when a version-manager shim leads PATH. [#168139](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168139). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac developer guidance covers signing and packaging a local build to replace a release installation. [#168527](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168527). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- The Mac memory readout now counts memory the system can reclaim, so available memory is no longer understated.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Mac host memory meters in Devices and the Gateway count reclaimable memory as available. [#144535](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144535), reports [#144176](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/144176) and [#156578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156578). Thanks [sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), [altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf), and reporters [hengtested-design](https://github.com/hengtested-design) and [s3d1K0](https://github.com/s3d1K0).

  </details>

- Windows source and package updates avoid more path, backup and service failures, and an early failed source update can restore a verified previous build. Some repairs must already be in the updater performing the update; an older broken updater may still need manual recovery.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Windows update recovery accepts short-name or case-variant paths to the same verified backup, avoiding a false rollback. [#168968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168968). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - The final plugin check for Windows updates from OpenClaw 2026.10.1 can use the pending-migration request expected by that updater. [#169031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169031). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Concurrent Windows updater starts coordinate initial handoff database creation to avoid false quarantine and lost records when the installed updater includes the fix. [#169404](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169404). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update service refresh recognizes equivalent Windows account names while protecting different or unresolved accounts. [#169481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169481) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor preserves internal dependency junctions when copying plugin captures between phases. [#169672](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169672), related to [#169586](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169586), by [@n572770](https://github.com/n572770).
  - Windows Git-source updates avoid recursive directory copies and can recover the verified previous build after a qualifying staging failure. [#170940](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170940), reported in [#170700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170700). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@stop-time](https://github.com/stop-time).
  - Windows updates now explain what to do when Task Scheduler denies access with an English error or `0x80070005`. [#171109](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171109) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@Rene-F](https://github.com/Rene-F) for the report.
  - Piping the default Windows installer no longer fails because no Node version was specified. If you specify one yourself, use a three-part version. [#183145](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183145), [issue #183088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/183088) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit) and [@ryandle](https://github.com/ryandle).
  - A Windows source install checks the checkout after a failed Git pull and stops with recovery guidance if it cannot confirm the original state. [#185378](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185378), [issue #127411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127411) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Windows updates and plugin loading handle deep paths and 8.3 short-name state paths that could block candidate migration checks. [#172769](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172769) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - A Windows pnpm update can proceed past a dangling link to an optional package that is not installed. If it fails before changing the installation or service, it no longer reports a restart failure; the installed updater must already contain this fix. [#181596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181596), [issue #181577](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/181577) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@SMF12138](https://github.com/SMF12138).
  - Windows update checks no longer flash a console window for SQLite inspection. [#174969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174969), [issue #174906](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174906) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@throwingbones](https://github.com/throwingbones).
  - Windows update recovery retains the security helper it needs after replacing the original installation. [#187470](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187470) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Windows runtime recovery and Bun-hosted Node discovery recognize quoted absolute PATH entries. [#181678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181678) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Links2008](https://github.com/Links2008).
  - Forced Windows Gateway reinstall tolerates normal launcher timestamp settlement and can restore the previous task if activation fails. [#176945](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176945), [issue #176872](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176872) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@cnnceart](https://github.com/cnnceart).
  - Windows Gateway stop and restart wait long enough to confirm a slow local connection refusal. If the check is still inconclusive, run `openclaw gateway status --deep` before retrying. [#174807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174807), [issue #174563](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174563) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng) and [@prizmizm](https://github.com/prizmizm).
  - Windows backup and Doctor cleanup can remove unused temporary backups without the recurring directory error. [#176836](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176836), [issue #176743](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176743) Thanks [@xutongxue233](https://github.com/xutongxue233), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@wg-mojo](https://github.com/wg-mojo).
  - Windows service changes and Mac Gateway stops no longer time out early or wait too long after a system clock change. [#182859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182859), [#183033](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183033) Thanks [@mikasa0818](https://github.com/mikasa0818), [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Windows commands now handle more paths with spaces, long paths and unusual characters without failing to start or misreading files. If an unattended command cannot use a Store PowerShell alias and its script needs PowerShell 7, install native PowerShell 7 rather than relying on the 5.1 fallback.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Windows node commands preserve prompts, links and file paths with quotes or spaces when started through `cmd.exe`. [#158160](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158160), reports [#142847](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/142847) and [#158129](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/158129). Thanks [Dimitri-lcht](https://github.com/Dimitri-lcht), [igs-rogenlo](https://github.com/igs-rogenlo), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [kassol](https://github.com/kassol).
  - Windows ARM64 shell launches retain the `SystemRoot` value needed to start when a replacement environment omits it. An explicit caller value still takes precedence. [#169338](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169338). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Installing the Windows Bun service no longer fails after a successful runtime check just because temporary-file cleanup must wait. [#169286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169286). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A Windows Bun Gateway can continue loading its settings when a temporary copy remains busy after a successful read. [#169337](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169337). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Windows Gateways with installed plugins can use the small-file copy path during startup. [#168313](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168313). Thanks [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Windows file checks keep distinguishing the intended file when path aliases or filesystem behavior could confuse them. [#169469](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169469). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Windows Bun plugins now use the same Gateway SDK state as the Gateway, avoiding a separate copy during loading. [#169482](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169482) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Agent deletion supports large NTFS file IDs and recovery retains exact identities. Older numeric records keep their rounding ambiguity until they are verified and upgraded. [#169485](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169485), reported in [#137416](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137416). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@pkinjo](https://github.com/pkinjo), [@xupeijian](https://github.com/xupeijian) and [@karljamoralin](https://github.com/karljamoralin).
  - On Windows with Bun, a successful Gateway startup or deep status check no longer fails solely because a temporary file is busy. Cleanup may wait until exit or a later start. [#169572](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169572) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
