  - Skill preparation avoids repeated environment reads when required binaries are missing, reducing work on long Windows search paths. [#171066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171066), reported in [#171040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171040). Thanks to [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@funnysubaru](https://github.com/funnysubaru).
  - Windows reports incompatible AppContainer child-pipe operations and native helpers, and improves access to private paths. [#171728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171728) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jonathanlindsay](https://github.com/jonathanlindsay), and [@chelsealong](https://github.com/chelsealong).
  - Windows startup spends less time reopening plugin files while checking the installed plugins. [#172333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172333) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [@SBC2016](https://github.com/SBC2016) for the report.
  - Windows Gateway startup no longer fails its database checks when the environment cannot be sent to the inspection process. [#176194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176194), [issue #176111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176111) Thanks [@skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@easyteacher](https://github.com/easyteacher).
  - Windows onboarding, Gateway and CLI processes no longer crash when the state folder is reached through a short Windows path. [#172768](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172768) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Windows can reopen small private database files and create them in long paths that previously failed. [Commit 47c7064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/47c7064d8fcd50515d1b220c6209b71a9aa4bfab)
  - Native plugins can load on affected Windows computers when security software changes only file metadata and the plugin's contents still verify. [#179949](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179949), [issue #179923](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/179923) Thanks [@coaiMax](https://github.com/coaiMax) and [@paulcam206](https://github.com/paulcam206).
  - Unattended Windows Gateway commands skip inaccessible Store PowerShell aliases. If only the Store alias is available, the fallback is Windows PowerShell 5.1; commands requiring PowerShell 7 syntax need a native MSI or ZIP installation of PowerShell 7. [#182898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182898), [issue #182652](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182652) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@tre1d](https://github.com/tre1d).
  - Windows Claude CLI turns using stored credentials avoid a startup delivery stall. Claude Code may still use its own login instead of the selected OpenClaw profile. [#174571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174571), [issue #174204](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174204), [account selection issue #163332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/163332) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@scalesmart](https://github.com/scalesmart), [@layne-11](https://github.com/layne-11) and [@pandysp](https://github.com/pandysp).
  - Windows port commands now explain that the operation is unsupported and suggest alternatives without changing the existing data or selected engine. [#183512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183512) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Android and Termux accept the same state database after ordinary SQLite writes while retaining replacement-file checks. [#167719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167719), report [#167623](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167623) and related [#167791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167791). Thanks [yxshee](https://github.com/yxshee), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and reporter [grabowskidaniel359-design](https://github.com/grabowskidaniel359-design).
  - Secret entry under Rosetta avoids a crash, APFS worktrees can use a matching Rosetta helper, and Windows private files support long paths. [#168942](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168942), related [#167663](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167663). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Windows source checkouts entered through short paths can prepare plugin SDK declarations. [#169427](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169427). Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Managed Linux desktops can start when a hidden display socket occupies the expected display, and host commands launched from an AppImage no longer inherit its conflicting library path.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - A Linux desktop can choose another display when a hidden socket already occupies the expected one. [#169392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169392), report [#169391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169391). Thanks [holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - SSH and other host commands launched from the Linux AppImage no longer inherit a conflicting library path. This also drops user-added `LD_LIBRARY_PATH` entries for those commands. [#183661](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183661), [issue #183656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/183656) Thanks [@demos-ra](https://github.com/demos-ra) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A failed DNS lookup no longer replaces an existing zone, and Linux camera commands tell you when a folder cannot be read instead of claiming no cameras exist. [#174424](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174424) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Windows no longer reports a failed local-model shutdown when the service has stopped and only its termination helper is still finishing.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Windows now reports the local model as stopped when its service has exited, even if a helper is still finishing. [#171587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171587), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) for the original repair and [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171472).

  </details>

### Installation, updates and backups

- On affected Linux filesystems, startup and Doctor can recover when a fast file copy is refused instead of failing outright. Credentials in covered process-cleanup errors are masked before the error is saved or shown.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Gateway startup and Doctor can retry a normal copy when ZFS refuses a fast reflink copy. [#170812](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170812) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [@Glukmann](https://github.com/Glukmann) for reporting [#169071](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169071).
  - Credentials in host process cleanup errors are masked before they reach retained results or notifications. [#170941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170941) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), following [#169063](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169063).
  - A failed local check no longer discards an existing OpenShell copy or hides the remote skills view ([#176458](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176458)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Supported Node versions are accepted again, with warnings for versions missing required security patches ([#186589](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186589)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Install and update paths receive the same Node compatibility and security advisory corrections ([#187626](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187626)). Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- [Doctor](/gateway/doctor/checks) and startup can bring back some older agent conversations blocked by an upgrade and rebuild certain damaged search indexes after backing up the database. Mixed or unexplained damage still needs manual attention; stop the Gateway and keep a verified backup before an offline repair.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Deleting one agent no longer falsely blocks registration for another with independent identity and database paths. [#170309](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170309), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor and Gateway migration can read large compressed conversation archives without loading the whole archive as one string. A single record beyond Node's string limit is still unsupported. [#170853](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170853), thanks to [@iamonlv426](https://github.com/iamonlv426) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Managed updates use private per-user temporary storage and can recover eligible old receipts when another user owns their former directory. [#170174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170174), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169382).
  - Updates and Doctor can retain SQLite recovery backups in deeply nested paths that previously ran out of temporary path space. [#170867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170867), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - On Linux, eligible child tools such as Claude CLI can be given a lower priority under memory pressure. This is best effort and can be changed or disabled. [#170899](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170899), reported in [#170869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170869). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@eliavb43](https://github.com/eliavb43).
  - On Linux and Mac, Claude CLI processes no longer remain running after their Gateway disappears in the covered cases. [#171016](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171016), reported in [#170868](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170868). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@eliavb43](https://github.com/eliavb43).
  - Installations with many agents spend less time repeating checks when listing or starting them. [#171371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171371), reported in [#171351](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171351). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT).
  - An unused agent database no longer blocks Gateway startup. If a secondary agent's database is incompatible, healthy agents can still start and the affected agent gets `openclaw doctor --fix` guidance. [#171372](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171372), related to [#171348](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171348). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@LifeViwer](https://github.com/LifeViwer).
  - Credential file updates reuse Gateway readers and reconnect retained observers after a restart. [#171246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171246). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Long streamed replies use less Gateway CPU while preserving text corrections and Unicode boundaries. [#171361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171361), reported in [#171360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171360). Thanks [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - If you delete an agent that is still working, the wait ends with the conversation you need to let finish before retrying. [#172511](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172511), [issue #172416](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172416). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Starting a helper does less repeated work on installations with many saved runs. [#172480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172480) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Installations with many conversations spend less time dispatching database work. [#172532](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172532) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Migration refuses a Claude instruction file with invalid UTF-8 instead of importing changed text. Convert or save the file as UTF-8, then retry. [#172953](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172953) Thanks to [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Starting two agents at once no longer lets the later initialization displace the first agent's database. [#174251](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174251) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Upgrades can restore verified empty agent stores created by the Mac app. A required store that remains unavailable now makes Doctor report failure; populated or uncertain stores need their own eligible repair. [#174297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174297) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Affected Mac-created agents with existing conversations can become available again through update repair or `openclaw doctor --fix --non-interactive` after verified backups. This narrowly scoped recovery may restore data from a pending deletion; other uncertain stores stay unavailable and may need manual restoration. See the [database recovery guidance](/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery). [#175007](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175007) Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - When Gateway startup or media migration detects a corrupt agent database, the notice now points to that agent's recovery command instead of suggesting only a general fix. Stop the Gateway and back up the database and its SQLite sidecars before running `openclaw doctor --session-sqlite recover --session-sqlite-agent <id>` with the same state and configuration. Recovery is not automatic, and some earlier Doctor refusals may not show this hint. [PR #170249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170249), reported in [#169390](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169390). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@xiajiahang](https://github.com/xiajiahang), [@nkeil](https://github.com/nkeil), and [@jonathanlindsay](https://github.com/jonathanlindsay) for the report.
  - If an older sign-in file cannot be read, Doctor keeps the saved profiles and points to the file to inspect or restore instead of removing them. [PR #170256](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170256), fixing [#168959](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168959). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@nosmile99](https://github.com/nosmile99), and [@Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot) for the report.
  - Gateway commands refuse SQLite file links that could reach live OpenClaw data through variables. You can still inspect a private database copy. [PR #170253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170253). The separate working-directory issue [#134612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134612) was reported by [@phuc-nt](https://github.com/phuc-nt). Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
  - The first database open can wait briefly when another operation is temporarily changing its structure. [PR #170300](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170300). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A first conversation can be saved while usage information refreshes, even if its database has not yet been created. [PR #170308](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170308). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Saving a conversation no longer rolls back when its database connection changes at the same moment. [PR #170423](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170423). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recovery leaves your configuration alone and warns you if it cannot save the original before replacing it. [#171217](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171217), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot) for [reporting](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171187).
  - Doctor and legacy SDK data selection respect the active profile and its state directory. [#171439](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171439), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor can import older conversations whose repeated session headers differ only by timestamp and keeps the original archive. Conflicting headers still stop the import. [#172133](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172133), reported in [#172109](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172109). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [wangxiajun68](https://github.com/wangxiajun68).
  - Migration leaves an older notes file untouched if it contains invalid text encoding, rather than changing its contents. [#172293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172293). Thanks to [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Repair leaves retired agents' saved data untouched and points to their locations while fixing active conversations. [#172295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172295), related to [#172259](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172259). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).
  - Doctor can finish pending repairs on the same version without pulling unrelated old repair work into a newer upgrade. [#172390](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172390). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Bun installations no longer interrupt some Doctor and update maintenance work when passing database tasks to a worker. [#172175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172175). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Startup now stops with an incompatibility error rather than opening a database whose version or structure it cannot safely use. [#172265](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172265). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor avoids repeatedly scanning migration history when stale session import records point to archived transcripts. [#172890](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172890), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DongwonGim](https://github.com/DongwonGim) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172809), and [@skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223) for investigating.
  - Doctor can back up and rebuild certain incompatible search indexes while keeping agent data. For offline repair, stop the Gateway and use its Node runtime with `openclaw doctor --fix`; mixed damage still needs another recovery path. [Recovery guidance](/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery) · [#176234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176234). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@ndakota79](https://github.com/ndakota79).
  - Startup can back up and rebuild a stale agent search index before the agent opens; unexplained damage or a failed repair still keeps that agent unavailable. [#176833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176833). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@ndakota79](https://github.com/ndakota79).
  - `openclaw doctor --fix` can restore eligible built-in agent databases held after a schema update. Until then, the affected agent stays unavailable, while healthy agents can start. [#176783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176783). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@PennyVibe](https://github.com/PennyVibe).
  - After you free disk space and the checks pass, an affected agent can save new work without restarting the Gateway. Check any interrupted writes yourself; OpenClaw does not replay them. [#176902](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176902). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A migration leaves unreadable text files or their original archives intact instead of importing altered text, and keeps damaged Skill Workshop recovery bundles available ([#180861](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180861)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666).
  - Doctor stops before moving or discarding unreadable node-host and Web Push files or an interrupted claim. Back up the affected files and correct the copies before retrying ([#180850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180850)). Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor can finish repairing plugins after importing verified conversations even when an older archive is missing, while still warning about that gap ([#178779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178779), [#167385](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167385)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@lostsock1](https://github.com/lostsock1) and [@IliyaNexus](https://github.com/IliyaNexus).
  - Doctor no longer repeats old migration checks and notices on unchanged data during the same repair ([#180576](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180576)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@MertBasar0](https://github.com/MertBasar0).
  - Existing agent database reads can continue after a compatible structure refresh without reopening unnecessarily. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0644d3a70aa1397d61b8d4ac66c9b815fcb432fb)
  - After an unsafe database open or cleanup failure, OpenClaw no longer trusts an earlier read check; a refusal before opening leaves healthy reads intact. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/acc5395d48360aedc1dd7353085e1d9d315b3efc)
  - Startup does less repeat checking of old migration files, and Doctor clears obsolete missing-history notices. A temporary ownership interruption gets a bounded retry. [#170936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170936), [report #170693](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170693). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@NOVA-Openclaw](https://github.com/NOVA-Openclaw).
  - After storage recovers, closing the database can clear a lock left by completed work so later protected operations can proceed. [#173225](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173225), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - Existing agent databases are updated on first use of this version; the upgrade can take longer when you have more agents or saved data. [#169315](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169315), [issue #169254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169254). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Changing settings, plugins or credentials is less likely to interrupt work already underway or discard unrelated changes. Some plugin reloads wait until active work finishes; if it never finishes, the saved change can remain pending.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - An authorized Main-session reset can bring back a conversation archived during restart recovery, retaining its transcript and starting with fresh model context. It does not resume the interrupted task. [#176675](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176675), [#137264](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137264), [#173412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/173412) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@enki](https://github.com/enki), [@r3n3x](https://github.com/r3n3x) and [@cavatison](https://github.com/cavatison).
  - Managed installs use the supported patched Node version, and source builders use pnpm 12.15.0. Immediate join and hook turns now follow the event's access rules; restarted subagents use the accepted caller's authority. [#181074](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181074), [#179992](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179992) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
  - Partial HTML-like tags in streamed replies avoid unnecessary full parsing where their meaning is clear. [#180765](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180765), [#180720](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180720) Thanks [@JasonBuildAI](https://github.com/JasonBuildAI) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Long streams containing tool-call XML avoid repeated parsing of settled text. [#181397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181397), [#175156](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/175156) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@JasonBuildAI](https://github.com/JasonBuildAI).
  - When a turn inherits sign-in access, it now uses the configured credential owner rather than another profile. [#181812](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181812) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - If your role grants access to no agents, the agent list returns promptly instead of waiting for model information. [#182159](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182159) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Reject explicitly blank `--identity-file` and `--workspace` values in `openclaw agents set-identity`. Omit a flag to use its normal default. [#182622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182622) Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Agent and team creation messages no longer display unsafe formatting from names; stored names and JSON results remain unchanged. [#183360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183360) Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - When conversation compaction fails, JSON callers receive a usable error tied to the conversation they requested. [#184131](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184131) Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Browsing OpenCode conversations no longer times out early or waits too long after a system clock change. [#182295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182295) Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Mac conversation navigation keeps its intended wait time even if the system clock changes. [#182627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182627) Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Starting or stopping a managed local model no longer waits the wrong length of time after a clock change. [#182778](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182778) Thanks [@mikasa0818](https://github.com/mikasa0818) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Setting or removing configuration no longer applies an edit based on an outdated view of its references or owner; a cleared copied conversation owner is reported in [#184194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184194). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - A configuration change now retries preparation if newer settings supersede it, and a plugin reload waits for active work to finish in [#185573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185573). A saved configuration can remain pending if that work never settles. Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Changing only whether sign-in is available no longer unnecessarily rebuilds an agent's prepared runtime in [#185250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185250). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and to [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for reporting [#161468](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161468).
  - A helper turn waits for its owner's newly published sign-in settings before it starts in [8501893](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/85018936a3383184812792bdc58c98d1b53311dc).
  - A later model or credential change no longer causes an unnecessary restart from an older reload in [b285a4e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b285a4e9d8915c48d3164125ee515f0f9a1a2123).
  - Replacing or disabling the Muse plugin now releases its open native sessions in [#184941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184941). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [pstarkgit](https://github.com/pstarkgit).
  - Binding or unbinding an agent by channel name now recognizes that channel consistently in [#184717](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184717), addressing [#184636](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/184636). Thanks to [a-raphie](https://github.com/a-raphie), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - A false conversation conflict no longer blocks recording native token usage in [#184427](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184427). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Cleanup no longer repeatedly waits on an outdated native binding view, including when request tracing is active, in [7cc1ca6](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7cc1ca64798cf9897b3f27e1751ae2a8e73d462d).
  - Deleting an agent waits for its usage cleanup, and failed usage refresh cleanup can retry in [5ac29c6](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5ac29c6c966ea36ca05fe3585fe56520f70bc474) and [6a3b706](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6a3b7067d5e98fa9eb4055eb10e99c85591d352e). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A heartbeat check blocked by an agent being deleted can resume without restarting the Gateway in [8484ee5](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8484ee5268faea844415392eff5a965068d8c207).
  - Retrying a hook no longer spends as long waiting to check whether its agent is being deleted in [20d12e8](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/20d12e8a4dce7af751315e08494467d38d14d35f). Thanks to [VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
  - Node 26 worker processes no longer abort when their input comes from a file or is ignored in [#187516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187516) and its backport [#187553](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187553). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - The upgrade adds indexes used by conversation lookups and delivery cleanup, and scans existing records as it applies them. [#169414](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169414). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Use the committed role policy after profile lookup for a pending operator turn, so a temporary downgrade restored during lookup does not reject it. A persistent downgrade still restricts the turn. [#169607](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169607). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Preserve unrelated credential updates when Model Setup activation fails, and provide async replacements for deprecated synchronous OAuth SDK methods. [#169633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169633). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - `/status` and `session_status` show which sign-in profile or safe credential source the session is using, without displaying the secret. [#169721](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169721), [issue #82020](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/82020) and [earlier investigation #90689](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/90689). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559) and reporter [@davidjoshua7692-code](https://github.com/davidjoshua7692-code).
  - Gateway access checks now respect a profile role downgrade or deletion instead of retaining its old access. [#169799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169799). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A Claw MCP setup retry that succeeds no longer returns an old failure message. [#169756](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169756). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Config set, patch and unset dry runs work while the Gateway owns state, and an unchanged offline set no longer creates an absent state database in [#170202](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170202). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The database upgrade retains ACP resume information and saved update progress. It does not make PostgreSQL a supported runtime database. [PR #170280](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170280), with the storage design in [#169254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169254). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- [Update preparation](/install/updating) refreshes the selected target before you confirm, and supported package and Bun installs handle more interrupted or unusual first-upgrade paths.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - The Inbox's Dev Git update card refreshes the target when opened and checks it again before you confirm. Opening the card alone does not update anything. [#158053](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158053). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - A specified update timeout now also covers Doctor and service preparation; if stopping the service has begun, OpenClaw can still finish that stop and restore it. [#166000](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166000). Thanks to [dankarization](https://github.com/dankarization) and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Allow valid Dev updates and Mac recovery to proceed when saved receipt hints are missing or stale. An older updater may still need the documented manual route for its first upgrade. [#167459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167459), [reported in #167441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167441). Thanks to [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - On supported hosts, `install.sh --runtime bun` can install with a verified pinned Bun runtime and set up the Gateway service. A fresh `--no-onboard` install does not create that service. [#168832](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168832) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - On FreeBSD, an interrupted npm update can be repaired when its helper was created by an updater with this fix. Package repair alone does not restart or verify the Gateway. [#168775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168775) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The first upgrade from stable 2026.10.1 retains the files needed to finish its cleanup after package replacement. [#168740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168740) and [#168849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168849) by [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), with thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for independently checking the compatibility mappings in #168740.
  - A configuration edit during an update no longer silently changes the selected target, and a busy plugin change asks you to retry. [#168882](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168882) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update subprocesses retain your configured Gateway memory limits and any explicit override. [#168889](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168889), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and issue reporter [@bluet](https://github.com/bluet).
  - If an update fails after migrating data, its error points to repair for the affected profile and explains why maintenance stopped. [#168905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168905), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@ezimerman](https://github.com/ezimerman) for the incident report and repair follow-up.
  - An updater with this fix no longer copies unrelated neighboring packages or fails when those packages change. [#168927](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168927), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Package updates spend less time checking the same unchanged runtime files repeatedly. [#168958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168958), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A managed update accepted for background work now says it is pending, not failed; the helper still has to finish before the result is known. [#168993](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168993), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and issue reporter [@sayitfast](https://github.com/sayitfast).
  - An installation managed by pacman now tells you to update through your system package manager rather than trying to replace itself. [#169055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169055), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sahilsatralkar](https://github.com/sahilsatralkar), and issue reporter [@Pegorim](https://github.com/Pegorim).
  - Uninterrupted npm updates on Unix-like systems avoid repeat checks of a newly prepared package; recovery still verifies its contents. [#169075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169075), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recovery from an interrupted update no longer waits the wrong length of time after a clock change. [#169168](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169168), thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - The first update from a Bun-pinned macOS npm-global 2026.9.9 install needs a temporary switch to Node, an update through 2026.10.1, then a return to Bun. [Bun guide](/install/bun), [#168901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168901), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Installation now checks a custom Bun path before changing anything and explains that the executable must be named `bun` or `bun.exe`. [#168982](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168982), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Opt-in Bun installations receive the updated build's memory-limit and worker-lifecycle fixes. [#169009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169009), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Source builders can make a runtime-only build, also used by Team server deployments. [#169039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169039), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Updates and [rollback](/install/updating/rollback-and-recovery) now leave more unrelated settings and checkout edits alone when a later step fails. Keep a matching database backup before restoring older code, because code rollback alone does not undo a data migration.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - A suspiciously shortened configuration file no longer replaces the healthy recovery copy that Doctor can use to restore your settings. [#152565](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152565), [reported in #152509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/152509). Thanks to [LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and [yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
  - Doctor and updates now keep a blocked local plugin's saved settings intact. The plugin stays blocked until you fix its unsafe path. [#168111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168111), [reported in #168066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168066). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - A Linux Gateway keeps running if its log folder disappears during log rotation. File logs may still be lost, and OpenClaw warns about that. [#160433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160433), [related report #130807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/130807). Thanks to [masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino), [WadeOwenWatts](https://github.com/WadeOwenWatts), [Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - A Tailscale outage during startup no longer leaves channels suppressed after the connection returns. Restore Tailscale and restart the Gateway to recover. [#162266](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162266), [related report #144845](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/144845). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [odrobnik](https://github.com/odrobnik).
  - Gateway worker memory limits work again with ordinary combinations of Node memory flags. The explicit `--exit-on-contradictory-flags` mode remains incompatible with some heap flags. [#166581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166581), [reports #157575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157575) and [#157630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/157630). Thanks to [valkyriweb](https://github.com/valkyriweb), [xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn), [goslingmanagment](https://github.com/goslingmanagment), and [waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault).
  - Checking a remote Gateway restart no longer waits the wrong length of time after a clock change. [#168087](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168087). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Cleanup keeps the latest recovery copy and other copies still needed to investigate an uncertain update. [Update capture cleanup](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168177). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Updates without a restart and older installed updaters continue running Doctor checks in their supported order. [Doctor inspection timing](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168350). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - `openclaw database verify-preservation` compares sealed original and candidate captures for the supported schema 24-to-25 migration. It requires both manifest digests and does not approve activation. Inspection of vector-backed tables may fail. [Database preservation check](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168321). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A linked plugin whose path and health are verified can finish migration during an update; unavailable or unrecognized plugin paths remain pending. [Linked plugin migration](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168190). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Repair can recognize an update still legitimately running instead of taking over its files. [Concurrent update repair](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168197), [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168000). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@farisoftfry](https://github.com/farisoftfry).
  - An updater with this fix can continue if a busy database prevents an optional progress entry, while still requiring records needed for recovery. [Update database contention](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168210), [contention report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168072). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna).
  - If an update cannot finish backing up the original state, its warning identifies the affected databases and backup error; the incomplete copy remains for investigation, not automatic restore. [#169282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169282), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169259). Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
  - New pre-migration recovery copies now include older Canvas and Memory files. Existing backups do not gain files they already missed. [#169436](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169436). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Backup SQLite discovery uses the current runtime, so affected Bun installations do not need a separate Node executable for this step. [#183476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183476) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - If you cancel workspace preparation and Git cleanup is uncertain, OpenClaw reports that uncertainty and keeps temporary recovery copies. [#184226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184226) Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
  - Retry temporary Windows package rollback failures and report retained recovery paths when the installed package root is missing ([#185160](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185160), [#187274](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187274)). Thanks to [@liuwqgit](https://github.com/liuwqgit), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and reporter [@syxscott](https://github.com/syxscott).
  - An immediate Git rollback now leaves newly detected checkout edits alone and shows manual recovery commands. Updater staging and built Control UI files are outside this check ([#185201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185201)). Thanks to [@wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web) and reporter [@syxscott](https://github.com/syxscott).
  - Completed supervised updates now appear in update history, allowing eligible earlier migration backups to be cleaned up explicitly while offline ([#185392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185392)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update verification now stops repeating a confirmed access refusal and can check local Gateway readiness when its other safeguards allow it ([#185995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185995)). Thanks to [@Yun-0000](https://github.com/Yun-0000) and reporter [@lakemike](https://github.com/lakemike).
  - Retain the selected installed Node runtime and service authority through eligible managed package updates ([#187108](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187108)). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Retain failed Doctor details in update history and advise stopping the Gateway through its owner and running `openclaw update repair` when service inspection is unavailable ([#187574](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187574)). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@patrick-erichsen](https://github.com/patrick-erichsen).
  - Verified package updates can clean up migration copies that are no longer needed, including copies associated with a recorded restore refusal ([#187581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187581)). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Doctor can repair an app-managed Gateway's settings and data after confirming it is offline; the app still owns service startup and shutdown. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b5176e33011942035ea43cbdcbd2cae0df944934)
  - Recovery checks that a migration backup is still the same file before using it. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/28df9fe159fc9fb833d25e2c534d5e780f9e4c50)
  - Offline Doctor and update maintenance can finish cleanup even if an older maintenance policy was retired. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/019fdde9a9ba6d9dbf6d1f41733d3e685df67220)
  - If Doctor cannot capture the original state in time, the warning names the deadline and advises checking your backup before retrying after the failure is resolved. Helper maintenance also stops after its parent disappears. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0b5fb78e3a2db38bf1b915a1bb7a8b2797d4867b), thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - After repairing the Gateway service, OpenClaw checks the current app autostart setting again. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/215cadc147444a22bf7f2d20963a8c56c961e1c9), thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - A healthy Gateway can pass update restart checks despite an old authentication warning; an actual failed health check or version mismatch still blocks verification. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b391dee01d810309067c18c8c681e2d984758724), thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- After an update, the result now says whether the core installation finished but channel, plugin or restart checks still need attention. A recorded success describes that update, not the Gateway's current health.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Update status shows the recorded failure, recovery steps and last completed activation. That history does not establish whether the Gateway is healthy right now. [Update status and recovery](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168203). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Before an update, status and dry run can show database differences for a prepared candidate. They cannot judge an unprepared target or promise activation just because versions match. [Immutable update inspection](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168295). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update output shows how long preparation, cleanup, activation and Doctor checks took. Most of these timings become available on the next update after installing this version. [Update phase timings](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168290). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Update results show time spent preparing the runtime and service. The handoff time may appear during this installation; other new timings need the updated updater on a later run. [Updater and service timings](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168409). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - A recovery helper can clear a completed update record blocking an older updater while keeping the record for inspection. [Completed receipt recovery](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168388), [reported macOS blocker](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168092), [related Linux report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166135). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@k94118](https://github.com/k94118), [@kimjinsuhh](https://github.com/kimjinsuhh), [@GoldenrodScript](https://github.com/GoldenrodScript) and [@neilofneils404](https://github.com/neilofneils404).
  - Remove obsolete warnings for completed plugin migrations and keep package-verification time limits steady when the system clock changes. [#169004](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169004), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@adenzhou1350](https://github.com/adenzhou1350), and issue reporter [@WG-Mojo](https://github.com/WG-Mojo).
  - A newer CLI can clear some aborted 2026.9.8 update records, but warns when it cannot recheck their old contents. If that cleanup fails, repair reports the failure. [#169027](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169027), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@chedong](https://github.com/chedong) for the report and investigation.
  - A completed update keeps its success record even if reporting it fails, while accounts still stopped after repair receive a warning. [#169037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169037), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@kvnloo](https://github.com/kvnloo), and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Before another update, repair can clear eligible stale package records and archive completed results. A dry run shows the plan without changing files. [#169038](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169038), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@mkdelta221](https://github.com/mkdelta221), [@waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault), [@clawdgawd](https://github.com/clawdgawd), [@yoyo837](https://github.com/yoyo837), [@lishewen](https://github.com/lishewen), [@nukk-pain](https://github.com/nukk-pain), [@jullianatolentino](https://github.com/jullianatolentino), [@joshpetras](https://github.com/joshpetras), and [@creanet-64](https://github.com/creanet-64) for the reports and recovery follow-ups.
  - Update failures explain dependency links that reach OpenClaw-owned files and show the observed Gateway service status when readiness cannot be verified. See the [runtime retention guidance](/cli/update#runtime-retention-failures). [#169280](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169280). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - If an update cannot read an optional setting, its warning now says why and leaves the current service unchanged. [#169284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169284), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169261). Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Doctor avoids misleading backup warnings for several bundled migrations and gives guidance when a plugin's migration coverage is unknown. See [plugin migration recovery coverage](/cli/doctor/state-migrations#plugin-migration-recovery-coverage). [#169295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169295). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - An update can finish with a channel warning only after the Gateway itself passes service, version and readiness checks. The channel still needs attention. [#172494](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172494), reported in [#172391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172391). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@sbc2016](https://github.com/sbc2016).
  - If refreshing shell completions times out after the installation and cleanup finish, the update can still report success with a warning. Run `openclaw completion --write-state` to refresh completions manually. [#172502](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172502), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor no longer drops older channel settings that still await migration while saving unrelated update repairs. [#172791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172791), thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - An upgrade can replace eligible old Linux Gateway service files with private permissions; a separate Doctor repair still needs your approval. [#172790](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172790), thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@wei887-os](https://github.com/wei887-os) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/152533).
  - Update warnings identify a missing pacman or FreeBSD pkg executable instead of suggesting a database repair for a package manager that is unavailable. [#173293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173293), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), and [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
  - A verified core installation can finish with a warning if later plugin checks cannot complete. Retry them with `openclaw update repair`, stopping the Gateway first if exclusive access is required; a failed readiness check or uncertain cleanup still blocks completion. [#173341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173341), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@yoyo837](https://github.com/yoyo837) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171096).
  - Updating an unhealthy Gateway in the same installation no longer consumes the new Gateway's startup window waiting for the old one; the replacement still must pass its checks. [#173358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173358), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A successful update report clears plugin warnings that have been resolved and points to Doctor when more work remains. [#173395](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173395), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi), and [@bhedanikhilkumar-code](https://github.com/bhedanikhilkumar-code).
  - A global package update using `--no-restart` can still be followed by a Gateway-initiated restart. The flag skips only the updater's restart; check Gateway status after startup and keep recovery backups until it is verified. [#173409](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173409), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@mwbroxson](https://github.com/mwbroxson) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171311).

  </details>

- Supported [update recovery](/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) can resume more updates blocked by temporary contention or abandoned work, while refusing to repeat steps whose outcome is uncertain. An older broken updater may still need the documented manual recovery path.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Slow database checks no longer falsely exhaust systemd service-inspection time during Linux Doctor or update repair. The checks can still make the overall command slow. [Linux maintenance inspection](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168161), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168031). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@Tosko4](https://github.com/Tosko4).
  - Temporary progress-output congestion no longer aborts database metadata inventory in an updater containing the fix. An older updater that fails before activation cannot acquire this fix during that attempt. [Update progress output](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168493), [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/161042). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@creanet-64](https://github.com/creanet-64).
  - The restart check for an update now uses its intended time limit. [Update readiness deadline](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168496). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A newly activated Gateway is not reported as ready until its session list can be read. An empty list counts as a successful read. [Activation session check](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168561). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Team-managed updates can finish migration verification after Doctor removes an obsolete optional Workshop table. [Managed update verification](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168577). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recording an update no longer holds the database writer while waiting for its first approval, so scheduled work and cleanup have more room to run. The final save still requires the lock. [#169283](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169283), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169260). Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
  - Windows updates entered through short or differently cased paths keep the files their running updater needs and can clean up abandoned copies. [#169292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169292). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Windows database-recovery helpers now pass their temporary-folder checks; this does not make every update command available on Windows. [#169397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169397). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - If a disk remount changes device numbers during an update, a separately installed helper can resolve an eligible unfinished installation without discarding its recovery evidence. Follow the [remount recovery guide](/install/updating#recover-an-unfinished-publication-after-a-remount) before using it. [#169410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169410), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169050). Thanks [@patrickschiller](https://github.com/patrickschiller) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Git updates clean up their temporary worktree when progress reporting fails and retain the original update error alongside cleanup diagnostics. [#169454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169454), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169446). Thanks [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).
  - Update and Doctor checks no longer fail just because an installation has no agents, and they refuse unsafe links in Canvas folders before migration. [#169516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169516). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - On resolved npm-global installations, the updater avoids retaining unrelated global packages while keeping the dependencies it reaches. The first upgrade from an older updater still uses that updater's behavior. [#169551](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169551), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169373), [related investigation](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169521). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen) and [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant).
  - Doctor can keep an update going if a later process check fails after it already verified the updater. It still refuses one it never verified. [#169563](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169563). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  Several of these update fixes depend on the updater already installed when an update starts. Installing a corrected candidate does not change an older updater's earlier steps.

  - If service refresh refuses before changing anything, repair can restart the preserved and rechecked Gateway service while still reporting the refusal. [#169658](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169658). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor can continue under a verified updater even if it cannot inspect a higher supervisor. New repair commands keep their identity if setup fails; older anonymous records still need attention. [#169665](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169665). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Managed Team upgrades use the candidate version's Doctor migration when supported; unknown future database versions and downgrades remain blocked. [#169752](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169752). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Saved ACP conversation information can be brought over once its plugin is available, without adding it to a conversation that has closed or been replaced. [#169759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169759). Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).
  - Doctor can try to restore the Gateway after a temporary cleanup failure clears on retry, but still reports the failure; unfinished cleanup continues to block restart. [#169783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169783). Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).
  - Update recovery no longer rolls back a verified healthy Gateway after migration and discards its newer saved work. [#169841](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169841). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Supported older Node updaters no longer mistake a clock transition for lost ownership and undo an already activated update. [#169915](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169915). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - A Doctor command that never started no longer leaves a false running-process record that blocks another recovery attempt. [#169936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169936). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor checks deferred until after restart now run against the updated Gateway and report any work they could not finish. [#169940](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169940), building on [#168350](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168350). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - New or repaired pnpm Gateway services use a stable project path, and nested pnpm installations can be recognized for updates. Existing version-pinned service commands may need repair. See [#170003](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170003) and [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169059). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@xilopaint](https://github.com/xilopaint) and [@k-sekula](https://github.com/k-sekula).
  - Offline Doctor and update recovery now pause scheduled database housekeeping so it cannot alter a recovery copy while one is being made in [#170022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170022). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update repair can settle eligible journals after filesystem device numbers change, including a verified newer manual installation. Older updaters may need the documented recovery helper first. See [#170046](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170046), [#169050](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169050) and [#169352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169352). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@patrickschiller](https://github.com/patrickschiller) and [@SergeiAug](https://github.com/SergeiAug).
  - Doctor now checks for unsupported old files before changing a database, so you can take the supported intermediate upgrade route if it refuses. See [#170051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170051) and [#169918](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169918). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@bautrey](https://github.com/bautrey).
  - Once the installed updater contains this fix, pnpm 12 update cleanup leaves the launcher pointing at the new package. A launcher already broken still needs manual repair. See [#170131](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170131) and [#169244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169244). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant) and [@k-sekula](https://github.com/k-sekula).
  - Standalone repair that must wait now reports an incomplete result and tells you how to stop the right Gateway. An update's own final step instead reports a warning. See [#170139](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170139) and [#169462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169462). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@jjgao](https://github.com/jjgao).
  - Doctor can find abandoned updater files that were blocking later npm updates. See [#170170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170170) and [#169463](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169463). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sunnyshu0925](https://github.com/sunnyshu0925) and [@warhangel](https://github.com/warhangel).
  - Linux update repair can clean up abandoned updater files even when ordinary login processes use their parent directory. A live holder still blocks cleanup. [#171488](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171488) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Thanks to [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for [reporting the issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171410).
  - Scheduled work can proceed while an update waits for permission to save its result. Both the installed updater and its worker need this fix. [#171493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171493) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Thanks to [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for the reports and investigation in [#169260](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169260) and [#171411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171411), and for the earlier partial repair.
  - If Doctor fails before changing databases, an update can restore the previous package and databases. Unattributed database writes still prevent automatic restoration. [#172151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172151). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - An update no longer refuses its database check just because an active agent is writing. Older installed updaters may still warn while capturing the original state. [#172196](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172196). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - If Doctor cannot inspect the updater's process tree after an otherwise verified installation, the update stays installed and reports how to finish maintenance. [#172247](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172247), reported in [#172213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172213). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@homeworld520](https://github.com/homeworld520).
  - Agent data can migrate correctly when updating from 2026.10.5 beta.2 or beta.3 to a main-based build. [#172249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172249). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - A dev-channel Git update fetches the selected upstream and stops with an explanation if that remote is invalid or excluded. [#172199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172199). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - If cleanup of a prepared update copy fails, the recovery guide explains how to proceed and what safe error detail to include in a report. [#172171](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172171), related to [#172104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172104). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@warezio](https://github.com/warezio).

  </details>

- Doctor and common configuration commands do less repeated scanning, and warnings better distinguish a real repair problem from an unchanged or already-settled one.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Read-only Doctor checks no longer repeatedly scan the entire database when no writer is cached. [#169745](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169745). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor repair spends less time rescanning unchanged state while it continues. [#169905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169905). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Routine configuration reads no longer check the deferred plugin migration ledger. Doctor and managed updates retain pending repair information. [PR #170230](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170230). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor and conversation reads no longer load model-provider connections just to clean up saved transcript checkpoints. [#170585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170585). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Common `openclaw config get`, `set` and `validate` commands no longer wait for plugin database checks they do not need. [#170685](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170685). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor reuses a valid database check while backing up plugins instead of making you wait for the same scan repeatedly. [#170934](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170934), [report #170773](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170773), related [#169745](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169745). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@rozenpl](https://github.com/rozenpl), and [@jb510](https://github.com/jb510).
  - Database workers can reuse a valid check when they reopen the same file instead of rescanning it. [#167739](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167739). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Chat turns spend less time on repeated database checks and bookkeeping. [#167965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167965). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Chat turns and idle pairing checks do less database work; an external supervisor must hold the installation offline before claiming it. [#168002](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168002). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fixes a database-lock loss that could crash OpenClaw when an agent reads a live SQLite file. The broader recurring-crash report remains unresolved. [SQLite lock protection](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168274), [related crash report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167671). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@3n8](https://github.com/3n8).
  - Gateway startup and later worker opens avoid repeated full database scans after index repair. [Database checks after index repair](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168517), [related startup investigation](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168468). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@TommyLei666](https://github.com/TommyLei666).
  - A Gateway running for a long time no longer keeps finished-run histories from model versions it has already replaced. [Model generation memory retention](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168584). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Node uses its normal compiler settings again; some Mac watcher and certificate-related stalls are still unresolved. [Process shutdown](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168587). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Backup records do not silently retry an uncertain Gateway write. Approving a device while offline still requires exclusive access to its state. [Backup records and device approval](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168466). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor no longer warns about node hosting on a local-only Gateway that never enabled it. A separate Windows connection problem is not fixed by this change. [Node-hosting diagnostics](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168218), [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/164396). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@absol89](https://github.com/absol89).
  - Vault setup gives the specific correction for invalid secret IDs, including the required path and field format. [Vault setup errors](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168272). Thanks [@nocodet888-arch](https://github.com/nocodet888-arch) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Termux can save a database snapshot when the usual filesystem copy path is unavailable. [#168629](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168629) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [@yxshee](https://github.com/yxshee) for reporting the issue.
  - Doctor can check Telegram and WhatsApp settings without starting their full channel runtimes, reducing unnecessary waiting. [#168869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168869) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Rechecking an unchanged one-time schedule no longer creates an extra Doctor recovery copy. Actual repairs still keep backups. [#168938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168938), thanks [@harshitgupta31415](https://github.com/harshitgupta31415) and issue reporter [@wg-mojo](https://github.com/wg-mojo).
  - Doctor no longer warns about a different system Node when the Node selected for the Gateway passes; if the system Node fails its database check, the message names that executable. [#169007](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169007), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Yun-0000](https://github.com/Yun-0000), and issue reporter [@amueller80](https://github.com/amueller80).
  - Doctor cleans up its private plugin inspection copies after checking them ([01ead786](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/01ead786dc19b1cf9921dfcf90917206d876dfc1)).
  - Doctor keeps plugin repair and diagnostic steps available until its remaining checks finish ([310bce6a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/310bce6a751791a9f61ce7a3bfcb3fd79653642b)).

  </details>

- Package and source updates are less likely to lose their launcher or running updater files during replacement, and verified early failures can leave the previous Gateway usable. Several protections must already be in the installed updater before that attempt begins.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Older updaters can validate a candidate without rejecting it solely for Doctor bookkeeping changes in [#170195](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170195). The separate startup hang in [#169953](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169953) remains unresolved. Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@maestrokling](https://github.com/maestrokling) and [@frxiaobei](https://github.com/frxiaobei).
  - An update or Doctor repair can retry if another database writer finishes just as it checks for access in [#170196](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170196). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Repair now loads the target's settings before choosing a repair model; temporary settings used for that repair do not replace your saved configuration. [PR #170276](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170276). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Managed updates record a refusal and restore the Gateway when a requester's access is revoked while its channel is disabled. [PR #170365](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170365). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Large configuration responses no longer truncate the candidate read that refreshes eligible Gateway service definitions. If that optional read still fails, wait for the update to finish and run `openclaw gateway install --force` with the same `--profile` or `--dev` option. [PR #170373](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170373), fixing [#170331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170331). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for reporting and investigation, and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for advice.
  - Run eligible Linux managed update handoffs in a separate user service, outside the invoking agent and Gateway lifetime and the Gateway unit's memory limit. [#187215](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187215). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and reporter [@masterdevill](https://github.com/masterdevill).
  - Bun updates no longer carry an already-finished database-copy lock into the next update step. [#170519](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170519). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Package repair can settle an untouched preparation after a remount once it verifies the installed package and launchers. It refuses changed live content or unfinished publication. [#170676](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170676). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the fix and [git-larry-roots](https://github.com/git-larry-roots) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170443).
  - Successful snapshot-backed state reads no longer fail solely because best-effort temporary cleanup is busy. The warning remains and cleanup can happen later. [#170605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170605). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An update or rollback can give a busy but responsive Gateway time to finish, while still checking that the expected version is ready ([#170696](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170696)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Removing a Linux Gateway or node service now refreshes systemd so the removed service disappears.
