If that refresh fails, run `systemctl --user daemon-reload`; uninstalling again will not retry it. [#173048](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173048), thanks [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/127359).
  - Prepared source updaters retain database backup helpers before replacing the runtime, avoiding a missing-module failure when creating rollback snapshots. [#173587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173587), thanks [@vishnusenthil-16](https://github.com/vishnusenthil-16) and [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/173754).
  - Update restart preparation waits for a verified new Gateway to publish ownership before treating its startup as a failure. [#173588](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173588), thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Gateway and node service changes can check their selected runtime without copying the entire database again. [#172500](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172500), thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - Source updates retain the running updater's modules after activation, avoiding missing-module errors during later update steps. [#174867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174867). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@vishnusenthil-16](https://github.com/vishnusenthil-16).
  - After an authentication rejection, update verification can use local readiness and runtime identity checks and record a warning. [#175735](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175735). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@sesgoe](https://github.com/sesgoe).
  - Update repair reports available Gateway ownership details and the stalled inspection phase when a service check times out. [#177560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177560). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@sdamian](https://github.com/sdamian).
  - Doctor started by an update now uses the update's time limit when none is specified separately. [#173862](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173862). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@jsf9789](https://github.com/jsf9789).
  - With `--no-restart`, an update checks the running build as well as its version before reporting verification. [#174649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174649). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Setup reports a failed installed Gateway restart and shows recovery commands instead of reporting success. [#174767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174767). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223).
  - Gateway service installation no longer lets the retired `CLAWDBOT_GATEWAY_PASSWORD` variable suppress token setup. Operators relying on it need a supported password or token. [#175416](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175416). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Fresh provider setup retains the selected model's metadata without inadvertently restricting the model picker to that model. Existing explicit restrictions still apply. [#175945](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175945). Thanks [@matthematics1137](https://github.com/matthematics1137), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@jb-dev1](https://github.com/jb-dev1).
  - Interrupted update recovery accepts matching symlink launchers with different recorded modes while retaining the other integrity checks. An installation already blocked on an older updater needs a recovery helper containing this fix. [Recovery guidance](/install/updating#recover-an-unfinished-publication-after-a-remount) · [#177059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177059). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@BodegaClaw](https://github.com/BodegaClaw).

  </details>

- Restoring a supported older backup keeps its command-approval allowlist instead of turning it into a deny-all policy.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Preserves supported legacy approval policies while still refusing malformed data. [#164297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164297), included in [#181464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181464). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@dor-sr](https://github.com/dor-sr), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@shanselman](https://github.com/shanselman) for the combined backport.

  </details>

- Supported [backups and rollback](/install/backups#restore) can restore more verified older archives and keep recovery copies when an update cannot safely undo database changes. Restoring older code alone does not undo migrated data; use the matching database backup when needed.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - An updater containing the snapshot fix avoids falsely reporting an unchanged agent database as changed when an exited writer left SQLite sidecars. Later database changes can still prevent restoration. See [rollback and recovery](/install/updating/rollback-and-recovery). [PR #170351](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170351), reported in [#170335](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170335). Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Supported older global and agent snapshots can be verified and restored. Newer, inconsistent or wrong-owner snapshots remain refused. [#170492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170492). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the fix and [davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170244).
  - Package updates retain unsealed database snapshots for manual recovery after successful activation. The updater performing the update must contain this fix. [#170523](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170523). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the fix and [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for the [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170335).
  - Updated installers report unsafe recovery-file permissions or a changed update owner before attempting recovery ([#170787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170787), [#170076](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170076)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@zrzavywa](https://github.com/zrzavywa), and [@niriakot](https://github.com/niriakot).
  - A Gateway can retain verified ownership after sleep or a backward clock change, and an authorized repair can restart it when access is blocked ([#170790](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170790), [#169829](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169829), [#170525](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170525), [#170597](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170597)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen), [@AnvinaLI](https://github.com/AnvinaLI), [@sgh6688](https://github.com/sgh6688), [@waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault), [@andersgreg](https://github.com/andersgreg), and [@shawnholt](https://github.com/shawnholt).
  - Private update checks help preserve database compatibility if an upgrade from 2026.9.2 with native plugins is refused. Databases already migrated by an earlier failed update still need a matching pre-update backup or a compatible newer package. [#171015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171015), [report #170939](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170939), related [#153657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153657), [#158414](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158414) and [#168340](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168340). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@bamorem](https://github.com/bamorem).
  - Update status checks current files and session history before showing old migration warnings, so resolved findings can disappear while uncertain ones remain. [#177096](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177096). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ScientificProgrammer](https://github.com/ScientificProgrammer), [@jj5638521](https://github.com/jj5638521) and [@ourcoms2018](https://github.com/ourcoms2018).
  - If `TMPDIR` points to another volume, updates can put temporary plugin and database-check files there. Backups and durable files still need space on their usual volumes. [#178009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178009). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
  - Update rehearsal omits the false loopback-only onboarding warning caused by its temporary configuration, while keeping genuine Doctor warnings. [#178155](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178155). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
  - Before downloading an incompatible package, an update checks the Gateway's Node version and explains how to change a pinned service runtime ([#180769](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180769)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sglion777](https://github.com/sglion777), [@Almost-Certified-ops](https://github.com/Almost-Certified-ops), [@billpang1-sketch](https://github.com/billpang1-sketch), [@rayluo88](https://github.com/rayluo88) and [@jcheng510](https://github.com/jcheng510).
  - Managed source installation repair can handle changed files without turning app autostart back on after you disabled it ([#180356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180356)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Keep snapshots marked for manual database recovery when Doctor refuses automatic restoration after capture. The installed updater must contain the fix to protect its own cleanup ([#180010](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180010), [#170335](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170335), [#170523](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170523)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
  - An update can keep a verified database copy with an outdated search index for Doctor or startup to rebuild. The installed updater must already contain this fix ([#181206](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181206)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@ndakota79](https://github.com/ndakota79).
  - Use the installation's own pnpm 10 shim and global bin path during update preflight. The installed updater must already contain the repair ([#181456](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181456)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@rayluo88](https://github.com/rayluo88).
  - If an update or Doctor command loses its supervising process, its helper exits so a later command or Gateway start can recover. When maintenance is still active, the refusal names the holder and points to update status ([#181695](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181695)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@PonySlayer28](https://github.com/PonySlayer28).
  - Doctor limits media-repair scans and undoes an in-progress rewrite if it loses maintenance access. [#171655](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171655) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Cancelling a Doctor migration can now interrupt its pre-migration backup rather than leaving the backup running unnoticed. [#171947](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171947) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Supported Doctor migrations can back up only the tables they will change after verifying the source. Other repairs still use a full backup. [#172671](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172671), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Older backups with hardlinks can be verified and restored when each link has one clear target. Missing or ambiguous targets are still refused. [#173070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173070), thanks [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@mgonto](https://github.com/mgonto) for the [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/89257).
  - SQLite backup listing recognizes Git worktrees and points operators to Git backup history. [#176656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176656). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restarting the same official Docker container can reuse its verified recovery copy while Doctor continues repairs. Recreating the container or starting a new activation still needs a new copy. [#173711](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173711). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@8bitsforge](https://github.com/8bitsforge).
  - Backup imports now reject an invalid file-size count instead of interpreting it as a different size ([#178585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178585), [#178491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/178491)). Thanks [@YangManBOBO](https://github.com/YangManBOBO) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- Update checks refuse more unsafe replacements before changing a running installation and give clearer permission errors. Avoid editing installation files during an update; a shell command that combines maintenance with Gateway startup may not receive the same live-file protection.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - A source build no longer fails because a runtime check starts before Control UI files finish publishing. [#170455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170455). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Repeated starts from a source checkout can reuse compiled modules rather than compiling them again. [#170514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170514). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the fix and [LouisGameDev](https://github.com/LouisGameDev) for the [earlier report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/73037).
  - An optional dreaming-schedule check no longer stops a core update or Doctor repair when it fails. Existing jobs remain, and a warning points to `openclaw doctor --fix` to retry. A successful schedule read also remains usable if temporary cleanup fails ([#170754](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170754), [#147956](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/147956)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80), and [@anhnt3](https://github.com/anhnt3).
  - Standalone Doctor can repair state for an app-owned Gateway after confirming it is offline and belongs to the same account. The Mac app still handles its restart and update ([#170768](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170768), [#170724](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170724)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@shanselman](https://github.com/shanselman).
  - Update repair and Doctor now wait longer for a force-stopped helper to exit before declaring cleanup uncertain ([#170779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170779)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor no longer reports a false database-checkpoint failure while another authorized offline maintenance task owns the state ([#170783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170783)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Explain recovery after a failed Gateway restart preparation or failed embeddings cleanup. The related reports provide context for the broader cleanup ([#170826](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170826), [#40226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/40226), [#58789](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/58789), [#82712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/82712), [#103736](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/103736), [#112336](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/112336), [#137417](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137417)). A failed restart preparation can require reapplying configuration or a manual restart. Failed embeddings cleanup blocks the affected agent and provider scope until restart. Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An update can keep checking its state even if it cannot show one temporary progress message; that message may be missing. [#170955](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170955), related [#168493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168493) and [#170978](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170978) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Update processes can exit after finishing instead of remaining open because of migrated work. [#170978](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170978) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor loads valid settings before checking context-engine plugins, avoiding a plugin check based on incomplete configuration. [#170984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170984), [report #170715](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170715) and [#170689](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170689). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@sasan1200](https://github.com/sasan1200).
  - An explicit `openclaw update --timeout` now covers both the update and its recovery. Recovery may still be pending while database writes finish. [#171077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171077), [report #170666](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170666). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini).
  - An update refuses to replace files used by a running Gateway if it cannot inspect the service. On Windows, it also refuses when it cannot identify the running Gateway's package; a failed maintenance resume is reported, not hidden. [#171101](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171101), [reports #170774](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170774) and [#171036](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171036). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), reporter [@homeworld520](https://github.com/homeworld520) and source-audit reporter [@Yolo521-bot](https://github.com/Yolo521-bot).
  - After Doctor repair, channel tokens loaded from environment variables no longer appear to be saved as plaintext. Literal saved tokens still get a warning. [#171116](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171116), [report #171022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171022). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@exGeni](https://github.com/exGeni).
  - Update checks no longer mistake a recognized maintenance command for a running Gateway. Avoid chaining maintenance and Gateway startup in one shell command, because that combined command may also evade the live-installation check. [#170462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170462). Thanks to [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  </details>

- Installation and Doctor errors now point to the failed step and a more useful retry or repair path. If your configuration includes other files, review a proposed single-file repair before accepting it because it can revert settings from those included files.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Recognize the Dutch Scheduled Task access-denied message and show the existing Windows elevation or manual-update guidance. An older installed updater still needs the elevated or manual first update ([#178219](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/178219), [#170956](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170956)). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@Rene-F](https://github.com/Rene-F) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Return a structured JSON error for an invalid update repair channel without changing the configured channel ([#180822](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180822)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@leon-sk668](https://github.com/leon-sk668).
  - Escape invalid update-channel values in human and JSON diagnostics so control characters cannot create misleading terminal lines ([#181298](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181298)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@TUARAN](https://github.com/TUARAN).
  - Service inspection now preserves empty command arguments, so Windows tasks and Linux services are checked as they were configured ([#181213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181213)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@notlakshitha](https://github.com/notlakshitha) and [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80).
  - Blocked update messages distinguish retained installation custody from an updater that is still running and suggest status and repair commands. [#182774](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182774) Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - Source updates on Node or the pinned Bun runtime retain the files they still need while replacing the installation. [#183429](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183429) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Queued service maintenance commands keep their elapsed-time budgets when the system clock changes. [#183104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183104) Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Debug logging no longer corrupts JSON results from update checks and Doctor recovery. Checking a candidate still requires a compatible updater. [#182850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182850) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor no longer suggests an ineffective Skill Workshop grant for an agent that deliberately denies the tool. It still offers a hint for a profile-only exclusion. [#171314](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171314), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini) for [reporting](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171274).
  - Repair missing session columns before opening affected agents. If a cold read-only access reports that repair is needed, restart the Gateway or run `openclaw doctor --fix`. [#171634](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171634) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Doctor advises retrying after SQLite snapshot contention instead of suggesting a backup restore for that condition. It still refuses repair when readiness cannot be verified. [#176779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176779). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@WG-Mojo](https://github.com/WG-Mojo).
  - In the demonstrated single-file configuration, Doctor can back up and remove empty retired plugin install metadata. If your configuration uses includes, this recovery may revert unrelated settings. [#173851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173851). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@fengfan201314-eng](https://github.com/fengfan201314-eng).
  - If Doctor spends ten minutes capturing the original state, it begins cancelling that work and shows how to check or start the Gateway if restoration fails. Cancellation itself can take longer ([#181941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181941)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@kenrohde](https://github.com/kenrohde) and [@kqtxmaster-boop](https://github.com/kqtxmaster-boop).
  - Provider registration keeps a default OAuth profile repair when explicit repairs are also declared. [#183183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/183183) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - If a terminal hangup interrupts a repair, Doctor attempts to restore the managed Gateway it stopped. [#182901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182901) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Null-Bug-Null](https://github.com/Null-Bug-Null) for the related report.
  - Doctor can bring older conversation text into its current format after reopening the database in [#170126](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170126). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- [Doctor](/gateway/doctor/checks) no longer blocks recovery cleanup with a false warning that the database changed during its inspection.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  The sidecar-check repair from [#186411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186411), adapted for Doctor in [#188854](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188854). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@kaguranananana](https://github.com/kaguranananana) for the original work, and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) for the adaptation.

  </details>

- Fixes supported [Bun-only installs](/install/bun-compatibility#bun-only-installs) leaving the `openclaw` command unavailable, including when a broken Node command is found first.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Installation recognizes the supported Bun executable selected for the package and can continue launcher setup past an unavailable Node command. A missing launcher can be created for Bun's standard global package and bin folders. [Runtime detection](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/79c6be816494b1a7c7fc57223089570f443cc24b), [missing launcher](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/dd896a5ef4834ec55be5be78f05cb6df7788d23f) and [Node fallback](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/964e7558463b0999cb3de74786e51528c392a5ef). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Doctor's btrfs repair no longer leaves verified migration backups permanently ineligible for normal cleanup.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Keep verified migration backups eligible for `openclaw update cleanup` after Doctor repairs a btrfs database directory. Cleanup still needs its usual verification and consent; backups blocked by an earlier repair and retained NOCOW directories are not removed by this fix. [#169893](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169893) by [@xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [@farisoftfry](https://github.com/farisoftfry) for [the report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169674).

  </details>

- [Git backup](/install/backups#versioned-backups-to-a-git-repository) and pruning now preserve unrelated files and staged edits. Opt-in secret exclusions omit identified bundled-plugin credentials, not every possible secret or anything already in Git history; restoring a redacted backup may require signing in again.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Apply `--exclude-secrets` to PostgreSQL Git exports while retaining the schema needed for restore. [#174698](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174698). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - Omit identified bundled-plugin credentials from redacted SQLite and PostgreSQL Git backups while preserving unrelated plugin state. Readable Discord thread bindings retain routing fields but omit their webhook token. [#186953](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186953). Thanks [@art22s](https://github.com/art22s), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna).
  - Recognize SQLite databases by header for managed snapshots and unmanaged-copy warnings. [#148857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148857), [reported in #148796](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/148796). Thanks to [LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [migelizaga-rgb](https://github.com/migelizaga-rgb).
  - Preserve unrelated files and staging in Git backups, and support verification and restoration of historical refs. Declared artifact collisions and corrupt backups still stop the operation. [#162706](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162706). Thanks to [oshaughnessy-junior](https://github.com/oshaughnessy-junior), [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - When SQLite backup listing is pointed at a Git backup repository, explain the mismatch and suggest the Git history command. [#167935](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167935), [reported in #167934](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167934). Thanks to [PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13) and [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>

- After a session database is replaced, new work refuses an old database location instead of silently using the wrong conversations. If a write's result is uncertain, check what was saved before retrying.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - After repairing a conversation, its participant list updates immediately instead of showing old membership. [#171481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171481) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Opening a conversation with pending approvals no longer waits on unrelated database writes before showing them. [#171605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171605) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reads can resume after a temporary cleanup failure when it is safe to retry. Failed native retirement or a protected file replacement still blocks them. [#171709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171709) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Thanks to [residentb2b](https://github.com/residentb2b) for the [report and diagnosis](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162085), [homeworld520](https://github.com/homeworld520) for the detailed reproduction, and [sunny251008-debug](https://github.com/sunny251008-debug) for the affected-version report.
  - An interrupted main-session task remains eligible for another startup recovery if shutdown stops its accepted continuation before execution. The existing three-attempt limit and exhaustion notice still apply. [#172023](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172023), reported in [#172000](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172000). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [jeanmonet](https://github.com/jeanmonet).
  - An older finished database read no longer interferes with a newer read using the same query ([697e5bc1](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/697e5bc126a32cbd2ab704938ade75e5d34ebc6d)).
  - Creating the first state database can wait briefly when another operation temporarily holds its structure ([fa23864b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/fa23864b643566823648ef8060ed5ba5e2442a40)). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reading conversation details no longer races a simultaneous save of its transcript. [Merge commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/c0a235bbbb25df3938ffd2c1d13984e231de4557)
  - Accept session context through an alias of the same physical database while continuing to reject a different copied store. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/cba73231243d85ff0e9e34d7b3c35cd869b0430b), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A first conversation turn now loads its settings before deciding where to save the transcript. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/accded4b85c7075b196475cfb3db95505115a1f4)
  - Initializing an unrelated database feature no longer changes who can access a profile. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/c445da4029e17aca9bf1288c08389dc35698d634), thanks to [joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
  - A completed conversation deletion stays completed after its database worker exits normally. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3d9e3fb92051d8e5dc248b226599432aad8ccc26)
  - A repaired conversation view now shows the participant information copied during repair. [Commit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4aa62334122ed29f605f9820c4604539b3ccee77)
  - If creating an outdated database is refused, another agent's healthy conversation reads continue. [#169895](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169895). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Queued database work can wait briefly for a temporary structure change without running out of order in [#170135](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170135). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Health checks can connect while conversations load in [#170151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170151). Readiness continues to wait for initialization. Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Reject first-send session creation if the prepared session database was replaced before confirmation ([#170716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170716)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An existing session-store handle stays with its admitted database if an alias changes, while a fresh lookup resolves the new target. [#171375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171375), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Memory-backed session callers receive publication errors after committed writes instead of false success. The data remains saved, and committed trajectory batches are not appended again on a later flush. [#171419](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171419), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Agent setup now refuses an old conversation ownership view if the conversation changed while setup ran ([e338bef1](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e338bef1348d7c9842242be657b3f595169d9127)).
  - A conversation read no longer falsely reports that its data changed when tracking starts mid-read ([3a9aef66](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3a9aef6652a496192c3a98e046ec5bf961128559)).
  - The owner of a shared conversation can continue sending turns, and pending-input failures remain visible ([f1fbf170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f1fbf170dea3c0c631031e477dc61dc06627fba1)).
  - Saving state gets one retry if a handoff removes its lock folder at the same moment ([5f925029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5f925029e0800504fc04dd41c0cd297d37636c0f)).

  </details>

- On busy installations with many agents or sessions, Gateway startup and the first message do less blocking preparation work. A required agent still has to finish preparing before the Gateway reports ready.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Limits startup read bursts and retries temporary capacity rejections so healthy agents are less likely to remain degraded after a fleet starts. [#168638](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168638) by [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT), [@wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web), and [@karaaslanz](https://github.com/karaaslanz).
  - Move cold session and Memory preparation reads off the Gateway main thread. Before a Memory database exists, deep Memory status may incorrectly report an installed vector extension as unavailable. [#169164](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169164), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Restore unmanaged in-process restarts after configuration reload recovery and show the cause of lock reacquisition failures. [#169167](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169167), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Session catalog loading can finish when a Unix login shell ignores termination during executable-path discovery. [#169507](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169507). Thanks [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - Catalog refreshes reuse unchanged installed native ESM plugin modules, avoiding repeated retained copies. Replacing installed source requires a worker restart. [#169272](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169272), [earlier work](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162080), [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/159662). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@mcooks-cmd](https://github.com/mcooks-cmd), [@svdinu-jpg](https://github.com/svdinu-jpg) and [@eduherranz](https://github.com/eduherranz).
  - Gateway startup can prepare independent services at the same time instead of waiting for each in sequence. [#169328](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169328). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Starting a built Gateway can reuse compiled modules for that build. [#169365](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169365). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Queued requests can run between plugins loading at startup or reload. One plugin that blocks while loading can still delay them. [#169519](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169519). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A first message can prepare its model while OpenClaw records the workspace starting state. [#169592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169592). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A restart can reuse verified plugin migration information instead of checking it all again. [#169613](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169613). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - After a clean shutdown, startup can reuse an eligible database check; otherwise it performs the full check. [#169649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169649). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - An update can prepare Gateway code before switching versions. If that preparation fails, the Gateway still starts normally without it. [#169656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169656). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A slow service inspection no longer uses up the connection-check time allowed during Gateway startup. [#169787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169787). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Saved pinned conversations no longer make startup preparation loop and block the conversation list. [#169821](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169821). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Conversation-saving failures show useful recovery guidance, including complete session-list summaries, in [#170048](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170048). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Usage totals update after conversation compaction; normal shutdown cancellation makes less log noise, and memory indexing does less shutdown work. [PR #170239](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170239). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Control UI session requests under a probe-prefixed custom base path wait for startup instead of receiving a premature 403. Root probes and authentication retain their usual behavior. [PR #170316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170316). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway startup no longer gets stuck when an agent database is still preparing as a worker recovers. [PR #170348](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170348). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Gateway startup prepares independent services together and loads its chat connection code before saying it is ready. [PR #170401](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170401). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Your first message can start a conversation even when its configured database has not yet been created. [#170464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170464). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - A first message in a new conversation is no longer refused because an old usage refresh finished. [#170572](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170572). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - The default Gateway handshake deadline is 30 seconds, giving busy hosts more time to connect. Repeated connections can also avoid unnecessary owner-profile work. [#170621](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170621). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Agent turns and messages between conversations can use the updated runtime after overlapping startup refreshes ([#170877](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170877), [#170762](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170762)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
  - Usage and placement checks continue, attachments remain available for conversations with oversized history, and externally managed installs are identified as such. Native updates still require adoption ([#170905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170905)). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Installations with many agents can report startup readiness before the initial skills scan finishes; skills needed by a new turn are still prepared on demand. [#170933](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170933) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Startup checks independent conversations together instead of waiting for each one. [#171006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171006), related [#170849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170849) and [#170933](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170933) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Conversation views begin preparing while the Gateway connection starts, without skipping readiness checks. [#171028](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171028) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Compatible builds can reuse a verified database check after a clean shutdown, once the updated check has run. [#171038](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171038) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Startup does less recovery work for unrelated saved helper conversations and loads less task data at once. [#171046](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171046) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recover agent sessions when an overlapping config or secrets refresh replaces startup preparation. [#171054](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171054), [report #171018](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171018), related [#150755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150755) and [#165107](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165107). Thanks to [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), and [@homeworld520](https://github.com/homeworld520).
  - Return a readiness failure while a required agent database is still preparing, with a diagnostic for the affected agent. [#171063](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171063) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Workers save compiled code while running instead of leaving that work until shutdown; on slow storage, early work may briefly wait for the save. [#171298](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171298), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Starting the Gateway in a terminal no longer pauses the main process to flush its compiled-code cache. [#171358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171358), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Standalone agent turns using workspace skills prepare their runtime configuration before reading it. [#171209](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171209), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Recover a Gateway startup that has not bound its listener for over five minutes with `openclaw gateway stop`, then retry startup. [#171678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171678) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks to [TommyLei666](https://github.com/TommyLei666) for [reporting the issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171513).
  - Large fleets avoid repeated directory preparation and scheduling work. [#171805](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171805) addresses the [fleet report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171700), incorporating work from [#171778](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171778). The separate [SQLite store-capacity issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162497) remains. Thanks to [Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668), [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [chelsealong](https://github.com/chelsealong), and [609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT).
  - Bun Gateway startup handles SQLite admission timeouts without treating a worker's clock deadline as already expired. [#172756](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172756), thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
  - A Docker health check can find the Gateway through `OPENCLAW_GATEWAY_PORT` if it cannot read settings or ask a running instance for its port. [#172765](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172765), thanks [@xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
  - If a settings refresh replaces startup model preparation, the Gateway waits for the newer preparation instead of treating cancellation as a startup failure. A real failure still appears. [#173366](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173366), thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Jackten](https://github.com/Jackten) for the [related report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/173357).
  - Plugin loading does less file copying, which may shorten startup or the first response depending on the installation ([#180895](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180895)). Thanks [@Roddmason](https://github.com/Roddmason), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@n572770](https://github.com/n572770).
  - Creating a first conversation can proceed even while a usage update waits for database access ([6d479f5c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6d479f5c893a687e92683b7e896be26e4c8bedd3)).

  </details>

- Stock Bun now requires 1.4.4 or newer; the pinned OpenClaw Bun fork remains supported. Bun’s certificate fix is partial, so use [patched Node](/install/node-compatibility) when current rejection of compromised certificate keys is required. Read-only Kubernetes deployments also need the writable-cache setting with their image update to start successfully.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Include the missing workspace manifest in Docker production dependency builds ([a2d159ca](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a2d159cadcaf384fc6f7f4d84a7dc785e0fa401d)).
  - The documented Control UI baseline is Safari 26.2 or later, Chrome 147 or later, and Firefox 150 or later. The Node CLI and Gateway requirements are unchanged. [Platform requirements](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168595), [platform plan](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168114). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Package-only create, validate, build, dev and inspect commands remain available while the Gateway runs. Offline removal of adopted Claw ownership keeps the original agent and workspace. [Installed Claw state ownership](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168586). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Startup and package installation now require stock Bun 1.4.4 or newer. The [Bun compatibility guide](/install/bun-compatibility#requirements) explains the remaining saved-session and QUIC limitations. [#171627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171627) and the updated requirement in [#184336](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184336). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
  - Existing Kubernetes deployments using the read-only-root recipe need the writable cache setting from the updated manifest with their image upgrade. Updating the image alone can leave the Gateway unable to start. [#172377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172377), reported in [#172355](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172355). Thanks to [@saftall](https://github.com/saftall) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Source builders can package a standalone Bun-based build. [#172384](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172384). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Initial setup applies supported changes through the running Gateway and creates starter workspace files when the agent first runs. [#185109](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/185109) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  </details>

- Logs now repeat fewer warnings for an already-known conversation problem, while keeping current failures visible. This does not restore turns that were previously blocked.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - This change does not bring back conversation turns already blocked by a previous context error. [Steered conversation context](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168394), [report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168014). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@robertoronderosjr](https://github.com/robertoronderosjr).
  - A previously recorded conversation-context problem now warns once rather than repeating on each later run; blocked turns remain blocked. [#167595](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167595), [related reports #163566](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/163566) and [#135628](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/135628). Thanks to [jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman), [Clode2026](https://github.com/Clode2026), and [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Old helper-permission warnings now appear once per run instead of repeating with every recovery check in [#170120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170120). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Warn once per unchanged oversized bootstrap file, revision and limit while keeping truncation details available in `/context`. [#171074](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171074) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Connection access logs now include the sign-in method as ordinary information rather than a warning; warning-only logs will not retain those entries. [#171144](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171144) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Successful Gateway requests under one second no longer fill ordinary logs; failures and slower requests remain visible. [#171150](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171150) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  </details>

- Updates and Doctor now get past more temporary maintenance failures and show a clearer reason when recovery stops. An unattended startup failure still needs an explicit repair request; it does not launch a repair agent automatically.

  <details class="release-source-toggle release-source-inline">
  <summary>More details</summary>

  - Skip a second npm install when the updated updater receives an unsafe-schema refusal, preserving the original recovery guidance. Older running updaters still need the [manual Windows recovery path](/platforms/windows#updating-from-2026-9-4) for the reported 2026.9.4 upgrade. [#171164](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171164), [report #171004](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171004). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@TommyLei666](https://github.com/TommyLei666).
  - Failed post-update verification records available child failure details, and repair and cleanup explain when the Gateway must be stopped first. Cleanup preview remains available while it is running. [#171252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171252), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [yoyo837](https://github.com/yoyo837) for [reporting](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171096).
  - Backups identify the database from their saved copy instead of probing the live database while work is being written. [#171295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171295), thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Building the Control UI from different source folders no longer produces a false update-check rejection. [#171398](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171398), thanks to [fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
  - After an unattended update or foreground startup fails, repair now waits for an explicit request instead of starting on its own. [#171677](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171677) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks to [mwbroxson](https://github.com/mwbroxson) for [reporting the issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171191).
  - An update can resume its progress display after temporary Doctor maintenance. [#171454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171454) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Thanks to [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for [reporting the issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171409).
  - Name a failed candidate snapshot check in the update headline and retry guidance. [#171457](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171457) by [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Thanks to [DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) for [reporting the issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171408).
  - Keep the `openclaw gateway restart` reminder when an update with `--no-restart` changes both core and plugins. [#171687](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171687) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
  - Let managed updates wait through temporary database maintenance and retain recovery permission if that wait expires. Actual data-risk refusals still block recovery. [#171699](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171699) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks to [fdovarela](https://github.com/fdovarela) for [reporting the issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171376).
  - If a filesystem cannot support the requested SQLite no-copy-on-write repair, Doctor stops with an explanation and leaves the original database alone. The [reported host issue](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171933) is separate from the [Docker image prerequisites](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171930). [#171991](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171991) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with thanks to reporter [kitiyeung](https://github.com/kitiyeung) and [skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223) for the preflight suggestion.
  - When a repair worker fails, update status now receives a reason instead of an unexplained stop. [#171781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171781), addressing [#169999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169999). Thanks to [SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
  - Doctor can finish checks and admitted repairs when a child process closes its input early. If Doctor's own output closes, it exits with status 141 after admitted repairs and Gateway restoration. [#171780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171780) follows the narrower [Codex probe fix](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171248). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [dtump](https://github.com/dtump) for the [original report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171169), [joshpetras](https://github.com/joshpetras) for ARM64 corroboration, and [cbhawthorne84](https://github.com/cbhawthorne84) for the [earlier maintenance report](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/156712).
  - Asking the setup assistant to diagnose a problem only inspects it. To make repairs, run `openclaw doctor --fix` on the Gateway computer. [#171960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171960), with thanks to co-author [vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
  - A verified completed installation can settle its unfinished update record and allow the next update to proceed. Other unfinished states can still require `openclaw update repair`. [#171993](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171993), reported in [#171955](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171955). Thanks to [steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [teetee2tone](https://github.com/teetee2tone).
  - A native update can continue after Doctor finishes quickly instead of treating its completed check as a failure. [#172132](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172132) by [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  - Automatic repair now checks whether the Gateway answers before reporting recovery. [#167080](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167080). Thanks to [RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  - Doctor loads its settings before checking plugins, so a non-interactive run no longer reports a false Codex registration error. [#170445](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170445). Thanks to [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  </details>
